================================================================================
  BLUEPRINT ARSITEKTUR & ATURAN BISNIS: POLARIS AI GENERATION & VISUAL ENGINE
================================================================================

1. FILOSOFI & LATAR BELAKANG BISNIS:
------------------------------------
A. Masalah Fundamental Generator Gambar AI Konvensional:
   - Model generatif murni (DALL-E 3 / Midjourney / Flux) TIDAK memiliki layout deterministik.
   - AI selalu menghasilkan teks cacat, typo, huruf terbalik, dan bahasa asing tidak terbaca.
   - Wajah anggota dewan jika digambar oleh AI menghasilkan deformasi (kartunis/deepfake),
     yang berisiko fatal terhadap reputasi politik dan melanggar etika kampanye.
   - Logo partai politik (Golkar, PDI-P, Gerindra, dll) atau lembaga (DPR/DPRD) selalu
     terdistorsi dan melanggar pakem resmi lambang negara/partai jika digenerate AI.

B. Paradigma Solusi: 4-Layer Composite Architecture:
   - LAYER 1 [Dasar / AI-Generated Canvas]:
     AI hanya bertugas menghasilkan karya fotografi dokumenter 35mm / risograph
     yang realistis dan humanis, TANPA TEKS, TANPA LOGO, dan dengan zona ruang
     kosong (negative space) yang terkontrol secara ketat.
   - LAYER 2 [Tengah / Subjek Asli Tokoh & Isolasi]:
     Foto fisik asli anggota dewan dari Katalog Foto Profil (PNG transparan)
     ditempelkan ke kanvas dengan bayangan kontak alami (ambient drop-shadow) dan
     penyamaan pencahayaan (tone matching via Sharp). Wajah 100% otentik.
   - LAYER 3 [Atas / Tipografi & Data Deterministik]:
     Seluruh teks judul (Bebas Neue / Anton / Oswald), kutipan pernyataan, nama dewan,
     gelar, kolom editorial, dan barcode dirender langsung oleh kode program via Satori + SVG.
     100% tajam, pixel-perfect, bebas halusinasi.
   - LAYER 4 [Paling Depan / Logo Resmi Terkunci]:
     Logo partai dan logo lembaga (DPR RI/DPRD) ditempatkan di sudut aman
     (corner-locked) dari file SVG/PNG resmi dan dilarang keras digenerate AI.

2. DUAL-ENGINE AI IMAGE GENERATION (GOOGLE IMAGEN 3 + OPENAI DALL-E):
---------------------------------------------------------------------
- Primary Generator: Google Imagen 3 (`imagen-3.0-generate-002`)
  - Mendukung negative prompt native: melarang teks, watermark, angka, logo, dan distorsi subjek.
  - Rasio aspek native 4:5 (1080x1350px) dan 9:16.
- Automatic Failover: OpenAI DALL-E 3 (`1024x1792` / `1024x1024`) jika kuota Google habis atau terjadi error API.
- Prompt Compiler menyuntikkan arahan sinematik: "atmospheric 35mm archival photography, editorial lighting, cinematic texture, high visual depth, clean empty negative space".

3. METODOLOGI "ATM" & 22 BLUEPRINT ARKETIPE POSTER MODULAR:
------------------------------------------------------------
Sistem membedah referensi desain menjadi 22 Arketipe Desain Terstruktur:
   1. POP_OUT_HERO           : TPL-01 "The Edge" (Subjek menerobos bingkai foto, judul besar di belakang bahu)
   2. STREET_JOURNALISM      : TPL-02 "POSTER PKL" (Duotone risograph merah-hitam, montase jurnalisme 3-kolom)
   3. STATEMENT_PROFILE      : TPL-03 "Steve Jobs" (Potret tokoh karismatik berwibawa, kutipan visi, monokrom/navy)
   4. RETRO_CAMPAIGN         : TPL-04 "Poster Acara" (Vintage 70s-80s, badge edukasi publik, warna retro)
   5. TORN_PAPER_NEWS        : TPL-05 "Techno Veritas" (Broadsheet koran sobek tengah, 3 lingkaran data stat, 2 thumbnail)
   6. RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION : TPL-06 "Earthquake Infografis" (Split 2-kolom mitigasi bencana, circular magnifier inset)
   7. MODULAR_GRID_CARDS     : TPL-07 "5 Modern Font Pairings" (5 kartu framework bernomor 01-05 dengan aksen blok)
   8. CONSTRUCTIVIST_BAUHAUS : TPL-08 "Design Trends 2026" (Manifesto konstruktivis, pita diagonal, tipografi brutal)
   9. THRESHOLD_RISOGRAPH    : TPL-09 "Design Poster / Oke Gas" (Kontras ekstrem kuning-hitam stensil sablon)
  10. SWISS_DUOTONE_EDITORIAL: TPL-10 "Modern Typography / Swiss" (Grid presisi internasional, hitam-putih minimalis)
  11. ISOMETRIC_CREATIVE     : TPL-11 "Creative Poster" (Badge stiker 3D pill modern, gradien kreatif)
  12. STORYBOOK_ILLUSTRATED  : TPL-12 "Fishing Poster" (Bingkai ornamen klasik, serif pastoral, konservasi lingkungan)
  13. TYPOGRAPHIC_MASK       : TPL-13 "Beautiful Poster" (Mask tipografi raksasa, white space luas dan bersih)
  14. KINETIC_MOTION         : TPL-14 "Fly High" (Garis kecepatan dinamis, sudut miring 12 derajat, oranye elektrik)
  15. EXECUTIVE_SEMINAR      : TPL-15 "Seminar Nasional" (Midnight navy & gold luxury, simposium kebijakan tinggi)
  16. TORN_VISION            : TPL-16 "Hold Your Vision" (Koyakan kertas diagonal pemisah gelap-terang)
  17. GALLERY_EXHIBITION     : TPL-17 "Chasing Portraits" (Plakat kurasi museum seni, tipografi editorial halus)
  18. POP_MAGAZINE_COLLAGE   : TPL-18 "Hello Tokyo" (Zine jalanan neo-tokyo, multi-box street pop culture)
  19. POLAROID_ADVENTURE     : TPL-19 "Go Wild" (Ekspedisi blusukan daerah, kartu polaroid, earth-tone hangat)
  20. VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE: TPL-20 "The S News" (Koran antik sejarah krem, narasi panjang, multi-photo)
  21. BROADSHEET_NEWSPAPER   : TPL-21 "Epoch Times" (Koran investigasi 3 kolom padat, lead editorial kuat)
  22. TECHNICAL_GRID_CALLOUT : TPL-22 "Hazard Callout" (Hazard yellow, grid teknis tajam, kutipan lugas)
  23. STANDARD_EDITORIAL     : Layout default fallback yang bersih, elegan, dan adaptif untuk semua topik.

4. SINGLE-PASS MULTI-CROP STRATEGY (1x CALL AI IMAGE -> BANYAK SLOT):
----------------------------------------------------------------------
- Untuk poster kompleks dengan 2-5 sub-foto, sistem TIDAK memanggil generator AI berkali-kali.
- Sharp `MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions()` memotong koordinat spesifik dari 1 kanvas AI dasar
  atau mengambil dari `tenantMembers.photoGallery` secara lokal (< 50ms, Rp 0) dan menyuntikkannya ke tag `<img>` Satori.

5. DETERMINISTIC SATORI + SHARP COMPOSITION PIPELINE:
-----------------------------------------------------
- Tipografi diukur dan dirender menggunakan font asli (.ttf): Bebas Neue, Anton, Oswald, Plus Jakarta Sans, Playfair Display.
- Menghasilkan output WebP/PNG beresolusi tinggi (1080x1350px) dalam waktu < 800ms.
================================================================================

import { pgTable, uuid, varchar, timestamp, text, boolean, integer, jsonb, unique } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { legislativeLevelEnum, operatorAssignmentStatusEnum } from './enums.js';

export const electoralDistricts = pgTable('electoral_districts', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  dapilCode: varchar('dapil_code', { length: 50 }).notNull().unique(),
  dapilName: varchar('dapil_name', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  provinceName: varchar('province_name', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  regencyCoverage: text('regency_coverage').array().notNull(),
  totalVoters: integer('total_voters'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const tenantMembers = pgTable('tenant_members', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull().unique(),
  passwordHash: varchar('password_hash', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  fullName: varchar('full_name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
  phoneNumber: varchar('phone_number', { length: 30 }).notNull(),
  partyAffiliation: varchar('party_affiliation', { length: 100 }),
  legislativeLevel: legislativeLevelEnum('legislative_level').notNull(),
  electoralDistrictId: uuid('electoral_district_id').references(() => electoralDistricts.id, { onDelete: 'restrict' }),
  customDapilName: varchar('custom_dapil_name', { length: 100 }),
  personalCoverage: text('personal_coverage').array().default([]),
  commissionId: uuid('commission_id'),
  commissionName: varchar('commission_name', { length: 150 }),
  photoUrl: text('photo_url'),
  photoGallery: text('photo_gallery').array().default([]),
  gender: varchar('gender', { length: 20 }),
  birthDate: varchar('birth_date', { length: 30 }),
  education: text('education'),
  courses: text('courses').array(),
  issueInterests: text('issue_interests').array(),
  isVerified: boolean('is_verified').default(false).notNull(),
  accountStatus: varchar('account_status', { length: 30 }).default('ACTIVE').notNull(),
  username: varchar('username', { length: 50 }).unique(),
  mustChangePassword: boolean('must_change_password').default(false).notNull(),
  temporaryPasswordPlaintextPreview: varchar('temporary_password_plaintext_preview', { length: 100 }),
  passwordChangedAt: timestamp('password_changed_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  internalNotes: text('internal_notes'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const emailOtps = pgTable('email_otps', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  otpCode: varchar('otp_code', { length: 10 }).notNull(),
  expiresAt: timestamp('expires_at', { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  isUsed: boolean('is_used').default(false).notNull(),
  attempts: integer('attempts').default(0).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const systemAdmins = pgTable('system_admins', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull().unique(),
  passwordHash: varchar('password_hash', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  fullName: varchar('full_name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
  role: varchar('role', { length: 50 }).default('SUPERADMIN').notNull(),
  isActive: boolean('is_active').default(true).notNull(),
  avatarUrl: text('avatar_url'),
  lastLoginAt: timestamp('last_login_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const masterPoliticalParties = pgTable('master_political_parties', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  code: varchar('code', { length: 30 }).notNull().unique(),
  name: varchar('name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
  ballotNumber: integer('ballot_number'),
  primaryColor: varchar('primary_color', { length: 20 }).notNull(),
  secondaryColor: varchar('secondary_color', { length: 20 }),
  logoUrl: text('logo_url'),
  description: text('description'),
  isActive: boolean('is_active').default(true).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const masterCommissions = pgTable('master_commissions', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  legislativeLevel: legislativeLevelEnum('legislative_level').notNull(),
  code: varchar('code', { length: 50 }).notNull(),
  name: varchar('name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
  focusAreas: text('focus_areas').array(),
  partnerMinistries: text('partner_ministries').array(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const tenantActivityLogs = pgTable('tenant_activity_logs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }).notNull(),
  activityType: varchar('activity_type', { length: 50 }).notNull(),
  category: varchar('category', { length: 30 }).default('AUTH').notNull(),
  description: text('description').notNull(),
  ipAddress: varchar('ip_address', { length: 45 }),
  userAgent: text('user_agent'),
  metadata: jsonb('metadata'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const operatorTenantAssignments = pgTable('operator_tenant_assignments', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  operatorId: uuid('operator_id').notNull().references(() => systemAdmins.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  canDirectPublish: boolean('can_direct_publish').default(false).notNull(),
  status: operatorAssignmentStatusEnum('status').default('ACTIVE').notNull(),
  notes: text('notes'),
  assignedByAdminId: uuid('assigned_by_admin_id').references(() => systemAdmins.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  unqOperatorTenant: unique().on(t.operatorId, t.tenantId),
}));

export const operatorAuditLogs = pgTable('operator_audit_logs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  operatorId: uuid('operator_id').notNull().references(() => systemAdmins.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  action: varchar('action', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  targetEntity: varchar('target_entity', { length: 50 }).notNull(),
  targetEntityId: uuid('target_entity_id'),
  details: jsonb('details'),
  ipAddress: varchar('ip_address', { length: 50 }),
  userAgent: text('user_agent'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

import { pgTable, uuid, varchar, text, integer, timestamp, jsonb, bigint, unique } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { tenantMembers, systemAdmins } from './identity.js';
import { contentStatusEnum, assetTypeEnum, contentCreationContextEnum } from './enums.js';

export const contentPublications = pgTable('content_publications', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  title: varchar('title', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  slug: varchar('slug', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  excerpt: text('excerpt').notNull(),
  bodyContentMarkdown: text('body_content_markdown').notNull(),
  wordCount: integer('word_count').notNull(),
  status: contentStatusEnum('status').default('DRAFT').notNull(),
  canonicalUrl: text('canonical_url').notNull(),
  commentCount: integer('comment_count').default(0).notNull(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id'),
  createdByOperatorId: uuid('created_by_operator_id').references(() => systemAdmins.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  createdContext: contentCreationContextEnum('created_context').default('MEMBER').notNull(),
  publishedAt: timestamp('published_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({

  unqTenantSlug: unique().on(t.tenantId, t.slug),
}));

export const mediaAssets = pgTable('media_assets', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  publicationId: uuid('publication_id').notNull().references(() => contentPublications.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  assetType: assetTypeEnum('asset_type').notNull(),
  r2StorageUrl: text('r2_storage_url').notNull(),
  cdnPublicUrl: text('cdn_public_url').notNull(),
  promptUsed: text('prompt_used'),
  mimeType: varchar('mime_type', { length: 50 }).default('image/webp').notNull(),
  fileSizeBytes: bigint('file_size_bytes', { mode: 'number' }).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const socialSyndicationPacks = pgTable('social_syndication_packs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  publicationId: uuid('publication_id').notNull().unique().references(() => contentPublications.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  instagramCaption: text('instagram_caption'),
  twitterThreads: jsonb('twitter_threads'),
  whatsappBroadcastText: text('whatsapp_broadcast_text'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

import { pgTable, uuid, varchar, text, boolean, timestamp, unique, jsonb, integer, index } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { tenantMembers } from './identity.js';
import { socialPlatformEnum } from './enums.js';

export const portalConfigs = pgTable('portal_configs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().unique().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  subdomainSlug: varchar('subdomain_slug', { length: 63 }).notNull().unique(),
  customDomain: varchar('custom_domain', { length: 255 }).unique(),
  customDomainStatus: varchar('custom_domain_status', { length: 30 }).default('UNVERIFIED').notNull(),
  dnsVerificationToken: varchar('dns_verification_token', { length: 64 }),
  dnsVerificationExpiresAt: timestamp('dns_verification_expires_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  domainVerifiedAt: timestamp('domain_verified_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  isActive: boolean('is_active').default(true).notNull(),
  metaTitle: varchar('meta_title', { length: 150 }),
  metaDescription: text('meta_description'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const portalThemeSettings = pgTable('portal_theme_settings', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  portalId: uuid('portal_id').notNull().unique().references(() => portalConfigs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  primaryHexColor: varchar('primary_hex_color', { length: 7 }).default('#1890ff').notNull(),
  secondaryHexColor: varchar('secondary_hex_color', { length: 7 }).default('#001529').notNull(),
  fontFamily: varchar('font_family', { length: 50 }).default('Inter, sans-serif').notNull(),
  heroBannerUrl: text('hero_banner_url'),
  officialPhotoUrl: text('official_photo_url'),
  headlineTagline: varchar('headline_tagline', { length: 255 }),
  bioBiography: text('bio_biography'),
  layoutTemplateId: varchar('layout_template_id', { length: 50 }).default('standard-default').notNull(),
  layoutSchema: jsonb('layout_schema'),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const portalDocuments = pgTable('portal_documents', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  portalId: uuid('portal_id').notNull().references(() => portalConfigs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  title: varchar('title', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  docType: varchar('doc_type', { length: 50 }).default('REPORT_TRIWULAN').notNull(),
  description: text('description'),
  fileUrl: text('file_url').notNull(),
  fileSizeBytes: integer('file_size_bytes'),
  downloadCount: integer('download_count').default(0).notNull(),
  publishedAt: timestamp('published_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const socialLinks = pgTable('social_links', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  portalId: uuid('portal_id').notNull().references(() => portalConfigs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  platform: socialPlatformEnum('platform').notNull(),
  profileUrl: text('profile_url').notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  unqPortalPlatform: unique().on(t.portalId, t.platform),
}));

export const portalNewsletterSubscribers = pgTable('portal_newsletter_subscribers', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  portalId: uuid('portal_id').notNull().references(() => portalConfigs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  isActive: boolean('is_active').default(true).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  unqPortalEmail: unique().on(t.portalId, t.email),
  idxPortalSubscribers: index('idx_portal_subscribers').on(t.portalId, t.createdAt),
}));

import { pgTable, uuid, varchar, text, integer, real, timestamp, boolean, jsonb, index, check } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { tenantMembers } from './identity.js';
import {
  researchJobStatusEnum,
  questionIntentEnum,
  questionStatusEnum,
  sourceTypeEnum,
  publisherTypeEnum,
  sourceLifecycleEnum,
  claimStatusEnum,
  supportTypeEnum,
} from './enums.js';

/**
 * 1. RESEARCH JOBS: Sesi / Pekerjaan Riset Otonom POLARIS
 */
export const researchJobs = pgTable('research_jobs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  topic: varchar('topic', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  userPrompt: text('user_prompt').notNull(),
  targetRegion: varchar('target_region', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  status: researchJobStatusEnum('status').default('PENDING').notNull(),
  evidenceSufficient: boolean('evidence_sufficient').default(false).notNull(),
  qcPassed: boolean('qc_passed').default(false).notNull(),
  summaryReport: text('summary_report'),
  metadata: jsonb('metadata').default(sql`'{}'::jsonb`),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  tenantStatusIdx: index('idx_research_jobs_tenant_status').on(t.tenantId, t.status),
}));

/**
 * 2. RESEARCH QUESTIONS: Pertanyaan Riset Berstruktur (Causal / Factual Requirements)
 */
export const researchQuestions = pgTable('research_questions', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  question: text('question').notNull(),
  intentCategory: questionIntentEnum('intent_category').default('POLICY_EVALUATION').notNull(),
  temporalBound: varchar('temporal_bound', { length: 50 }),
  geographicScope: varchar('geographic_scope', { length: 100 }),
  minRequiredEvidence: integer('min_required_evidence').default(1).notNull(),
  status: questionStatusEnum('status').default('UNRESOLVED').notNull(),
  resolutionSummary: text('resolution_summary'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobIdx: index('idx_research_questions_job').on(t.researchJobId),
}));

/**
 * 3. SOURCES: Registri Sumber Universal & Status Siklus Hidup
 */
export const sources = pgTable('sources', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  url: text('url').notNull(),
  canonicalUrl: text('canonical_url'),
  sourceType: sourceTypeEnum('source_type').notNull(),
  publisherName: varchar('publisher_name', { length: 255 }),
  publisherType: publisherTypeEnum('publisher_type').default('MEDIA').notNull(),
  publisherCredibilityScore: real('publisher_credibility_score').default(0.5).notNull(),
  doi: varchar('doi', { length: 100 }),
  isPeerReviewed: boolean('is_peer_reviewed'),
  hasRetractionsOrCorrections: boolean('has_retractions_or_corrections').default(false).notNull(),
  lifecycleStatus: sourceLifecycleEnum('lifecycle_status').default('DISCOVERED').notNull(),
  rejectionReason: text('rejection_reason'),
  retrievedAt: timestamp('retrieved_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  urlIdx: index('idx_sources_url').on(t.url),
  lifecycleIdx: index('idx_sources_lifecycle').on(t.lifecycleStatus),
}));

/**
 * 4. RESEARCH JOB SOURCES: Relasi Banyak-ke-Banyak Antara Job dan Sumber
 */
export const researchJobSources = pgTable('research_job_sources', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  relevanceScore: real('relevance_score').default(0.5).notNull(),
  queryUsed: text('query_used'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobSourceIdx: index('idx_research_job_sources_pair').on(t.researchJobId, t.sourceId),
}));

/**
 * 5. SOURCE VERSIONS: Snapshot Isi Sumber & Bukti Audit Trail
 */
export const sourceVersions = pgTable('source_versions', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  contentHash: varchar('content_hash', { length: 64 }).notNull(),
  title: text('title').notNull(),
  rawContent: text('raw_content'),
  extractedText: text('extracted_text').notNull(),
  httpStatus: integer('http_status').default(200).notNull(),
  fetchedAt: timestamp('fetched_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  sourceHashIdx: index('idx_source_versions_source_hash').on(t.sourceId, t.contentHash),
}));

/**
 * 6. SOURCE ASSESSMENTS: Riwayat Penilaian Relevansi & Kredibilitas
 */
export const sourceAssessments = pgTable('source_assessments', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  substantiveScore: real('substantive_score').notNull(),
  methodologyScore: real('methodology_score'),
  recencyScore: real('recency_score').notNull(),
  overallScore: real('overall_score').notNull(),
  assessmentReason: text('assessment_reason').notNull(),
  isAccepted: boolean('is_accepted').notNull(),
  evaluatedAt: timestamp('evaluated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

/**
 * 7. EVIDENCE SPANS: Petikan Kutipan & Bukti Faktual Granular
 */
export const evidenceSpans = pgTable('evidence_spans', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  sourceVersionId: uuid('source_version_id').references(() => sourceVersions.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  verbatimQuote: text('verbatim_quote').notNull(),
  contextSnippet: text('context_snippet').notNull(),
  sectionOrPage: varchar('section_or_page', { length: 100 }),
  confidenceScore: real('confidence_score').default(1.0).notNull(),
  isVerified: boolean('is_verified').default(true).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  sourceIdx: index('idx_evidence_spans_source').on(t.sourceId),
}));

/**
 * 8. CLAIMS: Klaim Faktual / Argumen Kebijakan yang Diselidiki
 */
export const claims = pgTable('claims', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  researchQuestionId: uuid('research_question_id').references(() => researchQuestions.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  claimStatement: text('claim_statement').notNull(),
  status: claimStatusEnum('status').default('UNSUPPORTED').notNull(),
  confidenceScore: real('confidence_score').default(0.0).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobClaimIdx: index('idx_claims_job').on(t.researchJobId),
}));

/**
 * 9. CLAIM EVIDENCE: Pemetaan Relasi Klaim dengan Bukti
 */
export const claimEvidence = pgTable('claim_evidence', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  claimId: uuid('claim_id').notNull().references(() => claims.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  evidenceSpanId: uuid('evidence_span_id').notNull().references(() => evidenceSpans.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  supportType: supportTypeEnum('support_type').default('SUPPORTS').notNull(),
  relationshipExplanation: text('relationship_explanation'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  claimEvidencePairIdx: index('idx_claim_evidence_pair').on(t.claimId, t.evidenceSpanId),
}));

/**
 * 10. RESEARCH OUTPUTS: Hasil Naskah Riset & Bibliografi Lengkap
 */
export const researchOutputs = pgTable('research_outputs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  title: varchar('title', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  executiveSummary: text('executive_summary').notNull(),
  markdownBody: text('markdown_body').notNull(),
  bibliographyJson: jsonb('bibliography_json').notNull(),
  qcReport: jsonb('qc_report').notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

import { pgTable, varchar, text, integer, timestamp, jsonb, index } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';

/**
 * Tabel poster_reference_blueprints: Menyimpan blueprint referensi desain poster
 * yang dapat ditambahkan dan dianalisis secara dinamis via AI Vision.
 */
export const posterReferenceBlueprints = pgTable(
  'poster_reference_blueprints',
  {
    id: varchar('id', { length: 100 }).primaryKey(),
    name: varchar('name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
    referenceFile: text('reference_file').notNull(),
    archetype: varchar('archetype', { length: 60 }).notNull(),
    description: text('description').notNull(),
    tags: text('tags').array().notNull(),
    aspectRatio: varchar('aspect_ratio', { length: 10 }).default('4:5').notNull(),
    colorPalette: jsonb('color_palette').notNull(),
    scenePromptHint: text('scene_prompt_hint').notNull(),
    negativeSpaceZone: text('negative_space_zone').notNull(),
    slotManifest: jsonb('slot_manifest').notNull(),
    fixedConstraints: jsonb('fixed_constraints'),
    variationRules: jsonb('variation_rules'),
    status: varchar('status', { length: 20 }).default('DRAFT').notNull(), // 'DRAFT' | 'APPROVED' | 'ARCHIVED'
    version: integer('version').default(1).notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
    updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (table) => ({
    statusIdx: index('idx_poster_blueprints_status').on(table.status),
    archetypeIdx: index('idx_poster_blueprints_archetype').on(table.archetype),
  })
);

export type PosterReferenceBlueprintRecord = typeof posterReferenceBlueprints.$inferSelect;
export type NewPosterReferenceBlueprintRecord = typeof posterReferenceBlueprints.$inferInsert;

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/config/image-provider.config.ts (31 baris, 1.3 KB) ---

/**
 * Konfigurasi Provider Generator Gambar (Layer 1 Background Canvas)
 * 
 * Pengaturan Hardcode:
 * Anda dapat mengubah konstanta HARDCODED_IMAGE_PROVIDER di bawah ini:
 * - 'GOOGLE_IMAGEN' : Menggunakan Google Imagen 3 (imagen-3.0-generate-002) - Default
 * - 'OPENAI_DALLE'   : Menggunakan OpenAI DALL-E 3 / chatgpt-image-latest
 * 
 * Sistem juga mendukung override dinamis via file .env:
 * IMAGE_GENERATOR_PROVIDER=GOOGLE_IMAGEN | OPENAI_DALLE
 */

export type ImageGeneratorProvider = 'GOOGLE_IMAGEN' | 'OPENAI_DALLE';

// ============================================================================
// HARDCODED SWITCH (Ubah nilai di bawah ini untuk mengganti AI utama gambar)
// ============================================================================
export const HARDCODED_IMAGE_PROVIDER: ImageGeneratorProvider = 'GOOGLE_IMAGEN';

/**
 * Menentukan provider gambar aktif dengan prioritas:
 * 1. Environment variable IMAGE_GENERATOR_PROVIDER (jika disetel valid)
 * 2. Nilai hardcode HARDCODED_IMAGE_PROVIDER
 */
export function getActiveImageProvider(): ImageGeneratorProvider {
  const envProvider = process.env.IMAGE_GENERATOR_PROVIDER?.toUpperCase() as ImageGeneratorProvider | undefined;
  if (envProvider === 'GOOGLE_IMAGEN' || envProvider === 'OPENAI_DALLE') {
    return envProvider;
  }
  return HARDCODED_IMAGE_PROVIDER;
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/adapters/google-imagen.adapter.ts (196 baris, 6.4 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { GoogleGenAI } from '@google/genai';
import { googleGenAiClient } from '../client.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';

export interface GenerateGoogleImagenParams {
  prompt: string;
  aspectRatio?: '4:5' | '1:1' | '16:9' | '9:16' | '3:4' | '4:3';
  tenantId?: string;
  stylePromptSuffix?: string;
  imageNegativeAllow?: string[];
}

export class GoogleImagenAdapter {
  private client: GoogleGenAI | null;

  constructor(apiKey?: string) {
    if (apiKey) {
      this.client = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });
    } else {
      this.client = googleGenAiClient;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Menghasilkan kanvas dasar fotografis/artistik Layer 1 menggunakan Google Imagen 3.
   * Dilengkapi native negative prompt untuk menjamin nol artefak teks & logo.
   */
  public async generateImage(
    params: GenerateGoogleImagenParams
  ): Promise<{ temporaryImageUrl: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const apiKey =
      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ||
      process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ||
      process.env.GOOGLE_GENAI_API_KEY;

    if (!this.client && apiKey) {
      this.client = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });
    }

    if (!this.client) {
      throw new Error(
        '[GoogleImagenAdapter] GEMINI_API_KEY belum disetel pada file environment (.env).'
      );
    }

    const preferredModel =
      process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL || 'imagen-3.0-generate-002';
    const aspectRatio = params.aspectRatio || '4:5';

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'google-imagen-generation',
      model: preferredModel,
      tenantId: params.tenantId,
      metadata: {
        promptLength: params.prompt.length,
        requestedAspectRatio: aspectRatio,
      },
    });

    try {
      // Prompt conditioning adaptif sesuai gaya blueprint
      const defaultSuffix = 'editorial lighting, authentic atmosphere, high visual depth, clean empty negative space';
      const suffix = params.stylePromptSuffix || defaultSuffix;
      const refinedPrompt = `${params.prompt}, ${suffix}`;
      let temporaryImageUrl: string | undefined;

      // Filter negative prompt yang menghormati elemen yang diizinkan blueprint
      const allowed = params.imageNegativeAllow || [];
      const negativeList = [
        'person in center',
        'human portrait in center',
        'floating body',
        'man in center',
        'double subject',
        'cropped head',
        'text',
        'words',
        'letters',
        'typography',
        'newspaper clippings',
        'headlines',
        'mastheads',
        'numbers',
        'logo',
        'emblem',
        'watermark',
        'signature',
        'cartoonish',
        'low quality',
        'oversaturated',
        'ui cards',
        'text boxes',
        'info cards',
        'rectangular overlays',
        'layout grids',
        'hex codes',
        'color swatches',
        'poster template',
        'graphic design layout',
        'labels',
        'captions',
        'gibberish text',
        'big bold headlines',
      ];
      if (!allowed.includes('banner')) negativeList.push('banner');
      if (!allowed.includes('frame')) negativeList.push('frame');
      if (!allowed.includes('border')) negativeList.push('border');

      const negativePrompt = negativeList.join(', ');

      // Jalur 1: Google AI Studio Multimodal Image Model (gemini-3.1-flash-image / Imagen via Developer API)
      const studioImageModel =
        process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL && !process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL.startsWith('imagen-')
          ? process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL
          : 'gemini-3.1-flash-image';

      try {
        const contentResponse = await this.client.models.generateContent({
          model: studioImageModel,
          contents: `${refinedPrompt}. Visual aspect ratio: ${aspectRatio}. Generate a pure, textless photograph or illustration artwork only. DO NOT design a poster, DO NOT draw any UI cards, boxes, frames, banners, or graphic layout templates, and DO NOT render any letters, words, numbers, or hex color codes anywhere in the image. Strict negative constraint: clean background, no text, no typography, no words, no human portrait in center, no double subjects.`,
        });

        const parts = contentResponse.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts || [];
        for (const p of parts) {
          if (p.inlineData && p.inlineData.data) {
            const mime = p.inlineData.mimeType || 'image/jpeg';
            temporaryImageUrl = `data:${mime};base64,${p.inlineData.data}`;
            break;
          }
        }
      } catch (studioErr: any) {
        console.warn(
          `[GoogleImagenAdapter] Jalur AI Studio (${studioImageModel}) memberi sinyal: ${studioErr.message}. Beralih ke jalur Vertex Imagen...`
        );
      }

      // Jalur 2: Vertex AI Enterprise Imagen (imagen-3.0-generate-002 / imagen-4.0)
      if (!temporaryImageUrl) {
        const enterpriseModel =
          process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL && process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL.startsWith('imagen-')
            ? process.env.GOOGLE_IMAGE_MODEL
            : 'imagen-3.0-generate-002';

        const response = await this.client.models.generateImages({
          model: enterpriseModel,
          prompt: refinedPrompt,
          config: {
            numberOfImages: 1,
            aspectRatio: aspectRatio as any,
            outputMimeType: 'image/jpeg',
            negativePrompt,
          },
        });

        const generated = response.generatedImages?.[0];
        const imageBytes = generated?.image?.imageBytes;
        if (imageBytes) {
          temporaryImageUrl = `data:image/jpeg;base64,${imageBytes}`;
        }
      }

      if (!temporaryImageUrl) {
        throw new Error(
          'Google Imagen / Gemini AI tidak mengembalikan bytes gambar yang valid.'
        );
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 1000,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: {
          model: preferredModel,
          aspectRatio,
          dataUrlPrefix: temporaryImageUrl.slice(0, 50),
        },
      });

      return {
        temporaryImageUrl,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message || String(error),
      });

      throw new Error(
        `[GoogleImagenError] Gagal menghasilkan gambar Imagen 3: ${error.message || String(error)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/types.ts (225 baris, 7.2 KB) ---

/**
 * POLARIS Creative Poster Engine v2.0 - Multi-Block Collage Contracts
 * Mendefinisikan tipe data kontrak untuk arketipe poster,
 * slot koordinat deterministik, multi-block editorial grid, dan prompt compiler.
 */

import type {
  CreativePosterArchetype,
  PosterAspectRatio,
  SubjectCompositionMode,
  PosterLayoutVariant,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  PosterCardArrangement,
  ZoneRect,
  SurfaceMode,
  CompositionZone,
  CompositionPlan,
  BlueprintFixedConstraints,
  BlueprintVariationRules,
  ResolvedColorTokens,
  LayoutBlockSpec,
} from '@polaris/shared-types';
import {
  resolveColorAuthority,
  LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA,
} from '@polaris/shared-types';

export type {
  CreativePosterArchetype,
  PosterAspectRatio,
  SubjectCompositionMode,
  PosterLayoutVariant,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  PosterCardArrangement,
  ZoneRect,
  SurfaceMode,
  CompositionZone,
  CompositionPlan,
  BlueprintFixedConstraints,
  BlueprintVariationRules,
  ResolvedColorTokens,
  LayoutBlockSpec,
};
export {
  resolveColorAuthority,
  LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA,
};

export interface PosterSlotCoordinate {
  x: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari lebar kanvas)
  y: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari tinggi kanvas)
  w: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  h?: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  anchor?: 'top-left' | 'top-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-right' | 'center';
}

/**
 * Definisi Slot Teks Multi-Blok (Untuk layout koran/infografis kompleks)
 */
export interface EditorialTextBlockSpec {
  slotId: string; // e.g., 'left_positive', 'left_negative_box', 'center_giant_title', 'center_story', 'right_cause', 'bottom_banner'
  role: 'COLUMN_SECTION' | 'SOLID_COLOR_BOX' | 'GIANT_STACKED_TITLE' | 'BOTTOM_FULL_BANNER';
  coordinate: PosterSlotCoordinate;
  typography: {
    headerFont: 'Anton' | 'Bebas Neue' | 'Oswald';
    headerSizePx: number;
    headerColor: string;
    bodyFont: 'Plus Jakarta Sans' | 'Space Mono' | 'Oswald';
    bodySizePx: number;
    bodyColor: string;
    boxBgColor?: string;
    textAlign: 'left' | 'justify' | 'center';
  };
  constraints: {
    headerMaxChars: number;
    bodyMinChars: number;
    bodyMaxChars: number;
  };
  defaultReferenceHeader: string;
}

/**
 * Aturan Substitusi Subjek Visual 1:1 (Amati, Tiru, Modifikasi)
 */
export interface VisualCutoutSlotSpec {
  slotId: string; // e.g., 'upper_right_cutout', 'mid_left_hero_cutout', 'bottom_street_scene'
  spatialZonePrompt: string;
  originalReferenceSubject: string;
  photoTreatment: 'BW_HALFTONE_CUTOUT' | 'DUOTONE_RED_BLACK' | 'VINTAGE_WARM';
}

export interface SynthesizedEditorialCopy {
  mainBannerTitle: string;
  mainBannerSubheader: string;
  mainBannerDefinitionBody: string;
  blocks: Record<
    string,
    {
      header: string;
      body: string;
    }
  >;
  mappedVisualCutouts: Record<string, string>; // slotId -> English visual description
  // Field pendukung arketipe infografis & statistik dinamis:
  statCircles?: Array<{ topLabel: string; value: string; description: string }>;
  numberedItems?: Array<{ number: string; title: string; subtitle?: string; summary: string }>;

  // Modifikasi Dinamis Layout & Warna Aktif oleh AI:
  layoutVariant?: PosterLayoutVariant;
  headerPlacement?: PosterHeaderPlacement;
  cardArrangement?: PosterCardArrangement;
  colorMood?: string;
  primaryAccent?: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  cardBackground?: string;
  cardBorder?: string;
}

export interface PosterSlotManifest {
  canvas: {
    width: number;
    height: number;
    aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  };
  editorialBlocks?: EditorialTextBlockSpec[];
  visualCutoutSlots?: VisualCutoutSlotSpec[];
  slots: {
    // Zona Subjek Tokoh Dewan (Layer 2)
    subjectPhoto: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      popOutEffect?: boolean;       // Apakah kepala/bahu menembus bingkai luar
      popOutOverflowTopPx?: number; // Jarak tembus ke atas dalam pixel
      shadowOpacity?: number;       // 0.0 - 1.0
      shadowBlur?: number;          // Blur radius dalam pixel
      duotoneTint?: string;         // Hex warna jika menggunakan mode duotone
      harmonizeFilter?: 'GRAYSCALE_HIGH_CONTRAST' | 'NATURAL';
    };
    // Zona Judul Utama (Layer 3)
    headlineBlock: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      maxLines: number;
      fontFamily: 'Bebas Neue' | 'Anton' | 'Oswald';
      fontSizePx: number;
      textColor: string;
      bgColor?: string;             // Jika menggunakan banner solid block
      isUppercase?: boolean;
      interlockingWithSubject?: boolean; // Menimpa atau berada di balik pundak
    };
    // Zona Pernyataan / Kutipan Ringkas
    quoteStatement?: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      fontFamily: string;
      fontSizePx: number;
      textColor: string;
    };
    // Zona Identitas Anggota Dewan (Nama, Gelar, Jabatan)
    memberIdentity: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      nameFontSizePx: number;
      titleFontSizePx: number;
      nameColor: string;
      titleColor: string;
      badgeStyle?: 'minimal' | 'glassmorphic' | 'solid-ribbon';
    };
    // Zona Logo Partai / Lembaga (Layer 4 - Corner Locked)
    officialEmblem: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      maxHeightPx: number;
      placement: 'top-left' | 'top-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-right';
      showInstitutionBadge?: boolean; // Label teks pendamping (e.g., "KOMISI III // DPR RI")
    };
    // Aksen Editorial (Barcode, Stempel Edisi, Tanggal)
    editorialAccents?: {
      showBarcode?: boolean;
      barcodeCoordinate?: PosterSlotCoordinate;
      editionTag?: string;
      editionCoordinate?: PosterSlotCoordinate;
    };
  };
}

export interface PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  archetype: CreativePosterArchetype;
  referenceFile: string;
  description: string;
  tags: string[];
  aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  colorPalette: {
    primaryAccent: string;   // Warna sorotan utama (e.g., Crimson Red, Safety Orange, Gold)
    secondaryAccent?: string;// Warna aksen sekunder senada
    backgroundBase: string;  // Warna latar (e.g., Warm Cream #F5F2EB, Pure Black #111111)
    textColor: string;       // Warna teks utama (#FFFFFF atau #111111)
    subTextColor: string;    // Warna teks sekunder
    cardBackground?: string; // Warna latar kartu teks kontras
    cardBorder?: string;     // Warna border kartu teks
  };
  scenePromptHint: string;   // Instruksi gaya visual untuk AI (Grain, lighting, shot perspective)
  negativeSpaceZone: string; // Instruksi ruang kosong agar tidak menabrak teks
  slotManifest: PosterSlotManifest;
  fixedConstraints?: BlueprintFixedConstraints;
  variationRules?: BlueprintVariationRules;
}

export interface GenerateCreativePosterParams {
  topic: string;
  headline?: string;
  quote?: string;
  tenantId: string;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle?: string;
  partyName?: string;
  partyLogoUrl?: string;
  institutionLogoUrl?: string;
  subjectPhotoUrl: string; // Foto cutout PNG transparan
  aspectRatio?: PosterAspectRatio;
  forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
  isOfficialDutyMode?: boolean; // True jika reses dinas (Logo DPR), False jika suara partai (Logo Partai)
}

export interface CompiledPosterPrompt {
  blueprintId: string;
  archetype: CreativePosterArchetype;
  englishPrompt: string;
  aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  canvasDimensions: { width: number; height: number };
  synthesizedCopy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
}

/**
 * POLARIS Curated Harmonious Editorial Color Palettes
 * Sistem Palet Warna Senada & Berestetika Tinggi.
 * Menghilangkan kebosanan satu warna monoton dan menjamin kontras keterbacaan tipografi.
 */

export interface HarmoniousColorPalette {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  primaryAccent: string;   // Warna sorotan utama (e.g. #059669)
  secondaryAccent: string; // Warna sorotan sekunder senada (e.g. #34D399)
  backgroundBase: string;  // Warna latar kertas/kanvas (e.g. #F0FDF4 atau #F8F9FA)
  textColor: string;       // Warna teks utama (#FFFFFF atau #111827)
  subTextColor: string;    // Warna teks sekunder
  cardBackground: string;  // Latar belakang kartu kontras protektif (e.g. rgba(6, 78, 59, 0.85))
  cardBorder: string;      // Border kartu (e.g. rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.25))
}

/**
 * Mengambil palet warna senada secara acak untuk variasi visual non-monoton
 */
export function getRandomHarmoniousPalette(excludePaletteId?: string): HarmoniousColorPalette {
  let pool = HARMONIOUS_COLOR_PALETTES;
  if (excludePaletteId && pool.length > 1) {
    const filtered = pool.filter((p) => p.id !== excludePaletteId);
    if (filtered.length > 0) pool = filtered;
  }
  const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length);
  return pool[idx];
}

/**
 * Menyelesaikan palet warna berdasarkan nama mood AI atau ID palet, dengan fallback acak
 */
export function resolveHarmoniousPalette(
  moodOrId?: string,
  excludePaletteId?: string
): HarmoniousColorPalette {
  if (!moodOrId) return getRandomHarmoniousPalette(excludePaletteId);

  const normalized = moodOrId.toUpperCase().trim();
  const directMatch = HARMONIOUS_COLOR_PALETTES.find(
    (p) =>
      p.id.toUpperCase() === normalized ||
      p.name.toUpperCase().includes(normalized) ||
      normalized.includes(p.id.replace('PAL-', ''))
  );
  if (directMatch) return directMatch;

  return getRandomHarmoniousPalette(excludePaletteId);
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/selector.ts (160 baris, 6.1 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG } from './catalog.js';
import { CreativePosterArchetype, PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint } from './types.js';

export interface SelectBlueprintOptions {
  topic?: string;
  forcedBlueprintId?: string;
  forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
  excludeBlueprintId?: string; // Untuk tombol "Acak Variasi Lain" agar tidak dapat yang sama persis
  forcedBrandHex?: string;
  strictlyEnforceBrandColor?: boolean;
}

export class PosterBlueprintSelector {
  /**
   * 3-Step Filtered Roulette RNG Selection Engine:
   * 1. Negative Filtering (Tone Guard): Menjaga isu krisis/duka dari template pop/komedi.
   * 2. Broad Candidate Pool: Mengambil Top 8-10 relevan + 2-3 Wildcard (Candidate Pool 8-12).
   * 3. Random Draw di dalam Pool: Menjamin variasi luas tanpa kebosanan Max-Score.
   */
  public static async selectBestBlueprintAsync(
    options: SelectBlueprintOptions = {}
  ): Promise<PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint> {
    const { PosterBlueprintRepository } = await import('./blueprint-repository.js');
    const catalog = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog();
    return this.selectBestBlueprint(options, catalog);
  }

  public static selectBestBlueprint(
    options: SelectBlueprintOptions = {},
    catalog: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint[] = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG
  ): PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
    const {
      topic = '',
      forcedBlueprintId,
      forcedArchetype,
      excludeBlueprintId,
      forcedBrandHex,
      strictlyEnforceBrandColor,
    } = options;

    // 1. Jika pemanggil memaksakan ID template tertentu secara eksplisit
    if (forcedBlueprintId) {
      const explicitMatch = catalog.find((b) => b.id === forcedBlueprintId);
      if (explicitMatch) {
        return this.applyBrandColor(explicitMatch, forcedBrandHex, strictlyEnforceBrandColor);
      }
    }

    // 2. Kumpulan awal seluruh katalog
    let pool = [...catalog];

    // Exclude template sebelumnya (Anti-Repeat / Cooldown Memory)
    if (excludeBlueprintId && pool.length > 1) {
      const filtered = pool.filter((b) => b.id !== excludeBlueprintId);
      if (filtered.length > 0) {
        pool = filtered;
      }
    }

    // Jika dipaksakan arketipe tertentu
    if (forcedArchetype) {
      const archetypePool = pool.filter((b) => b.archetype === forcedArchetype);
      if (archetypePool.length > 0) {
        pool = archetypePool;
      }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // STEP 1: TONE GUARD / NEGATIVE FILTERING
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const normalizedTopic = topic.toLowerCase();
    const isCrisisOrGrave =
      /bencana|banjir|gempa|kebakaran|korupsi|meninggal|duka|darurat|krisis|evakuasi|wafat|longsor|tragedi/i.test(
        normalizedTopic
      );

    if (isCrisisOrGrave && pool.length > 4) {
      // Buang template pop/komedi yang tidak pantas untuk isu berduka/kritis
      const popTemplateIds = [
        'TPL-11-CREATIVE-POP-COLLAGE',
        'TPL-18-POP-ZINE-COLLAGE',
        'TPL-22-POP-CULTURE-COMMENTARY',
      ];
      const safePool = pool.filter((b) => !popTemplateIds.includes(b.id));
      if (safePool.length > 0) {
        pool = safePool;
      }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // STEP 2: BROAD RELEVANCE POOL (Top 8-10) + WILDCARD (2-3)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const scoredPool = pool.map((blueprint) => {
      let score = 0;
      const textToMatch = `${blueprint.name} ${blueprint.description} ${blueprint.tags.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase();
      const topicWords = normalizedTopic.split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length > 3);

      for (const word of topicWords) {
        if (textToMatch.includes(word)) score += 3;
      }

      // Skor kecocokan tag tematik
      if (/jalan|jembatan|infrastruktur|bangunan|gedung/i.test(normalizedTopic)) {
        if (blueprint.tags.some((t) => /infrastruktur|sinematik|modern|arsitektur/i.test(t))) score += 4;
      }
      if (/pasar|pedagang|sembako|umkm|tani|pupuk|nelayan/i.test(normalizedTopic)) {
        if (blueprint.tags.some((t) => /kolase|investigatif|risograph|vintage/i.test(t))) score += 4;
      }
      if (/anggaran|apbd|apbn|audit|keuangan|dana/i.test(normalizedTopic)) {
        if (blueprint.tags.some((t) => /infografis|data|grid|broadsheet/i.test(t))) score += 4;
      }

      return { blueprint, score };
    });

    // Urutkan berdasarkan skor relevansi
    scoredPool.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

    // Ambil kandidat relevan teratas (Top 8 - 10)
    const topCandidates = scoredPool.slice(0, Math.min(8, scoredPool.length)).map((s) => s.blueprint);

    // Ambil 2 - 3 Wildcard dari sisa pool yang belum masuk untuk menjaga keanekaragaman
    const remaining = scoredPool.slice(topCandidates.length).map((s) => s.blueprint);
    const wildcards: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint[] = [];
    if (remaining.length > 0) {
      const shuffledRemaining = [...remaining].sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random());
      wildcards.push(...shuffledRemaining.slice(0, Math.min(3, shuffledRemaining.length)));
    }

    const candidatePool = [...topCandidates, ...wildcards];

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // STEP 3: TRUE RANDOM DRAW DI DALAM CANDIDATE POOL (8-12 Variasi)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * candidatePool.length);
    const chosen = candidatePool[randomIndex] || pool[0];

    return this.applyBrandColor(chosen, forcedBrandHex, strictlyEnforceBrandColor);
  }

  /**
   * Menjaga identitas warna asli blueprint, hanya menimpa primaryAccent jika brand diwajibkan
   */
  private static applyBrandColor(
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
    forcedBrandHex?: string,
    strictlyEnforceBrandColor?: boolean
  ): PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
    if (strictlyEnforceBrandColor && forcedBrandHex) {
      return {
        ...blueprint,
        colorPalette: {
          ...blueprint.colorPalette,
          primaryAccent: forcedBrandHex,
        },
      };
    }
    return blueprint;
  }
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/blueprint-repository.ts (185 baris, 6.1 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { db, posterReferenceBlueprints, eq } from '@polaris/database';
import { POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG } from './catalog.js';
import { PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint } from './types.js';

export class PosterBlueprintRepository {
  private static cachedCatalog: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint[] | null = null;
  private static lastCachedAt: number = 0;
  private static CACHE_TTL_MS = 60_000; // 60 detik

  /**
   * Mengambil katalog blueprint aktif.
   * Menggabungkan katalog statis (22 blueprint) dengan blueprint kustom yang berstatus APPROVED dari DB.
   * Wajib aman (resilient) dengan graceful fallback ke katalog statis jika DB belum siap/migrasi belum berjalan.
   */
  public static async getActiveCatalog(customDb?: any): Promise<PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint[]> {
    const database = customDb || db;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (this.cachedCatalog && now - this.lastCachedAt < this.CACHE_TTL_MS) {
      return this.cachedCatalog;
    }

    try {
      const dbBlueprints = await database
        .select()
        .from(posterReferenceBlueprints)
        .where(eq(posterReferenceBlueprints.status, 'APPROVED'));

      if (!dbBlueprints || dbBlueprints.length === 0) {
        this.cachedCatalog = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG;
        this.lastCachedAt = now;
        return this.cachedCatalog;
      }

      // Gabungkan blueprint statis dan DB menggunakan Map (ID unik)
      const catalogMap = new Map<string, PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint>();
      for (const bp of POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG) {
        catalogMap.set(bp.id, bp);
      }

      for (const row of dbBlueprints) {
        catalogMap.set(row.id, {
          id: row.id,
          name: row.name,
          archetype: row.archetype as any,
          referenceFile: row.referenceFile,
          description: row.description,
          tags: row.tags,
          aspectRatio: row.aspectRatio as any,
          colorPalette: row.colorPalette as any,
          scenePromptHint: row.scenePromptHint,
          negativeSpaceZone: row.negativeSpaceZone,
          slotManifest: row.slotManifest as any,
          fixedConstraints: row.fixedConstraints as any,
          variationRules: row.variationRules as any,
        });
      }

      this.cachedCatalog = Array.from(catalogMap.values());
      this.lastCachedAt = now;
      return this.cachedCatalog;
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn(
        `[PosterBlueprintRepository] Query database blueprint gagal (${err?.message || err}). Menggunakan fallback katalog statis.`
      );
      return POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Menyimpan blueprint hasil analisis referensi baru ke DB dengan status DRAFT
   */
  public static async saveDraftBlueprint(
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint
  ): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await db
        .insert(posterReferenceBlueprints)
        .values({
          id: blueprint.id,
          name: blueprint.name,
          archetype: blueprint.archetype,
          referenceFile: blueprint.referenceFile,
          description: blueprint.description,
          tags: blueprint.tags,
          aspectRatio: blueprint.aspectRatio,
          colorPalette: blueprint.colorPalette,
          scenePromptHint: blueprint.scenePromptHint,
          negativeSpaceZone: blueprint.negativeSpaceZone,
          slotManifest: blueprint.slotManifest,
          fixedConstraints: blueprint.fixedConstraints,
          variationRules: blueprint.variationRules,
          status: 'DRAFT',
          version: 1,
        })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({
          target: posterReferenceBlueprints.id,
          set: {
            name: blueprint.name,
            archetype: blueprint.archetype,
            referenceFile: blueprint.referenceFile,
            description: blueprint.description,
            tags: blueprint.tags,
            colorPalette: blueprint.colorPalette,
            scenePromptHint: blueprint.scenePromptHint,
            negativeSpaceZone: blueprint.negativeSpaceZone,
            slotManifest: blueprint.slotManifest,
            fixedConstraints: blueprint.fixedConstraints,
            variationRules: blueprint.variationRules,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          },
        });

      // Invalidate cache
      this.cachedCatalog = null;
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(
        `[PosterBlueprintRepository] Gagal menyimpan draft blueprint ${blueprint.id}:`,
        err
      );
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Menyemai (seed) ke-22 blueprint statis ke database jika tabel masih kosong
   */
  public static async seedStaticCatalogToDb(customDb?: any): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    seededCount: number;
    inserted: number;
    skipped: number;
  }> {
    const database = customDb || db;
    let inserted = 0;
    let skipped = 0;

    for (const bp of POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG) {
      try {
        const existing = await database
          .select({ id: posterReferenceBlueprints.id })
          .from(posterReferenceBlueprints)
          .where(eq(posterReferenceBlueprints.id, bp.id));

        if (existing.length > 0) {
          skipped++;
          continue;
        }

        await database.insert(posterReferenceBlueprints).values({
          id: bp.id,
          name: bp.name,
          archetype: bp.archetype,
          referenceFile: bp.referenceFile,
          description: bp.description,
          tags: bp.tags,
          aspectRatio: bp.aspectRatio,
          colorPalette: bp.colorPalette,
          scenePromptHint: bp.scenePromptHint,
          negativeSpaceZone: bp.negativeSpaceZone,
          slotManifest: bp.slotManifest,
          fixedConstraints: bp.fixedConstraints,
          variationRules: bp.variationRules,
          status: 'APPROVED',
          version: 1,
        });

        inserted++;
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn(`[PosterBlueprintRepository] Gagal menyemai ${bp.id}:`, err?.message);
        skipped++;
      }
    }

    this.cachedCatalog = null;
    return { success: true, seededCount: inserted, inserted, skipped };
  }

  /**
   * Reset cache memori (untuk keperluan testing)
   */
  public static clearCache(): void {
    this.cachedCatalog = null;
    this.lastCachedAt = 0;
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/composition-planner.ts (301 baris, 9.9 KB) ---

import {
  CompositionPlan,
  CompositionZone,
  PosterLayoutVariant,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  PosterCardArrangement,
  SurfaceMode,
  SubjectCompositionMode,
  resolveColorAuthority,
} from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint, SynthesizedEditorialCopy } from './types.js';

export interface PlanCompositionOptions {
  blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  compositionMode?: SubjectCompositionMode;
  seed?: string | number;
  tenantBrandHex?: string;
  strictlyEnforceBrandColor?: boolean;
}

export class CompositionPlanner {
  /**
   * Merancang CompositionPlan deterministik & adaptif
   * berdasarkan variasi aturan blueprint dan keputusan Art Director AI.
   */
  public static planComposition(options: PlanCompositionOptions): CompositionPlan {
    const {
      blueprint,
      copy,
      compositionMode = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY',
      tenantBrandHex,
      strictlyEnforceBrandColor,
    } = options;

    const allowedVariants: PosterLayoutVariant[] =
      blueprint.variationRules?.allowedLayoutVariants || [
        'TOP_BANNER',
        'BOTTOM_ANCHOR',
        'EDITORIAL_SPLIT',
        'CENTERED_HERO',
      ];

    // 1. Tentukan layoutVariant yang valid
    let layoutVariant: PosterLayoutVariant = copy.layoutVariant || 'TOP_BANNER';
    if (!allowedVariants.includes(layoutVariant)) {
      layoutVariant = allowedVariants[0] || 'TOP_BANNER';
    }

    // 2. Tentukan headerPlacement & cardArrangement
    const allowedPlacements: PosterHeaderPlacement[] =
      blueprint.variationRules?.allowedHeaderPlacements || ['top', 'bottom', 'center'];
    let headerPlacement: PosterHeaderPlacement = copy.headerPlacement || 'top';
    if (!allowedPlacements.includes(headerPlacement)) {
      headerPlacement = layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' ? 'bottom' : 'top';
    }

    const allowedArrangements: PosterCardArrangement[] =
      blueprint.variationRules?.allowedCardArrangements || [
        'stacked',
        'horizontal_split',
        'single_hero_card',
        'grid_compact',
      ];
    let cardArrangement: PosterCardArrangement = copy.cardArrangement || 'stacked';
    if (!allowedArrangements.includes(cardArrangement)) {
      cardArrangement = allowedArrangements[0] || 'stacked';
    }

    // 3. Resolusi warna otoritas tunggal (Single Color Authority)
    const resolvedColors = resolveColorAuthority({
      blueprintPalette: blueprint.colorPalette,
      tenantBrandHex,
      strictlyEnforceBrandColor,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodSecondary: copy.secondaryAccent,
      canMutatePalette: blueprint.variationRules?.canMutatePalette ?? true,
    });

    // 4. Konstruksi Zona Spasial adaptif berdasarkan layoutVariant
    const zones = this.buildZones(
      layoutVariant,
      headerPlacement,
      cardArrangement,
      compositionMode
    );

    const defaultSurface: SurfaceMode =
      blueprint.fixedConstraints?.baseBackgroundTone === 'DARK'
        ? 'SCRIM_GRADIENT'
        : 'NONE';

    return {
      blueprintId: blueprint.id,
      archetype: blueprint.archetype,
      layoutVariant,
      headerPlacement,
      cardArrangement,
      zones,
      primaryAccent: resolvedColors.primaryAccent,
      secondaryAccent: resolvedColors.secondaryAccent,
      textColor: resolvedColors.textColor,
      backgroundBase: resolvedColors.backgroundBase,
      surfaceMode: defaultSurface,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Menghasilkan daftar zona spasial berkoordinat normalisasi (0.0 - 1.0)
   */
  private static buildZones(
    layoutVariant: PosterLayoutVariant,
    headerPlacement: PosterHeaderPlacement,
    cardArrangement: PosterCardArrangement,
    compositionMode: SubjectCompositionMode
  ): CompositionZone[] {
    const zones: CompositionZone[] = [];

    // Official Emblem (Corner Locked)
    zones.push({
      id: 'official_emblem',
      role: 'OFFICIAL_EMBLEM',
      rect: { x: 0.82, y: 0.03, w: 0.12, h: 0.05 },
      zIndex: 10,
    });

    if (layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR') {
      // Judul di bawah, visual focal di atas
      zones.push({
        id: 'visual_focal',
        role: 'VISUAL_FOCAL_POINT',
        rect: { x: 0.05, y: 0.08, w: 0.90, h: 0.48 },
        zIndex: 1,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'editorial_body',
        role: 'EDITORIAL_BODY',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.58, w: 0.84, h: 0.16 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'headline',
        role: 'HEADLINE',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.75, w: 0.84, h: 0.16 },
        zIndex: 6,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'member_signature',
        role: 'MEMBER_SIGNATURE',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.92, w: 0.84, h: 0.05 },
        zIndex: 7,
      });
    } else if (layoutVariant === 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT') {
      // Split kolom kiri (naskah & judul) vs kolom kanan (visual)
      zones.push({
        id: 'headline',
        role: 'HEADLINE',
        rect: { x: 0.06, y: 0.10, w: 0.44, h: 0.22 },
        zIndex: 6,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'editorial_body',
        role: 'EDITORIAL_BODY',
        rect: { x: 0.06, y: 0.34, w: 0.44, h: 0.54 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'visual_focal',
        role: 'VISUAL_FOCAL_POINT',
        rect: { x: 0.52, y: 0.10, w: 0.42, h: 0.78 },
        zIndex: 1,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'member_signature',
        role: 'MEMBER_SIGNATURE',
        rect: { x: 0.06, y: 0.90, w: 0.88, h: 0.06 },
        zIndex: 7,
      });
    } else if (layoutVariant === 'CENTERED_HERO') {
      // Pernyataan berpusat di tengah kanvas
      zones.push({
        id: 'subheader',
        role: 'SUBHEADER',
        rect: { x: 0.10, y: 0.12, w: 0.80, h: 0.06 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'headline',
        role: 'HEADLINE',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.36, w: 0.84, h: 0.24 },
        zIndex: 6,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'editorial_body',
        role: 'EDITORIAL_BODY',
        rect: { x: 0.10, y: 0.65, w: 0.80, h: 0.20 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'member_signature',
        role: 'MEMBER_SIGNATURE',
        rect: { x: 0.10, y: 0.90, w: 0.80, h: 0.05 },
        zIndex: 7,
      });
    } else {
      // TOP_BANNER (Default Klasik)
      zones.push({
        id: 'subheader',
        role: 'SUBHEADER',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.05, w: 0.84, h: 0.04 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'headline',
        role: 'HEADLINE',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.09, w: 0.84, h: 0.18 },
        zIndex: 6,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'visual_focal',
        role: 'VISUAL_FOCAL_POINT',
        rect: { x: 0.05, y: 0.29, w: 0.90, h: 0.44 },
        zIndex: 1,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'editorial_body',
        role: 'EDITORIAL_BODY',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.74, w: 0.84, h: 0.16 },
        zIndex: 5,
      });
      zones.push({
        id: 'member_signature',
        role: 'MEMBER_SIGNATURE',
        rect: { x: 0.08, y: 0.92, w: 0.84, h: 0.05 },
        zIndex: 7,
      });
    }

    // Jika mode Tokoh (FIGURE_CENTERPIECE) diaktifkan
    if (compositionMode === 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE') {
      zones.push({
        id: 'subject_figure',
        role: 'SUBJECT',
        rect: { x: 0.50, y: 0.32, w: 0.46, h: 0.68 },
        zIndex: 4,
      });
    }

    return zones;
  }

  /**
   * Mengonversi CompositionPlan menjadi instruksi spasial optik dan pencahayaan murni
   * untuk model difusi (Imagen / DALL-E).
   * Dilarang keras menyebut nama komponen UI, tipografi, atau kode HEX.
   * Dilengkapi kompensasi crop (+12% padding) jika target kanvas 9:16 dipotong ke 4:5.
   */
  public static zonesToSpatialPrompt(
    plan: CompositionPlan,
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
    isPreCrop916 = false
  ): string {
    const compensation = isPreCrop916 ? 0.12 : 0.0;
    const { layoutVariant } = plan;

    let spatialDirectives = '';

    if (layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR') {
      const bottomClearancePct = Math.round((0.38 + compensation) * 100);
      spatialDirectives = `
[SPATIAL COMPOSITION - BOTTOM-WEIGHTED CALM COMPOSITION]
- The upper 60% of the frame features the primary authentic environmental focal point, architectural depth, or landscape.
- The bottom ${bottomClearancePct}% of the canvas MUST be an uncluttered, quiet, low-contrast negative space with soft directional shadows, a smooth dark gradient, or a calm uniform surface with minimal visual detail.
`.trim();
    } else if (layoutVariant === 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT') {
      spatialDirectives = `
[SPATIAL COMPOSITION - ASYMMETRICAL EDITORIAL COMPOSITION]
- The left 50% of the canvas MUST be a calm, uniform, low-detail atmospheric field with soft ambient lighting and minimal visual noise.
- The right 50% of the canvas features the rich contextual documentary environment, architectural depth, and authentic textures.
`.trim();
    } else if (layoutVariant === 'CENTERED_HERO') {
      spatialDirectives = `
[SPATIAL COMPOSITION - CENTERED COMPOSITION]
- The central 40% horizontal band and upper margins MUST remain clean, expansive, and spacious negative space with minimal visual noise and a gentle atmospheric field.
- Surrounding outer borders carry soft ambient vignette and organic depth without cluttering the center.
`.trim();
    } else {
      // TOP_BANNER / Default
      const topClearancePct = Math.round((0.35 + compensation) * 100);
      const bottomClearancePct = Math.round((0.22 + compensation) * 100);
      spatialDirectives = `
[SPATIAL COMPOSITION - TOP-FOCUSED OPEN COMPOSITION]
- The upper ${topClearancePct}% of the canvas MUST be an uncluttered, soft-focus atmospheric open sky or calm uniform surface with minimal visual detail and clean breathing room.
- The central section houses the primary documentary environment, authentic regional infrastructure, or landscape depth.
- The bottom ${bottomClearancePct}% MUST provide a quiet, softly lit foreground with gentle shadows and minimal visual distraction.
`.trim();
    }

    return spatialDirectives;
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/reference-vision-analyzer.ts (243 baris, 8.7 KB) ---
