import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint, CreativePosterArchetype } from './types.js';

export interface VisionAnalysisParams {
  imageBuffer?: Buffer;
  imageUrl?: string;
  imageBase64?: string;
  referenceFileName: string;
  topicHint?: string;
}

const VALID_ARCHETYPES: CreativePosterArchetype[] = [
  'POP_OUT_HERO',
  'STREET_JOURNALISM',
  'STATEMENT_PROFILE',
  'RETRO_CAMPAIGN',
  'TORN_PAPER_NEWS',
  'RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION',
  'MODULAR_GRID_CARDS',
  'CONSTRUCTIVIST_BAUHAUS',
  'THRESHOLD_RISOGRAPH',
  'SWISS_DUOTONE_EDITORIAL',
  'ISOMETRIC_CREATIVE_COLLAGE',
  'STORYBOOK_ILLUSTRATED',
  'TYPOGRAPHIC_MASK',
  'KINETIC_MOTION',
  'EXECUTIVE_SEMINAR',
  'TORN_VISION',
  'GALLERY_EXHIBITION',
  'POP_MAGAZINE_COLLAGE',
  'POLAROID_ADVENTURE',
  'VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE',
  'BROADSHEET_NEWSPAPER',
  'TECHNICAL_GRID_CALLOUT',
];

/**
 * PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer: Menganalisis file referensi visual poster
 * menggunakan OpenAI GPT-4o Vision untuk mengekstrak geometri, palet warna,
 * gaya fotografi, dan batasan arsitektur menjadi blueprint terstruktur.
 */
export class PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer {
  public static async analyzePosterReference(
    params: VisionAnalysisParams
  ): Promise<PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint> {
    const { imageBuffer, imageUrl, imageBase64, referenceFileName, topicHint } = params;

    let targetDataUrl = imageUrl;
    if (!targetDataUrl && imageBuffer) {
      targetDataUrl = `data:image/jpeg;base64,${imageBuffer.toString('base64')}`;
    } else if (!targetDataUrl && imageBase64) {
      targetDataUrl = imageBase64.startsWith('data:')
        ? imageBase64
        : `data:image/jpeg;base64,${imageBase64}`;
    }

    if (!targetDataUrl) {
      throw new Error(
        '[PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer] Gagal: Tidak ada imageBuffer, imageUrl, atau imageBase64 yang diberikan.'
      );
    }

    const systemPrompt = `Anda adalah Master Art Director dan Computational Design Architect untuk POLARIS Creative Poster Engine.
Tugas Anda adalah membedah gambar referensi poster desain ini secara presisi dan menghasilkan spesifikasi JSON blueprint yang type-safe.

PILIHAN ARTIKETIPE RESMI (Wajib pilih salah satu yang paling mendekati):
${VALID_ARCHETYPES.join(', ')}

FORMAT OUTPUT JSON WAJIB:
{
  "name": "Nama Blueprint Deskriptif",
  "archetype": "SALAH_SATU_DARI_LIST_DI_ATAS",
  "description": "Deskripsi komposisi visual poster dalam 2-3 kalimat",
  "tags": ["tag1", "tag2", "tag3"],
  "aspectRatio": "4:5",
  "colorPalette": {
    "primaryAccent": "#HEX",
    "secondaryAccent": "#HEX",
    "backgroundBase": "#HEX",
    "textColor": "#HEX",
    "subTextColor": "#HEX",
    "cardBackground": "rgba(...)",
    "cardBorder": "rgba(...)"
  },
  "scenePromptHint": "Instruksi fotografi/seni kanvas latar belakang dalam Bahasa Inggris (gaya pencahayaan, lensa, tekstur kertas/dinding, NO text, NO typography, NO watermark)",
  "negativeSpaceZone": "Instruksi ruang kosong bersih dalam Bahasa Inggris untuk tempat meletakkan teks (contoh: clean empty negative space on top 35%)",
  "fixedConstraints": {
    "aspectRatio": "4:5",
    "zeroLogoInCanvas": true,
    "allowTextInImage": false,
    "enforcePrimaryBrandAccent": true,
    "imageNegativeAllow": ["banner", "border"],
    "toneSafety": "ALL_TOPICS"
  },
  "variationRules": {
    "allowedLayoutVariants": ["TOP_BANNER", "BOTTOM_ANCHOR"],
    "allowedHeaderPlacements": ["top", "bottom"],
    "allowedCardArrangements": ["stacked", "horizontal_split"],
    "canMutatePalette": true,
    "allowedColorMoods": ["SERIOUS_GOVERNANCE", "EMERGENCY_CRISIS", "HOPEFUL_PROGRESSIVE"]
  }
}`;

    const userPrompt = `Analisis file referensi poster: "${referenceFileName}".
${topicHint ? `Petunjuk konteks tema: ${topicHint}` : ''}
Ekstrak geometri, palet warna, dan blueprint desain sesuai instruksi.`;

    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
        {
          role: 'user',
          content: [
            { type: 'text', text: userPrompt },
            {
              type: 'image_url',
              image_url: {
                url: targetDataUrl,
                detail: 'high',
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      temperature: 0.2,
    });

    const content = response.choices[0]?.message?.content;
    if (!content) {
      throw new Error('[PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer] OpenAI tidak mengembalikan respons analisis vision.');
    }

    const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
    return this.validateAndSanitizeBlueprint(parsed, referenceFileName);
  }

  /**
   * Memvalidasi dan melengkapi nilai default blueprint agar type-safe
   */
  public static validateAndSanitizeBlueprint(
    raw: any,
    referenceFileName: string
  ): PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
    const rawArchetype = String(raw.archetype || '').toUpperCase();
    const archetype: CreativePosterArchetype = VALID_ARCHETYPES.includes(
      rawArchetype as any
    )
      ? (rawArchetype as CreativePosterArchetype)
      : 'POP_OUT_HERO';

    const cleanId =
      raw.id ||
      `TPL-23-CUSTOM-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}-${Math.floor(
        Math.random() * 1000
      )}`;

    const palette = raw.colorPalette || {};

    return {
      id: cleanId,
      name: raw.name || `Custom Reference - ${referenceFileName.slice(0, 30)}`,
      archetype,
      referenceFile: referenceFileName,
      description: raw.description || 'Poster blueprint tergenerasi otomatis dari AI Vision analysis.',
      tags: Array.isArray(raw.tags) && raw.tags.length > 0 ? raw.tags : ['custom', 'vision-ingested'],
      aspectRatio: raw.aspectRatio === '9:16' ? '9:16' : '4:5',
      colorPalette: {
        primaryAccent: palette.primaryAccent || '#EA580C',
        secondaryAccent: palette.secondaryAccent || '#FB923C',
        backgroundBase: palette.backgroundBase || '#0F172A',
        textColor: palette.textColor || '#FFFFFF',
        subTextColor: palette.subTextColor || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)',
        cardBackground: palette.cardBackground || 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)',
        cardBorder: palette.cardBorder || 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)',
      },
      scenePromptHint:
        raw.scenePromptHint ||
        'cinematic editorial lighting, clean negative space, photographic authenticity, fine grain texture, zero text, zero typography',
      negativeSpaceZone:
        raw.negativeSpaceZone ||
        'large clean empty negative space on top 40% with generous breathing room for vector typography',
      slotManifest: {
        canvas: { width: 1080, height: 1350, aspectRatio: '4:5' },
        slots: {
          subjectPhoto: { x: 0.54, y: 0.38, w: 0.44, h: 0.62 },
          headlineBlock: {
            x: 0.05,
            y: 0.06,
            w: 0.90,
            maxLines: 2,
            fontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
            fontSizePx: 96,
            textColor: '#FFFFFF',
          },
          memberIdentity: {
            x: 0.05,
            y: 0.90,
            w: 0.90,
            nameFontSizePx: 22,
            titleFontSizePx: 12,
            nameColor: '#FFFFFF',
            titleColor: palette.primaryAccent || '#EA580C',
          },
          officialEmblem: { x: 0.05, y: 0.03, w: 0.20, maxHeightPx: 32, placement: 'top-left' },
        },
      },
      fixedConstraints: {
        primaryAccentLocked: raw.fixedConstraints?.primaryAccentLocked ?? false,
        baseBackgroundTone:
          raw.fixedConstraints?.baseBackgroundTone ||
          (palette.backgroundBase?.toLowerCase().startsWith('#0') ||
          palette.backgroundBase?.toLowerCase().startsWith('#1')
            ? 'DARK'
            : 'LIGHT'),
        requiredFontFamilies: {
          headline: raw.fixedConstraints?.requiredFontFamilies?.headline || 'Bebas Neue',
          body: raw.fixedConstraints?.requiredFontFamilies?.body || 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
        },
        imageNegativeAllow: raw.fixedConstraints?.imageNegativeAllow || ['border', 'frame'],
        toneSafety: raw.fixedConstraints?.toneSafety || 'ALL_TOPICS',
      },
      variationRules: {
        allowedLayoutVariants: raw.variationRules?.allowedLayoutVariants || [
          'TOP_BANNER',
          'BOTTOM_ANCHOR',
        ],
        allowedHeaderPlacements: raw.variationRules?.allowedHeaderPlacements || ['top', 'bottom'],
        allowedCardArrangements: raw.variationRules?.allowedCardArrangements || [
          'stacked',
          'horizontal_split',
        ],
        canMutatePalette: raw.variationRules?.canMutatePalette !== false,
        allowedColorMoods: raw.variationRules?.allowedColorMoods || [
          'SERIOUS_GOVERNANCE',
          'EMERGENCY_CRISIS',
          'HOPEFUL_PROGRESSIVE',
        ],
      },
    };
  }
}

import {
  PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
  CompiledPosterPrompt,
  SynthesizedEditorialCopy,
  SubjectCompositionMode,
  PosterLayoutVariant,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  PosterCardArrangement,
  LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA,
  LayoutBlockSpec,
} from './types.js';
import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { resolveHarmoniousPalette, getRandomHarmoniousPalette } from './palettes.js';
import { TwoStageArticleEngine } from '../two-stage-article.engine.js';
import { MasterEditorialSanitizer } from '../master-editorial-sanitizer.js';
import { CompositionPlanner } from './composition-planner.js';

export interface CompilePosterPromptOptions {
  topic: string;
  groundedContextSummary?: string; // Diambil dari hasil AutonomousResearchOrchestrator
  blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint;
  compositionMode?: SubjectCompositionMode;
  tenantBrandHex?: string;
}

/**
 * Konversi nilai RGB dari string HEX ke deskripsi warna alami fotografi/seni rupa.
 * Mencegah model difusi mencetak kode HEX literal (seperti #F8FAFC) di atas kanvas gambar.
 */
export function hexToNaturalColorName(hex?: string, fallbackName = 'neutral tone'): string {
  if (!hex || typeof hex !== 'string') return fallbackName;
  const cleanHex = hex.trim().replace(/^#/, '').toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{3}$|^[0-9a-f]{6}$/.test(cleanHex)) return fallbackName;

  const fullHex =
    cleanHex.length === 3
      ? cleanHex
          .split('')
          .map((c) => c + c)
          .join('')
      : cleanHex;

  // Exact map untuk palet dan token desain umum polaris
  const exactMap: Record<string, string> = {
    'dc2626': 'deep crimson red',
    '9e0b0f': 'deep maroon red',
    'ea580c': 'warm terracotta orange',
    'fb923c': 'soft amber orange',
    'f5b800': 'vibrant golden amber',
    '0f172a': 'deep midnight navy',
    '1e293b': 'dark slate navy',
    '0a0a0a': 'deep charcoal black',
    '111827': 'rich dark charcoal',
    '18181b': 'deep charcoal black',
    'f8fafc': 'clean alabaster white',
    'f6f3eb': 'warm ivory cream',
    'f5f5f0': 'warm off-white',
    'f4f1ea': 'warm vintage paper cream',
    'ffffff': 'pure white',
    '000000': 'pitch black',
    '059669': 'deep emerald green',
    '10b981': 'vibrant emerald green',
    '2563eb': 'rich royal cobalt blue',
    '4f46e5': 'deep royal indigo',
    '7c3aed': 'regal purple',
    'e11d48': 'vibrant rose red',
    '881337': 'deep burgundy',
    'd97706': 'rich vintage amber',
    '0d9488': 'deep nordic teal',
    '14b8a6': 'vibrant turquoise teal',
    'f97316': 'warm sunset coral',
    '84cc16': 'vibrant lime accent',
    '64748b': 'cool slate gray',
    '94a3b8': 'soft muted slate',
    'cbd5e1': 'light slate gray',
    'e2e8f0': 'pale soft gray',
  };

  if (exactMap[fullHex]) {
    return exactMap[fullHex]!;
  }

  // Fallback kalkulasi HSL dari RGB
  const r = parseInt(fullHex.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255;
  const g = parseInt(fullHex.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255;
  const b = parseInt(fullHex.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;

  const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
  const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  let h = 0;
  let s = 0;
  const l = (max + min) / 2;

  if (max !== min) {
    const d = max - min;
    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
    switch (max) {
      case r:
        h = ((g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0)) * 60;
        break;
      case g:
        h = ((b - r) / d + 2) * 60;
        break;
      case b:
        h = ((r - g) / d + 4) * 60;
        break;
    }
  }

  if (s < 0.12) {
    if (l > 0.88) return 'clean soft white';
    if (l > 0.70) return 'warm light off-white';
    if (l > 0.45) return 'neutral medium gray';
    if (l > 0.20) return 'dark muted slate';
    return 'deep charcoal shadow';
  }

  const lightnessPrefix = l > 0.72 ? 'pale ' : l < 0.28 ? 'deep ' : l > 0.55 ? 'warm ' : 'rich ';

  if (h >= 345 || h < 15) return `${lightnessPrefix}crimson red`;
  if (h >= 15 && h < 45) return `${lightnessPrefix}amber orange`;
  if (h >= 45 && h < 70) return `${lightnessPrefix}warm gold`;
  if (h >= 70 && h < 165) return `${lightnessPrefix}emerald green`;
  if (h >= 165 && h < 195) return `${lightnessPrefix}teal`;
  if (h >= 195 && h < 255) return `${lightnessPrefix}sapphire blue`;
  if (h >= 255 && h < 290) return `${lightnessPrefix}indigo violet`;
  if (h >= 290 && h < 345) return `${lightnessPrefix}magenta rose`;

  return fallbackName;
}

/**
 * Filter pemurni akhir yang deterministik untuk prompt difusi gambar Layer 1.
 * Menghilangkan seluruh pola HEX dan kata pemicu tipografi/UI agar tidak bocor ke model gambar.
 */
export function sanitizeImageGenerationPrompt(rawPrompt: string): string {
  if (!rawPrompt) return '';

  // 1. Pisahkan bagian mandat ZERO-TEXT agar larangan eksplisit tetap utuh
  const mandateHeader = '[CRITICAL ZERO-HUMAN / ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-COORDINATE MANDATE]';
  const mandateIndex = rawPrompt.indexOf(mandateHeader);

  let mainPart = rawPrompt;
  let mandatePart = '';

  if (mandateIndex !== -1) {
    mainPart = rawPrompt.slice(0, mandateIndex);
    mandatePart = rawPrompt.slice(mandateIndex);
  }

  // 2. Bersihkan seluruh kode HEX di seluruh bagian
  mainPart = mainPart.replace(/#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b/g, 'clean tone');
  mandatePart = mandatePart.replace(/#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b/g, '');

  // 3. Bersihkan pemicu tipografi / headline / teks di bagian adegan
  mainPart = mainPart.replace(
    /\b(for\s+)?(giant\s+|huge\s+|bold\s+|large\s+|sharp\s+|minimalist\s+|editorial\s+|formal\s+|organic\s+|serif\s+|sans-serif\s+)?(typography|typographic|headlines?|subheadlines?|subheadings?|mastheads?|text|titles?|fonts?|lettering)(\s+overlays?|\s+banners?|\s+blocks?)?\b/gi,
    'clean open space'
  );

  // 4. Bersihkan pemicu kartu / kotak / banner / callout / badge
  mainPart = mainPart.replace(
    /\b(editorial\s+|summary\s+|bulletin\s+|info\s+|callout\s+|ui\s+|analytical\s+|diagrammatic\s+)?(cards?|boxes|banners?|columns?|callouts?|badges?|stickers?|plakat)\b/gi,
    'clean visual zones'
  );

  // 5. Bersihkan istilah cetak / media grafis
  mainPart = mainPart.replace(
    /\b(newspaper|broadsheet|magazine|poster|zine|infographic|flyer|brochure)\b/gi,
    'editorial artwork'
  );

  // 6. Normalisasi spasi dan frasa berulang
  mainPart = mainPart
    .replace(/\b(clean open space)(\s+(clean open space|clean visual zones))+\b/gi, 'clean open space')
    .replace(/\b(clean visual zones)(\s+(clean visual zones|clean open space))+\b/gi, 'clean visual zones')
    .replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, ' ');

  return `${mainPart}${mandatePart}`.trim();
}

export class PosterPromptCompiler {
  public static hexToNaturalColorName = hexToNaturalColorName;
  public static sanitizeImageGenerationPrompt = sanitizeImageGenerationPrompt;
  /**
   * TAHAP 1: Menyusun Naskah Multi-Blok & Analogi Objek Visual 1:1 menggunakan LLM (Structured JSON)
   * Menegakkan Business Rule BR-01 (Isomorfisme Panjang Karakter)
   */
  public static async synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals(
    topic: string,
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
    groundedContext?: string
  ): Promise<SynthesizedEditorialCopy> {
    const blockSpecs: LayoutBlockSpec[] =
      LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA[blueprint.archetype] ||
      (blueprint.slotManifest.editorialBlocks || []).map((b) => ({
        slotId: b.slotId,
        roleDescription: 'Seksi blok editorial naskah',
        headerMaxChars: b.constraints.headerMaxChars,
        bodyMinChars: b.constraints.bodyMinChars,
        bodyMaxChars: b.constraints.bodyMaxChars,
        defaultHeader: b.defaultReferenceHeader,
      }));

    const blockConstraintsText =
      blockSpecs.length > 0
        ? blockSpecs
            .map(
              (b) =>
                `- Slot ID "${b.slotId}": Header maks ${b.headerMaxChars} karakter (${b.roleDescription}). Body WAJIB antara ${b.bodyMinChars} s/d ${b.bodyMaxChars} karakter.`
            )
            .join('\n')
        : '- Format teks naskah sesuai kebutuhan arketipe editorial.';

    const sampleBlocks: Record<string, { header: string; body: string }> = {};
    for (const b of blockSpecs) {
      sampleBlocks[b.slotId] = {
        header: b.defaultHeader,
        body: `Deskripsi terukur dan konkret terkait penanganan ${topic} di lapangan.`,
      };
    }

    const visualSlotsText = (blueprint.slotManifest.visualCutoutSlots || [])
      .map(
        (v) =>
          `- Visual Slot ID "${v.slotId}": Deskripsikan elemen visual dunia riil/faktual di Indonesia yang secara langsung menggambarkan topik "${topic}" (tulis dalam Bahasa Inggris).`
      )
      .join('\n');

    const systemPrompt = `Anda adalah Art Director & Copywriter Infografis Kebijakan POLARIS.
Tugas Anda adalah menyusun naskah dan konsep visual poster baru bertopik: "${topic}".
Ikuti struktur slot tata letak dan batasan karakter yang ditentukan di bawah ini secara presisi.

${groundedContext ? `FAKTA RISET & DATA PENDUKUNG:\n${groundedContext.slice(0, 1800)}\n` : ''}

ATURAN BISNIS MUTLAK:
1. Setiap blok teks harus padat, bernas, menggunakan Bahasa Indonesia baku jurnalistik mengenai topik "${topic}".
2. PATUHI batas karakter untuk setiap Slot ID berikut agar tidak merusak grid desain:
${blockConstraintsText}

3. ELEMEN VISUAL (DALAM BAHASA INGGRIS):
Untuk setiap slot visual, deskripsikan elemen visual dunia riil/faktual yang secara langsung menggambarkan "${topic}":
${visualSlotsText || `- Deskripsi visual realistis yang mencerminkan ${topic}`}

4. PANDUAN ART DIRECTOR DINAMIS (LAYOUT & WARNA SENADA):
Sebagai Art Director AI, Anda WAJIB menentukan variasi tata letak (layout) dan palet warna senada secara aktif agar desain unik, adaptif dengan isi artikel, dan tidak monoton bagi tiap pengguna:
- layoutVariant (Pilih salah satu persis):
  * "TOP_BANNER" : Judul tebal di posisi atas kanvas, kartu narasi & data di bagian bawah.
  * "BOTTOM_ANCHOR" : Judul masif berbobot di bagian bawah, kartu ringkasan mengambang di atas.
  * "EDITORIAL_SPLIT" : Tata letak asimetris majalah editorial (kolom kiri judul/naskah, kolom kanan kartu data).
  * "CENTERED_HERO" : Pernyataan inti tebal di tengah kanvas dengan framing aksen protektif.
- headerPlacement (Pilih salah satu persis): "top" | "bottom" | "center"
- cardArrangement (Pilih salah satu persis): "stacked" | "horizontal_split" | "single_hero_card" | "grid_compact"
- colorMood: Pilih salah satu palet yang paling harmonis dengan suasana topik:
  "EMERALD" (Lingkungan, pertanian, kesehatan),
  "ROYAL_INDIGO" (Tata kelola, inovasi digital, kebijakan formal),
  "CRIMSON_TERRACOTTA" (Tanggap darurat, peringatan dini, advokasi tegas),
  "MIDNIGHT_SAPPHIRE" (Infrastruktur, maritim, transportasi),
  "VINTAGE_AMBER" (Pasar tradisional, ekonomi kerakyatan, sejarah),
  "NORDIC_TEAL" (Pendidikan, transparansi, kesejahteraan sosial),
  "BURGUNDY_ROSE" (Perlindungan sosial, seni, hak asasi),
  "SUNSET_CORAL" (Pemuda, pariwisata, UMKM kreatif),
  "COBALT_GOLD" (Kedaulatan, prestasi nasional, kelembagaan tinggi),
  "CHARCOAL_LIME" (Teknologi masa depan, energi hijau modern),
  "MONOCHROME_MINIMAL" (Editorial hitam-putih prestisius, investigasi).

5. PANTANGAN MASTER EDITORIAL & ATURAN ANGKA FAKTA RISET:
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "nyata" (seperti "SOLUSI NYATA", "langkah nyata", "bantuan nyata", "dampak nyata"). Gunakan istilah "TERUKUR", "KONKRET", "LANGSUNG", atau "STRATEGIS".
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "sekadar" maupun "bukan sekadar". Gunakan kata "tidak hanya" atau kalimat lugas.
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda baca em-dash (—) maupun en-dash (–). Gunakan tanda koma atau tanda titik.
- DILARANG menggunakan Oxford comma (koma sebelum 'dan', 'serta', 'atau' pada perincian).
- ATURAN ANGKA & FAKTA RISET: Jika pada FAKTA RISET & DATA PENDUKUNG terdapat angka statistik riil (APBD, persentase, kuota, jumlah desa/kecamatan, tahun, atau nomor Perda/UU), WAJIB gunakan angka dan fakta riil tersebut ke dalam statCircles, blocks, dan numberedItems!
- LARANGAN KERAS ANGKA FIKTIF: DILARANG KERAS mengarang angka statistik fiktif (seperti 120+, 28 Unit, 1.450) jika angka tersebut tidak ada di dalam konteks fakta atau prompt pengguna! Jika tidak ada angka spesifik di konteks, gunakan nilai indikator kebijakan singkat yang relevan dengan topik (misal: "100%", "24 Jam", "Terpadu", "Prioritas", "5 Tahap", "Tepat Sasaran").
- ATURAN LABEL LINGKARAN (topLabel): topLabel pada statCircles WAJIB terdiri dari 1 KATA PENDEK SAJA (Maksimal 11 karakter, TANPA titik-titik), contoh: "SASARAN", "REALISASI", "ANGGARAN", "FOKUS", "TARGET", "DISTRIBUSI".
- ATURAN KALIMAT TUNTAS (ANTI-ELLIPSIS): Untuk setiap body pada blocks dan summary pada numberedItems, tulislah 1 atau 2 kalimat pendek yang PADAT dan TUNTAS (selalu berakhir dengan tanda titik '.') dengan panjang tepat di kisaran 85 s/d 135 karakter agar seluruh kalimat tampil utuh di poster tanpa pernah terpotong tanda titik-titik (...)!

Keluarkan output HANYA dalam format JSON murni:
{
  "layoutVariant": "TOP_BANNER",
  "headerPlacement": "top",
  "cardArrangement": "stacked",
  "colorMood": "EMERALD",
  "mainBannerTitle": "JUDUL UTAMA 2-4 KATA TEGAS (Maks 34 karakter)",
  "mainBannerSubheader": "Subjudul pengantar di atas judul utama (Maks 65 karakter)",
  "mainBannerDefinitionBody": "Paragraf definisi/kesimpulan komprehensif di bagian paling bawah banner (Wajib antara 280 s/d 360 karakter)",
  "blocks": ${JSON.stringify(sampleBlocks, null, 4)},
  "statCircles": [
    { "topLabel": "TARGET", "value": "100%", "description": "Fokus intervensi utama sesuai fakta topik." },
    { "topLabel": "PRIORITAS", "value": "Utama", "description": "Kawal realisasi kebijakan secara terukur." },
    { "topLabel": "CAKUPAN", "value": "Dapil", "description": "Pengawasan menyeluruh di wilayah konstituen." }
  ],
  "numberedItems": [
    { "number": "01", "title": "Audit Kelayakan", "subtitle": "Inspeksi Berkala", "summary": "Pemeriksaan teknis menyeluruh terhadap seluruh fasilitas dan regulasi terkait." },
    { "number": "02", "title": "Respons Cepat", "subtitle": "Satuan Reaksi 24 Jam", "summary": "Pengerahan tim terpadu untuk penanganan langsung tanpa hambatan birokrasi." },
    { "number": "03", "title": "Bantuan Terpadu", "subtitle": "Logistik & Layanan", "summary": "Distribusi dukungan langsung dan pelayanan terintegrasi bagi masyarakat." },
    { "number": "04", "title": "Partisipasi Warga", "subtitle": "Pendampingan Terbuka", "summary": "Membangun keterlibatan aktif komunitas dan forum aspirasi warga." },
    { "number": "05", "title": "Kepastian Regulasi", "subtitle": "Payung Hukum Tegas", "summary": "Penerbitan kebijakan standar perlindungan hak dan pelayanan publik." }
  ],
  "mappedVisualCutouts": {
    "upper_right_cutout": "Indonesian community members or professionals actively engaged in this field",
    "mid_left_hero_cutout": "An Indonesian citizen representative or frontline figure standing proudly looking forward",
    "bottom_street_scene": "Authentic Indonesian urban or regional environment showcasing daily civic life and activity related to the topic"
  }
}`;

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.25,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'system', content: systemPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}') as SynthesizedEditorialCopy;

      // Resolusi palet warna senada aktif dari pilihan Art Director AI
      const resolvedPalette = resolveHarmoniousPalette(parsed.colorMood || blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent);
      parsed.primaryAccent = resolvedPalette.primaryAccent;
      parsed.secondaryAccent = resolvedPalette.secondaryAccent;
      parsed.cardBackground = resolvedPalette.cardBackground;
      parsed.cardBorder = resolvedPalette.cardBorder;

      // Validasi & Clamping Nilai Dinamis Layout
      const validVariants: PosterLayoutVariant[] = ['TOP_BANNER', 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR', 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT', 'CENTERED_HERO'];
      if (!parsed.layoutVariant || !validVariants.includes(parsed.layoutVariant)) {
        parsed.layoutVariant = 'TOP_BANNER';
      }

      const validPlacements: PosterHeaderPlacement[] = ['top', 'bottom', 'center'];
      if (!parsed.headerPlacement || !validPlacements.includes(parsed.headerPlacement)) {
        parsed.headerPlacement = parsed.layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' ? 'bottom' : 'top';
      }

      const validArrangements: PosterCardArrangement[] = ['stacked', 'horizontal_split', 'single_hero_card', 'grid_compact'];
      if (!parsed.cardArrangement || !validArrangements.includes(parsed.cardArrangement)) {
        parsed.cardArrangement = 'stacked';
      }

      return this.enforceCharacterBudgets(parsed, blueprint);
    } catch (error) {
      // Fallback deterministik kontekstual jika LLM timeout
      const randomPalette = getRandomHarmoniousPalette();
      const variants: PosterLayoutVariant[] = ['TOP_BANNER', 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR', 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT', 'CENTERED_HERO'];
      const fallbackVariant = variants[Math.floor(Math.random() * variants.length)];

      const fallbackBlocks: Record<string, { header: string; body: string }> = {};
      for (const spec of blockSpecs) {
        fallbackBlocks[spec.slotId] = {
          header: MasterEditorialSanitizer.cleanHeadline(spec.defaultHeader),
          body: MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(
            `Secara substantif, penanganan isu ${topic} memerlukan pengawasan anggaran ketat dan respons terpadu di lapangan demi perlindungan warga.`
          ),
        };
      }

      const fallbackCopy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy = {
        layoutVariant: fallbackVariant,
        headerPlacement: fallbackVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' ? 'bottom' : 'top',
        cardArrangement: 'stacked',
        colorMood: randomPalette.name,
        primaryAccent: randomPalette.primaryAccent,
        secondaryAccent: randomPalette.secondaryAccent,
        cardBackground: randomPalette.cardBackground,
        cardBorder: randomPalette.cardBorder,
        mainBannerTitle: this.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(topic.toUpperCase(), 34),
        mainBannerSubheader: `PENGEMBANGAN KEBIJAKAN PUBLIK • Fokus kajian: ${topic.slice(0, 30)}`,
        mainBannerDefinitionBody: `Secara substantif, penanganan isu ${topic} membutuhkan kolaborasi terpadu antara regulasi pemerintah daerah, pengawasan anggaran parlemen, serta partisipasi aktif masyarakat secara berkelanjutan di tingkat akar rumput.`,
        blocks: fallbackBlocks,
        statCircles: [
          { topLabel: 'PRIORITAS', value: 'Utama', description: `Pengawalan aspirasi dan kebijakan terpadu terkait ${topic.slice(0, 24)}.` },
          { topLabel: 'SASARAN', value: '100%', description: 'Transparansi pelaksanaan program tepat sasaran di lapangan.' },
          { topLabel: 'KOMITMEN', value: 'Tegas', description: 'Pengawasan berkelanjutan demi perlindungan hak dan kesejahteraan warga.' },
        ],
        numberedItems: [
          { number: '01', title: 'Audit Kebijakan', subtitle: 'Evaluasi Komprehensif', summary: `Analisis menyeluruh terhadap tata kelola dan dampak ${topic.slice(0, 24)}.` },
          { number: '02', title: 'Aksi Lapangan', subtitle: 'Respons Langsung', summary: 'Turun ke lapangan meninjau titik kritis bersama masyarakat dan relawan.' },
          { number: '03', title: 'Penyaluran Bantuan', subtitle: 'Logistik & Layanan', summary: 'Fasilitasi bantuan langsung dan advokasi birokrasi tepat sasaran bagi warga.' },
          { number: '04', title: 'Konsolidasi Warga', subtitle: 'Partisipasi Publik', summary: 'Membangun rukun warga tanggap dan forum aspirasi akar rumput yang berdaya.' },
          { number: '05', title: 'Regulasi Perlindungan', subtitle: 'Kepastian Hukum', summary: 'Mendorong pengesahan perda perlindungan hak publik dan anggaran berpihak.' },
        ],
        mappedVisualCutouts: {
          upper_right_cutout: `Indonesian field workers and equipment addressing ${topic}`,
          mid_left_hero_cutout: `Indonesian frontline worker or citizen actively involved in ${topic}`,
          bottom_street_scene: `Authentic Indonesian street environment showing community activity related to ${topic}`,
        },
      };

      return this.enforceCharacterBudgets(fallbackCopy, blueprint);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Pemotongan judul kata-per-kata yang aman (Word-Safe Truncation).
   * DILARANG memotong huruf di tengah kata (seperti 'BERKELANJUTA') dan DILARANG menambah '...'.
   */
  public static wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(title: string, maxChars = 34): string {
    const clean = (title || '').trim();
    if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;
    const words = clean.split(/\s+/);
    let accumulated = '';
    for (const word of words) {
      const next = accumulated ? `${accumulated} ${word}` : word;
      if (next.length <= maxChars) {
        accumulated = next;
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    return accumulated || words[0] || clean;
  }

  /**
   * Pembatas kalimat cerdas anti-ellipsis menggantung (Clean Sentence Clamping).
   * Memotong pada tanda titik/titik koma terdekat jika posisi >= 55% teks,
   * atau memotong pada batas kata utuh dan mengakhiri dengan tanda titik yang tuntas.
   */
  public static smartSentenceClamp(text: string, maxChars: number): string {
    const clean = (text || '').trim();
    if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;

    const sliced = clean.slice(0, maxChars);
    const periodMatch = sliced.lastIndexOf('.');
    const semicolonMatch = sliced.lastIndexOf(';');
    const sentenceBoundary = Math.max(periodMatch, semicolonMatch);

    if (sentenceBoundary >= Math.floor(maxChars * 0.55)) {
      return clean.slice(0, sentenceBoundary + 1).trim();
    }

    const lastSpace = sliced.lastIndexOf(' ');
    const safeText = lastSpace > Math.floor(maxChars * 0.55) ? sliced.slice(0, lastSpace) : sliced;
    return `${safeText.trim().replace(/[,;:\-\s]+$/, '')}.`;
  }

  /**
   * Guardrail deterministik untuk memotong kalimat secara rapi jika LLM melebihi batas karakter
   * serta menyucikan teks dari mikrolinguistik terlarang (Zero-Tolerance: em-dash, Oxford comma, 'sekadar', 'nyata')
   */
  private static enforceCharacterBudgets(
    copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy,
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint
  ): SynthesizedEditorialCopy {
    // 1. Sanitasi Mikrolinguistik Master Editorial untuk seluruh teks
    if (copy.mainBannerTitle) {
      copy.mainBannerTitle = this.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(
        MasterEditorialSanitizer.cleanHeadline(copy.mainBannerTitle),
        34
      );
    }
    if (copy.mainBannerSubheader && copy.mainBannerSubheader.length > 85) {
      copy.mainBannerSubheader = this.smartSentenceClamp(
        MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(copy.mainBannerSubheader),
        85
      );
    }
    if (copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody && copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody.length > 380) {
      copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody = this.smartSentenceClamp(
        MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody),
        380
      );
    }

    // 3. Kunci numberedItems (untuk MODULAR_GRID_CARDS & RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION)
    if (Array.isArray(copy.numberedItems)) {
      copy.numberedItems = copy.numberedItems.map((item) => ({
        ...item,
        title: this.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(item.title || ''), 34),
        summary: this.smartSentenceClamp(MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(item.summary || ''), 135),
      }));
    }

    // 4. Kunci statCircles
    if (Array.isArray(copy.statCircles)) {
      copy.statCircles = copy.statCircles.map((stat) => {
        const rawLabel = MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(stat.topLabel || '').trim();
        const firstWord = rawLabel.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'TARGET';
        const cleanTopLabel = (firstWord.length <= 11 ? firstWord : firstWord.slice(0, 11)).toUpperCase();

        return {
          ...stat,
          topLabel: cleanTopLabel,
          value: (stat.value || '').trim().slice(0, 10),
          description: this.smartSentenceClamp(MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(stat.description || ''), 85),
        };
      });
    }

    // 5. Kunci editorialBlocks untuk seluruh arketipe (LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA)
    const blockSpecs: LayoutBlockSpec[] =
      LAYOUT_BLOCK_SCHEMA[blueprint.archetype] ||
      (blueprint.slotManifest.editorialBlocks || []).map((b) => ({
        slotId: b.slotId,
        roleDescription: 'Seksi blok editorial naskah',
        headerMaxChars: b.constraints.headerMaxChars,
        bodyMinChars: b.constraints.bodyMinChars,
        bodyMaxChars: b.constraints.bodyMaxChars,
        defaultHeader: b.defaultReferenceHeader,
      }));

    if (!copy.blocks) copy.blocks = {};

    for (const spec of blockSpecs) {
      let target = copy.blocks[spec.slotId];
      if (!target) {
        target = {
          header: MasterEditorialSanitizer.cleanHeadline(spec.defaultHeader),
          body: MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(
            `Penanganan isu memerlukan langkah konkret, respons terpadu, dan pengawasan anggaran ketat.`
          ),
        };
        copy.blocks[spec.slotId] = target;
      }
      if (target.header) {
        const sanitizedHeader = MasterEditorialSanitizer.cleanHeadline(target.header);
        target.header = this.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(sanitizedHeader, spec.headerMaxChars);
      }
      if (target.body) {
        const sanitizedBody = MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(target.body);
        target.body = this.smartSentenceClamp(sanitizedBody, Math.max(spec.bodyMaxChars, 135));
      }
    }
    return copy;
  }

  private static smartTruncate(text: string, maxChars: number, addEllipsis = true): string {
    const clean = text.trim();
    if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;
    const limit = addEllipsis ? Math.max(3, maxChars - 3) : maxChars;
    const sliced = clean.slice(0, limit);
    const lastSpace = sliced.lastIndexOf(' ');
    const safeText = lastSpace > limit * 0.6 ? sliced.slice(0, lastSpace) : sliced;
    return addEllipsis ? `${safeText.trim()}...` : safeText.trim();
  }

  /**
   * TAHAP 2: Mengompilasi Prompt Kolase Visual (100% Tanpa Teks, Siap Di-overlay Satori)
   * Polymorphic Art Direction per Arketipe Desain
   */
  public static async compileScenePrompt(
    options: CompilePosterPromptOptions
  ): Promise<CompiledPosterPrompt> {
    const {
      topic,
      groundedContextSummary,
      blueprint,
      compositionMode = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY',
      tenantBrandHex,
    } = options;

    const synthesizedCopy = await this.synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals(
      topic,
      blueprint,
      groundedContextSummary
    );

    const rawTopic = options.topic || '';
    const cleanTopic = rawTopic
      .replace(/^Infografis Publik:\s*/i, '')
      .replace(/^Poster Publik:\s*/i, '')
      .replace(/menampilkan\s+[^,.]+(dengan|yang|untuk)/gi, '$1')
      .replace(/tokoh\s+[^,.]+/gi, '')
      .replace(/dengan latar belakang/gi, 'berlatar')
      .trim();

    const rawAccentHex = tenantBrandHex || blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent || '#9E0B0F';
    const rawBaseHex = blueprint.colorPalette.backgroundBase || '#F4F1EA';
    const accentColorName = hexToNaturalColorName(rawAccentHex, 'vibrant accent tone');
    const baseColorName = hexToNaturalColorName(rawBaseHex, 'neutral paper tone');
    const cutouts = synthesizedCopy.mappedVisualCutouts || {};

    let englishPrompt = '';

    const zeroMandate = `
[CRITICAL ZERO-HUMAN / ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-COORDINATE MANDATE]
- Absolutely ZERO human faces, ZERO portraits, ZERO people, ZERO politicians, and ZERO human silhouettes.
- DO NOT generate any text, letters, numbers, words, headlines, coordinate labels, percentage values, axis markers (no "x:" or "y:"), signs, or watermarks.
- DO NOT generate any logos, emblems, or brand signatures.
- Absolutely ZERO coordinate labels (no "x:" or "y:").

    if (blueprint.archetype === 'TORN_PAPER_NEWS') {

      englishPrompt = `

      // TPL-21: CIVIC BROADSHEET CHRONICLE

${zeroMandate}
      `.trim();
    } else {
      // DYNAMIC FALLBACK: Memanfaatkan blueprint.scenePromptHint & blueprint.negativeSpaceZone secara cerdas
      const sceneHint =
        blueprint.scenePromptHint ||
        `High-impact documentary photographic background capturing: "${cleanTopic}".`;
      const negSpace =
        blueprint.negativeSpaceZone ||
        `Generous clean negative space with tranquil open surfaces and low visual distraction.`;

      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & EDITORIAL FINE ART ART DIRECTION]
A sophisticated documentary photographic or illustration artwork (vertical 4:5 ratio) capturing the topic: "${cleanTopic}".
Artistic Direction: ${sceneHint}
Color Tone: Base tone in ${baseColorName} with sophisticated accent highlights in ${accentColorName}.
Composition Guidelines: ${negSpace}

${zeroMandate}
`.trim();
    }

    const { width, height } = blueprint.slotManifest.canvas;
    const isPreCrop916 = blueprint.aspectRatio === '4:5';
    const compositionPlan = CompositionPlanner.planComposition({
      blueprint,
      copy: synthesizedCopy,
      compositionMode,
      tenantBrandHex,
    });

    const spatialNegativeDirectives = CompositionPlanner.zonesToSpatialPrompt(
      compositionPlan,
      blueprint,
      isPreCrop916
    );

    const rawUnifiedEnglishPrompt = `${spatialNegativeDirectives}\n\n${englishPrompt}`.trim();
    const sanitizedEnglishPrompt = sanitizeImageGenerationPrompt(rawUnifiedEnglishPrompt);

    return {
      blueprintId: blueprint.id,
      archetype: blueprint.archetype,
      englishPrompt: sanitizedEnglishPrompt,
      aspectRatio: blueprint.aspectRatio,
      canvasDimensions: { width, height },
      synthesizedCopy,
      compositionPlan,
    };
  }
}

======================================================================
--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/master-editorial-sanitizer.ts (212 baris, 7.9 KB) ---
======================================================================
/**
 * Master Editorial Sanitizer (POLARIS AI Pure Fabrication)
 *
 * Menegakkan Business Rule BR-04 (Zero-Tolerance Lexical & Punctuation Invariants):
 * 1. Anti Titik Dua & Em-Dash pada Judul/Heading
 * 2. Pembersihan Diksi Terlarang ("sekadar" & "nyata" -> "konkret")
 * 3. Pembersihan AI Slop Blacklist ("angin segar", "garda terdepan", "tonggak bersejarah", dll)
 * 4. Penghapusan Oxford Comma pada perincian elemen (dan/serta/atau)
 * 5. Penggantian Em-dash dengan tanda koma alami
 * 6. Normalisasi preposisi baku (di antaranya, di mana, ke sana, ke mana)
 */
export class MasterEditorialSanitizer {
  /**
   * Sanitizer khusus judul/headline agar bebas dari titik dua (:), em dash (—),
   * dan formula klise "Topik: Subjudul"
   */
  public static cleanHeadline(rawTitle?: string, fallback: string = ''): string {
    if (!rawTitle || typeof rawTitle !== 'string') {
      return (fallback || 'Analisis Kebijakan Strategis').trim();
    }

    let title = rawTitle.replace(/^["'“”‘’\s]+|["'“”‘’\s]+$/g, '').trim();

    // 1. Transformasi formula klise "Topik: Subjudul"
    if (title.includes(':')) {
      const parts = title.split(':').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      if (parts.length > 1) {
        const first = parts[0];
        const second = parts[1];

        if (/^(jalan menuju|upaya|ikhtiar|langkah|menuju|mewujudkan|sebuah tinjauan)\s+/i.test(second)) {
          const connector = second.replace(/^(jalan|upaya|ikhtiar|langkah)\s+menuju/i, 'Menuju');
          title = `${first} ${connector}`;
        } else if (second.length >= 25 && !second.toLowerCase().startsWith('studi')) {
          title = second;
        } else {
          title = `${first} demi ${second}`;
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Bersihkan karakter titik dua dan strip/dash pemisah sisa
    title = title.replace(/\s*:\s*/g, ' ').replace(/\s+-\s+/g, ' ');
    title = title.replace(/[:—–]/g, '').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim();

    // 3. Bersihkan tanda kutip pembungkus
    title = title.replace(/^["'“”‘’]+|["'“”‘’]+$/g, '').trim();

    // 4. Pastikan disucikan dari kata terlarang dan slop
    title = this.sanitizeForbiddenWords(title);
    title = this.removeAiSlop(title);

    return title || fallback;
  }

  /**
   * Menghapus kata terlarang sesuai Master Editorial Rules (Zero-Tolerance)
   * - "bukan sekadar" -> "tidak hanya"
   * - "sekadar" -> "hanya"
   * - "[kata] nyata" -> "[kata] konkret"
   * - "nyata" mandiri -> "konkret" (tanpa merusak 'menyatakan', 'pernyataan', 'kenyataan', 'ternyata')
   */
  public static sanitizeForbiddenWords(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    // 1. Substitusi "bukan sekadar" -> "tidak hanya"
    text = text.replace(/\bbukan\s+sekadar\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'TIDAK HANYA' : 'tidak hanya'
    );

    // 2. Substitusi "sekadar" -> "hanya"
    text = text.replace(/\bsekadar\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'HANYA' : 'hanya'
    );

    // 3. Kolokasi umum [verba/nomina] + "nyata" -> [kata] + "konkret"
    text = text.replace(
      /\b(langkah|dampak|bukti|ikhtiar|solusi|komitmen|kontribusi|perubahan|hasil|kerja|karya|tindakan|wujud|upaya|peran|aksi|manfaat|dukungan|terobosan|kebijakan|bantuan)\s+nyata\b/gi,
      (match, p1) => {
        const isAllUpper = match === match.toUpperCase();
        return `${p1} ${isAllUpper ? 'KONKRET' : 'konkret'}`;
      }
    );

    // 4. "yang nyata" / "secara nyata"
    text = text.replace(/\byang\s+nyata\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'YANG KONKRET' : 'yang konkret'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bsecara\s+nyata\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'SECARA KONKRET' : 'secara konkret'
    );

    // 5. Bersihkan sisa kata mandiri 'nyata' tanpa merusak morfem kata turunan
    text = text.replace(/\bnyata\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'KONKRET' : 'konkret'
    );

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Menghilangkan metafora klise AI (AI Slop Blacklist)
   */
  public static removeAiSlop(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    text = text.replace(/\bangin\s+segar\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'PELUANG BARU' : 'peluang baru'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\btonggak\s+(?:bersejarah|penting)\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'CAPAIAN PENTING' : 'capaian penting'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bgarda\s+terdepan\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'LINI UTAMA' : 'lini utama'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bmenatap\s+masa\s+depan\s+(?:yang\s+)?gemilang\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'MEMBANGUN KEMAJUAN TERUKUR' : 'membangun kemajuan terukur'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\b(?:merajut|rajutan?)\s+asa\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'MEMBANGUN OPTIMISME' : 'membangun optimisme'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bmembuka\s+tabir\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'MENGUNGKAP FAKTA' : 'mengungkap fakta'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bsinergi\s+(?:yang\s+)?komprehensif\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'KOLABORASI TERPADU' : 'kolaborasi terpadu'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bpada\s+hakikatnya\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'PADA DASARNYA' : 'pada dasarnya'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bseyogianya\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'SEHARUSNYA' : 'seharusnya'
    );
    text = text.replace(/\bniscaya\b/gi, (match) =>
      match === match.toUpperCase() ? 'PASTI' : 'pasti'
    );

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Menghapus Oxford comma pada perincian 3 elemen atau lebih sebelum "dan", "serta", "atau"
   */
  public static removeOxfordComma(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+dan\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 dan $2');
    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+serta\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 serta $2');
    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+atau\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 atau $2');

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Menghapus em dash (— atau –) dan menggantinya dengan koma alami
   */
  public static removeEmDash(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    return rawText.replace(/\s*[—–]\s*/g, ', ');
  }

  /**
   * Normalisasi ejaan preposisi umum yang sering salah sambung
   */
  public static normalizePrepositions(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    text = text.replace(/\bdiantaranya\b/gi, 'di antaranya');
    text = text.replace(/\bdimana\b/gi, 'di mana');
    text = text.replace(/\bkesana\b/gi, 'ke sana');
    text = text.replace(/\bkemana\b/gi, 'ke mana');

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Membersihkan format heading bab dari titik dua dan em-dash
   */
  public static cleanHeadings(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    // Heading bab bernomor: "## Bab I: Analisis" -> "## Bab I. Analisis"
    text = text.replace(/^(##\s+Bab\s+[IVXLCDM\d]+)\s*[:—–-]\s*/gim, '$1. ');

    // Heading subbab Markdown umum: "## Subbab: Analisis" -> "## Subbab Analisis"
    text = text.replace(/^(#{2,3}\s+[^:\n\r]+)\s*[:—–]\s*/gm, '$1 ');

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Eksekusi seluruh sanitasi mikrolinguistik deterministik
   */
  public static sanitize(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawText;

    text = this.cleanHeadings(text);
    text = this.removeEmDash(text);
    text = this.removeOxfordComma(text);
    text = this.normalizePrepositions(text);
    text = this.sanitizeForbiddenWords(text);
    text = this.removeAiSlop(text);

    return text;
  }
}

import {
  ILLMProviderPort,
  GenerateArticleParams,
  GeneratedArticleResult,
  GeneratedSocialPackResult,
  GenerateCivicAnswerParams,
  GeneratePublicConciergeParams,
  GeneratedConciergeResult,
} from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { InfographicDataSpecDto } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { openaiClient } from './client.js';
import { PromptTemplates } from './prompts.js';
import { PromptSanitizer } from './guardrails/prompt-sanitizer.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from './telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';
import { TwoStageArticleEngine } from './two-stage-article.engine.js';
import {
  PosterBlueprintSelector,
  PosterPromptCompiler,
  CreativePosterArchetype,
  PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
  CompiledPosterPrompt,
} from './creative-poster/index.js';
import { GoogleImagenAdapter } from './adapters/google-imagen.adapter.js';
import { getActiveImageProvider } from './config/image-provider.config.js';
import { MasterEditorialSanitizer } from './master-editorial-sanitizer.js';

export class OpenAIAIEngineAdapter implements ILLMProviderPort {
  private readonly googleImagenAdapter = new GoogleImagenAdapter();


  public async generatePublicConcierge(
    params: GeneratePublicConciergeParams
  ): Promise<GeneratedConciergeResult> {
    const inspection = PromptSanitizer.inspect(params.userMessage);

    // Jika tertolak oleh pre-inference guardrail, catat sebagai REFUSAL (0 token, 0 cost)
    if (!inspection.isValid) {
      const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
        operation: 'public-concierge-chat',
        model: 'heuristic-guardrail',
        metadata: { rejectionReason: inspection.rejectionReason },
      });

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'REFUSAL',
        errorMessage: inspection.rejectionReason,
        outputPreview: inspection.preBakedReply,
      });

      return {
        reply: inspection.preBakedReply || 'Pertanyaan tidak dapat diproses.',
        suggestedAction: inspection.suggestedAction,
        isSafeRefusal: true,
        tokensUsed: 0,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    }

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'public-concierge-chat',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      metadata: { rawInputLength: params.userMessage.length },
    });

    try {
      const historyContext = (params.history || [])
        .slice(-3)
        .map((h) => `${h.role === 'user' ? 'Pengunjung' : 'Asisten'}: ${h.content.slice(0, 150)}`)
        .join('\n');

      const systemPrompt = PromptTemplates.getPublicConciergePrompt(
        inspection.sanitizedText,
        historyContext
      );

      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.25,
        max_tokens: 300,
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: inspection.sanitizedText },
        ],
      });

      const reply = response.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || '';
      const inputTokens = response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0;
      const outputTokens = response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0;

      const isRefusal = reply.toLowerCase().includes('hanya dapat membantu menjawab pertanyaan seputar') ||
                        reply.toLowerCase().includes('diprogram khusus untuk memberikan informasi seputar');

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens,
        outputTokens,
        status: isRefusal ? 'REFUSAL' : 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: reply,
      });

      return {
        reply,
        suggestedAction: inspection.suggestedAction,
        isSafeRefusal: isRefusal,
        tokensUsed: inputTokens + outputTokens,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      return {
        reply: 'Mohon maaf, sistem asisten POLARIS sedang mengalami antrean jaringan. Silakan segarkan halaman.',
        suggestedAction: 'NONE',
        isSafeRefusal: true,
        tokensUsed: 0,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    }
  }

  public async generateArticle(params: GenerateArticleParams): Promise<GeneratedArticleResult> {
    return TwoStageArticleEngine.execute(params);
  }

  public async generateDalleImage(
    englishPrompt: string,
    tenantId?: string,
    size: '1024x1792' | '1024x1024' | '1792x1024' = '1024x1792'
  ): Promise<{ temporaryImageUrl: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const preferredModel = process.env.OPENAI_IMAGE_MODEL || 'chatgpt-image-latest';

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'dalle-image-generation',
      model: preferredModel,
      tenantId,
      metadata: { promptLength: englishPrompt.length, requestedSize: size },
    });

    // Urutan model yang dicoba secara adaptif jika model utama tidak tersedia pada API key
    const candidateModels = [preferredModel];
    if (preferredModel !== 'chatgpt-image-latest') {
      candidateModels.push('chatgpt-image-latest');
    }
    if (preferredModel !== 'dall-e-3') {
      candidateModels.push('dall-e-3');
    }

    let lastError: Error | null = null;
    let activeModel = preferredModel;

    for (const modelToTry of candidateModels) {
      try {
        activeModel = modelToTry;

        // Pemetaan dimensi yang didukung oleh masing-masing model
        let resolvedSize: any = size;
        if (modelToTry === 'chatgpt-image-latest') {
          if (size === '1024x1792') {
            resolvedSize = '1024x1536'; // Rasio tegak portrait poster didukung oleh chatgpt-image-latest
          } else if (size === '1792x1024') {
            resolvedSize = '1536x1024'; // Rasio lanskap didukung oleh chatgpt-image-latest
          } else {
            resolvedSize = '1024x1024';
          }
        }

        const requestParams: any = {
          model: modelToTry,
          prompt: englishPrompt,
          size: resolvedSize,
          n: 1,
        };

        // dall-e-3 mendukung opsi quality, chatgpt-image-latest menentukan kualitas secara dinamis
        if (modelToTry === 'dall-e-3') {
          requestParams.quality = 'standard';
        }

        const response = await openaiClient.images.generate(requestParams);
        const first = response.data?.[0];

        let temporaryImageUrl: string | undefined;
        if (first?.url) {
          temporaryImageUrl = first.url;
        } else if (first?.b64_json) {
          temporaryImageUrl = `data:image/png;base64,${first.b64_json}`;
        }

        if (!temporaryImageUrl) {
          throw new Error('AI Generator tidak mengembalikan URL atau data gambar yang valid.');
        }

        await telemetry.endTrace({
          inputTokens: 1000,
          outputTokens: 0,
          status: 'SUCCESS',
          outputPreview: { temporaryImageUrl: temporaryImageUrl.slice(0, 100), model: activeModel },
        });

        return {
          temporaryImageUrl,
          canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
        };
      } catch (err: any) {
        lastError = err;
        console.warn(`[AIEngineAdapter] Model gambar '${modelToTry}' gagal: ${err.message}. Mencoba fallback berikutnya...`);
        // Jika error adalah model does not exist atau izin model, lanjutkan ke model berikutnya di loop
      }
    }

    const finalErrorMessage = lastError ? lastError.message : 'Semua model gambar gagal diinisialisasi.';
    await telemetry.endTrace({
      inputTokens: 0,
      outputTokens: 0,
      status: 'FAILED',
      errorMessage: finalErrorMessage,
    });

    throw new Error(`[AIEngineDalleError] Gagal me-render poster DALL-E: ${finalErrorMessage}`);
  }

  /**
   * Menghasilkan kanvas dasar menggunakan Google Imagen 3 (native 4:5 + negative prompt)
   */
  public async generateGoogleImagen(params: {
    prompt: string;
    aspectRatio?: '4:5' | '1:1' | '16:9' | '9:16' | '3:4' | '4:3';
    tenantId?: string;
    stylePromptSuffix?: string;
    imageNegativeAllow?: string[];
  }): Promise<{ temporaryImageUrl: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    return await this.googleImagenAdapter.generateImage(params);
  }

  /**
   * generateCreativePosterBackground memilih template secara Constrained RNG,
   * mengompilasi prompt fotografi clean-art tanpa teks, dan menghasilkan background AI
   * menggunakan provider aktif (Google Imagen 3 atau OpenAI DALL-E) dengan failover otomatis.
   */
  public async generateCreativePosterBackground(params: {
    topic: string;
    groundedContextSummary?: string;
    compositionMode?: import('./creative-poster/types.js').SubjectCompositionMode;
    forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
    excludeBlueprintId?: string;
    tenantBrandHex?: string;
    tenantId?: string;
  }): Promise<{
    temporaryImageUrl: string;
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint;
    compiledPrompt: CompiledPosterPrompt;
    canonicalTraceId: string;
  }> {
    const blueprint = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({
      topic: params.topic,
      forcedArchetype: params.forcedArchetype,
      excludeBlueprintId: params.excludeBlueprintId,
      forcedBrandHex: params.tenantBrandHex,
    });

    const compiledPrompt = await PosterPromptCompiler.compileScenePrompt({
      topic: params.topic,
      groundedContextSummary: params.groundedContextSummary,
      compositionMode: params.compositionMode,
      blueprint,
      tenantBrandHex: params.tenantBrandHex,
    });

    const targetSize =
      compiledPrompt.aspectRatio === '9:16' || compiledPrompt.aspectRatio === '4:5'
        ? '1024x1792'
        : '1024x1024';

    const activeProvider = getActiveImageProvider();
    let imageResult: { temporaryImageUrl: string; canonicalTraceId: string };

    if (activeProvider === 'GOOGLE_IMAGEN') {
      try {
        console.log(
          `[AIEngineAdapter] Menghasilkan kanvas seni Layer 1 via Google Imagen 3 (aspek rasio: ${compiledPrompt.aspectRatio || '4:5'})...`
        );
        imageResult = await this.generateGoogleImagen({
          prompt: compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
          aspectRatio: (compiledPrompt.aspectRatio as any) || '4:5',
          tenantId: params.tenantId,
          stylePromptSuffix: blueprint.scenePromptHint,
          imageNegativeAllow: blueprint.fixedConstraints?.imageNegativeAllow,
        });
      } catch (googleError: any) {
        console.warn(
          `[AIEngineAdapter] Google Imagen 3 gagal (${googleError.message}). Mengaktifkan failover otomatis ke OpenAI DALL-E...`
        );
        imageResult = await this.generateDalleImage(
          compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
          params.tenantId,
          targetSize
        );
      }
    } else {
      console.log(
        `[AIEngineAdapter] Menghasilkan kanvas seni Layer 1 via OpenAI DALL-E (target size: ${targetSize})...`
      );
      imageResult = await this.generateDalleImage(
        compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
        params.tenantId,
        targetSize
      );
    }

    return {
      temporaryImageUrl: imageResult.temporaryImageUrl,
      blueprint,
      compiledPrompt,
      canonicalTraceId: imageResult.canonicalTraceId,
    };
  }

  public async generateCivicAnswer(
    params: GenerateCivicAnswerParams
  ): Promise<{ answer: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'generate-civic-answer-grounded',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      tenantId: params.tenantId,
      metadata: { representative: params.representativeName, dapil: params.dapilName },
    });

    try {
      const systemPrompt = PromptTemplates.getCivicAssistantPrompt(
        params.groundedRegulations,
        params.representativeName,
        params.partyAffiliation,
        params.dapilName
      );

      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.2,
        max_tokens: 300,
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: params.citizenQuestion },
        ],
      });

      const answer = response.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || '';
      const inputTokens = response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0;
      const outputTokens = response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0;

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens,
        outputTokens,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: answer,
      });

      return {
        answer,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[AIEngineCivicAnswerError] Gagal menyusun respon warga: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  public async generateEmbedding(
    text: string,
    tenantId?: string
  ): Promise<{ embedding: number[]; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const cleanText = text.trim();
    if (!cleanText) {
      throw new Error('[EmbeddingError] Teks input untuk embedding tidak boleh kosong.');
    }

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'text-embedding-rag',
      model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
      tenantId,
      metadata: { textLength: cleanText.length },
    });

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.embeddings.create({
        model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
        input: cleanText,
      });

      const embedding = response.data[0]?.embedding;
      if (!embedding || embedding.length !== 1536) {
        throw new Error('Dimensi embedding yang dikembalikan tidak sesuai.');
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage.total_tokens,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: { dimensions: embedding.length },
      });

      return {
        embedding,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[AIEngineEmbeddingError] Gagal menghasilkan embedding: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  public async generateInfographicSpec(articleContent: string, tenantId?: string): Promise<InfographicDataSpecDto> {
    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      temperature: 0.2,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: PromptTemplates.getInfographicSystemPrompt() },
        { role: 'user', content: `Artikel rujukan:\n\n${articleContent}` },
      ],
    });

    return JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
  }

  public async generateDallePrompt(infographicSpec: InfographicDataSpecDto): Promise<string> {
    return PromptTemplates.getDalleVisualPrompt(infographicSpec.headline, infographicSpec.policyTakeaway);
  }

  public async generateSocialSnippets(articleContent: string): Promise<GeneratedSocialPackResult> {
    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      temperature: 0.4,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: PromptTemplates.getSocialSnippetSystemPrompt() },
        { role: 'user', content: `Artikel rujukan:\n\n${articleContent}` },
      ],
    });

    const parsed = JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}') as GeneratedSocialPackResult;
    return {
      instagramCaption: MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(parsed.instagramCaption || ''),
      twitterThreads: (parsed.twitterThreads || []).map((t) =>
        MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(t || '')
      ),
      whatsappBroadcast: MasterEditorialSanitizer.sanitize(parsed.whatsappBroadcast || ''),
    };
  }

  public async performVisionOCR(params: {
    base64Data: string;
    mimeType: string;
    fileName: string;
  }): Promise<string> {
    const imageUrl = params.base64Data.startsWith('data:')
      ? params.base64Data
      : `data:${params.mimeType};base64,${params.base64Data}`;

    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      temperature: 0.1,
      max_tokens: 2000,
      messages: [
        {
          role: 'system',
          content: 'Ekstrak seluruh teks dan data tabel dari dokumen lampiran ke dalam format Markdown rapi.',
        },
        {
          role: 'user',
          content: [
            { type: 'text', text: `Transkripsikan isi dokumen "${params.fileName}":` },
            { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: imageUrl, detail: 'high' } },
          ],
        },
      ],
    });

    return response.choices[0]?.message?.content || 'Tidak ada teks yang terdeteksi.';
  }
}

import { ConciergePromptBuilder } from './prompts/concierge.prompt.js';

export class PromptTemplates {
  public static getPublicConciergePrompt(userMessage: string, historyContext = ''): string {
    return ConciergePromptBuilder.buildPrompt(userMessage, historyContext);
  }

  public static getCivicAssistantPrompt(
    groundedRegulations: string,
    representativeName: string,
    partyAffiliation?: string | null,
    dapilName?: string | null
  ): string {
    return `Anda adalah "POLARIS Civic Assistant", representasi digital resmi dari ${representativeName} (Fraksi ${partyAffiliation || 'Parlemen'}, Dapil ${dapilName || 'Daerah Pemilihan'}).

TUGAS UTAMA:
Menjawab pertanyaan warga dengan ramah, lugas, solutif, dan berwibawa, berdasarkan rujukan hukum dan regulasi resmi yang tersedia.

KONTEKS REGULASI DAERAH YANG DITEMUKAN (SUMBER KEBENARAN TUNGGAL):
${groundedRegulations || 'Tidak ditemukan pasal regulasi daerah spesifik yang relevan dengan pertanyaan ini.'}

PANDUAN MENJAWAB:
1. Batasi jawaban maksimal 3 hingga 4 kalimat yang padat dan informatif.
2. JIKA regulasi yang ditemukan relevan, sebutkan nomor pasal/perda terkait secara ringkas sebagai dasar jawaban.
3. JIKA konteks regulasi kosong atau tidak menjawab pertanyaan secara presisi, JANGAN MENGARANG ATURAN FIKTIF. Sampaikan secara sopan bahwa aspirasi tersebut akan dikoordinasikan langsung oleh tim dewan ke instansi dinas teknis terkait, dan sarankan warga menggunakan tombol "Kirim Aspirasi" untuk tindak lanjut resmi.
4. Akhiri dengan salam hormat khas pelayanan publik yang ramah.`;
  }

  public static getArticleSystemPrompt(): string {
    return `Anda adalah "POLARIS", Digital Chief of Staff dan Tenaga Ahli Senior Parlemen untuk Anggota Dewan.
Tugas Anda adalah menulis artikel analisis kebijakan publik yang komprehensif, teknokratis, dan berbobot setara dengan kajian opini/investigasi media nasional terkemuka (minimal 2.000 hingga 2.500 kata).

PANDUAN FORMAT KONTEN BERAGAM & DINAMIS (WAJIB):
Konten TIDAK BOLEH monoton teks saja! Artikel harus memiliki irama visual jurnalistik modern yang kaya dan bervariasi:

   Setelah lead paragraf, sajikan ringkasan eksekutif bernas yang menggarisbawahi urgensi isu dan arah intervensi kebijakan.

   - Bab 5: Rekomendasi Solutif & Komitmen Aksi Pengawasan Dewan

8. PANTANGAN MASTER EDITORIAL & GARIS PEMISAH (ZERO-TOLERANCE):
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "sekadar" maupun "bukan sekadar". Gunakan kata "tidak hanya" atau formulasi lugas lainnya.
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "nyata" (seperti "dampak nyata", "langkah nyata", "solusi nyata", "ikhtiar nyata"). Paparkan fakta dan capaian konkret.
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda baca em dash (—) maupun en dash (–). Gunakan koma, titik dua, titik, atau tanda kurung.
   - DILARANG menggunakan Oxford comma (koma sebelum 'dan', 'serta', 'atau' pada perincian).
   - DILARANG KERAS MENYISIPKAN GARIS PEMISAH HORIZONTAL (seperti "---", "***", "___", atau deretan tanda hubung) di bawah judul bab maupun di sela-sela paragraf. Transisi wajib murni mengalir alami melalui spasi paragraf normal.
9. Format Output wajib berupa JSON terstruktur murni tanpa markdown pembungkus.`;
  }

  public static getInfographicSystemPrompt(): string {

3. WhatsApp Peer Memo (Closed-System / Kolega Dewan & Fraksi): Format pesan WhatsApp resmi dan kolegial yang ditujukan KHUSUS untuk sesama rekan anggota dewan / pimpinan fraksi / komisi (peer-to-peer / close system, BUKAN broadcast warga publik). Gunakan gaya bahasa teknokratis, kolegial, dan bernas (misal: salam pembuka "Yth. Rekan-rekan Anggota Dewan / Pimpinan Fraksi..."). Format pesan WA harus terstruktur rapi:

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/guardrails/prompt-sanitizer.ts (66 baris, 2.8 KB) ---

export interface SanitizeResult {
    isValid: boolean;
    sanitizedText: string;
    rejectionReason?: string;
    preBakedReply?: string;
    suggestedAction: 'VIEW_PRICING' | 'REGISTER' | 'NONE';
}

export class PromptSanitizer {
    public static readonly MAX_INPUT_CHARS = 250;

    private static readonly INJECTION_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
        /ignore\s+(all\s+)?(previous\s+)?instructions/i,
        /abaikan\s+(semua\s+)?(instruksi|perintah|aturan)/i,
        /dan\s+mode/i,
        /jailbreak/i,
        /pretend\s+you\s+are/i,
        /bertindaklah\s+sebagai/i,
        /you\s+are\s+now\s+an?\s+unrestricted/i,
        /(system\s+prompt|instruksi\s+sistem)/i,
        /(prompt|system)\s+dump/i,
        /(tuliskan|cetak|print|show|bocorkan)\s+(system\s+prompt|instruksi\s+sistem)/i,
        /bikin(kan)?\s+(kode|script|program|python|javascript|sql)/i,
        /write\s+(a\s+)?(script|code|malware|exploit)/i,
    ];

    private static readonly OFF_TOPIC_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
        /\b(siapa|who\s+is)\s+(presiden|president|perdana\s+menteri|prime\s+minister)\b/i,
        /\b(resep|masak|recipe|cook(ing)?|cuaca|weather|matematika|mathematics|\bmath\b)\b/i,
        /\b(berapa\s+hasil|calculate|solve\s+this)\b/i,
    ];

    public static inspect(rawText: string): SanitizeResult {
        if (!rawText || rawText.trim().length === 0) {
            return {
                isValid: false,
                sanitizedText: '',
                rejectionReason: 'Pesan kosong.',
                preBakedReply: 'Halo! Ada yang bisa saya bantu terkait fitur, layanan, atau paket lisensi POLARIS?',
                suggestedAction: 'NONE',
            };
        }

        const clamped = rawText.trim().slice(0, this.MAX_INPUT_CHARS);
        if ([...this.INJECTION_PATTERNS, ...this.OFF_TOPIC_PATTERNS].some((pattern) => pattern.test(clamped))) {
            return {
                isValid: false,
                sanitizedText: clamped,
                rejectionReason: 'Pola manipulasi prompt atau pertanyaan di luar konteks terdeteksi.',
                preBakedReply:
                    'Mohon maaf, saya adalah asisten resmi POLARIS yang diprogram khusus untuk memberikan informasi seputar fitur, paket harga, dan layanan platform POLARIS.',
                suggestedAction: 'NONE',
            };
        }

        const lower = clamped.toLowerCase();
        let suggestedAction: SanitizeResult['suggestedAction'] = 'NONE';
        if (['harga', 'biaya', 'paket', 'tarif', 'bayar'].some((word) => lower.includes(word))) {
            suggestedAction = 'VIEW_PRICING';
        } else if (['daftar', 'register', 'buat akun', 'coba'].some((word) => lower.includes(word))) {
            suggestedAction = 'REGISTER';
        }

        return { isValid: true, sanitizedText: clamped, suggestedAction };
    }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/context-capsule.ts (58 baris, 2.7 KB) ---

export interface RawContextSources {
  regionName: string;
  regulations: Array<{ reference: string; content: string }>;
  recentNews: Array<{ title: string; summary: string; portal: string }>;
  memberWritingSample?: string;
  comparisonRegionData?: string;
  externalWebSources?: Array<{ url: string; title?: string; content: string }>;
  attachedDocuments?: Array<{ name: string; type: string; extractedContent: string }>;
  framingStance?: string;
}

export class ContextCapsuleBuilder {
  /**
   * assembleContext menggabungkan seluruh layer fakta menjadi satu teks
   * grounding terstruktur untuk disuntikkan ke LLM.
   */
  public static assembleContext(sources: RawContextSources): string {
    const regulationSection = sources.regulations.length > 0
      ? sources.regulations.map((r, i) => `[Regulasi ${i + 1}] ${r.reference}:\n${r.content}`).join('\n\n')
      : 'Data regulasi daerah khusus belum tersedia.';

    const newsSection = sources.recentNews.length > 0
      ? sources.recentNews.map((n, i) => `[Berita ${i + 1} - ${n.portal}] ${n.title}: ${n.summary}`).join('\n')
      : 'Tidak ada berita daerah signifikan dalam 24 jam terakhir.';

    const comparisonSection = sources.comparisonRegionData
      ? `\n\n--- DATA KOMPARASI REGIONAL (STUDI BANDING) ---\n${sources.comparisonRegionData}`
      : '';

    const writingStyleSection = sources.memberWritingSample
      ? `\n\n--- CONTOH PREFERENSI GAYA BAHASA RESMI DEWAN ---\n"${sources.memberWritingSample}"\n(Catatan: Pertahankan diksi dan bobot argumentasi serupa dengan contoh ini).`
      : '';

    const webSection = sources.externalWebSources && sources.externalWebSources.length > 0
      ? `\n\n--- RUJUKAN SITUS WEB & RISET INTERNET TERKINI (LIVE INGESTION) ---\n` +
        sources.externalWebSources.map((w, i) => `[Tautan Web ${i + 1}] ${w.url}${w.title ? ` (${w.title})` : ''}:\n${w.content}`).join('\n\n')
      : '';

    const docSection = sources.attachedDocuments && sources.attachedDocuments.length > 0
      ? `\n\n--- DOKUMEN & FOTO LAMPIRAN DEWAN (HASIL OCR & EKSTRAKSI DATA TABEL) ---\n` +
        sources.attachedDocuments.map((d, i) => `[Dokumen ${i + 1}] ${d.name} (${d.type}):\n${d.extractedContent}`).join('\n\n')
      : '';

    const framingSection = sources.framingStance
      ? `\n\n--- ARAHAN SUDUT PANDANG / FRAMING POLITIK DEWAN ---\nFokus Framing: ${sources.framingStance}`
      : '';

    return `=== KAPSUL KONTEKS POLARIS (GROUNDING DATA LENGKAP) ===
WILAYAH TARGET: ${sources.regionName}

--- FAKTA REGULASI & PERATURAN RESMI AKTIF ---
${regulationSection}

--- DISKURSUS PUBLIK & BERITA MEDIA TERKINI (CRON CRAWLER) ---
${newsSection}${webSection}${docSection}${framingSection}${comparisonSection}${writingStyleSection}
======================================================`;
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/autonomous-research.orchestrator.ts (579 baris, 19.1 KB) ---
