import {
  db,
  researchJobs,
  researchQuestions,
  sources,
  researchJobSources,
  sourceVersions,
  sourceAssessments,
  evidenceSpans,
  claims,
  claimEvidence,
  researchOutputs,
  eq,
} from '@polaris/database';
import {
  ResearchJobStatus,
  SourceType,
  PublisherType,
  SourceLifecycle,
  ClaimStatus,
  SupportType,
} from '@polaris/shared-types';

import { ResearchPlannerEngine, ResearchPlanInput, ResearchPlanOutput } from './research-planner.engine.js';

import { AcademicDiscoveryConnector } from './connectors/academic-discovery.connector.js';
import { InternalKnowledgeConnector } from './connectors/internal-knowledge.connector.js';
import { WebDiscoveryConnector } from './connectors/web-discovery.connector.js';
import { SourceCredibilityAssessor } from './source-credibility.assessor.js';
import { ClaimEvidenceExtractor } from './claim-evidence.extractor.js';
import { DeterministicQualityGates } from './deterministic-gates.js';

export interface AutonomousResearchJobInput {
  tenantId: string;
  topic: string;
  userPrompt: string;
  targetRegion: string;
  queryEmbedding?: number[];
  attachedDocuments?: Array<{ name: string; type: string; extractedContent: string }>;
  providedUrls?: string[];
  onProgress?: (step: string, percent: number) => Promise<void> | void;
}

export interface AutonomousResearchJobResult {
  jobId: string;
  mainTopic: string;
  evidenceSufficient: boolean;
  qcPassed: boolean;
  totalSourcesEvaluated: number;
  verifiedSourcesCount: number;
  totalEvidenceSpans: number;
  claimsCount: number;
  groundedContextText: string;
  evidenceIdMap: Map<string, string>; // Maps "EV-1" to database UUID
}

export class AutonomousResearchOrchestrator {
  /**
   * Menjalankan siklus riset otonom POLARIS secara menyeluruh (End-to-End Autonomous Research)
   */
  public static async executeResearchPipeline(
    input: AutonomousResearchJobInput
  ): Promise<AutonomousResearchJobResult> {
    const emit = async (msg: string, pct: number) => {
      if (input.onProgress) {
        await input.onProgress(msg, pct);
      }
    };

    // 1. Inisialisasi Job Riset di Basis Data
    await emit('Menginisialisasi sesi riset otonom di database...', 5);
    const [job] = await db
      .insert(researchJobs)
      .values({
        tenantId: input.tenantId,
        topic: input.topic,
        userPrompt: input.userPrompt,
        targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
        status: ResearchJobStatus.PLANNING,
      })
      .returning();

    const jobId = job.id;

    // 2. TAHAP 1: Research Planner (Divide-and-Conquer Questions)
    await emit('Merumuskan pertanyaan riset kausal & parameter bukti...', 12);
    const attachedSummary = (input.attachedDocuments || [])
      .map((d) => `[${d.name}]: ${d.extractedContent.slice(0, 500)}`)
      .join('\n');

    const plan: ResearchPlanOutput = await ResearchPlannerEngine.createPlan({
      userPrompt: input.userPrompt,
      targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
      attachedDocsSummary: attachedSummary,
      providedUrls: input.providedUrls,
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
    });

    // Simpan daftar pertanyaan riset ke tabel research_questions
    const insertedQuestions: Array<{ id: string; question: string; minRequiredEvidence: number }> = [];
    for (const q of plan.questions) {
      const [qRow] = await db
        .insert(researchQuestions)
        .values({
          researchJobId: jobId,
          question: q.question,
          intentCategory: q.intentCategory,
          temporalBound: q.temporalBound,
          geographicScope: q.geographicScope,
          minRequiredEvidence: q.minRequiredEvidence || 1,
        })
        .returning();
      insertedQuestions.push({
        id: qRow.id,
        question: qRow.question,
        minRequiredEvidence: qRow.minRequiredEvidence,
      });
    }

    // 3. TAHAP 2: Multi-Source Discovery (Internal Knowledge + Web + Academic Crossref)
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.SEARCHING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Mengambil regulasi internal daerah & arsip diskursus berita...', 22);
    const internalKnowledge = await InternalKnowledgeConnector.retrieveInternalKnowledge({
      queryEmbedding: input.queryEmbedding,
      targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
      policySector: plan.internalRetrievalPlan.mediaSector,
      keywords: plan.internalRetrievalPlan.mediaKeywords,
      maxRegulations: 3,
      maxNews: 4,
    });

    await emit('Menelusuri publikasi ilmiah bereputasi (Crossref DOI) & tautan web...', 32);
    const academicPapers = plan.academicQueries && plan.academicQueries.length > 0
      ? await AcademicDiscoveryConnector.searchCrossref(plan.academicQueries[0], 3)
      : [];

    const webCandidates = input.providedUrls && input.providedUrls.length > 0
      ? await WebDiscoveryConnector.fetchMultipleUrls(input.providedUrls)
      : [];

    // 4. TAHAP 3: Source Registry, Deduplication & Credibility Assessment
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.EVALUATING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Menilai substansi, kesegaran & integritas metodologi sumber...', 42);

    interface RegisteredSourceItem {
      sourceId: string;
      versionId: string;
      title: string;
      url: string;
      publisherName: string;
      extractedText: string;
      isAccepted: boolean;
    }

    const evaluatedSources: RegisteredSourceItem[] = [];

    // Proses A: Registrasi Sumber Regulasi Internal
    for (const reg of internalKnowledge.regulations) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: `internal://regulation/${reg.documentId}`,
          canonicalUrl: `internal://regulation/${reg.documentId}`,
          sourceType: SourceType.INTERNAL_REGULATION,
          publisherName: 'Pemerintah Daerah / JDIH',
          publisherType: PublisherType.GOVERNMENT,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.95,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(reg.chunkContent),
          title: reg.structuralReference,
          extractedText: reg.chunkContent,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: reg.similarityScore,
        queryUsed: 'Vector HNSW Cosine Search',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: reg.structuralReference,
        url: s.url,
        publisherName: 'JDIH / Regulasi Daerah',
        extractedText: reg.chunkContent,
        isAccepted: true,
      });
    }

    // Proses B: Registrasi Sumber Berita Internal (media_discourses)
    for (const news of internalKnowledge.news) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: news.originalUrl,
          canonicalUrl: news.originalUrl,
          sourceType: SourceType.INTERNAL_NEWS,
          publisherName: news.portalName,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.80,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(news.cleanSummary),
          title: news.articleTitle,
          extractedText: news.cleanSummary,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: 0.80,
        queryUsed: 'Civic Media Diskursus Filter',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: news.articleTitle,
        url: s.url,
        publisherName: news.portalName,
        extractedText: news.cleanSummary,
        isAccepted: true,
      });
    }

    // Proses C: Asesmen dan Registrasi Paper Akademik Crossref
    for (const paper of academicPapers) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: paper.url,
          canonicalUrl: `https://doi.org/${paper.doi}`,
          sourceType: SourceType.ACADEMIC_PAPER,
          publisherName: paper.journalTitle || 'Academic Publisher',
          publisherType: PublisherType.ACADEMIC,
          doi: paper.doi,
          isPeerReviewed: paper.isPeerReviewed,
          hasRetractionsOrCorrections: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.88,
          lifecycleStatus: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections
            ? SourceLifecycle.REJECTED
            : SourceLifecycle.ASSESSED,
        })
        .returning();

      const textBody = `${paper.title}. Penulis: ${paper.authors.join(', ')} (${paper.publicationYear || 'N/A'}). Jurnal: ${paper.journalTitle || ''}. ${paper.abstractSnippet || ''}`;
      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(textBody),
          title: paper.title,
          extractedText: textBody,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      const assessment = await SourceCredibilityAssessor.assessSource(
        {
          url: paper.url,
          domain: 'crossref.org',
          title: paper.title,
          extractedText: textBody,
          publisherType: PublisherType.ACADEMIC,
          doi: paper.doi,
          isPeerReviewed: paper.isPeerReviewed,
          hasRetractions: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections,
        },
        plan.mainTopic
      );

      await db.insert(sourceAssessments).values({
        sourceId: s.id,
        substantiveScore: assessment.substantiveScore,
        methodologyScore: assessment.methodologyScore,
        recencyScore: assessment.recencyScore,
        overallScore: assessment.overallScore,
        assessmentReason: assessment.assessmentReason,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });

      await db
        .update(sources)
        .set({ lifecycleStatus: assessment.lifecycleStatus })
        .where(eq(sources.id, s.id));

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: assessment.overallScore,
        queryUsed: plan.academicQueries[0] || 'Academic Query',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: paper.title,
        url: paper.url,
        publisherName: paper.journalTitle || 'Jurnal Akademik',
        extractedText: textBody,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });
    }

    // Proses D: Asesmen dan Registrasi Tautan Web Eksternal
    for (const web of webCandidates) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: web.url,
          canonicalUrl: web.canonicalUrl,
          sourceType: SourceType.EXTERNAL_WEB,
          publisherName: web.publisherName,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.FETCHED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: web.contentHash,
          title: web.title,
          rawContent: web.rawContent,
          extractedText: web.extractedText,
          httpStatus: web.httpStatus,
        })
        .returning();

      const assessment = await SourceCredibilityAssessor.assessSource(
        {
          url: web.url,
          domain: web.domain,
          title: web.title,
          extractedText: web.extractedText,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
        },
        plan.mainTopic
      );

      await db.insert(sourceAssessments).values({
        sourceId: s.id,
        substantiveScore: assessment.substantiveScore,
        methodologyScore: assessment.methodologyScore,
        recencyScore: assessment.recencyScore,
        overallScore: assessment.overallScore,
        assessmentReason: assessment.assessmentReason,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });

      await db
        .update(sources)
        .set({ lifecycleStatus: assessment.lifecycleStatus })
        .where(eq(sources.id, s.id));

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: assessment.overallScore,
        queryUsed: 'User Supplied URL',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: web.title,
        url: web.url,
        publisherName: web.publisherName || web.domain,
        extractedText: web.extractedText,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });
    }

    // Proses E: Registrasi Dokumen Lampiran Pengguna (User Attached Documents / Reses / Dokumen Lapangan)
    if (input.attachedDocuments && input.attachedDocuments.length > 0) {
      for (const doc of input.attachedDocuments) {
        if (!doc.extractedContent || doc.extractedContent.trim().length === 0) continue;

        const [s] = await db
          .insert(sources)
          .values({
            url: `attachment://${encodeURIComponent(doc.name)}`,
            canonicalUrl: `attachment://${encodeURIComponent(doc.name)}`,
            sourceType: SourceType.INTERNAL_REGULATION,
            publisherName: `Lampiran Lapangan / Dokumen (${doc.name})`,
            publisherType: PublisherType.GOVERNMENT,
            publisherCredibilityScore: 0.98,
            lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED,
          })
          .returning();

        const [sv] = await db
          .insert(sourceVersions)
          .values({
            sourceId: s.id,
            contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(doc.extractedContent),
            title: doc.name,
            extractedText: doc.extractedContent,
            httpStatus: 200,
          })
          .returning();

        await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
          researchJobId: jobId,
          sourceId: s.id,
          relevanceScore: 0.98,
          queryUsed: 'User Attachment Ingestion',
        });

        evaluatedSources.push({
          sourceId: s.id,
          versionId: sv.id,
          title: doc.name,
          url: s.url,
          publisherName: `Lampiran Lapangan / Dokumen (${doc.name})`,
          extractedText: doc.extractedContent,
          isAccepted: true,
        });
      }
    }

    // 5. TAHAP 4: Claim & Evidence Extraction
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.EXTRACTING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Mengekstrak klaim kebijakan & bukti kutipan verbatim...', 58);

    const acceptedSources = evaluatedSources.filter((s) => s.isAccepted);
    const extractedClaims = await ClaimEvidenceExtractor.extractClaimsAndEvidence(
      plan.mainTopic,
      insertedQuestions.map((q, idx) => ({ index: idx + 1, question: q.question })),
      acceptedSources
    );

    // Simpan claims dan evidence_spans ke DB
    const evidenceCountPerQuestion: Record<string, number> = {};
    const evidenceIdMap = new Map<string, string>(); // 'EV-1' -> database uuid
    const evidenceDetailMap = new Map<string, { id: string; quote: string; context: string }>();
    let spanCounter = 1;

    for (const c of extractedClaims) {
      // Petakan ke researchQuestionId
      const targetQuestion =
        c.questionIndex && c.questionIndex > 0 && c.questionIndex <= insertedQuestions.length
          ? insertedQuestions[c.questionIndex - 1]
          : insertedQuestions[0];

      const [claimRow] = await db
        .insert(claims)
        .values({
          researchJobId: jobId,
          researchQuestionId: targetQuestion?.id,
          claimStatement: c.claimStatement,
          status: c.status,
          confidenceScore: c.confidenceScore,
        })
        .returning();

      for (const ev of c.evidenceItems) {
        const shortToken = `EV-${spanCounter++}`;
        const [evSpan] = await db
          .insert(evidenceSpans)
          .values({
            sourceId: ev.sourceId,
            verbatimQuote: ev.verbatimQuote,
            contextSnippet: ev.contextSnippet,
            sectionOrPage: ev.sectionOrPage,
            confidenceScore: 0.95,
            isVerified: true,
          })
          .returning();

        evidenceIdMap.set(shortToken, evSpan.id);
        evidenceDetailMap.set(shortToken, {
          id: evSpan.id,
          quote: ev.verbatimQuote,
          context: ev.contextSnippet,
        });

        await db.insert(claimEvidence).values({
          claimId: claimRow.id,
          evidenceSpanId: evSpan.id,
          supportType: ev.supportType,
          relationshipExplanation: ev.relationshipExplanation,
        });

        if (targetQuestion) {
          evidenceCountPerQuestion[targetQuestion.id] =
            (evidenceCountPerQuestion[targetQuestion.id] || 0) + 1;
        }
      }
    }

    // 6. TAHAP 5: Backend Deterministic Gate -> Evidence Sufficient?
    await emit('Memeriksa kecukupan bukti secara deterministik di backend...', 72);

    const totalEvidenceSpansCount = evidenceIdMap.size;
    const sufficiencyResult = DeterministicQualityGates.verifyEvidenceSufficiency(
      insertedQuestions,
      evidenceCountPerQuestion,
      totalEvidenceSpansCount
    );

    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({
        evidenceSufficient: sufficiencyResult.isSufficient,
        summaryReport: sufficiencyResult.gapAnalysisReason || 'Bukti riset mencukupi.',
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    // 7. TAHAP 6: Rakit Grounded Evidence Capsule Berindeks
    const evidenceCapsuleLines: string[] = [];
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`=== MANIFEST BUKTI TERVERIFIKASI POLARIS (GROUNDING RESMI) ===`);
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`TOPIK RISET: ${plan.mainTopic}`);
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`WILAYAH SASARAN: ${input.targetRegion}\n`);

    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`--- DAFTAR BUKTI BERSERTIFIKAT (SITASI RESMI) ---`);
    for (const [token, detail] of evidenceDetailMap.entries()) {
      evidenceCapsuleLines.push(
        `[${token}] (ID: ${detail.id}) - Kutipan: "${detail.quote}" (Konteks: ${detail.context})`
      );
    }

    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`\n--- DAFTAR SUMBER RUJUKAN RESMI ---`);
    for (const src of acceptedSources) {
      evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`- [${src.publisherName}] ${src.title}: ${src.url}`);
    }
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`===========================================================`);

    const groundedContextText = evidenceCapsuleLines.join('\n');

    await emit('Siklus riset otonom selesai. Siap diteruskan ke Evidence-Grounded Writer.', 85);

    return {
      jobId,
      mainTopic: plan.mainTopic,
      evidenceSufficient: sufficiencyResult.isSufficient,
      qcPassed: false, // Menunggu tahap penulisan dan QC gate
      totalSourcesEvaluated: evaluatedSources.length,
      verifiedSourcesCount: acceptedSources.length,
      totalEvidenceSpans: totalEvidenceSpansCount,
      claimsCount: extractedClaims.length,
      groundedContextText,
      evidenceIdMap,
    };
  }
}

import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';
import { QuestionIntent } from '@polaris/shared-types';

export interface ResearchPlanInput {
  userPrompt: string;
  targetRegion: string;
  attachedDocsSummary?: string;
  providedUrls?: string[];
  tenantId?: string;
}

export interface PlannedResearchQuestion {
  question: string;
  intentCategory: QuestionIntent;
  temporalBound?: string;
  geographicScope: string;
  minRequiredEvidence: number;
  searchQueries: string[];
}

export interface ResearchPlanOutput {
  mainTopic: string;
  coreProblemStatement: string;
  questions: PlannedResearchQuestion[];
  internalRetrievalPlan: {
    regulationKeywords: string[];
    mediaSector?: string;
    mediaKeywords: string[];
  };
  academicQueries: string[];
  externalWebQueries: string[];
}

export class ResearchPlannerEngine {
  /**
   * Merumuskan rencana riset terstruktur (Divide-and-Conquer) dari instruksi awal pengguna.
   * Mencegah query dangkal 1-2 kata dan mewajibkan causal question & parameter bukti.
   */
  public static async createPlan(input: ResearchPlanInput): Promise<ResearchPlanOutput> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'autonomous-research-planner',
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
      metadata: { targetRegion: input.targetRegion, promptLength: input.userPrompt.length },
    });

    const systemPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Lead Research Planner & Policy Methodologist".
Tugas Anda adalah merumuskan rencana investigasi dan riset kebijakan yang mendalam dan terstruktur dari arahan pengguna.

=== PRINSIP PERUMUSAN RISET OTONOM POLARIS ===
1. PERTANYAAN KAUSAL & SUBSTANTIF (ANTI-SUPERFICIAL):
   - JANGAN membuat pertanyaan dangkal atau hanya mengandalkan 1-2 kata kunci terpisah.
   - Rumuskan 3 hingga 5 pertanyaan riset yang spesifik, memiliki hubungan sebab-akibat (causal), dan menguji klaim kebijakan.
2. KLASIFIKASI INTENSI:
   Tiap pertanyaan riset WAJIB dikategorikan ke salah satu intensi:
   - REGULATORY: Dasar hukum, perda, perbup, undang-undang, harmonisasi regulasi.
   - STATISTICAL_BENCHMARK: Angka APBD, BPS, capaian indikator makro, studi komparasi daerah.
   - PUBLIC_OPINION: Sentimen media massa lokal, keluhan warga, dinamika politik parlemen.
   - POLICY_EVALUATION: Analisis efektivitas program, hambatan lapangan, evaluasi belanja publik.
   - FACTUAL_VERIFICATION: Konfirmasi fakta konkret atas insiden/isu yang dilaporkan pengguna.
3. BATASAN WAKTU & WILAYAH (TEMPORAL & GEOGRAPHIC BOUND):
   - Nyatakan batasan waktu (misal: "2024-2026" atau "5 tahun terakhir").
   - Lingkup wilayah: "${input.targetRegion}" atau benchmark nasional yang relevan.
4. ATURAN JUDUL:
   - Judul riset utama (mainTopic) WAJIB bernas, profesional, dan DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda titik dua (":").
5. STRATEGI PENCARIAN GANDA (INTERNAL + AKADEMIK + WEB):
   - Siapkan query pencarian majemuk yang kaya kata kunci kontekstual (bukan 1 kata lepas).

Format Output WAJIB berupa JSON murni dengan skema:
{
  "mainTopic": "Judul investigasi tanpa tanda titik dua",
  "coreProblemStatement": "Ringkasan 2-3 kalimat mengenai akar masalah yang diselidiki",
  "questions": [
    {
      "question": "Kalimat pertanyaan riset lengkap dan berbobot",
      "intentCategory": "REGULATORY" | "STATISTICAL_BENCHMARK" | "PUBLIC_OPINION" | "POLICY_EVALUATION" | "FACTUAL_VERIFICATION",
      "temporalBound": "2023-2026",
      "geographicScope": "${input.targetRegion}",
      "minRequiredEvidence": 1,
      "searchQueries": ["query pencarian spesifik 1", "query pencarian spesifik 2"]
    }
  ],
  "internalRetrievalPlan": {
    "regulationKeywords": ["kata kunci perda/regulasi"],
    "mediaSector": "INFRASTRUKTUR" | "PERTANIAN" | "PENDIDIKAN" | "KESEHATAN" | "BANSOS_UMKM" | "LAINNYA",
    "mediaKeywords": ["kata kunci berita lokal"]
  },
  "academicQueries": ["query jurnal/penelitian komparatif"],
  "externalWebQueries": ["query portal berita nasional / data kementerian"]
}`;

    const userContent = `WILAYAH SASARAN: ${input.targetRegion}
ARAHAN / PROMPT PENGGUNA:
"""${input.userPrompt}"""
${input.attachedDocsSummary ? `\nRINGKASAN LAMPIRAN:\n${input.attachedDocsSummary}` : ''}
${input.providedUrls && input.providedUrls.length > 0 ? `\nTAUTAN DILAMPIRKAN:\n${input.providedUrls.join('\n')}` : ''}

Susun rencana riset otonom terlengkap sekarang.`;

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o',
        temperature: 0.2,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: userContent },
        ],
      });

      const rawJson = response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}';
      const parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson) as ResearchPlanOutput;

      // Bersihkan tanda titik dua pada mainTopic jika model tergelincir
      if (parsed.mainTopic) {
        parsed.mainTopic = parsed.mainTopic.replace(/:/g, ' - ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Plan: ${parsed.mainTopic} (${parsed.questions?.length || 0} questions)`,
      });

      return parsed;
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[ResearchPlannerEngine] Gagal merumuskan rencana riset: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/claim-evidence.extractor.ts (150 baris, 5.0 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { ClaimStatus, SupportType } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';


export interface VerifiedSourceInput {
  sourceId: string;
  sourceVersionId?: string;
  title: string;
  publisherName?: string;
  url: string;
  extractedText: string;
}

export interface ExtractedEvidenceSpan {
  sourceId: string;
  sourceVersionId?: string;
  verbatimQuote: string;
  contextSnippet: string;
  sectionOrPage?: string;
  confidenceScore: number;
}

export interface ExtractedClaimWithEvidence {
  questionIndex?: number;
  claimStatement: string;
  status: ClaimStatus;
  confidenceScore: number;
  evidenceItems: Array<{
    verbatimQuote: string;
    contextSnippet: string;
    sectionOrPage?: string;
    supportType: SupportType;
    relationshipExplanation: string;
    sourceId: string;
  }>;
}

export class ClaimEvidenceExtractor {
  /**
   * Mengekstrak klaim faktual terverifikasi dan cuplikan kutipan bukti (evidence spans)
   * dari sekumpulan sumber yang telah lolos asesmen kredibilitas.
   */
  public static async extractClaimsAndEvidence(
    researchTopic: string,
    questions: Array<{ index: number; question: string }>,
    sources: VerifiedSourceInput[]
  ): Promise<ExtractedClaimWithEvidence[]> {
    if (sources.length === 0) return [];

    const sourcesContext = sources.map((s, idx) => `[SUMBER #${idx + 1} | ID: ${s.sourceId}]
Judul: ${s.title} (${s.publisherName || s.url})
Teks Rujukan:
"""${s.extractedText.slice(0, 2500)}"""`).join('\n\n');

    const questionsContext = questions
      .map((q) => `Pertanyaan ${q.index}: ${q.question}`)
      .join('\n');

    const extractionPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Senior Fact Extraction Specialist".
Tugas Anda adalah memetakan fakta konkret, angka, regulasi, dan peristiwa dari SUMBER TERVERIFIKASI untuk menjawab PERTANYAAN RISET.

TOPIK: "${researchTopic}"

PERTANYAAN RISET YANG HARUS DIJAWAB:
${questionsContext}

SUMBER-SUMBER TERVERIFIKASI:
${sourcesContext}

PANDUAN EKSTRAKSI BUKTI RIGID (ZERO HALLUCINATION):
1. Setiap klaim (claimStatement) HARUS didukung oleh KUTIPAN KATA PER KATA (verbatimQuote) yang benar-benar ada di teks rujukan.
2. DILARANG membuat kutipan fiktif. Jika sebuah pertanyaan riset tidak terjawab oleh sumber yang ada, JANGAN mengarang klaim.
3. Cantumkan [ID: ...] sumber yang menjadi rujukan kutipan secara presisi.
4. supportType: 'SUPPORTS' (mendukung klaim), 'REFUTES' (membantah klaim), atau 'NEUTRAL_CONTEXT'.

Hasilkan JSON murni dengan format:
{
  "claims": [
    {
      "questionIndex": 1,
      "claimStatement": "Pernyataan fakta konkret / temuan kebijakan yang didapat dari sumber",
      "status": "VERIFIED" | "PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED",
      "confidenceScore": 0.95,
      "evidenceItems": [
        {
          "sourceId": "ID dari header SUMBER rujukan",
          "verbatimQuote": "Kutipan persis kata-per-kata dari teks sumber",
          "contextSnippet": "1-2 kalimat konteks di sekitar kutipan",
          "sectionOrPage": "Bab II / Paragraf 3 / Rilis Pers",
          "supportType": "SUPPORTS",
          "relationshipExplanation": "Mengapa kutipan ini membuktikan klaim tersebut"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}`;

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'claim-evidence-extraction',
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      metadata: { researchTopic, sourcesCount: sources.length, questionsCount: questions.length },
    });

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o',
        temperature: 0.1,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: extractionPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
      const rawClaims = Array.isArray(parsed.claims) ? parsed.claims : [];

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Extracted ${rawClaims.length} claims`,
      });

      return rawClaims.map((c: any) => ({
        questionIndex: c.questionIndex,
        claimStatement: c.claimStatement || '',
        status: (c.status as ClaimStatus) || ClaimStatus.VERIFIED,
        confidenceScore: Number(c.confidenceScore) || 0.9,
        evidenceItems: (c.evidenceItems || []).map((ev: any) => ({
          verbatimQuote: ev.verbatimQuote || '',
          contextSnippet: ev.contextSnippet || ev.verbatimQuote || '',
          sectionOrPage: ev.sectionOrPage,
          supportType: (ev.supportType as SupportType) || SupportType.SUPPORTS,
          relationshipExplanation: ev.relationshipExplanation || '',
          sourceId: ev.sourceId || sources[0].sourceId,
        })),
      }));
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      console.warn(`[ClaimEvidenceExtractor] Ekstraksi klaim gagal: ${err.message}`);
      return [];
    }

  }
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/deterministic-gates.ts (123 baris, 4.3 KB) ---
======================================================================
export interface QuestionEvidenceStatus {
  questionId: string;
  questionText: string;
  minRequiredEvidence: number;
  actualEvidenceCount: number;
}

export interface EvidenceSufficiencyResult {
  isSufficient: boolean;
  totalEvidenceCount: number;
  unresolvedQuestions: QuestionEvidenceStatus[];
  gapAnalysisReason?: string;
}

export interface QualityControlResult {
  qcPassed: boolean;
  verifiedCitationsCount: number;
  rogueCitations: string[];
  failureReason?: string;
  citationIntegrityRate: number; // 0.0 - 1.0
}

export class DeterministicQualityGates {
  /**
   * GERBANG 1: Evidence Sufficient?
   * Memvalidasi kecukupan bukti secara deterministik di backend (bukan sekadar keputusan model AI).
   * Mencegah proses lanjut ke penulisan jika pertanyaan kunci belum memiliki bukti terdaftar.
   */
  public static verifyEvidenceSufficiency(
    questions: Array<{ id: string; question: string; minRequiredEvidence: number }>,
    evidenceCountPerQuestion: Record<string, number>,
    totalSpansAvailable: number
  ): EvidenceSufficiencyResult {
    const unresolvedQuestions: QuestionEvidenceStatus[] = [];

    for (const q of questions) {
      const actualCount = evidenceCountPerQuestion[q.id] || 0;
      if (actualCount < q.minRequiredEvidence) {
        unresolvedQuestions.push({
          questionId: q.id,
          questionText: q.question,
          minRequiredEvidence: q.minRequiredEvidence,
          actualEvidenceCount: actualCount,
        });
      }
    }

    // Syarat cukup:
    // 1. Total bukti konkret >= 2
    // 2. Maksimal hanya 1 pertanyaan sekunder yang boleh kurang (toleransi parsial), atau 0 jika pertanyaan < 3
    const maxAllowedUnresolved = questions.length >= 4 ? 1 : 0;
    const isSufficient =
      totalSpansAvailable >= 2 && unresolvedQuestions.length <= maxAllowedUnresolved;

    let gapAnalysisReason: string | undefined;
    if (!isSufficient) {
      gapAnalysisReason = `Bukti belum memadai: Terdapat ${unresolvedQuestions.length} pertanyaan riset tanpa dukungan data yang cukup (Total bukti terdaftar: ${totalSpansAvailable}). Membutuhkan targeted follow-up search.`;
    }

    return {
      isSufficient,
      totalEvidenceCount: totalSpansAvailable,
      unresolvedQuestions,
      gapAnalysisReason,
    };
  }

  /**
   * GERBANG 2: QC Passed? (Citation & Evidence Grounding Verification)
   * Backend memeriksa keaslian sitasi di naskah draf:
   * 1. Mengekstrak seluruh token sitasi (e.g. [^EV-...] atau [EV-...]).
   * 2. Mencocokkan dengan set ID bukti yang sah di database.
   * 3. Menolak naskah jika terdapat 'Rogue Citation' (sitasi buatan yang tidak ada di DB).
   */
  public static verifyCitationQualityControl(
    articleMarkdown: string,
    validEvidenceIds: Set<string>,
    minExpectedCitations: number = 2
  ): QualityControlResult {
    // Regex menangkap [^EV-xxx] atau [EV-xxx] atau [Bukti EV-xxx]
    const citationRegex = /\[(?:\^|Bukti:?\s*)?(EV-[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+)\]/gi;
    const matches = Array.from(articleMarkdown.matchAll(citationRegex));

    const citedIds = Array.from(new Set(matches.map((m) => m[1])));
    const verifiedIds: string[] = [];
    const rogueCitations: string[] = [];

    for (const id of citedIds) {
      if (validEvidenceIds.has(id)) {
        verifiedIds.push(id);
      } else {
        rogueCitations.push(id);
      }
    }

    const verifiedCitationsCount = verifiedIds.length;
    const totalCitedCount = citedIds.length;
    const citationIntegrityRate =
      totalCitedCount > 0 ? verifiedCitationsCount / totalCitedCount : 0;

    // Kriteria Lulus QC:
    // 1. Tidak ada sitasi fiktif (rogueCitations == 0)
    // 2. Minimal jumlah sitasi terverifikasi terpenuhi (jika ada bukti terdaftar)
    let qcPassed = true;
    let failureReason: string | undefined;

    if (rogueCitations.length > 0) {
      qcPassed = false;
      failureReason = `QC Gagal: Ditemukan ${rogueCitations.length} token sitasi fiktif yang tidak terdaftar di basis data evidence: [${rogueCitations.join(', ')}].`;
    } else if (validEvidenceIds.size >= minExpectedCitations && verifiedCitationsCount < 1) {
      qcPassed = false;
      failureReason = `QC Gagal: Naskah tidak menyertakan sitasi eksplisit ke bukti yang telah disediakan sistem.`;
    }

    return {
      qcPassed,
      verifiedCitationsCount,
      rogueCitations,
      failureReason,
      citationIntegrityRate,
    };
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/source-credibility.assessor.ts (195 baris, 7.3 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { PublisherType, SourceLifecycle } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';


export interface SourceCandidateInput {
  url: string;
  domain: string;
  title: string;
  extractedText: string;
  publisherType?: PublisherType;
  doi?: string;
  isPeerReviewed?: boolean;
  hasRetractions?: boolean;
  publicationYear?: number;
}

export interface CredibilityAssessmentResult {
  publisherCredibilityScore: number;
  substantiveScore: number;
  methodologyScore?: number;
  recencyScore: number;
  overallScore: number;
  isAccepted: boolean;
  lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle;
  assessmentReason: string;
  rejectionReason?: string;
}

export class SourceCredibilityAssessor {
  /**
   * Menghitung skor reputasi penerbit berdasarkan domain / jenis institusi.
   * PENTING: Skor penerbit adalah SATU faktor sinyal awal, BUKAN bukti kebenaran mutlak.
   */
  public static calculatePublisherBaselineScore(domain: string, publisherType?: PublisherType): number {
    const d = domain.toLowerCase();
    if (d.endsWith('.go.id') || d.endsWith('.gov') || d.endsWith('.mil.id')) {
      return 0.90; // Domain pemerintah / regulasi resmi
    }
    if (d.endsWith('.ac.id') || d.endsWith('.edu') || publisherType === PublisherType.ACADEMIC) {
      return 0.88; // Lembaga riset & perguruan tinggi
    }
    // Media arus utama / pers kredibel
    const establishedMedia = ['kompas.com', 'tempo.co', 'antaranews.com', 'bisnis.com', 'kontan.co.id', 'detik.com'];
    if (establishedMedia.some((m) => d.includes(m))) {
      return 0.82;
    }
    if (publisherType === PublisherType.USER) {
      return 0.75; // Lampiran dokumen kerja langsung dari pengguna dewan
    }
    // Domain publik umum
    return 0.50;
  }

  /**
   * Menilai kelayakan dan kredibilitas sumber secara menyeluruh (Substansi + Metodologi + Kesegaran).
   */
  public static async assessSource(
    candidate: SourceCandidateInput,
    researchTopic: string
  ): Promise<CredibilityAssessmentResult> {
    const publisherScore = this.calculatePublisherBaselineScore(candidate.domain, candidate.publisherType);

    // Filter awal teks kosong / terlalu pendek
    if (!candidate.extractedText || candidate.extractedText.trim().length < 150) {
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: 0.1,
        recencyScore: 0.5,
        overallScore: 0.2,
        isAccepted: false,
        lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Konten teks terlalu dangkal atau tidak memuat data faktual (kurang dari 150 karakter).',
        rejectionReason: 'EMPTY_OR_SHALLOW_CONTENT',
      };
    }

    // Jika paper akademik memiliki catatan retraksi, tolak otomatis
    if (candidate.hasRetractions) {
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: 0.2,
        substantiveScore: 0.2,
        methodologyScore: 0.1,
        recencyScore: 0.5,
        overallScore: 0.15,
        isAccepted: false,
        lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Publikasi ilmiah memiliki catatan retraksi (retraction) atau koreksi fatal pada Crossref.',
        rejectionReason: 'ACADEMIC_RETRACTION_FLAG',
      };
    }

    const evaluationPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Evidence Verification Auditor".
Tugas Anda adalah menilai substansi dan kualitas fakta dari materi rujukan berikut terkait topik riset kebijakan.

TOPIK RISET: "${researchTopic}"
SUMBER DITELITI:
- URL: ${candidate.url}
- Judul: ${candidate.title}
- Penerbit: ${candidate.domain} (${candidate.publisherType || 'MEDIA'})
${candidate.doi ? `- DOI: ${candidate.doi} (Peer-reviewed: ${candidate.isPeerReviewed ? 'Ya' : 'Belum Terkonfirmasi'})` : ''}

CUPLIKAN KONTEN:
"""${candidate.extractedText.slice(0, 1500)}"""

PANDUAN PENILAIAN KRITIS:
1. JANGAN menilai sumber semata-mata dari domain whitelist. Periksa data riil, argumen, dan angka yang disajikan.
2. substantiveScore (0.0 - 1.0): Seberapa konkret data lapangan, regulasi, atau indikator yang diutarakan? (Bukan opini retoris).
3. recencyScore (0.0 - 1.0): Seberapa relevan waktu/kesegaran data ini dengan masalah yang diselidiki?
4. methodologyScore (0.0 - 1.0): Kualitas metodologi atau keabsahan sumber rujukan primer.
5. overallScore: Rata-rata tertimbang (Substansi 50%, Metodologi 25%, Kesegaran 25%).
6. isAccepted: true jika overallScore >= 0.60 dan tidak ada fabrikasi hoax yang jelas.

Keluarkan JSON murni:
{
  "substantiveScore": 0.85,
  "methodologyScore": 0.80,
  "recencyScore": 0.90,
  "overallScore": 0.85,
  "isAccepted": true,
  "assessmentReason": "Penjelasan singkat 1-2 kalimat mengapa sumber ini layak atau tidak layak dijadikan bukti riset."
}`;

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'source-credibility-assessment',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      metadata: { url: candidate.url, domain: candidate.domain, doi: candidate.doi },
    });

    try {
      const completion = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.1,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: evaluationPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
      const substantive = Number(parsed.substantiveScore) || 0.5;
      const methodology = Number(parsed.methodologyScore) || 0.5;
      const recency = Number(parsed.recencyScore) || 0.5;

      // Gabungkan dengan baseline publisher
      const weightedOverall = Math.min(
        1.0,
        Math.max(
          0.0,
          substantive * 0.45 + methodology * 0.25 + recency * 0.15 + publisherScore * 0.15
        )
      );

      const isAccepted = weightedOverall >= 0.58 && Boolean(parsed.isAccepted);

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: completion.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: completion.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Score: ${weightedOverall}, Accepted: ${isAccepted}`,
      });

      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: substantive,
        methodologyScore: methodology,
        recencyScore: recency,
        overallScore: Math.round(weightedOverall * 100) / 100,
        isAccepted,
        lifecycleStatus: isAccepted ? SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED : SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: parsed.assessmentReason || 'Evaluasi substansi selesai.',
        rejectionReason: isAccepted ? undefined : 'INSUFFICIENT_SUBSTANTIVE_OR_METHODOLOGY_SCORE',
      };
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      // Fallback deterministik berbasis panjang konten & publisher score

      const fallbackScore = Math.min(1.0, publisherScore * 0.6 + 0.2);
      const isAccepted = fallbackScore >= 0.6;
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: 0.6,
        recencyScore: 0.6,
        overallScore: fallbackScore,
        isAccepted,
        lifecycleStatus: isAccepted ? SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED : SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Penilaian fallback otomatis berdasarkan profil penerbit dan panjang teks.',
      };
    }
  }
}
