  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus THRESHOLD_RISOGRAPH:
 * Terinspirasi dari "Design poster (treshold) / Oke Gas / Street Action"
 * Ciri khas:
 * - Warna kontras ekstrem: Caution Yellow (#FACC15), Heavy Black (#09090B), Bold Red
 * - Stensil sudut tajam, badge peringatan dinamis, tipografi industrial
 * - 100% perlindungan kontras melalui kartu pelindung solid bertekstur
 */
export function buildThresholdRisographLayout(params: ThresholdRisographLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const yellow = resolvedColors?.primaryAccent || primaryAccent || '#FACC15';
  const black = resolvedColors?.isDark !== false ? '#09090B' : '#0F172A';
  const rawTitle = copy.mainBannerTitle || headline || 'AKSI KONKRET PARLEMEN';
  const displayTitle = rawTitle.toUpperCase();
  const displaySubheader = copy.mainBannerSubheader || 'GERAK CEPAT // ADVOKASI MASYARAKAT';
  const displayQuote = quote || copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || 'Tidak ada waktu untuk berdiam diri. Kebijakan harus bergerak cepat menjawab persoalan rakyat.';

  const blocks = copy.blocks || {};
  const blockA = blocks['action_1'] || { header: 'TARGET CEPAT', body: 'Penyaluran bantuan sosial dan penanganan infrastruktur mendesak.' };
  const blockB = blocks['action_2'] || { header: 'PENGAWASAN', body: 'Audit lapangan langsung untuk memastikan dana desa tepat sasaran.' };

  const titleFontSize = getAdaptiveFontSize(displayTitle, 88, 54, 16);

  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        width: `${width}px`,
        height: `${height}px`,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        position: 'relative',
        padding: '36px 42px',
        fontFamily: 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
      },
      children: [
        // ===================================================================
        // 1. TOP INDUSTRIAL BANNER & STENCIL HEADER
        // ===================================================================
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: {
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              gap: '10px',
            },
            children: [
              // Header Card
              {
                type: 'div',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'row',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    backgroundColor: black,
                    border: `2px solid ${yellow}`,
                    padding: '10px 18px',
                    boxShadow: `4px 4px 0px ${yellow}`,
                  },
                  children: [
                    {
                      type: 'div',
                      props: {
                        style: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' },
                        children: [
                          ...(logoDataUri
                            ? [
                                {
                                  type: 'img',
                                  props: {
                                    src: logoDataUri,
                                    style: { height: '28px', objectFit: 'contain' },
                                  },
                                },
                              ]
                            : []),
                          {
                            type: 'span',
                            props: {
                              style: {
                                fontFamily: 'Anton',
                                fontSize: '18px',
                                color: yellow,
                                letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                              },
                              children: 'POLARIS // RISOGRAPH SERIES',
                            },
                          },
                        ],
                      },
                    },
                    {
                      type: 'div',
                      props: {
                        style: {
                          backgroundColor: yellow,
                          color: black,
                          fontFamily: 'Anton',
                          fontSize: '12px',
                          padding: '4px 8px',
                          letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                        },
                        children: editionTag.toUpperCase(),
                      },
                    },
                  ],
                },
              },

              // Hazard Subtitle Tag
              {
                type: 'div',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    backgroundColor: yellow,
                    color: black,
                    fontFamily: 'Anton',
                    fontSize: '13px',
                    padding: '4px 12px',
                    width: 'fit-content',
                    letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                    boxShadow: `3px 3px 0px ${black}`,
                  },
                  children: displaySubheader.toUpperCase(),
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        },

        // ===================================================================
        // 2. CENTERPIECE MASSIVE STENCIL HEADLINE & IMPACT BLOCKS
        // ===================================================================
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: {
              display: 'flex',

            },
            children: [
              // Stencil Title Box

                        },
                        children: displayTitle,
                      },
                    },

              // Dual Action Cards

                  },

                          border: `2px solid ${black}`,
                          padding: '14px 16px',

                          gap: '4px',
                          boxShadow: `4px 4px 0px ${black}`,

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens, resolveColorAuthority } from '../types.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../core/parametric-renderer.js';

export interface SwissDuotoneEditorialLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus SWISS_DUOTONE_EDITORIAL:
 * Terinspirasi dari Swiss International Typographic Style (grid ketat, kontras monokrom tinggi)
 * dirombak menggunakan ParametricSatoriRenderer & EditorialSurface.
 */
export function buildSwissDuotoneEditorialLayout(
  params: SwissDuotoneEditorialLayoutParams
): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#2563EB',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#60A5FA',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE',
      mastheadStyle: 'MODERN_MINIMAL',
      headlineFontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 84,
      headlineSurface: 'NONE',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      signatureBorderSide: 'left',
    },
  });
}

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens } from '../types.js';
import { getAdaptiveFontSize, wordSafeHeadlineTruncate, smartSentenceClamp, getProtectiveCardStyle } from '../core/adaptive-typography.js';

export interface ExecutiveSeminarLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus EXECUTIVE_SEMINAR:
 * Terinspirasi dari "download (3) / Seminar Nasional & Executive Symposium"
 * Ciri khas:
 * - Warna prestisius: Midnight Navy (#0F172A) & Burnished Gold (#D97706)
 * - Struktur grid simposium formal: Header Konferensi, Agenda Strategis, Panel Ahli
 * - Kartu pelindung berkelas diplomatik dengan kontras tinggi
 */
export function buildExecutiveSeminarLayout(params: ExecutiveSeminarLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const gold = resolvedColors?.primaryAccent || primaryAccent || '#D97706';
  const navy = resolvedColors?.isDark !== false ? '#0F172A' : '#1E293B';
  const rawTitle = copy.mainBannerTitle || headline || 'SIMPOSIUM KEBIJAKAN NASIONAL';
  const displayTitle = rawTitle.toUpperCase();
  const displaySubheader = copy.mainBannerSubheader || 'FORUM DIALOG STRATEGIS & KONSULTASI PUBLIK 2026';
  const displayQuote = quote || copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || 'Pertukaran gagasan ilmiah dan serapan aspirasi rakyat adalah fondasi kokoh lahirnya legislasi yang adil.';

  const blocks = copy.blocks || {};
  const topic1 = blocks['topic_1'] || { header: 'TRANSFORMASI EKONOMI', body: 'Strategi ketahanan fiskal daerah dan penguatan industri rakyat.' };
  const topic2 = blocks['topic_2'] || { header: 'TATA KELOLA PUBLIK', body: 'Digitalisasi layanan publik dan transparansi penganggaran daerah.' };

  const titleFontSize = getAdaptiveFontSize(displayTitle, 80, 48, 18);

  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        width: `${width}px`,
        height: `${height}px`,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens, resolveColorAuthority } from '../types.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../core/parametric-renderer.js';

export interface TornVisionLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus TORN_VISION:
 * Terinspirasi dari "Hold Your Vision & Diagonal Paper Contrast"
 * Ciri khas: Tipografi inspiratif berbobot tebal dan kontras adaptif
 * dirombak menggunakan ParametricSatoriRenderer & EditorialSurface.
 */
export function buildTornVisionLayout(params: TornVisionLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#3B82F6',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#60A5FA',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'BOLD_MANIFESTO',
      mastheadStyle: 'VINTAGE_RIBBON',
      headlineFontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 86,
      headlineSurface: 'SCRIM_GRADIENT',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      signatureBorderSide: 'left',
    },
  });
}

  const displayTitle = rawTitle.toUpperCase();

  const displayQuote = quote || copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || 'Setiap potret perjuangan warga adalah pengingat bahwa kekuasaan parlemen ada semata-mata untuk melayani.';

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens, resolveColorAuthority } from '../types.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../core/parametric-renderer.js';

export interface PopMagazineCollageLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus POP_MAGAZINE_COLLAGE:
 * Terinspirasi dari majalah jalanan urban kontemporer (Neo-Tokyo / Street Zine)
 * dirombak menggunakan ParametricSatoriRenderer & EditorialSurface.
 */
export function buildPopMagazineCollageLayout(params: PopMagazineCollageLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#F43F5E',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#14B8A6',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'DATA_INFOGRAPHIC',
      mastheadStyle: 'BOLD_STAMP',
      headlineFontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 88,
      headlineSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      signatureBorderSide: 'left',
    },
  });
}

 * dirombak menggunakan ParametricSatoriRenderer.
 */
export function buildPolaroidAdventureLayout(params: PolaroidAdventureLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#4D7C0F',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#84CC16',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL',
      mastheadStyle: 'BOLD_STAMP',
      headlineFontFamily: 'Anton',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 88,
      headlineSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureBorderSide: 'left',
      defaultPhotoStyle: 'POLAROID',
    },
  });
}

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens, resolveColorAuthority } from '../types.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../core/parametric-renderer.js';

export interface BroadsheetNewspaperLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

/**
 * Layout Khusus BROADSHEET_NEWSPAPER:
 * Terinspirasi dari "Epoch Times & Broadsheet Investigative Newspaper"
 * Ciri khas: Format koran harian investigasi berwibawa tinggi dengan tipografi Serif
 * dirombak menggunakan ParametricSatoriRenderer & EditorialSurface.
 */
export function buildBroadsheetNewspaperLayout(params: BroadsheetNewspaperLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#1E3A8A',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#3B82F6',
        backgroundBase: '#F4EFE6',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE',
      mastheadStyle: 'BROADSHEET_CLASSIC',
      headlineFontFamily: 'DejaVu Serif',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 82,
      headlineSurface: 'NONE',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      signatureBorderSide: 'top',
    },
  });
}

              alignItems: 'center',
              boxShadow: `4px 4px 0px ${black}`,
            },
            children: [
              {
                type: 'div',
                props: {
                  style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '2px' },
                  children: [
                    {
                      type: 'span',
                      props: {
                        style: {
                          fontFamily: 'Anton',
                          fontSize: '20px',
                          color: black,
                          letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                        },
                        children: memberName.toUpperCase(),
                      },
                    },
                    {
                      type: 'span',
                      props: {
                        style: {
                          fontSize: '11px',
                          color: '#451A03',
                          fontWeight: 700,
                        },
                        children: memberTitle.toUpperCase(),
                      },
                    },
                  ],
                },
              },
              {
                type: 'span',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    fontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
                    fontSize: '16px',
                    color: black,
                    letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                  },
                  children: 'VIRAL DIALOG // SUARA LANTANG RAKYAT',
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

import { SynthesizedEditorialCopy, CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens, resolveColorAuthority } from '../types.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../core/parametric-renderer.js';

export interface StandardEditorialLayoutParams {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  headline: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle: string;
  editionTag: string;
  primaryAccent: string;
  secondaryAccent?: string;
  archetype?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
}

export function buildStandardEditorialLayout(params: StandardEditorialLayoutParams): any {
  const {
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    primaryAccent,
    secondaryAccent,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors,
  } = params;

  const colors =
    resolvedColors ||
    resolveColorAuthority({
      tenantBrandHex: primaryAccent || copy.primaryAccent,
      colorMoodAccent: copy.colorMood,
      blueprintPalette: {
        primaryAccent: primaryAccent || '#EA580C',
        secondaryAccent: secondaryAccent || copy.secondaryAccent || '#38BDF8',
      },
    });

  return ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
    width,
    height,
    headline,
    quote,
    copy,
    memberName,
    memberTitle,
    editionTag,
    logoDataUri,
    compositionPlan,
    resolvedColors: colors,
    skinConfig: {
      structuralFamily: 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE',
      mastheadStyle: 'MODERN_MINIMAL',
      headlineFontFamily: 'Bebas Neue',
      headlineMaxFontSize: 92,
      headlineSurface: 'SCRIM_GRADIENT',
      columnsSurface: 'HAIRLINE',
      signatureSurface: 'SOLID_BAND',
      signatureBorderSide: 'left',
    },
  });
}

import sharp from 'sharp';
import satori from 'satori';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import { MultiRegionCropper } from '../multi-region-cropper.js';

  ComposeCreativePosterOptions,
  ComposedPosterResult,

  CompositionPlan,

  resolveColorAuthority,
  PosterQAReport,
} from './types.js';

export class CreativePosterCompositor {
  /**
   * Helper publik untuk mengekspor SVG Sobekan Kertas (Backward Compatibility)
   */
  public static buildTornPaperWingsSvg(canvasWidth: number, canvasHeight: number): Buffer {
    return buildTornPaperWingsSvg(canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
  }

  /**
   * composePoster: Merakit Kolase AI + Subjek Harmonized + Tipografi Vektor Satori
   */
  public static async composePoster(
    options: ComposeCreativePosterOptions
  ): Promise<ComposedPosterResult> {
    const {
      backgroundBuffer,
      subjectPhotoBuffer,
      officialLogoBuffer,
      headline,
      quote,
      memberName,
      memberTitle = 'ANGGOTA PARLEMEN',
      archetype = 'STREET_JOURNALISM',
      compositionMode = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY',
      synthesizedCopy,
      primaryAccentColor = '#9E0B0F',
      canvasWidth = 1080,
      canvasHeight = 1350,
      editionTag = 'POLARIS ADVOKASI',
      outputFormat = 'webp',
    } = options;

    // Otoritas Tunggal Warna
    const effectiveColors: ResolvedColorTokens =
      options.resolvedColors ||
      resolveColorAuthority({
        tenantBrandHex: options.primaryAccentColor || synthesizedCopy?.primaryAccent,
        colorMoodAccent: synthesizedCopy?.colorMood,
        blueprintPalette: {
          primaryAccent: options.primaryAccentColor || synthesizedCopy?.primaryAccent,
          secondaryAccent: options.secondaryAccentColor || synthesizedCopy?.secondaryAccent,
        },
      });

    // Jalankan analisis kontras Sharp jika backgroundBuffer dan zones tersedia
    let zoneSamples: any = undefined;
    if (options.compositionPlan?.zones && options.compositionPlan.zones.length > 0) {
      try {
        const sampled = await ZoneSampler.sampleZones(
          backgroundBuffer,
          options.compositionPlan.zones
        );
        zoneSamples = sampled.get('zone_headline') || sampled.get('headline') || Array.from(sampled.values())[0];
        if (zoneSamples) {
          effectiveColors.isDark = zoneSamples.isDark;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        // Abaikan dan lanjutkan dengan fallback warna aman
      }
    }

    // 1. Normalisasi Kanvas Dasar AI (Crop-aware gravity adaptif)
    const headerPlacement = options.compositionPlan?.headerPlacement || synthesizedCopy?.headerPlacement || 'top';
    const positionGravity = headerPlacement === 'bottom' ? 'north' : headerPlacement === 'top' ? 'south' : 'center';

    const baseCanvas = sharp(backgroundBuffer).resize({
      width: canvasWidth,
      height: canvasHeight,
      fit: 'cover',
      position: positionGravity,
    });

    const compositeLayers: sharp.OverlayOptions[] = [];

    // 2. Siapkan Logo Partai/Lembaga (Layer Terkunci)
    let logoDataUri: string | undefined;
    if (officialLogoBuffer && officialLogoBuffer.length > 0) {
      logoDataUri = `data:image/png;base64,${officialLogoBuffer.toString('base64')}`;
    }

    let qrCodeDataUri: string | undefined;
    if (options.qrCodeUrl) {
      try {
        qrCodeDataUri = await QRCode.toDataURL(options.qrCodeUrl, {
          width: 140,
          margin: 1,
          color: { dark: '#111827', light: '#FAF8F5' },
        });
      } catch {
        // Fallback gracefully
      }
    }

    // 3. Render Layer Tipografi Vektor via Satori (Polymorphic Layout Builder)
    const fonts = await FontLoader.getFonts();
    const copyData: SynthesizedEditorialCopy = synthesizedCopy || {
      mainBannerTitle: headline.slice(0, 22),
      mainBannerSubheader: `PENGEMBANGAN KEBIJAKAN PUBLIK • Get to Know More`,
      mainBannerDefinitionBody: quote || headline,
      blocks: {},
      mappedVisualCutouts: {},
    };

    // 3.5. Jika TORN_PAPER_NEWS, tambahkan Layer Mask Sobekan Kertas (Torn Paper Wings)
    if (archetype === 'TORN_PAPER_NEWS') {
      const tornPaperMask = buildTornPaperWingsSvg(canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
      compositeLayers.push({
        input: tornPaperMask,
        left: 0,
        top: 0,
      });
    }

    // Percabangan Polimorfik: Dispatch ke Layout Builder yang sesuai dengan arketipe
    const effectiveAccent =
      copyData.primaryAccent || primaryAccentColor || '#EA580C';
    const effectiveSecondary =
      copyData.secondaryAccent || options.secondaryAccentColor || '#FB923C';

    let vnode: any;

    if (archetype === 'STREET_JOURNALISM') {
      vnode = buildStreetJournalismLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'TORN_PAPER_NEWS') {
      const croppedThumbnails = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        backgroundBuffer,
        [
          { id: 'thumb1', leftRatio: 0.05, topRatio: 0.25, widthRatio: 0.35, heightRatio: 0.20, targetWidth: 160, targetHeight: 100 },
          { id: 'thumb2', leftRatio: 0.05, topRatio: 0.70, widthRatio: 0.35, heightRatio: 0.20, targetWidth: 160, targetHeight: 100 },
        ],
        options.galleryPhotos
      );

      let qrCodeDataUri: string | undefined;
      try {
        qrCodeDataUri = await QRCode.toDataURL(
          'https://polaris.gov.id/advokasi/tanggap-darurat',
          { width: 140, margin: 1, color: { dark: '#111827', light: '#FAF8F5' } }
        );
      } catch {
        // Fallback gracefully
      }

      vnode = buildTornPaperVeritasLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        croppedThumbnails,
        qrCodeDataUri,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION') {
      const croppedThumbnails = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        backgroundBuffer,
        [
          { id: 'insetCircle', leftRatio: 0.30, topRatio: 0.20, widthRatio: 0.40, heightRatio: 0.40, targetWidth: 260, targetHeight: 260 },
        ],
        options.galleryPhotos
      );

      vnode = buildRetroMitigationLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        croppedThumbnails,
        logoDataUri,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'MODULAR_GRID_CARDS') {
      const croppedThumbnails = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        backgroundBuffer,
        [
          { id: 'card1', leftRatio: 0.05, topRatio: 0.32, widthRatio: 0.45, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 280, targetHeight: 180, grayscale: true },
          { id: 'card2', leftRatio: 0.50, topRatio: 0.32, widthRatio: 0.45, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 280, targetHeight: 180, grayscale: true },
          { id: 'card3', leftRatio: 0.05, topRatio: 0.52, widthRatio: 0.45, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 280, targetHeight: 180, grayscale: true },
          { id: 'card4', leftRatio: 0.50, topRatio: 0.52, widthRatio: 0.45, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 280, targetHeight: 180, grayscale: true },
          { id: 'card5', leftRatio: 0.25, topRatio: 0.42, widthRatio: 0.50, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 280, targetHeight: 180, grayscale: true },
        ],
        options.galleryPhotos
      );

      vnode = buildModularCardsLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        croppedThumbnails,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE') {
      const croppedThumbnails = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        backgroundBuffer,
        [
          { id: 'subPhoto', leftRatio: 0.05, topRatio: 0.15, widthRatio: 0.45, heightRatio: 0.35, targetWidth: 320, targetHeight: 200 },
        ],
        options.galleryPhotos
      );

      vnode = buildVintageGazetteLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        croppedThumbnails,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'POP_OUT_HERO') {
      vnode = buildPopOutHeroLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'STATEMENT_PROFILE') {
      vnode = buildStatementProfileLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'RETRO_CAMPAIGN') {
      vnode = buildRetroCampaignLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,

      });
    } else if (archetype === 'CONSTRUCTIVIST_BAUHAUS') {
      vnode = buildConstructivistBauhausLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,

    } else if (archetype === 'THRESHOLD_RISOGRAPH') {

        editionTag,

        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'EXECUTIVE_SEMINAR') {
      vnode = buildExecutiveSeminarLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'TORN_VISION') {
      vnode = buildTornVisionLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,

        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'GALLERY_EXHIBITION') {
      const croppedThumbnails = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        backgroundBuffer,
        [
          { id: 'exhibit1', leftRatio: 0.10, topRatio: 0.20, widthRatio: 0.40, heightRatio: 0.25, targetWidth: 240, targetHeight: 160 },
          { id: 'exhibit2', leftRatio: 0.52, topRatio: 0.20, widthRatio: 0.40, heightRatio: 0.25, targetWidth: 240, targetHeight: 160 },
        ],
        options.galleryPhotos
      );
      vnode = buildGalleryExhibitionLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,

        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,

        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        croppedThumbnails,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'POP_MAGAZINE_COLLAGE') {
      vnode = buildPopMagazineCollageLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,

        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,

        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'POLAROID_ADVENTURE') {
      vnode = buildPolaroidAdventureLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'BROADSHEET_NEWSPAPER') {
      vnode = buildBroadsheetNewspaperLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else if (archetype === 'TECHNICAL_GRID_CALLOUT') {
      vnode = buildTechnicalGridCalloutLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    } else {
      vnode = buildStandardEditorialLayout({
        width: canvasWidth,
        height: canvasHeight,
        headline,
        quote,
        copy: copyData,
        memberName,
        memberTitle,
        editionTag,
        primaryAccent: effectiveAccent,
        secondaryAccent: effectiveSecondary,
        archetype,
        logoDataUri,
        compositionPlan: options.compositionPlan,
        resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
      });
    }

    const svgString = await satori(vnode, {
      width: canvasWidth,
      height: canvasHeight,
      fonts,
    });

    // 4. Jika Mode FIGURE_CENTERPIECE dan foto PNG Anggota Dewan tersedia:
    // Terapkan Aturan Desain Profesional: Bottom-Anchored & Rule of Thirds (Anti-Melayang di Tengah!)
    if (
      compositionMode === 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE' &&
      subjectPhotoBuffer &&
      subjectPhotoBuffer.length > 0
    ) {
      let targetW: number;
      let targetH: number;
      let left: number;
      let top: number;

      if (archetype === 'STREET_JOURNALISM') {
        // Tokoh diletakkan di sepertiga kanan (x: 54%), berjangkar kuat ke batas bawah
        targetW = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.44);
        targetH = Math.round(canvasHeight * 0.62);
        left = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.54);
        top = canvasHeight - targetH;
      } else if (archetype === 'STATEMENT_PROFILE') {
        // Tokoh berdiri berwibawa di sisi kanan (x: 50%), berjangkar ke batas bawah
        targetW = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.50);
        targetH = Math.round(canvasHeight * 0.70);
        left = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.50);
        top = canvasHeight - targetH;
      } else if (archetype === 'POP_OUT_HERO') {
        // Tokoh dramatis menembus frame tengah-kanan
        targetW = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.52);
        targetH = Math.round(canvasHeight * 0.74);
        left = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.46);
        top = canvasHeight - targetH;
      } else {
        // Arketipe lainnya: Sepertiga kanan berjangkar ke bawah
        targetW = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.46);
        targetH = Math.round(canvasHeight * 0.66);
        left = Math.round(canvasWidth * 0.52);
        top = canvasHeight - targetH;
      }

      const harmonizedSubject = await SubjectHarmonizer.harmonizeSubjectPhoto(
        subjectPhotoBuffer,
        archetype,
        targetW,
        targetH
      );

      compositeLayers.push({
        input: harmonizedSubject,
        left,
        top,
      });
    }


    // 5. Masukkan Layer Tipografi Vektor Satori (Z-Index paling depan / on top)
    compositeLayers.push({
      input: Buffer.from(svgString),
      top: 0,
      left: 0,
    });

    // 6. Eksekusi Komposisi Awal
    const finalPipeline = baseCanvas.composite(compositeLayers);

    let outputBuffer =
      outputFormat === 'png'
        ? await finalPipeline.png().toBuffer()
        : await finalPipeline.webp({ quality: 92, effort: 4 }).toBuffer();

    // 7. Visual Quality Evaluation (Fase 3 QA Gate)
    let qaReport: PosterQAReport | undefined = undefined;

    if (options.enableVisualQA !== false) {
      try {
        qaReport = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
          compositeBuffer: outputBuffer,
          plan: options.compositionPlan,
          resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
          surfaceMode: zoneSamples?.surfaceMode,
          canvasWidth,
          canvasHeight,
        });

        // 8. Self-Healing Revision Loop (Zero-Waste Satori Re-render)
        if (
          options.autoHealContrast &&
          !qaReport.passed &&
          qaReport.recommendation.action === 'ADJUST_SURFACE'
        ) {
          const decision = RevisionPolicy.evaluateDecision({
            report: qaReport,
            currentPlan: options.compositionPlan,
            currentSurfaceMode: zoneSamples?.surfaceMode,
          });

          if (decision.healed && decision.newSurfaceMode) {
            const healedPlan = options.compositionPlan
              ? RevisionPolicy.applyHealingToPlan(options.compositionPlan, decision)
              : undefined;

            const healedVNode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
              width: canvasWidth,
              height: canvasHeight,
              headline,
              quote,
              copy: copyData,
              memberName,
              memberTitle,
              editionTag,
              logoDataUri,
              qrCodeDataUri,
              compositionPlan: healedPlan,
              resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
              skinConfig: {
                headlineSurface: decision.newSurfaceMode,
                columnsSurface: decision.newSurfaceMode,
              },
            });

            const healedSvgString = await satori(healedVNode, {
              width: canvasWidth,
              height: canvasHeight,
              fonts,
            });

            const healedLayers = [...compositeLayers];
            healedLayers[healedLayers.length - 1] = {
              input: Buffer.from(healedSvgString),
              top: 0,
              left: 0,
            };

            const healedPipeline = sharp(backgroundBuffer)
              .resize(canvasWidth, canvasHeight, { fit: 'cover', position: 'center' })
              .composite(healedLayers);

            const healedBuffer =
              outputFormat === 'png'
                ? await healedPipeline.png().toBuffer()
                : await healedPipeline.webp({ quality: 92, effort: 4 }).toBuffer();

            const reevaluatedQA = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
              compositeBuffer: healedBuffer,
              plan: healedPlan,
              resolvedColors: effectiveColors,
              surfaceMode: decision.newSurfaceMode,
              canvasWidth,
              canvasHeight,
            });

            outputBuffer = healedBuffer;
            qaReport = {
              ...reevaluatedQA,
              recommendation: {
                ...reevaluatedQA.recommendation,
                reason: `Self-healed via Satori: ${decision.reason}`,
              },
            };
          }
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn(`[CreativePosterCompositor] QA Gate evaluation failed: ${err?.message}`);
      }
    }

    return {
      buffer: outputBuffer,
      mimeType: outputFormat === 'png' ? 'image/png' : 'image/webp',
      sizeBytes: outputBuffer.length,
      width: canvasWidth,
      height: canvasHeight,
      qaReport,
    };
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/core/parametric-renderer.ts (771 baris, 26.1 KB) ---

import {
  CompositionPlan,
  ResolvedColorTokens,
  SynthesizedEditorialCopy,
  SurfaceMode,
} from '../types.js';
import { VariantResolver } from './variant-resolver.js';
import {
  buildMastheadPrimitive,
  MastheadStyle,
  buildHeadlinePrimitive,
  buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive,
  buildStatBadgesPrimitive,
  buildPhotoFramePrimitive,
  PhotoFrameStyle,
  buildMemberSignaturePrimitive,
} from '../primitives/index.js';
import {
  wordSafeHeadlineTruncate,
  smartSentenceClamp,
  safeEditorialText,
} from './adaptive-typography.js';

export type StructuralFamily =
  | 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL'
  | 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE'
  | 'BOLD_MANIFESTO'
  | 'DATA_INFOGRAPHIC';

export interface ArchetypeSkinConfig {
  structuralFamily?: StructuralFamily;
  mastheadStyle?: MastheadStyle;
  headlineFontFamily?: 'Bebas Neue' | 'Anton' | 'DejaVu Serif' | 'Plus Jakarta Sans';
  headlineSurface?: SurfaceMode;
  headlineAlign?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right';
  headlineMaxFontSize?: number;
  columnsSurface?: SurfaceMode;
  signatureSurface?: SurfaceMode;
  signatureBorderSide?: 'left' | 'top' | 'none';
  defaultPhotoStyle?: PhotoFrameStyle;
}

export interface ParametricPosterOptions {
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  headline?: string;
  quote?: string;
  copy?: Partial<SynthesizedEditorialCopy>;
  memberName?: string;
  memberTitle?: string;
  editionTag?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  qrCodeDataUri?: string;
  subPhotos?: Array<{ dataUri: string; caption?: string; style?: PhotoFrameStyle }>;
  compositionPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  plan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  skinConfig?: ArchetypeSkinConfig;
}

/**
 * ParametricSatoriRenderer: Mesin perakit VNode Satori parametrik yang menyusun
 * 4 Kerangka Struktur (Structural Families) berbeda secara visual:
 * 1. CINEMATIC_MINIMAL: Poster film / alam / storybook, 0 stat circles, 65-70% kanvas bersih terbuka.
 * 2. EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE: Majalah Swiss / broadsheet, 0 stat circles, 2 kolom horizontal berdampingan.
 * 3. BOLD_MANIFESTO: Gerakan publik / Bauhaus / kinetic, 0 stat circles, 2 kartu aksi kontras tinggi.
 * 4. DATA_INFOGRAPHIC: Khusus indikator / data, HANYA di sini stat circles / badges dirender.
 */
export class ParametricSatoriRenderer {
  public static renderParametricPoster(options: ParametricPosterOptions): any {
    const compositionPlan = options.compositionPlan || options.plan;
    const resolvedColors = options.resolvedColors || options.colors || {
      primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
      secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
      backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
      textColor: '#FFFFFF',
      subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
      cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
      cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
      source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
      isDark: true,
    };
    const rawCopy: any = options.copy || {};
    const headline = options.headline || rawCopy.mainBannerTitle || rawCopy.headline || 'POLARIS';
    const quote = options.quote || rawCopy.mainBannerSubheader || rawCopy.quote || '';
    const copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy = {
      mainBannerTitle: headline,
      mainBannerSubheader: quote,
      mainBannerDefinitionBody: rawCopy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || '',
      blocks: rawCopy.blocks || {},
      mappedVisualCutouts: rawCopy.mappedVisualCutouts || {},
      statCircles: rawCopy.statCircles || [],
      numberedItems: rawCopy.numberedItems || [],
      layoutVariant: rawCopy.layoutVariant,
      headerPlacement: rawCopy.headerPlacement,
      cardArrangement: rawCopy.cardArrangement,
      ...rawCopy,
    };

    const {
      width = 1080,
      height = 1350,
      memberName = 'POLARIS MEMBER',
      memberTitle = 'ANGGOTA DEWAN',
      editionTag = 'EDISI RESMI',
      logoDataUri,
      qrCodeDataUri,
      subPhotos = [],
      skinConfig = {},
    } = options;

    const structuralFamily: StructuralFamily = skinConfig.structuralFamily || 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE';
    const layoutVariant = compositionPlan?.layoutVariant || copy.layoutVariant || 'TOP_BANNER';
    const headerPlacement = compositionPlan?.headerPlacement || copy.headerPlacement || 'top';
    const cardArrangement = compositionPlan?.cardArrangement || copy.cardArrangement || 'horizontal_split';

    const isDark = resolvedColors.isDark !== false;

    // 1. Primitive: Masthead
    const mastheadNode = buildMastheadPrimitive({
      style: skinConfig.mastheadStyle || (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? 'MODERN_MINIMAL' : 'BROADSHEET_CLASSIC'),
      editionTag,
      logoDataUri,
      colors: resolvedColors,
      isDark,
    });

    // 2. Primitive: Headline
    const headlineZoneSurface = compositionPlan?.zones?.find(
      (z) => z.role === 'HEADLINE'
    )?.surface;

    const displayTitle = wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(copy.mainBannerTitle || headline || 'POLARIS', 34);

    const headlineNode = buildHeadlinePrimitive({
      title: displayTitle,
      subheader: copy.mainBannerSubheader,
      definitionBody: structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? undefined : (quote || copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody),
      fontFamily: skinConfig.headlineFontFamily || (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? 'DejaVu Serif' : 'Bebas Neue'),
      maxFontSize: skinConfig.headlineMaxFontSize || (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? 96 : 88),
      surfaceMode: headlineZoneSurface || skinConfig.headlineSurface || (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? 'NONE' : 'HAIRLINE'),
      align: skinConfig.headlineAlign || 'left',
      colors: resolvedColors,
      isDark,
    });

    // 3. Primitive: Sub Photos (jika ada thumbnail)
    const photoNodes = subPhotos.map((p) =>
      buildPhotoFramePrimitive({
        photoDataUri: p.dataUri,
        caption: p.caption,
        style: p.style || skinConfig.defaultPhotoStyle || 'CLEAN_FRAME',
        colors: resolvedColors,
        isDark,
      })
    );

    // 4. Primitive: Member Signature
    const sigZoneSurface = compositionPlan?.zones?.find(
      (z) => z.role === 'MEMBER_SIGNATURE'
    )?.surface;

    const signatureNode = buildMemberSignaturePrimitive({
      memberName,
      memberTitle,
      qrCodeDataUri,
      surfaceMode: sigZoneSurface || skinConfig.signatureSurface || (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL' ? 'HAIRLINE' : 'SOLID_BAND'),
      borderAccentSide: skinConfig.signatureBorderSide || 'left',
      colors: resolvedColors,
      isDark,
    });

    // Assembly VNode pohon layout berdasarkan Structural Family & Layout Variant
    const rootStyles = VariantResolver.resolveRootContainer(
      layoutVariant,
      headerPlacement,
      '40px 48px'
    );

    let mainChildren: any[] = [];

    // =======================================================================
    // FAMILY 1: CINEMATIC_MINIMAL (Fine Art, Storybook, Polaroid, Film Poster)
    // 0 Stat Circles, 0 Large Columns Box. 65-70% kanvas terbuka bersih untuk karya AI.
    // =======================================================================
    if (structuralFamily === 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL') {
      const cleanQuote = smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(quote || copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || ''), 130);
      const pullQuoteNode = cleanQuote
        ? {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'row',
                alignItems: 'center',
                borderLeft: `3.5px solid ${resolvedColors.primaryAccent}`,
                paddingLeft: '16px',
                marginBottom: '16px',
                maxWidth: '850px',
              },
              children: [
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: skinConfig.headlineFontFamily === 'DejaVu Serif' ? 'DejaVu Serif' : 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
                      fontSize: '15px',
                      fontStyle: 'italic',
                      color: isDark ? '#F1F5F9' : '#0F172A',
                      lineHeight: 1.45,
                    },
                    children: `"${cleanQuote}"`,
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          }
        : null;

      const middleOpenSpace = {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flex: 1,
            width: '100%',
            minHeight: '440px',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
          },
          children: photoNodes.length > 0 ? [photoNodes[0]] : [],
        },
      };

      if (layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' || headerPlacement === 'bottom') {
        mainChildren = [
          mastheadNode,
          middleOpenSpace,
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
              children: [pullQuoteNode, headlineNode, signatureNode].filter(Boolean),
            },
          },
        ];
      } else {
        mainChildren = [
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
              children: [mastheadNode, headlineNode],
            },
          },
          middleOpenSpace,
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px', width: '100%' },
              children: [pullQuoteNode, signatureNode].filter(Boolean),
            },
          },
        ];
      }
    }

    // =======================================================================
    // FAMILY 2: EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE (Swiss Duotone, Broadsheet Newspaper, Gazette)
    // 0 Stat Circles. 2 Kolom Berdampingan Horizontal (50% kiri / 50% kanan) dipisah hairline rule.
    // =======================================================================
    else if (structuralFamily === 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE') {
      const blockEntries = Object.values(copy.blocks || {});
      const leftBlock = blockEntries[0] || {
        header: 'FOKUS KEBIJAKAN',
        body: copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || 'Evaluasi tata kelola anggaran dan keselarasan regulasi demi transparansi publik.',
      };
      const rightBlock = blockEntries[1] || {
        header: 'AKSI PARLEMEN',
        body: 'Pengawasan berkala dan pendampingan advokasi berkelanjutan bersama konstituen.',
      };

      const magazineColumnsNode = {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'row',
            width: '100%',
            backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.90)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94)',
            border: `1.5px solid ${isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.12)'}`,
            borderRadius: '8px',
            padding: '18px 22px',
            boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
            boxSizing: 'border-box',
            marginBottom: '16px',
          },
          children: [
            // Kolom Kiri (50%)
            {
              type: 'div',
              props: {
                style: {
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  flex: 1,
                  paddingRight: '18px',
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)' : 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12)'}`,
                  gap: '6px',
                },
                children: [
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontFamily: skinConfig.headlineFontFamily === 'Bebas Neue' ? 'Bebas Neue' : 'Anton',
                        fontSize: '17px',
                        color: resolvedColors.primaryAccent,
                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                      },
                      children: safeEditorialText(leftBlock.header || 'FOKUS KEBIJAKAN').toUpperCase().slice(0, 36),
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        lineHeight: 1.45,
                        color: isDark ? '#E2E8F0' : '#334155',
                      },
                      children: smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(leftBlock.body || ''), 135),
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            },
            // Kolom Kanan (50%)
            {
              type: 'div',
              props: {
                style: {
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  flex: 1,
                  paddingLeft: '18px',
                  gap: '6px',
                },
                children: [
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontFamily: skinConfig.headlineFontFamily === 'Bebas Neue' ? 'Bebas Neue' : 'Anton',
                        fontSize: '17px',
                        color: resolvedColors.primaryAccent,
                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                      },
                      children: safeEditorialText(rightBlock.header || 'AKSI PARLEMEN').toUpperCase().slice(0, 36),
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        lineHeight: 1.45,
                        color: isDark ? '#E2E8F0' : '#334155',
                      },
                      children: smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(rightBlock.body || ''), 135),
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      };

      const middleOpenSpace = {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flex: 1,
            width: '100%',
            minHeight: '260px',
          },
        },
      };

      mainChildren = [
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
            children: [mastheadNode, headlineNode],
          },
        },
        middleOpenSpace,
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
            children: [magazineColumnsNode, signatureNode],
          },
        },
      ];
    }

    // =======================================================================
    // FAMILY 3: BOLD_MANIFESTO (Bauhaus, Kinetic Motion, Torn Vision, Collage)
    // 0 Stat Circles. Pita judul tegas + 2 Kartu Aksi Kompak Berdampingan.
    // =======================================================================
    else if (structuralFamily === 'BOLD_MANIFESTO') {
      const blockEntries = Object.values(copy.blocks || {});
      const cardA = blockEntries[0] || {
        header: 'RESPONS CEPAT',
        body: copy.mainBannerDefinitionBody || 'Respons taktis langsung di lapangan menyerap aspirasi kritis.',
      };
      const cardB = blockEntries[1] || {
        header: 'KOMITMEN TEGAS',
        body: 'Kawal transparansi kebijakan daerah demi kesejahteraan warga.',
      };

      const manifestoCardsNode = {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'row',
            gap: '16px',
            width: '100%',
            marginBottom: '16px',
          },
          children: [
            {
              type: 'div',
              props: {
                style: {
                  flex: 1,
                  backgroundColor: isDark ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF',
                  borderLeft: `4px solid ${resolvedColors.primaryAccent}`,
                  borderTop: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderBottom: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderRadius: '6px',
                  padding: '14px 18px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '6px',
                  boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12)',
                },
                children: [
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontFamily: 'Anton',
                        fontSize: '16px',
                        color: resolvedColors.primaryAccent,
                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                      },
                      children: safeEditorialText(cardA.header).toUpperCase().slice(0, 32),
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        lineHeight: 1.4,
                        color: isDark ? '#CBD5E1' : '#475569',
                      },
                      children: smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(cardA.body), 110),
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            },
            {
              type: 'div',
              props: {
                style: {
                  flex: 1,
                  backgroundColor: isDark ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF',
                  borderLeft: `4px solid ${resolvedColors.secondaryAccent || resolvedColors.primaryAccent}`,
                  borderTop: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderRight: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderBottom: `1px solid ${isDark ? '#334155' : '#E2E8F0'}`,
                  borderRadius: '6px',
                  padding: '14px 18px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '6px',
                  boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12)',
                },
                children: [
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontFamily: 'Anton',
                        fontSize: '16px',
                        color: resolvedColors.secondaryAccent || resolvedColors.primaryAccent,
                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                      },
                      children: safeEditorialText(cardB.header).toUpperCase().slice(0, 32),
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        lineHeight: 1.4,
                        color: isDark ? '#CBD5E1' : '#475569',
                      },
                      children: smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(cardB.body), 110),
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      };

      const middleOpenSpace = {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flex: 1,
            width: '100%',
            minHeight: '260px',
          },
        },
      };

      mainChildren = [
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
            children: [mastheadNode, headlineNode],
          },
        },
        middleOpenSpace,
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', width: '100%' },
            children: [manifestoCardsNode, signatureNode],
          },
        },
      ];
    }

    // =======================================================================
    // FAMILY 4: DATA_INFOGRAPHIC (Pop Magazine, Indikator & Capaian Kinerja)
    // HANYA pada mode inilah Stat Badges Primitive (3 Lingkaran Data) dirender.
    // =======================================================================
    else {
      const statCircles = copy.statCircles && copy.statCircles.length > 0
        ? copy.statCircles
        : [
            { topLabel: 'CAPAIAN', value: '100%', description: 'Realisasi program tepat sasaran.' },
            { topLabel: 'PENERIMA', value: '2.500+', description: 'Warga terdata mendapat manfaat.' },
            { topLabel: 'EVALUASI', value: 'TERUJI', description: 'Transparansi anggaran berkala.' },
          ];

      // 4. Primitive: Stat Badges
      const statsNode = buildStatBadgesPrimitive({
        statCircles,
        numberedItems: copy.numberedItems,
        colors: resolvedColors,
        isDark,
      });

      // 5. Primitive: Editorial Columns
      const bodyZoneSurface = compositionPlan?.zones?.find(
        (z) => z.role === 'EDITORIAL_BODY' || z.role === 'COLUMNS' || z.role === 'CARDS'
      )?.surface;

      const columnsNode = buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive({
        blocks: copy.blocks || {},
        arrangement: cardArrangement,
        surfaceMode: bodyZoneSurface || skinConfig.columnsSurface || 'HAIRLINE',
        colors: resolvedColors,
        isDark,
        maxBlocks: layoutVariant === 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT' ? 2 : 3,
      });

      if (layoutVariant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' || headerPlacement === 'bottom') {
        mainChildren = [
          mastheadNode,
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '20px',
                width: '100%',
              },
              children: [
                ...(statsNode ? [statsNode] : []),
                ...(photoNodes.length > 0
                  ? [
                      {
                        type: 'div',
                        props: {
                          style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'row', gap: '16px' },
                          children: photoNodes,
                        },
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                ...(columnsNode ? [columnsNode] : []),
                headlineNode,
                signatureNode,
              ],
            },
          },
        ];
      } else if (layoutVariant === 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT') {
        mainChildren = [
          mastheadNode,
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'row',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
                width: '100%',
                gap: '32px',
                flex: '1',
                margin: '24px 0',
              },
              children: [
                {
                  type: 'div',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      flex: '1',
                      gap: '16px',
                    },
                    children: [headlineNode, ...(statsNode ? [statsNode] : [])],
                  },
                },
                {
                  type: 'div',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      flex: '1',
                      gap: '16px',
                    },
                    children: [
                      ...(columnsNode ? [columnsNode] : []),
                      ...(photoNodes.length > 0
                        ? [
                            {
                              type: 'div',
                              props: {
                                style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' },
                                children: photoNodes,
                              },
                            },
                          ]
                        : []),
                    ],
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          },
          signatureNode,
        ];
      } else if (layoutVariant === 'CENTERED_HERO') {
        mainChildren = [
          mastheadNode,
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                width: '100%',
                gap: '20px',
                flex: '1',
              },
              children: [
                headlineNode,
                ...(columnsNode ? [columnsNode] : []),
                ...(statsNode ? [statsNode] : []),
              ],
            },
          },
          signatureNode,
        ];
      } else {
        // TOP_BANNER (Default Data Infographic)
        mainChildren = [
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '18px',
                width: '100%',
              },
              children: [mastheadNode, headlineNode],
            },
          },
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '20px',
                width: '100%',
              },
              children: [
                ...(statsNode ? [statsNode] : []),
                ...(photoNodes.length > 0
                  ? [
                      {
                        type: 'div',
                        props: {
                          style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'row', gap: '16px' },
                          children: photoNodes,
                        },
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                ...(columnsNode ? [columnsNode] : []),
                signatureNode,
              ],
            },
          },
        ];
      }
    }

    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          width: `${width}px`,
          height: `${height}px`,
          ...rootStyles,
          position: 'relative',
          fontFamily: 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
        },
        children: mainChildren,
      },
    };
  }
}

import { SurfaceMode } from '../types.js';

export interface EditorialSurfaceOptions {
  luminance?: number;
  isDark?: boolean;
  brandHex?: string;
  accentHex?: string;
  borderRadius?: string;
  padding?: string;
}

export interface EditorialColorTokens {
  textPrimary: string;
  textMuted: string;
  textAccent: string;
  borderSubtle: string;
  surfaceBackground: string;
}

/**
 * EditorialSurface: Otoritas pembuatan visual surface dan kontras teks adaptif untuk Satori.
 * Menggantikan kartu UI dashboard tebal dengan perlakuan tipografi majalah editorial asli.
 */
export class EditorialSurface {
  /**
   * Menghitung palet teks dan border berdasarkan luminansi latar belakang
   * Menjamin rasio kontras WCAG AA (minimal 4.5:1 untuk teks biasa, 3:1 untuk headline tebal)
   */
  public static getTextColors(
    isDark: boolean,
    options?: { brandHex?: string; accentHex?: string }
  ): EditorialColorTokens {
    if (isDark) {
      return {
        textPrimary: '#FFFFFF',
        textMuted: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)',
        textAccent: options?.accentHex || options?.brandHex || '#F59E0B',
        borderSubtle: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)',
        surfaceBackground: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.45)',
      };
    } else {
      return {
        textPrimary: '#0F172A',
        textMuted: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.70)',
        textAccent: options?.accentHex || options?.brandHex || '#0284C7',
        borderSubtle: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.15)',
        surfaceBackground: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65)',
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Mengembalikan styling inline untuk Satori sesuai mode surface arsitektur
   */
  public static getTextSurface(
    mode: SurfaceMode = 'NONE',
    options?: EditorialSurfaceOptions
  ): Record<string, any> {
    const isDark = options?.isDark ?? true;
    const padding = options?.padding || '0px';
    const borderRadius = options?.borderRadius || '0px';

    switch (mode) {
      case 'NONE':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          background: 'transparent',
          border: 'none',
          boxShadow: 'none',
          padding,
          borderRadius,
        };

      case 'HAIRLINE':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          background: isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.03)',
          border: `1px solid ${isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14)' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.12)'}`,
          padding: padding === '0px' ? '20px 24px' : padding,
          borderRadius: borderRadius === '0px' ? '4px' : borderRadius,
          backdropFilter: 'blur(8px)',
        };

      case 'SCRIM_GRADIENT':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          background: isDark
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 0%, rgba(10, 15, 29, 0.75) 100%)'
            : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88) 100%)',
          padding: padding === '0px' ? '24px 28px' : padding,
          borderRadius,
        };

      case 'SOLID_BAND':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          background: isDark ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.92)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94)',
          borderTop: `2px solid ${options?.accentHex || (isDark ? '#F59E0B' : '#0F172A')}`,
          borderBottom: `1px solid ${isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)' : 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)'}`,
          padding: padding === '0px' ? '24px 32px' : padding,
          borderRadius,
        };

      default:
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          background: 'transparent',
          padding,
          borderRadius,
        };
    }
  }
}

import {
  PosterLayoutVariant,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  PosterCardArrangement,
} from '../types.js';

export interface LayoutContainerStyles {
  display: 'flex';
  flexDirection: 'column' | 'row';
  justifyContent: 'flex-start' | 'flex-end' | 'space-between' | 'center';
  alignItems: 'stretch' | 'flex-start' | 'center';
  padding: string;
  boxSizing: 'border-box';
}

export interface CardContainerStyles {
  display: 'flex';
  flexDirection: 'column' | 'row';
  flexWrap: 'wrap' | 'nowrap';
  gap: string;
  width: string;
}

/**
 * VariantResolver: Otoritas penentuan struktur Flexbox kanvas Satori berdasarkan
 * variasi dinamis layout (TOP_BANNER, BOTTOM_ANCHOR, EDITORIAL_SPLIT, CENTERED_HERO).
 */
export class VariantResolver {
  /**
   * Menghasilkan styling root flex container berdasarkan variant dan penempatan header
   */
  public static resolveRootContainer(
    variant: PosterLayoutVariant = 'TOP_BANNER',
    headerPlacement: PosterHeaderPlacement = 'top',
    canvasPadding = '64px'
  ): LayoutContainerStyles {
    if (variant === 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR' || headerPlacement === 'bottom') {
      return {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        justifyContent: 'flex-end',
        alignItems: 'stretch',
        padding: canvasPadding,
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
      };
    }

    if (variant === 'CENTERED_HERO' || headerPlacement === 'center') {
      return {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'stretch',
        padding: canvasPadding,
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
      };
    }

    // Default: TOP_BANNER atau EDITORIAL_SPLIT
    return {
      display: 'flex',
      flexDirection: 'column',
      justifyContent: 'space-between',
      alignItems: 'stretch',
      padding: canvasPadding,
      boxSizing: 'border-box',
    };
  }

  /**
   * Menghasilkan susunan flex container untuk kartu/blok sekunder
   */
  public static resolveCardContainer(
    arrangement: PosterCardArrangement = 'stacked',
    options?: { gap?: string }
  ): CardContainerStyles {
    const gap = options?.gap || '24px';

    switch (arrangement) {
      case 'horizontal_split':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'row',
          flexWrap: 'nowrap',
          gap,
          width: '100%',
        };

      case 'grid_compact':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'row',
          flexWrap: 'wrap',
          gap,
          width: '100%',
        };

      case 'single_hero_card':
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          flexWrap: 'nowrap',
          gap,
          width: '100%',
        };

      case 'stacked':
      default:
        return {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          flexWrap: 'nowrap',
          gap,
          width: '100%',
        };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Mengatur orientasi konten utama (split columns atau single vertical stream)
   */
  public static resolveContentDirection(
    variant: PosterLayoutVariant = 'TOP_BANNER'
  ): 'row' | 'column' {
    return variant === 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT' ? 'row' : 'column';
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/core/zone-sampler.ts (95 baris, 3.5 KB) ---
======================================================================
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { CompositionZone, ZoneRect, SurfaceMode } from '../types.js';

export interface SampledZoneMetrics {
  zoneId: string;
  luminance: number; // 0.0 (pitch black) s/d 1.0 (pure white)
  isDark: boolean; // luminance < 0.48
  hasHighVariance: boolean; // standard deviation > 38 (latar belakang ramai/tekstur tinggi)
  suggestedContrast: 'LIGHT_TEXT' | 'DARK_TEXT';
  suggestedSurface: SurfaceMode;
}

export class ZoneSampler {
  /**
   * Melakukan ekstraksi visual region Sharp pada buffer background AI
   * untuk menghitung luminansi, kontras, dan varians piksel per zona komposisi.
   */
  public static async sampleZones(
    imageBuffer: Buffer,
    zones: CompositionZone[]
  ): Promise<Map<string, SampledZoneMetrics>> {
    const results = new Map<string, SampledZoneMetrics>();
    if (!imageBuffer || imageBuffer.length === 0 || !zones || zones.length === 0) {
      return results;
    }

    try {
      const metadata = await sharp(imageBuffer).metadata();
      const imgWidth = metadata.width || 1080;
      const imgHeight = metadata.height || 1350;

      for (const zone of zones) {
        try {
          const rect = zone.rect;
          // Konversi rasio normalisasi (0.0 - 1.0) ke koordinat piksel absolut
          const left = Math.max(0, Math.min(imgWidth - 1, Math.round(rect.x * imgWidth)));
          const top = Math.max(0, Math.min(imgHeight - 1, Math.round(rect.y * imgHeight)));
          const width = Math.max(1, Math.min(imgWidth - left, Math.round(rect.w * imgWidth)));
          const height = Math.max(1, Math.min(imgHeight - top, Math.round(rect.h * imgHeight)));

          const stats = await sharp(imageBuffer)
            .extract({ left, top, width, height })
            .stats();

          const rMean = stats.channels[0]?.mean ?? 128;
          const gMean = stats.channels[1]?.mean ?? 128;
          const bMean = stats.channels[2]?.mean ?? 128;

          // Standar Luminansi Relatif ITU-R BT.601
          const luminance = (0.299 * rMean + 0.587 * gMean + 0.114 * bMean) / 255;
          const isDark = luminance < 0.48;

          const rStd = stats.channels[0]?.stdev ?? 0;
          const gStd = stats.channels[1]?.stdev ?? 0;
          const bStd = stats.channels[2]?.stdev ?? 0;
          const avgStd = (rStd + gStd + bStd) / 3;
          const hasHighVariance = avgStd > 38;

          let suggestedSurface: SurfaceMode = zone.surface || 'NONE';
          if (!zone.surface) {
            if (hasHighVariance && luminance > 0.35 && luminance < 0.65) {
              suggestedSurface = 'SCRIM_GRADIENT';
            } else if (hasHighVariance) {
              suggestedSurface = 'HAIRLINE';
            } else {
              suggestedSurface = 'NONE';
            }
          }

          results.set(zone.id, {
            zoneId: zone.id,
            luminance,
            isDark,
            hasHighVariance,
            suggestedContrast: isDark ? 'LIGHT_TEXT' : 'DARK_TEXT',
            suggestedSurface,
          });
        } catch {
          results.set(zone.id, {
            zoneId: zone.id,
            luminance: zone.contrastTarget === 'DARK_TEXT' ? 0.8 : 0.2,
            isDark: zone.contrastTarget !== 'DARK_TEXT',
            hasHighVariance: false,
            suggestedContrast: zone.contrastTarget || 'LIGHT_TEXT',
            suggestedSurface: zone.surface || 'NONE',
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[ZoneSampler] Gagal membaca metadata sharp background:', err);
    }

    return results;
  }
}
