import fs from 'fs/promises';
import path from 'path';

export interface SatoriFontConfig {
  name: string;
  data: Buffer;
  weight: 400 | 600 | 700;
  style: 'normal' | 'italic';
}

export class FontLoader {
  private static fontCache: SatoriFontConfig[] | null = null;

  /**
   * Menemukan direktori assets/fonts secara dinamis
   */
  public static async findFontsDir(): Promise<string> {
    const candidates = [
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../../assets/fonts'),
      '/home/pc/projects/polaris-platform/assets/fonts',
    ];

    for (const dir of candidates) {
      try {
        await fs.access(dir);
        return dir;
      } catch {
        // Coba kandidat berikutnya
      }
    }
    return path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'assets/fonts');
  }

  /**
   * Memuat file binary font lokal sekali ke memori (Singleton)
   * Menghindari bug fatal librsvg yang gagal memuat @import Google Fonts di Docker Linux
   */
  public static async getFonts(): Promise<SatoriFontConfig[]> {
    if (this.fontCache) return this.fontCache;

    const fontsDir = await this.findFontsDir();

    const [antonData, bebasData, jakartaData, dejavuData, dejavuBoldData] = await Promise.all([
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'Anton-Regular.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'DejaVuSerif.ttf')).catch(() => fs.readFile('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf')).catch(() => fs.readFile('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf')),
    ]);

    this.fontCache = [
      { name: 'Anton', data: antonData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Bebas Neue', data: bebasData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      // Daftarkan Plus Jakarta Sans di berbagai weight agar Satori tidak pernah gagal mencocokkan
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 600, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 700, style: 'normal' },
      // Daftarkan DejaVu Serif untuk typography koran/broadsheet
      { name: 'DejaVu Serif', data: dejavuData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'DejaVu Serif', data: dejavuBoldData, weight: 700, style: 'normal' },
    ];

    return this.fontCache;
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/core/subject-harmonizer.ts (59 baris, 2.1 KB) ---

import sharp from 'sharp';

export class SubjectHarmonizer {
  /**
   * Mengharmonisasikan foto cutout PNG Anggota Dewan agar menyatu dengan estetika poster.
   * Menggunakan Bottom-Anchored alignment dan Soft Gradient Feathering di tepi bawah
   * agar tubuh subjek tidak pernah terlihat melayang atau terpotong kaku.
   */
  public static async harmonizeSubjectPhoto(
    photoBuffer: Buffer,
    archetype: string,
    targetWidth: number,
    targetHeight: number
  ): Promise<Buffer> {
    // 1. Resize foto dengan fit: 'contain' dan posisi 'bottom'
    let pipeline = sharp(photoBuffer).resize({
      width: targetWidth,
      height: targetHeight,
      fit: 'contain',
      position: 'bottom',
      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },
    });

    if (archetype === 'STREET_JOURNALISM') {
      // Ubah ke High-Contrast B&W jurnalisme risograph
      pipeline = pipeline.grayscale().linear(1.22, -14).sharpen();
    } else if (archetype === 'VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE' || archetype === 'RETRO_CAMPAIGN') {
      // Sedikit tone hangat vintage
      pipeline = pipeline.modulate({ brightness: 1.02, saturation: 0.95 });
    }

    const resizedBuffer = await pipeline.png().toBuffer();

    // 2. Terapkan Soft Bottom Gradient Feathering (14% bagian bawah meluruh lembut ke transparan)
    // Menghilangkan efek "potongan pinggang kaku / melayang di udara"
    try {
      const featherSvg = Buffer.from(`
        <svg width="${targetWidth}" height="${targetHeight}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="bottomFeather" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="1" />
              <stop offset="86%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="1" />
              <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0" />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <rect width="${targetWidth}" height="${targetHeight}" fill="url(#bottomFeather)" />
        </svg>
      `);

      return await sharp(resizedBuffer)
        .composite([{ input: featherSvg, blend: 'dest-in' }])
        .png()
        .toBuffer();
    } catch {
      return resizedBuffer;
    }
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/core/adaptive-typography.ts (181 baris, 6.2 KB) ---
======================================================================
/**
 * POLARIS Adaptive Typography & Contrast Helpers
 * Menghitung ukuran font dinamis berdasarkan panjang teks (mencegah teks terpotong / hilang di Satori),
 * dan menyediakan styling pelindung kontras teks di atas gambar latar AI.
 */

/**
 * Menghitung ukuran font dinamis agar teks panjang tidak tumpah/terpotong (overflow clipping)
 */
/**
 * Menghitung ukuran font dinamis agar teks panjang tidak tumpah/terpotong (overflow clipping)
 * Memperhitungkan panjang kata tunggal ekstrem (misal "INFRASTRUKTUR", "BERKELANJUTAN")
 * agar font size turun ke rentang proporsional (52px - 68px) dan tidak terpotong tepi kanvas Satori.
 */
export function getAdaptiveFontSize(
  text: string,
  baseSize: number,
  minSize: number,
  charThreshold: number = 20
): number {
  const clean = (text || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return baseSize;

  const words = clean.split(/\s+/);
  const maxWordLength = Math.max(...words.map((w) => w.length), 0);

  let calculated = baseSize;

  if (clean.length > charThreshold) {
    const excess = clean.length - charThreshold;
    // Rasio reduksi halus (smooth step-down)
    const reductionFactor = Math.min(0.45, (excess / charThreshold) * 0.35);
    calculated = Math.round(baseSize * (1 - reductionFactor));
  }

  // Jika terdapat kata tunggal sangat panjang (seperti "INFRASTRUKTUR" 13 huruf atau "BERKELANJUTAN" 13 huruf)
  // turunkan ukuran font ke rentang proporsional (misal 52px - 68px)
  if (maxWordLength >= 13) {
    calculated = Math.min(calculated, Math.max(minSize, Math.round(baseSize * 0.72)));
  } else if (maxWordLength >= 11) {
    calculated = Math.min(calculated, Math.max(minSize, Math.round(baseSize * 0.82)));
  }

  return Math.max(minSize, Math.min(baseSize, calculated));
}

/**
 * Pemotongan judul utama yang aman terhadap batas kata (Word-Safe Headline Truncation).
 * DILARANG KERAS memotong di tengah kata dan DILARANG menambahkan '...' pada judul utama.
 */
export function wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(title: string, maxChars: number = 34): string {
  const clean = (title || '').trim();
  if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;

  const words = clean.split(/\s+/);
  const kept: string[] = [];
  let currentLen = 0;

  for (const w of words) {
    const candidateLen = kept.length === 0 ? w.length : currentLen + 1 + w.length;
    if (candidateLen <= maxChars) {
      kept.push(w);
      currentLen = candidateLen;
    } else {
      break;
    }
  }

  // Jika kata pertama saja sudah melebihi maxChars, pertahankan kata pertama tanpa slicing
  if (kept.length === 0 && words.length > 0) {
    return words[0]!;
  }

  return kept.join(' ');
}

/**
 * Pembersihan label lingkaran statistik (statCircles topLabel)
 * WAJIB 1 KATA PENDEK (maksimal 11 karakter), huruf kapital, TANPA titik-titik (...)
 */
export function cleanCircleLabel(label: string, maxChars: number = 11): string {
  if (!label) return 'SASARAN';
  const clean = label.trim().replace(/[.]+$/, '');
  const firstWord = clean.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
  if (firstWord.length <= maxChars) {
    return firstWord.toUpperCase();
  }
  return firstWord.slice(0, maxChars).toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Pemotongan kalimat tuntas tanpa ellipsis menggantung (Anti-Ellipsis Sentence Clamping).
 * Memprioritaskan pemotongan pada akhir kalimat utuh (. atau ;) jika posisinya >= 55% batas.
 * Jika harus memotong klausa, akhiri dengan tanda titik '.' yang rapi dan bersih.
 */
export function smartSentenceClamp(text: string, maxChars: number = 150): string {
  const clean = (text || '').trim();
  if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;

  const limitSlice = clean.slice(0, maxChars);
  const minThreshold = Math.floor(maxChars * 0.55);

  // Cari akhir kalimat utuh (. atau ;)
  const sentenceEndMatches = [...limitSlice.matchAll(/[.;!?]\s*/g)];
  let bestSentenceEndIndex = -1;

  for (const m of sentenceEndMatches) {
    const endPos = (m.index ?? 0) + 1;
    if (endPos >= minThreshold && endPos <= maxChars) {
      bestSentenceEndIndex = endPos;
    }
  }

  if (bestSentenceEndIndex > 0) {
    return clean.slice(0, bestSentenceEndIndex).trim();
  }

  // Jika tidak ada kalimat utuh, potong pada batas kata terdekat dan akhiri tanda titik '.'
  const lastSpace = limitSlice.lastIndexOf(' ');
  const safeText = lastSpace > minThreshold ? limitSlice.slice(0, lastSpace) : limitSlice;
  return `${safeText.replace(/[.,;:!?\s]+$/, '').trim()}.`;
}

/**
 * Pemotongan teks cerdas pada spasi terdekat
 * Untuk teks paragraf (>= 60 char), mengalihkan ke smartSentenceClamp agar bebas dari ellipsis menggantung.
 */
export function smartTruncate(text: string, maxChars: number, withEllipsis = true): string {
  const clean = (text || '').trim();
  if (clean.length <= maxChars) return clean;

  if (maxChars >= 60) {
    return smartSentenceClamp(clean, maxChars);
  }

  if (!withEllipsis) {
    const sliced = clean.slice(0, maxChars);
    const lastSpace = sliced.lastIndexOf(' ');
    return (lastSpace > maxChars * 0.6 ? sliced.slice(0, lastSpace) : sliced).trim();
  }

  const limit = Math.max(3, maxChars - 3);
  const sliced = clean.slice(0, limit);
  const lastSpace = sliced.lastIndexOf(' ');
  const safeText = lastSpace > limit * 0.6 ? sliced.slice(0, lastSpace) : sliced;
  return `${safeText.trim()}...`;
}

/**
 * Sanitasi deterministik teks editorial untuk layout poster
 * Membuang mikrolinguistik terlarang (nyata, sekadar, babu bhaiya, dsb.)
 */
export function safeEditorialText(text: string): string {
  if (!text) return '';
  return text
    .replace(/\bnyata\b/gi, 'konkret')
    .replace(/\bsekadar\b/gi, 'hanya')
    .replace(/\bbukan sekadar\b/gi, 'tidak hanya')
    .replace(/babu bhaiya/gi, 'rekan warga')
    .replace(/[—–]/g, '-')
    .trim();
}

/**
 * @deprecated Gunakan editorial-surface.ts (getTextSurface) untuk estetika terpadu tanpa kartu kaku
 * Card style pelindung kontras untuk menjamin teks 100% terbaca di atas visual AI (Backward Compatibility)
 */
export function getProtectiveCardStyle(options?: {
  bgColor?: string;
  borderColor?: string;
  borderRadius?: string;
  padding?: string;
}) {
  return {
    backgroundColor: options?.bgColor || 'rgba(10, 15, 29, 0.82)',
    border: `1.5px solid ${options?.borderColor || 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)'}`,
    borderRadius: options?.borderRadius || '8px',
    padding: options?.padding || '16px 20px',
    boxShadow: '0 12px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45)',
  };
}

import { ResolvedColorTokens } from '../types.js';

export type MastheadStyle =
  | 'BROADSHEET_CLASSIC'
  | 'MODERN_MINIMAL'
  | 'BOLD_STAMP'
  | 'VINTAGE_RIBBON';

export interface MastheadPrimitiveParams {
  style?: MastheadStyle;
  title?: string;
  editionTag?: string;
  subTag?: string;
  logoDataUri?: string;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Masthead Primitive: Header navigasi, masthead koran, atau stempel edisi resmi.
 */
export function buildMastheadPrimitive(params: MastheadPrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const {
    style = 'MODERN_MINIMAL',
    editionTag = params.title || 'EDISI RESMI',
    subTag = 'EDISI RESMI // 2026',
    logoDataUri,
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
  } = params;

  const textPrimary = isDark ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0F172A';
  const textMuted = isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.70)' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.65)';
  const borderSubtle = isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.12)';
  const accent = colors.primaryAccent;

  if (style === 'BROADSHEET_CLASSIC') {
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          width: '100%',
          borderTop: `2px solid ${textPrimary}`,
          borderBottom: `1px solid ${borderSubtle}`,
          padding: '8px 0px 10px 0px',
          gap: '6px',
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: {
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'row',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
              },
              children: [
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'DejaVu Serif',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      fontStyle: 'italic',
                      color: textMuted,
                      letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                    },
                    children: subTag.toUpperCase(),
                  },
                },
                ...(logoDataUri
                  ? [
                      {
                        type: 'img',
                        props: {
                          src: logoDataUri,
                          style: { height: '24px', objectFit: 'contain' },
                        },
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'DejaVu Serif',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      color: textPrimary,
                      letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                    },
                    children: editionTag.toUpperCase(),
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  if (style === 'BOLD_STAMP') {
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'row',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          width: '100%',
          backgroundColor: isDark ? '#09090B' : '#FFFFFF',
          border: `2px solid ${accent}`,
          padding: '8px 16px',
          boxShadow: `3px 3px 0px ${accent}`,
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' },
              children: [
                ...(logoDataUri
                  ? [
                      {
                        type: 'img',
                        props: {
                          src: logoDataUri,
                          style: { height: '26px', objectFit: 'contain' },
                        },
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'Anton',
                      fontSize: '15px',
                      color: accent,
                      letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                    },
                    children: editionTag.toUpperCase(),
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          },
          {
            type: 'span',
            props: {
              style: {
                fontSize: '10.5px',
                fontWeight: 800,
                color: isDark ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0F172A',
                letterSpacing: '0.06em',
              },
              children: subTag.toUpperCase(),
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  if (style === 'VINTAGE_RIBBON') {
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'row',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          width: '100%',
          paddingBottom: '12px',
          borderBottom: `2px solid ${accent}`,
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'div',
            props: {
              style: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' },
              children: [
                ...(logoDataUri
                  ? [
                      {
                        type: 'img',
                        props: {
                          src: logoDataUri,
                          style: { height: '28px', objectFit: 'contain' },
                        },
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'Anton',
                      fontSize: '16px',
                      color: textPrimary,
                      letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                    },
                    children: editionTag.toUpperCase(),
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          },
          {
            type: 'span',
            props: {
              style: {
                fontSize: '11px',
                color: textMuted,
                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
              },
              children: subTag.toUpperCase(),
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  // MODERN_MINIMAL (Default)
  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        width: '100%',
        paddingBottom: '12px',
        borderBottom: `1px solid ${borderSubtle}`,
      },
      children: [
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' },
            children: [
              ...(logoDataUri
                ? [
                    {
                      type: 'img',
                      props: {
                        src: logoDataUri,
                        style: { height: '28px', objectFit: 'contain' },
                      },
                    },
                  ]
                : []),
              {
                type: 'span',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    fontFamily: 'Anton',
                    fontSize: '16px',
                    color: textPrimary,
                    letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  },
                  children: editionTag.toUpperCase(),
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        },
        {
          type: 'span',
          props: {
            style: {
              fontSize: '10.5px',
              fontWeight: 700,
              color: '#FFFFFF',
              backgroundColor: accent,
              padding: '3px 8px',
              borderRadius: '3px',
              letterSpacing: '0.05em',
            },
            children: subTag.toUpperCase(),
          },
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/primitives/headline.primitive.ts (141 baris, 3.7 KB) ---

import { ResolvedColorTokens, SurfaceMode } from '../types.js';
import { getAdaptiveFontSize, smartTruncate } from '../core/adaptive-typography.js';
import { EditorialSurface } from '../core/editorial-surface.js';

export interface HeadlinePrimitiveParams {
  title: string;
  subheader?: string;
  definitionBody?: string;
  fontFamily?: 'Bebas Neue' | 'Anton' | 'DejaVu Serif' | 'Plus Jakarta Sans';
  headline?: string;
  subHeader?: string;
  surface?: SurfaceMode;
  maxFontSize?: number;
  minFontSize?: number;
  maxCharThreshold?: number;
  surfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
  align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right';
  maxWidth?: string;
}

/**
 * Headline Primitive: Merender judul utama, subheader, dan teaser definisi
 * dengan penyesuaian font adaptif dan perlakuan surface editorial.
 */
export function buildHeadlinePrimitive(params: HeadlinePrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const {
    title = params.headline,
    subheader = params.subHeader,
    definitionBody,
    fontFamily = 'Bebas Neue',
    maxFontSize = 96,
    minFontSize = 52,
    maxCharThreshold = 20,
    surfaceMode = params.surface || 'NONE',
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
    align = 'left',
    maxWidth = '100%',
  } = params;

  const displayTitle = (title || 'POLARIS').toUpperCase();
  const titleFontSize = getAdaptiveFontSize(
    displayTitle,
    maxFontSize,
    minFontSize,
    maxCharThreshold
  );

  const textTokens = EditorialSurface.getTextColors(isDark, {
    brandHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    accentHex: colors.secondaryAccent,
  });

  const surfaceStyle = EditorialSurface.getTextSurface(surfaceMode, {
    isDark,
    accentHex: colors.primaryAccent,
  });

  const children: any[] = [];

  if (subheader) {
    children.push({
      type: 'span',
      props: {
        style: {
          fontSize: '12px',
          fontWeight: 800,
          color: colors.primaryAccent,
          letterSpacing: '0.12em',
          textTransform: 'uppercase',
          textAlign: align,
          textShadow: isDark ? '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)' : 'none',
        },
        children: smartTruncate(subheader, 75).toUpperCase(),
      },
    });
  }

  children.push({
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        fontFamily,
        fontSize: `${titleFontSize}px`,
        color: textTokens.textPrimary,
        lineHeight: fontFamily === 'DejaVu Serif' ? 1.05 : 0.92,
        letterSpacing: fontFamily === 'DejaVu Serif' ? '0.01em' : '0.03em',
        textAlign: align,
        textShadow: isDark
          ? '0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.95), 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.85)'
          : 'none',
      },
      children: smartTruncate(displayTitle, 45),
    },
  });

  if (definitionBody) {
    children.push({
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          fontSize: '13px',
          color: textTokens.textMuted,
          lineHeight: 1.42,
          marginTop: '6px',
          textAlign: align,
          maxWidth: '92%',
        },
        children: smartTruncate(definitionBody, 200),
      },
    });
  }

  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        ...surfaceStyle,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: '6px',
        alignItems: align === 'center' ? 'center' : align === 'right' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
        maxWidth,
      },
      children,
    },
  };
}

import { PosterCardArrangement, ResolvedColorTokens, SurfaceMode } from '../types.js';
import { smartTruncate, safeEditorialText } from '../core/adaptive-typography.js';
import { EditorialSurface } from '../core/editorial-surface.js';

export interface EditorialBlockData {
  header: string;
  body: string;
  badge?: string;
}

export interface EditorialColumnsPrimitiveParams {
  blocks: Record<string, EditorialBlockData> | EditorialBlockData[];
  arrangement?: PosterCardArrangement;
  surfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
  maxBlocks?: number;
}

/**
 * EditorialColumns Primitive: Merender blok informasi jurnalistik dalam format kolom,
 * horizontal split, atau grid modular dengan pemisah hairline elegan.
 */
export function buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive(params: EditorialColumnsPrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const {
    blocks,
    arrangement = 'horizontal_split',
    surfaceMode = 'HAIRLINE',
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
    maxBlocks = 3,
  } = params;

  const entries: [string, EditorialBlockData][] = Array.isArray(blocks)
    ? blocks.slice(0, maxBlocks).map((b, i) => [`col_${i + 1}`, b])
    : Object.entries(blocks || {}).slice(0, maxBlocks);
  if (entries.length === 0) return null;

  const textTokens = EditorialSurface.getTextColors(isDark, {
    brandHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    accentHex: colors.secondaryAccent,
  });

  const surfaceStyle = EditorialSurface.getTextSurface(surfaceMode, {
    isDark,
    accentHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    borderRadius: '4px',
    padding: '16px 20px',
  });

  const isHorizontal = arrangement === 'horizontal_split';

  const blockNodes = entries.map(([key, block], idx) => {
    const cleanHeader = safeEditorialText(block.header || `POKOK ${idx + 1}`);
    const cleanBody = safeEditorialText(block.body || '');

    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          flex: isHorizontal ? '1' : 'none',
          gap: '6px',
          borderLeft: `2px solid ${idx === 0 ? colors.primaryAccent : colors.secondaryAccent}`,
          paddingLeft: '12px',
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'span',
            props: {
              style: {
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 800,
                color: idx === 0 ? colors.primaryAccent : colors.secondaryAccent,
                letterSpacing: '0.08em',
                textTransform: 'uppercase',
              },
              children: smartTruncate(cleanHeader, 40).toUpperCase(),
            },
          },
          {
            type: 'span',
            props: {
              style: {
                fontSize: '12px',
                color: textTokens.textPrimary,
                lineHeight: 1.45,
              },
              children: smartTruncate(cleanBody, 180),
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  });

  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        ...surfaceStyle,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: isHorizontal ? 'row' : 'column',
        gap: '20px',
        width: '100%',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
      },
      children: blockNodes,
    },
  };
}

import { ResolvedColorTokens } from '../types.js';
import { smartTruncate, safeEditorialText, cleanCircleLabel, smartSentenceClamp } from '../core/adaptive-typography.js';
import { EditorialSurface } from '../core/editorial-surface.js';

export interface StatCircleItem {
  topLabel: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}

export interface NumberedListItem {
  number: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  summary: string;
}

export interface StatBadgesPrimitiveParams {
  statCircles?: StatCircleItem[];
  stats?: StatCircleItem[];
  displayMode?: 'stat_circles' | 'numbered_items' | 'pill_badges';
  numberedItems?: NumberedListItem[];
  variant?: 'CIRCLES' | 'NUMBERED_STREAM' | 'PILL_BADGES';
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
}

/**
 * StatBadges Primitive: Merender lingkaran statistik (statCircles)
 * atau daftar bernomor (numberedItems 01-05) secara modular.
 */
export function buildStatBadgesPrimitive(params: StatBadgesPrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const statCircles = params.statCircles || params.stats;
  const variant =
    params.variant ||
    (params.displayMode === 'stat_circles' ? 'CIRCLES' : undefined) ||
    (statCircles && statCircles.length > 0 ? 'CIRCLES' : 'NUMBERED_STREAM');
  const {
    numberedItems,
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
  } = params;

  const textTokens = EditorialSurface.getTextColors(isDark, {
    brandHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    accentHex: colors.secondaryAccent,
  });

  if (variant === 'CIRCLES' && statCircles && statCircles.length > 0) {
    const circleNodes = statCircles.slice(0, 3).map((item, idx) => {
      const isFirst = idx === 0;
      const borderCol = isFirst ? colors.primaryAccent : colors.secondaryAccent;

      return {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            width: '120px',
            height: '120px',
            borderRadius: '60px',
            border: `2px solid ${borderCol}`,
            backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.70)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85)',
            padding: '10px',
            boxSizing: 'border-box',
          },
          children: [
            {
              type: 'span',
              props: {
                style: {
                  fontSize: '9px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: isFirst ? colors.primaryAccent : colors.secondaryAccent,
                  letterSpacing: '0.06em',
                  textTransform: 'uppercase',
                },
                children: cleanCircleLabel(safeEditorialText(item.topLabel || 'CAPAIAN'), 11),
              },
            },
            {
              type: 'span',
              props: {
                style: {
                  fontFamily: 'Anton',
                  fontSize: '26px',
                  color: textTokens.textPrimary,
                  lineHeight: 1.1,
                  margin: '2px 0',
                },
                children: item.value,
              },
            },
            {
              type: 'span',
              props: {
                style: {
                  fontSize: '8.5px',
                  color: textTokens.textMuted,
                  textAlign: 'center',
                  lineHeight: 1.2,
                },
                children: smartSentenceClamp(safeEditorialText(item.description || ''), 48),
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      };
    });

    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'row',
          gap: '16px',
          alignItems: 'center',
        },
        children: circleNodes,
      },
    };
  }

  if (numberedItems && numberedItems.length > 0) {
    const itemNodes = numberedItems.slice(0, 4).map((item, idx) => {
      const num = item.number || `0${idx + 1}`;
      return {
        type: 'div',
        props: {
          style: {
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'row',
            alignItems: 'flex-start',
            gap: '12px',
            paddingBottom: '8px',
            borderBottom: `1px solid ${textTokens.borderSubtle}`,
          },
          children: [
            {
              type: 'span',
              props: {
                style: {
                  fontFamily: 'Anton',
                  fontSize: '20px',
                  color: idx === 0 ? colors.primaryAccent : colors.secondaryAccent,
                  lineHeight: 1,
                  minWidth: '28px',
                },
                children: num,
              },
            },
            {
              type: 'div',
              props: {
                style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '2px', flex: '1' },
                children: [
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '11.5px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        color: textTokens.textPrimary,
                        letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                      },
                      children: smartTruncate(safeEditorialText(item.title), 40).toUpperCase(),
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'span',
                    props: {
                      style: {
                        fontSize: '10.5px',
                        color: textTokens.textMuted,
                        lineHeight: 1.35,
                      },
                      children: smartTruncate(safeEditorialText(item.summary), 100),
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      };
    });

    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '10px',
          width: '100%',
        },
        children: itemNodes,
      },
    };
  }

  return null;
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/primitives/photo-frame.primitive.ts (213 baris, 5.7 KB) ---

import { ResolvedColorTokens } from '../types.js';

export type PhotoFrameStyle = 'POLAROID' | 'CIRCULAR_INSET' | 'HALFTONE_CUTOUT' | 'CLEAN_FRAME';

export interface PhotoFramePrimitiveParams {
  photoDataUri?: string;
  photoBase64?: string;
  style?: PhotoFrameStyle | 'polaroid' | 'circular_inset' | 'halftone_cutout' | 'clean_frame';
  caption?: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
  rotationDeg?: number;
}

/**
 * PhotoFrame Primitive: Merender sub-foto dalam gaya Polaroid,
 * Circular Inset, Halftone Cutout, atau Clean Editorial Frame.
 */
export function buildPhotoFramePrimitive(params: PhotoFramePrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const photoDataUri = params.photoDataUri || params.photoBase64;
  const styleRaw = (params.style || 'CLEAN_FRAME').toUpperCase() as PhotoFrameStyle;
  const style: PhotoFrameStyle =
    styleRaw === ('POLAROID' as any)
      ? 'POLAROID'
      : styleRaw === ('CIRCULAR_INSET' as any)
        ? 'CIRCULAR_INSET'
        : styleRaw === ('HALFTONE_CUTOUT' as any)
          ? 'HALFTONE_CUTOUT'
          : 'CLEAN_FRAME';
  const {
    caption,
    width = 240,
    height = 180,
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
    rotationDeg = 0,
  } = params;

  if (!photoDataUri) return null;

  const accent = colors.primaryAccent;

  if (style === 'POLAROID') {
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          padding: '10px 10px 18px 10px',
          boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45)',
          transform: rotationDeg !== 0 ? `rotate(${rotationDeg}deg)` : undefined,
          border: '1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)',
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'img',
            props: {
              src: photoDataUri,
              style: {
                width: `${width}px`,
                height: `${height}px`,
                objectFit: 'cover',
              },
            },
          },
          ...(caption
            ? [
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      color: '#1E293B',
                      marginTop: '8px',
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                    },
                    children: caption.toUpperCase(),
                  },
                },
              ]
            : []),
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  if (style === 'CIRCULAR_INSET') {
    const size = Math.min(width, height);
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          width: `${size + 8}px`,
          height: `${size + 8}px`,
          borderRadius: `${(size + 8) / 2}px`,
          border: `3px solid ${accent}`,
          boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          backgroundColor: isDark ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF',
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'img',
            props: {
              src: photoDataUri,
              style: {
                width: `${size}px`,
                height: `${size}px`,
                borderRadius: `${size / 2}px`,
                objectFit: 'cover',
              },
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  if (style === 'HALFTONE_CUTOUT') {
    return {
      type: 'div',
      props: {
        style: {
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          border: `2px solid ${accent}`,
          padding: '4px',
          backgroundColor: isDark ? '#09090B' : '#FFFFFF',
          boxShadow: `4px 4px 0px ${accent}`,
        },
        children: [
          {
            type: 'img',
            props: {
              src: photoDataUri,
              style: {
                width: `${width}px`,
                height: `${height}px`,
                objectFit: 'cover',
              },
            },
          },
          ...(caption
            ? [
                {
                  type: 'span',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: 'Space Mono',
                      fontSize: '9.5px',
                      color: accent,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      padding: '4px 2px 2px 2px',
                    },
                    children: `// ${caption.toUpperCase()}`,
                  },
                },
              ]
            : []),
        ],
      },
    };
  }

  // CLEAN_FRAME (Default)
  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        border: `1px solid ${isDark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16)' : 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12)'}`,
        boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        borderRadius: '2px',
      },
      children: [
        {
          type: 'img',
          props: {
            src: photoDataUri,
            style: {
              width: `${width}px`,
              height: `${height}px`,
              objectFit: 'cover',
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

import { ResolvedColorTokens, SurfaceMode } from '../types.js';

export interface MemberSignaturePrimitiveParams {
  memberName?: string;
  memberTitle?: string;
  name?: string;
  title?: string;
  affiliationTag?: string;
  qrCodeDataUri?: string;
  showBarcodeAccent?: boolean;
  surfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  colors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  resolvedColors?: ResolvedColorTokens;
  isDark?: boolean;
  borderAccentSide?: 'left' | 'top' | 'none';
}

/**
 * MemberSignature Primitive: Merender blok identitas anggota dewan,
 * jabatan parlemen/dapil, dan kode QR resmi secara proporsional.
 */
export function buildMemberSignaturePrimitive(params: MemberSignaturePrimitiveParams): any {
  const colors = params.colors || params.resolvedColors || {
    primaryAccent: '#EA580C',
    secondaryAccent: '#FED7AA',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT' as const,
    isDark: true,
  };
  const {
    memberName = params.name || 'POLARIS MEMBER',
    memberTitle = params.title || 'ANGGOTA DEWAN',
    affiliationTag = 'DOKUMEN ADVOKASI PUBLIK',
    qrCodeDataUri,
    surfaceMode = 'SOLID_BAND',
    isDark = colors.isDark !== false,
    borderAccentSide = 'left',
  } = params;

  const textTokens = EditorialSurface.getTextColors(isDark, {
    brandHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    accentHex: colors.secondaryAccent,
  });

  const surfaceStyle = EditorialSurface.getTextSurface(surfaceMode, {
    isDark,
    accentHex: colors.primaryAccent,
    borderRadius: '2px',
    padding: '14px 22px',
  });

  const borderStyles: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (borderAccentSide === 'left') {
    borderStyles.borderLeft = `4px solid ${colors.primaryAccent}`;
  } else if (borderAccentSide === 'top') {
    borderStyles.borderTop = `3px solid ${colors.primaryAccent}`;
  }

  return {
    type: 'div',
    props: {
      style: {
        ...surfaceStyle,
        ...borderStyles,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        width: '100%',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
      },
      children: [
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '3px' },
            children: [
              {
                type: 'span',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    fontFamily: 'Anton',
                    fontSize: '21px',
                    color: textTokens.textPrimary,
                    letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                  },
                  children: memberName.toUpperCase(),
                },
              },
              {
                type: 'span',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    fontSize: '11px',
                    color: colors.primaryAccent,
                    fontWeight: 600,
                    letterSpacing: '0.04em',
                  },
                  children: smartTruncate(memberTitle, 45).toUpperCase(),
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        },
        {
          type: 'div',
          props: {
            style: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' },
            children: [
              ...(qrCodeDataUri
                ? [
                    {
                      type: 'img',
                      props: {
                        src: qrCodeDataUri,
                        style: {
                          width: '44px',
                          height: '44px',
                          borderRadius: '2px',
                          border: `1px solid ${textTokens.borderSubtle}`,
                        },
                      },
                    },
                  ]
                : []),
              {
                type: 'div',
                props: {
                  style: {
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    alignItems: 'flex-end',
                    gap: '2px',
                  },
                  children: [
                    {
                      type: 'span',
                      props: {
                        style: {
                          fontFamily: 'Space Mono',
                          fontSize: '9.5px',
                          color: textTokens.textMuted,
                          letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                        },
                        children: affiliationTag.toUpperCase(),
                      },
                    },
                    {
                      type: 'span',
                      props: {
                        style: {
                          fontSize: '10.5px',
                          color: colors.secondaryAccent,
                          fontWeight: 700,
                        },
                        children: 'POLARIS // GOV',
                      },
                    },
                  ],
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

export * from './poster-visual-qa-gate.js';

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/qa/poster-visual-qa-gate.ts (283 baris, 10.1 KB) ---

import sharp from 'sharp';
import {
  CompositionPlan,
  ResolvedColorTokens,
  PosterQAReport,
  PosterQAMetrics,
  PosterQARecommendation,
  SurfaceMode,
} from '../types.js';

export interface VisualQAEvaluationOptions {
  compositeBuffer: Buffer;
  plan?: CompositionPlan;
  resolvedColors: ResolvedColorTokens;
  surfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  canvasWidth?: number;
  canvasHeight?: number;
  visionAuditor?: (jpegBuffer: Buffer) => Promise<{
    score: number;
    issues: string[];
    needsRegeneration?: boolean;
    suggestedSurfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  }>;
}

/**
 * Menghitung relative luminance (WCAG 2.1 formula)
 */
function calculateRelativeLuminance(r: number, g: number, b: number): number {
  const rsRGB = r / 255;
  const gsRGB = g / 255;
  const bsRGB = b / 255;

  const rLinear = rsRGB <= 0.03928 ? rsRGB / 12.92 : Math.pow((rsRGB + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
  const gLinear = gsRGB <= 0.03928 ? gsRGB / 12.92 : Math.pow((gsRGB + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
  const bLinear = bsRGB <= 0.03928 ? bsRGB / 12.92 : Math.pow((bsRGB + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);

  return 0.2126 * rLinear + 0.7152 * gLinear + 0.0722 * bLinear;
}

/**
 * Mengonversi hex color string ke RGB [r, g, b]
 */
function hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace('#', '');
  if (clean.length === 3) {
    return [
      parseInt(clean[0] + clean[0], 16),
      parseInt(clean[1] + clean[1], 16),
      parseInt(clean[2] + clean[2], 16),
    ];
  }
  return [
    parseInt(clean.slice(0, 2), 16) || 255,
    parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16) || 255,
    parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16) || 255,
  ];
}

/**
 * Helper untuk normalisasi ZoneRect ke koordinat piksel absolut
 */
function getRectBounds(rect: any, canvasWidth: number, canvasHeight: number) {
  const rawX = rect?.x ?? 0;
  const rawY = rect?.y ?? 0;
  const rawW = rect?.w ?? rect?.width ?? 0;
  const rawH = rect?.h ?? rect?.height ?? 0;

  const x = rawX <= 1.0 ? rawX * canvasWidth : rawX;
  const y = rawY <= 1.0 ? rawY * canvasHeight : rawY;
  const width = rawW <= 1.0 ? rawW * canvasWidth : rawW;
  const height = rawH <= 1.0 ? rawH * canvasHeight : rawH;

  return { x, y, width, height };
}

/**
 * PosterVisualQAGate: Evaluator kualitas visual deterministik dan vision auditor
 * untuk mendeteksi low-contrast, text-subject collision, dan margin overflow.
 */
export class PosterVisualQAGate {
  public static async evaluateQuality(
    options: VisualQAEvaluationOptions
  ): Promise<PosterQAReport> {
    const {
      compositeBuffer,
      plan,
      resolvedColors,
      surfaceMode = 'NONE',
      canvasWidth = 1080,
      canvasHeight = 1350,
      visionAuditor,
    } = options;

    const issues: string[] = [];
    let marginIntegrityScore = 1.0;
    let overflowDetected = false;
    let contrastRatio = 4.5;
    let subjectCollisionScore = 0.0;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // TAHAP 1: Evaluasi Deterministik Cepat (< 50ms)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    // 1. Pemeriksaan Integritas Margin & Overflow
    const zones = plan?.zones || [
      {
        role: 'PRIMARY_HEADLINE' as const,
        rect: { x: 54, y: 80, w: canvasWidth - 108, h: 260 },
      },
    ];

    let invalidZones = 0;
    for (const zone of zones) {
      const { x, y, width, height } = getRectBounds(zone.rect, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);

      // Cek overflow kanvas
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x + width > canvasWidth || y + height > canvasHeight) {
        overflowDetected = true;
        issues.push(`ZONE_CANVAS_OVERFLOW: ${zone.role} keluar batas kanvas`);
      }

      // Cek batas margin aman (min 20px)
      if (x < 20 || y < 20 || x + width > canvasWidth - 20 || y + height > canvasHeight - 20) {
        invalidZones++;
      }
    }

    if (zones.length > 0) {
      marginIntegrityScore = Math.max(0, 1.0 - (invalidZones / zones.length) * 0.5);
      if (marginIntegrityScore < 0.8) {
        issues.push('MARGIN_TOO_TIGHT: Elemen teks berada terlalu dekat ke tepi kanvas');
      }
    }

    // 2. Analisis Kontras Luminansi pada Zona Headline Utama
    const headlineZone =
      zones.find((z) => z.role === 'PRIMARY_HEADLINE') || zones[0];
    const headlineBounds = getRectBounds(headlineZone.rect, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);

    try {
      const extractLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(canvasWidth - 50, Math.floor(headlineBounds.x)));
      const extractTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(canvasHeight - 50, Math.floor(headlineBounds.y)));
      const extractWidth = Math.max(50, Math.min(canvasWidth - extractLeft, Math.floor(headlineBounds.width)));
      const extractHeight = Math.max(50, Math.min(canvasHeight - extractTop, Math.floor(headlineBounds.height)));

      const stats = await sharp(compositeBuffer)
        .extract({
          left: extractLeft,
          top: extractTop,
          width: extractWidth,
          height: extractHeight,
        })
        .stats();

      const bgR = stats.channels[0]?.mean ?? 128;
      const bgG = stats.channels[1]?.mean ?? 128;
      const bgB = stats.channels[2]?.mean ?? 128;

      const bgLum = calculateRelativeLuminance(bgR, bgG, bgB);

      const [tR, tG, tB] = hexToRgb(resolvedColors.textColor || (resolvedColors.isDark ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0F172A'));
      const textLum = calculateRelativeLuminance(tR, tG, tB);

      const maxLum = Math.max(bgLum, textLum);
      const minLum = Math.min(bgLum, textLum);
      contrastRatio = Number(((maxLum + 0.05) / (minLum + 0.05)).toFixed(2));

      // Jika kontras < 3.0:1 dan tidak menggunakan surface protektif
      if (contrastRatio < 3.0 && surfaceMode === 'NONE') {
        issues.push(`LOW_CONTRAST_HEADLINE: Rasio kontras teks hanya ${contrastRatio}:1 (min 3.0:1)`);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Fallback aman jika ekstraksi gagal
      contrastRatio = 4.5;
    }

    // 3. Deteksi Benturan Subjek Foto & Zona Teks (Geometric Intersection)
    if (plan?.subjectBoundingBox) {
      const sub = getRectBounds(plan.subjectBoundingBox, canvasWidth, canvasHeight);
      const hl = headlineBounds;

      const interX1 = Math.max(sub.x, hl.x);
      const interY1 = Math.max(sub.y, hl.y);
      const interX2 = Math.min(sub.x + sub.width, hl.x + hl.width);
      const interY2 = Math.min(sub.y + sub.height, hl.y + hl.height);

      if (interX2 > interX1 && interY2 > interY1) {
        const interArea = (interX2 - interX1) * (interY2 - interY1);
        const headlineArea = hl.width * hl.height;
        const overlapRatio = interArea / headlineArea;

        subjectCollisionScore = Number(overlapRatio.toFixed(2));

        if (overlapRatio > 0.65) {
          issues.push(`MAJOR_SUBJECT_OCCLUSION: Subjek menutupi ${(overlapRatio * 100).toFixed(0)}% area headline`);
        } else if (overlapRatio > 0.30 && surfaceMode === 'NONE') {
          issues.push(`TEXT_SUBJECT_OVERLAP: Teks bersinggungan ${(overlapRatio * 100).toFixed(0)}% dengan subjek tanpa scrim`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Hitung Skor Deterministik (0.0 - 1.0)
    let score = 1.0;
    if (contrastRatio < 3.0 && surfaceMode === 'NONE') score -= 0.35;
    if (subjectCollisionScore > 0.65) score -= 0.40;
    else if (subjectCollisionScore > 0.30 && surfaceMode === 'NONE') score -= 0.20;
    if (marginIntegrityScore < 0.8) score -= 0.20;
    if (overflowDetected) score -= 0.50;

    score = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, Number(score.toFixed(2))));
    let passed = score >= 0.70 && !overflowDetected;
    let recommendationAction: 'NONE' | 'ADJUST_SURFACE' | 'REGENERATE_IMAGE' = 'NONE';
    let suggestedSurfaceMode: SurfaceMode | undefined = undefined;
    let scrimOpacityBoost: number | undefined = undefined;
    let reason: string | undefined = undefined;

    if (subjectCollisionScore > 0.65) {
      recommendationAction = 'REGENERATE_IMAGE';
      reason = 'Subjek foto terlalu dominan dan memblokir zona judul secara masif';
    } else if (contrastRatio < 3.0 || (subjectCollisionScore > 0.25 && surfaceMode === 'NONE')) {
      recommendationAction = 'ADJUST_SURFACE';
      suggestedSurfaceMode = 'SCRIM_GRADIENT';
      scrimOpacityBoost = 0.25;
      reason = 'Tingkatkan keterbacaan teks dengan menerapkan scrim gradient tanpa regenerasi gambar';
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // TAHAP 2: Multimodal Vision Auditor (Opsional & Non-blocking)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    if (visionAuditor) {
      try {
        const thumbnail = await sharp(compositeBuffer)
          .resize({ width: 540, withoutEnlargement: true })
          .jpeg({ quality: 80 })
          .toBuffer();

        const visionResult = await visionAuditor(thumbnail);
        if (visionResult && typeof visionResult.score === 'number') {
          // Gabungkan hasil vision dengan deterministik
          score = Number(((score * 0.6) + (visionResult.score * 0.4)).toFixed(2));
          if (visionResult.issues && visionResult.issues.length > 0) {
            issues.push(...visionResult.issues);
          }
          if (visionResult.needsRegeneration && recommendationAction !== 'REGENERATE_IMAGE') {
            recommendationAction = 'REGENERATE_IMAGE';
            reason = visionResult.issues[0] || 'Vision audit mendeteksi benturan visual berat';
          } else if (visionResult.suggestedSurfaceMode && recommendationAction === 'NONE') {
            recommendationAction = 'ADJUST_SURFACE';
            suggestedSurfaceMode = visionResult.suggestedSurfaceMode;
          }
          passed = score >= 0.70 && recommendationAction !== 'REGENERATE_IMAGE';
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn(`[PosterVisualQAGate] Vision audit dilewati setelah gagal: ${err?.message}`);
        // Fallback anggun: gunakan hasil deterministik
      }
    }

    const metrics: PosterQAMetrics = {
      marginIntegrityScore,
      contrastRatio,
      overflowDetected,
      subjectCollisionScore,
    };

    const recommendation: PosterQARecommendation = {
      action: recommendationAction,
      suggestedSurfaceMode,
      scrimOpacityBoost,
      reason,
    };

    return {
      passed,
      score,
      stage: visionAuditor ? 'VISION_AUDIT' : 'DETERMINISTIC',
      issues,
      metrics,
      recommendation,
    };
  }
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/creative-poster/qa/revision-policy.ts (126 baris, 3.9 KB) ---
======================================================================
import {
  PosterQAReport,
  CompositionPlan,
  SurfaceMode,
  PosterHeaderPlacement,
  CreativePosterArchetype,
} from '../types.js';

export interface RevisionDecision {
  action: 'NONE' | 'RE_RENDER_SATORI' | 'REGENERATE_IMAGE';
  healed: boolean;
  reason: string;
  newSurfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  newHeaderPlacement?: PosterHeaderPlacement;
  scrimBoost?: number;
  alternativeArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
}

export interface EvaluateRevisionOptions {
  report: PosterQAReport;
  currentPlan?: CompositionPlan;
  currentSurfaceMode?: SurfaceMode;
  imageRegenerationCount?: number;
  maxImageRegenerations?: number;
}

/**
 * RevisionPolicy: Kebijakan koreksi mandiri (Self-Healing) berprinsip Zero-Waste.
 * Mengutamakan perbaikan deterministik Satori/CSS sebelum memutuskan regenerasi gambar AI.
 */
export class RevisionPolicy {
  public static evaluateDecision(options: EvaluateRevisionOptions): RevisionDecision {
    const {
      report,
      currentPlan,
      currentSurfaceMode = 'NONE',
      imageRegenerationCount = 0,
      maxImageRegenerations = 1,
    } = options;

    // 1. Jika poster lulus QA, tidak ada revisi yang diperlukan
    if (report.passed) {
      return {
        action: 'NONE',
        healed: false,
        reason: 'Poster memenuhi standar kualitas visual QA',
      };
    }

    const { action, suggestedSurfaceMode, reason } = report.recommendation;

    // 2. Jika benturan visual berat memerlukan regenerasi gambar AI
    if (action === 'REGENERATE_IMAGE') {
      // Pembatasan Keras: Maksimal 1 kali regenerasi gambar untuk mencegah loop tak terbatas
      if (imageRegenerationCount >= maxImageRegenerations) {
        console.warn(
          `[RevisionPolicy] Batas maksimal regenerasi gambar (${maxImageRegenerations}x) tercapai. Menerapkan fallback perlindungan solid band satori.`
        );
        return {
          action: 'RE_RENDER_SATORI',
          healed: true,
          newSurfaceMode: 'SOLID_BAND',
          newHeaderPlacement: currentPlan?.headerPlacement === 'top' ? 'bottom' : 'top',
          reason: 'Fallback darurat: Penerapan Solid Band setelah batas regenerasi AI tercapai',
        };
      }

      return {
        action: 'REGENERATE_IMAGE',
        healed: false,
        reason: reason || 'Benturan elemen visual berat menuntut komposisi gambar AI baru',
      };
    }

    // 3. Zero-Waste Healing: Koreksi deterministik via Satori (< 200ms) tanpa regenerasi gambar
    const targetSurface: SurfaceMode =
      suggestedSurfaceMode ||
      (currentSurfaceMode === 'NONE'
        ? 'SCRIM_GRADIENT'
        : currentSurfaceMode === 'HAIRLINE'
          ? 'SCRIM_GRADIENT'
          : 'SOLID_BAND');

    // Jika subjek foto berada di bagian atas dan menabrak headline, geser penempatan header
    let targetHeaderPlacement = currentPlan?.headerPlacement;
    if (
      report.metrics.subjectCollisionScore > 0.35 &&
      currentPlan?.subjectBoundingBox &&
      currentPlan.subjectBoundingBox.y < 0.4
    ) {
      targetHeaderPlacement = 'bottom';
    }

    return {
      action: 'RE_RENDER_SATORI',
      healed: true,
      newSurfaceMode: targetSurface,
      newHeaderPlacement: targetHeaderPlacement,
      scrimBoost: report.recommendation.scrimOpacityBoost || 0.25,
      reason:
        reason ||
        'Koreksi keterbacaan tipografi melalui penyesuaian deterministik Editorial Surface',
    };
  }

  /**
   * Helper untuk mengaplikasikan pembaruan SurfaceMode & HeaderPlacement ke CompositionPlan
   */
  public static applyHealingToPlan(
    plan: CompositionPlan,
    decision: RevisionDecision
  ): CompositionPlan {
    if (!decision.healed) return plan;

    return {
      ...plan,
      headerPlacement: decision.newHeaderPlacement || plan.headerPlacement,
      layoutVariant:
        decision.newHeaderPlacement === 'bottom'
          ? 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR'
          : decision.newHeaderPlacement === 'top'
            ? 'TOP_BANNER'
            : plan.layoutVariant,
    };
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/multi-region-cropper.ts (107 baris, 4.4 KB) ---

import sharp from 'sharp';

export interface CropRegionSpec {
  id: string;
  leftRatio: number;   // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari lebar gambar)
  topRatio: number;    // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari tinggi gambar)
  widthRatio: number;  // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  heightRatio: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  targetWidth?: number;
  targetHeight?: number;
  grayscale?: boolean;
}

/**
 * MultiRegionCropper
 * Utilitas pemotong gambar otomatis berbasis Sharp.
 * Mengimplementasikan "Single-Pass Multi-Crop Strategy" di mana 1 panggilan AI Image
 * (atau 1 aset kanvas luas) dipotong menjadi beberapa sub-zona visual secara lokal di server (Rp 0 & < 50ms),
 * tanpa perlu memanggil API DALL-E berkali-kali.
 * Dilengkapi "Variance-Aware Cropping (Anti-Langit Kosong)" untuk mendeteksi area polos/hampa.
 */
export class MultiRegionCropper {
  public static async extractSubRegions(
    imageBuffer: Buffer,
    regions: CropRegionSpec[],
    galleryFallback?: string[]
  ): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
    const results: Record<string, string> = {};

    if (!imageBuffer || imageBuffer.length === 0) {
      return results;
    }

    try {
      const meta = await sharp(imageBuffer).metadata();
      const imgWidth = meta.width || 1080;
      const imgHeight = meta.height || 1350;

      for (let i = 0; i < regions.length; i++) {
        const spec = regions[i]!;

        // 1. Jika tersedia foto dari galeri dewan untuk slot ini, utamakan foto galeri
        if (galleryFallback && galleryFallback[i] && galleryFallback[i]!.startsWith('http')) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(galleryFallback[i]!);
            if (resp.ok) {
              const galleryBuffer = Buffer.from(await resp.arrayBuffer());
              let pipeline = sharp(galleryBuffer);
              if (spec.targetWidth && spec.targetHeight) {
                pipeline = pipeline.resize(spec.targetWidth, spec.targetHeight, { fit: 'cover' });
              }
              if (spec.grayscale) {
                pipeline = pipeline.grayscale();
              }
              const buf = await pipeline.png().toBuffer();
              results[spec.id] = `data:image/png;base64,${buf.toString('base64')}`;
              continue;
            }
          } catch {
            // Fallback ke pemotongan kanvas AI jika fetch galeri gagal
          }
        }

        // 2. Hitung koordinat piksel awal (Clamped)
        let left = Math.max(0, Math.min(imgWidth - 10, Math.round(spec.leftRatio * imgWidth)));
        let top = Math.max(0, Math.min(imgHeight - 10, Math.round(spec.topRatio * imgHeight)));
        let width = Math.max(10, Math.min(imgWidth - left, Math.round(spec.widthRatio * imgWidth)));
        let height = Math.max(10, Math.min(imgHeight - top, Math.round(spec.heightRatio * imgHeight)));

        // 3. Variance-Aware Cropping (Anti-Langit Kosong)
        // Periksa statistik piksel potongan awal untuk mencegah thumbnail putih polos
        try {
          const rawExtracted = await sharp(imageBuffer).extract({ left, top, width, height }).toBuffer();
          const cropStats = await sharp(rawExtracted).stats();
          const numChannels = Math.min(3, cropStats.channels.length);
          const sumStdev = cropStats.channels.slice(0, numChannels).reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.stdev, 0);
          const avgStdev = numChannels > 0 ? sumStdev / numChannels : 0;

          // Deteksi Area Polos / Langit Kosong (stdev < 18)
          if (avgStdev < 18) {
            const fallbackTopRatio = Math.min(0.65, 0.38 + i * 0.08);
            top = Math.max(0, Math.min(imgHeight - 10, Math.round(fallbackTopRatio * imgHeight)));
            height = Math.max(10, Math.min(imgHeight - top, Math.round(spec.heightRatio * imgHeight)));
          }
        } catch {
          // Jika analisis stats gagal, lanjutkan dengan koordinat awal
        }

        let pipeline = sharp(imageBuffer).extract({ left, top, width, height });

        if (spec.targetWidth && spec.targetHeight) {
          pipeline = pipeline.resize(spec.targetWidth, spec.targetHeight, { fit: 'cover' });
        }
        if (spec.grayscale) {
          pipeline = pipeline.grayscale().linear(1.15, -10).sharpen();
        }

        const croppedBuffer = await pipeline.png().toBuffer();
        results[spec.id] = `data:image/png;base64,${croppedBuffer.toString('base64')}`;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[MultiRegionCropper] Failed to extract sub-regions:', err);
    }

    return results;
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/image-optimizer.ts (25 baris, 637 B) ---
======================================================================
import sharp from 'sharp';

export interface OptimizedImageResult {
  buffer: Buffer;
  mimeType: 'image/webp';
  sizeBytes: number;
}

export class ImageOptimizer {
  /**
   * optimizePosterBuffer mengompresi gambar DALL-E ke WebP
   * untuk memangkas ukuran hingga 70% tanpa kehilangan ketajaman teks/grafis.
   */
  public static async optimizePosterBuffer(inputBuffer: Buffer): Promise<OptimizedImageResult> {
    const optimized = await sharp(inputBuffer)
      .webp({ quality: 85, effort: 4 })
      .toBuffer();

    return {
      buffer: optimized,
      mimeType: 'image/webp',
      sizeBytes: optimized.length,
    };
  }
}

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/src/storage.adapter.ts (157 baris, 5.1 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { PutObjectCommand, DeleteObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { IStoragePort, UploadStreamOptions } from '@polaris/core-domain';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { r2Client, R2_CONFIG } from './r2.client.js';
import { ImageOptimizer } from './image-optimizer.js';

function saveBufferLocally(buffer: Buffer, relativePath: string): string {
  try {
    const cleanPath = relativePath.replace(/^\//, '');
    const possibleRoots = [
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'uploads'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../uploads'),
      '/home/pc/projects/polaris-platform/uploads',
    ];
    let written = false;
    for (const root of possibleRoots) {
      try {
        const fullPath = path.resolve(root, cleanPath);
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(fullPath), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(fullPath, buffer);
        written = true;
      } catch {
        // coba root berikutnya
      }
    }
    const publicHost = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL
      ? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL.replace(/\/api\/v1\/?$/, '')
      : 'http://localhost:4000';
    return `${publicHost}/uploads/${cleanPath}`;
  } catch (err) {
    return `data:image/webp;base64,${buffer.toString('base64')}`;
  }
}

function isDummyR2Account(): boolean {
  const accountId = process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID || '';
  return !accountId || accountId.includes('your-cf-') || accountId.includes('mock-');
}

export class CloudflareR2StorageAdapter implements IStoragePort {
  /**
   * uploadFromUrl menyedot gambar sementara dari OpenAI DALL-E 3,
   * mengompresnya ke WebP, dan menyimpannya secara permanen ke R2 atau penyimpanan lokal.
   */
  public async uploadFromUrl(
    sourceUrl: string,
    destinationPath: string
  ): Promise<{ publicUrl: string; sizeBytes: number }> {
    let rawBuffer: Buffer;
    if (sourceUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
      const base64Part = sourceUrl.split(',')[1] || '';
      rawBuffer = Buffer.from(base64Part, 'base64');
    } else {
      const response = await fetch(sourceUrl);
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`[StorageFetchError] Gagal mengunduh aset dari URL: ${response.statusText}`);
      }
      const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
      rawBuffer = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);
    }

    // 2. Kompresi otomatis menggunakan Sharp ke format WebP
    const optimized = await ImageOptimizer.optimizePosterBuffer(rawBuffer);

    // 3. Pastikan ekstensi file di path tujuan berakhiran .webp
    const finalPath = destinationPath.endsWith('.webp') 
      ? destinationPath 
      : `${destinationPath.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.webp`;
    const cleanFinalPath = finalPath.replace(/^\//, '');

    // 4. Unggah buffer teroptimasi ke Cloudflare R2 jika kredensial aktif
    if (!isDummyR2Account()) {
      try {
        const command = new PutObjectCommand({
          Bucket: R2_CONFIG.bucketName,
          Key: cleanFinalPath,
          Body: optimized.buffer,
          ContentType: optimized.mimeType,
          ContentLength: optimized.sizeBytes,
        });

        await r2Client.send(command);

        const cleanPublicBaseUrl = R2_CONFIG.publicBaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '');
        return {
          publicUrl: `${cleanPublicBaseUrl}/${cleanFinalPath}`,
          sizeBytes: optimized.sizeBytes,
        };
      } catch (r2Err) {
        console.warn(`[StorageAdapter] R2 uploadFromUrl gagal, beralih ke penyimpanan lokal:`, r2Err);
      }
    }

    // Fallback: simpan ke disk lokal
    const localUrl = saveBufferLocally(optimized.buffer, cleanFinalPath);
    return {
      publicUrl: localUrl,
      sizeBytes: optimized.sizeBytes,
    };
  }

  /**
   * uploadBuffer mengunggah file buffer generik ke Cloudflare R2 atau fallback lokal
   */
  public async uploadBuffer(
    buffer: Buffer,
    options: UploadStreamOptions
  ): Promise<{ publicUrl: string }> {
    const cleanFinalPath = options.destinationPath.replace(/^\//, '');

    if (!isDummyR2Account()) {
      try {
        const command = new PutObjectCommand({
          Bucket: R2_CONFIG.bucketName,
          Key: cleanFinalPath,
          Body: buffer,
          ContentType: options.contentType,
          ContentLength: options.contentLength || buffer.length,
        });

        await r2Client.send(command);

        const cleanPublicBaseUrl = R2_CONFIG.publicBaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '');
        return {
          publicUrl: `${cleanPublicBaseUrl}/${cleanFinalPath}`,
        };
      } catch (r2Err) {
        console.warn(`[StorageAdapter] R2 uploadBuffer gagal, beralih ke penyimpanan lokal:`, r2Err);
      }
    }

    // Fallback: simpan ke disk lokal
    const localUrl = saveBufferLocally(buffer, cleanFinalPath);
    return {
      publicUrl: localUrl,
    };
  }

  /**
   * deleteFile menghapus aset dari Cloudflare R2
   */
  public async deleteFile(destinationPath: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!isDummyR2Account()) {
      try {
        const command = new DeleteObjectCommand({
          Bucket: R2_CONFIG.bucketName,
          Key: destinationPath,
        });

        await r2Client.send(command);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn(`[StorageAdapter] Gagal menghapus file dari R2:`, err);
      }
    }
  }
}

--- BERKAS: apps/worker-crawler/src/processors/studio-generation.processor.ts (69 baris, 2.2 KB) ---

import { Job } from 'bullmq';
import { OpenAIAIEngineAdapter } from '@polaris/ai-engine';
import { CloudflareR2StorageAdapter } from '@polaris/storage';
import { StudioGenerationJobPayload } from '../queues/studio.queue.js';
import {
  StudioFullGenerationHandler,
  StudioPosterRegenerationHandler,
  StudioInfographicGenerationHandler,
  StudioContextHelper,
} from './studio/index.js';

/**
 * StudioGenerationProcessor (Dispatcher & Orchestrator)
 *
 * Menerapkan pola Orchestrator/Dispatcher modular untuk pemrosesan BullMQ:
 * - StudioFullGenerationHandler (Riset Otonom, Analisis Kebijakan Panjang, QC Sitasi, Poster)
 * - StudioPosterRegenerationHandler (Regenerasi Khusus Poster Visual)
 * - StudioInfographicGenerationHandler (Generasi Khusus Infografik Publik)
 */
export class StudioGenerationProcessor {
  private static readonly aiEngine = new OpenAIAIEngineAdapter();
  private static readonly storageAdapter = new CloudflareR2StorageAdapter();

  /**
   * Router utama untuk memproses pekerjaan generasi studio
   */
  public static async process(job: Job<StudioGenerationJobPayload>): Promise<void> {
    const data = job.data;

    if (data.taskType === 'REGENERATE_POSTER') {
      await StudioPosterRegenerationHandler.execute(
        job,
        this.aiEngine,
        this.storageAdapter
      );
      return;
    }

    if (data.taskType === 'INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY') {
      await StudioInfographicGenerationHandler.execute(
        job,
        this.aiEngine,
        this.storageAdapter
      );
      return;
    }

    await StudioFullGenerationHandler.execute(
      job,
      this.aiEngine,
      this.storageAdapter
    );
  }

  /**
   * Backward-compatible delegation helpers
   */
  public static async resolveTenantBrandColor(tenantId: string, partyAffiliation?: string | null) {
    return await StudioContextHelper.resolveTenantBrandColor(tenantId, partyAffiliation);
  }

  public static async emitProgress(...args: Parameters<typeof StudioContextHelper.emitProgress>) {
    return await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(...args);
  }

  public static async emitNotification(...args: Parameters<typeof StudioContextHelper.emitNotification>) {
    return await StudioContextHelper.emitNotification(...args);
  }
}
