--- BERKAS: apps/worker-crawler/src/processors/studio/handlers/studio-poster-regeneration.handler.ts (318 baris, 11.3 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { Job } from 'bullmq';
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  db,
  contentPublications,
  mediaAssets,
  tenantQuotaLedgers,
  tenantMembers,
  electoralDistricts,
  withTenantContext,
} from '@polaris/database';
import { OpenAIAIEngineAdapter, AiTelemetryService } from '@polaris/ai-engine';
import { CloudflareR2StorageAdapter, CreativePosterCompositor } from '@polaris/storage';
import { AssetType } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiCostCalculator } from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { StudioGenerationJobPayload } from '../../../queues/studio.queue.js';
import { StudioContextHelper } from '../studio-context.helper.js';

export class StudioPosterRegenerationHandler {
  public static async execute(
    job: Job<StudioGenerationJobPayload>,
    aiEngine: OpenAIAIEngineAdapter,
    storageAdapter: CloudflareR2StorageAdapter
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { tenantId, publicationId, jobId } = job.data;

    try {
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        20,
        'Memuat naskah tersimpan untuk membuat poster...'
      );

      const article = await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
        const [record] = await tx
          .select({
            title: contentPublications.title,
            content: contentPublications.bodyContentMarkdown,
            excerpt: contentPublications.excerpt,
          })
          .from(contentPublications)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(contentPublications.id, publicationId),
              eq(contentPublications.tenantId, tenantId)
            )
          )
          .limit(1);
        return record;
      });

      if (!article?.content.trim()) {
        throw new Error('Naskah artikel tidak tersedia untuk pembuatan poster.');
      }

      // 1. Ambil data profil dewan dan resolusi dapil
      const [member] = await db
        .select({
          fullName: tenantMembers.fullName,
          partyAffiliation: tenantMembers.partyAffiliation,
          dapilId: tenantMembers.electoralDistrictId,
          customDapil: tenantMembers.customDapilName,
          photoUrl: tenantMembers.photoUrl,
          commissionName: tenantMembers.commissionName,
        })
        .from(tenantMembers)
        .where(eq(tenantMembers.id, tenantId))
        .limit(1);

      let dapilName = member?.customDapil || 'Dapil';
      if (member?.dapilId) {
        const [dapil] = await db
          .select({ dapilName: electoralDistricts.dapilName })
          .from(electoralDistricts)
          .where(eq(electoralDistricts.id, member.dapilId))
          .limit(1);
        if (dapil && !member.customDapil) dapilName = dapil.dapilName;
      }

      // 2. Cek blueprint poster sebelumnya agar regenerasi menghasilkan arketipe/variasi layout yang segar
      let lastBlueprintId: string | undefined;
      const [existingAsset] = await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
        return await tx
          .select({ promptUsed: mediaAssets.promptUsed })
          .from(mediaAssets)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(mediaAssets.publicationId, publicationId),
              eq(mediaAssets.assetType, AssetType.DALLE_POSTER)
            )
          )
          .limit(1);
      });

      if (existingAsset?.promptUsed) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(existingAsset.promptUsed);
          lastBlueprintId = parsed.blueprintId || parsed.blueprint;
        } catch {}
      }

      // Keputusan Desain Mutlak: AI tidak merender wajah tokoh dan sistem tidak menaruh foto tokoh pada poster
      const subjectPhotoBuffer: Buffer | undefined = undefined;
      const compositionMode: 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY' = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY';

      const tenantBrandHex = await StudioContextHelper.resolveTenantBrandColor(
        tenantId,
        member?.partyAffiliation
      );

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        40,
        'Menghasilkan kanvas seni kolase baru...'
      );

      // 4. Generate Background Kolase Tanpa Teks via AI Engine
      const articleExcerpt = article.excerpt || '';
      const articleSnippet = (article.content || '').slice(0, 1200);

      const groundedContextSummary = [
        `JUDUL & RINGKASAN EKSEKUTIF ARTIKEL: ${article.title} — ${articleExcerpt}`,
        articleSnippet ? `CUPLIKAN ISI & DATA ARTIKEL:\n${articleSnippet}` : '',
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n')
        .trim();

      const backgroundResult = await aiEngine.generateCreativePosterBackground({
        topic: article.title,
        groundedContextSummary,
        compositionMode,
        forcedArchetype: (job.data.forcedArchetype as any) || undefined,
        excludeBlueprintId: lastBlueprintId,
        tenantBrandHex,
        tenantId,
      });

      // 5. Unduh buffer background sementara dari AI
      let backgroundBuffer: Buffer;
      if (backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
        const base64Part = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.split(',')[1] || '';
        backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(base64Part, 'base64');
      } else {
        const resp = await fetch(backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl);
        backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(await resp.arrayBuffer());
      }

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        75,
        'Merender tipografi vektor Satori & harmonisasi foto...'
      );

      // 6. Komposisi deterministik Satori + Sharp
      const headlineText = job.data.headline || article.title;
      const quoteText = article.excerpt?.slice(0, 160) || article.content.slice(0, 160);

      const composedPoster = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
        backgroundBuffer,
        subjectPhotoBuffer,
        headline: headlineText,
        quote: quoteText,
        memberName: member?.fullName || 'ANGGOTA DEWAN',
        memberTitle: `${member?.commissionName || 'KOMISI DPR RI'} // ${dapilName}`,
        archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
        compositionMode,
        synthesizedCopy: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy,
        primaryAccentColor:
          backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy?.primaryAccent ||
          backgroundResult.blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent ||
          tenantBrandHex,
        secondaryAccentColor:
          backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy?.secondaryAccent ||
          backgroundResult.blueprint.colorPalette.secondaryAccent,
        canvasWidth: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.width,
        canvasHeight: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.height,
        editionTag: `POLARIS // ${dapilName.toUpperCase()}`,
        outputFormat: 'webp',
        compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
        enableVisualQA: true,
        autoHealContrast: true,
      });

      // Catat metrik evaluasi visual poster ke telemetry
      if (composedPoster.qaReport) {
        AiTelemetryService.recordPosterQAMetrics({
          tenantId,
          archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
          score: composedPoster.qaReport.score,
          passed: composedPoster.qaReport.passed,
          issues: composedPoster.qaReport.issues,
          metrics: composedPoster.qaReport.metrics,
          recommendation: composedPoster.qaReport.recommendation,
        }).catch((telErr) =>
          console.warn('[StudioProcessor] QA telemetry write skipped:', telErr?.message)
        );
      }

      // 7. Unggah hasil komposit ke Cloudflare R2 / Storage
      let finalPublicUrl = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl;
      let finalSizeBytes = composedPoster.sizeBytes;
      const destPath = `posters/${tenantId}/${Date.now()}-${publicationId}.webp`;

      try {
        const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadBuffer(composedPoster.buffer, {
          destinationPath: destPath,
          contentType: 'image/webp',
          contentLength: composedPoster.sizeBytes,
        });
        finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
      } catch (storageErr) {
        console.warn(
          `[StudioProcessor] GCS/R2 buffer upload gagal, mencoba upload URL cadangan:`,
          storageErr
        );
        try {
          const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadFromUrl(
            backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl,
            destPath
          );
          finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
          finalSizeBytes = uploadResult.sizeBytes;
        } catch (fallbackErr) {
          console.warn(
            `[StudioProcessor] Upload URL cadangan juga gagal, beralih ke data base64:`,
            fallbackErr
          );
          finalPublicUrl = `data:image/webp;base64,${composedPoster.buffer.toString('base64')}`;
        }
      }

      await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
        await tx
          .delete(mediaAssets)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(mediaAssets.publicationId, publicationId),
              eq(mediaAssets.assetType, AssetType.DALLE_POSTER)
            )
          );

        await tx.insert(mediaAssets).values({
          publicationId,
          assetType: AssetType.DALLE_POSTER,
          r2StorageUrl: finalPublicUrl,
          cdnPublicUrl: finalPublicUrl,
          promptUsed: JSON.stringify({
            blueprintId: backgroundResult.blueprint.id,
            blueprint: backgroundResult.blueprint.name,
            archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
            compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
            compiledPrompt: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
            qaReport: composedPoster.qaReport,
          }),
          mimeType: 'image/webp',
          fileSizeBytes: finalSizeBytes,
        });

        const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
        await tx
          .insert(tenantQuotaLedgers)
          .values({
            tenantId,
            billingCycleMonth: currentMonth,
            articleLimit: 0,
            articleUsed: 0,
            dalleLimit: 0,
            dalleUsed: 1,
            totalTokensConsumed: 0,
            estimatedCostUsd: String(AiCostCalculator.DALLE3_COST_PER_IMAGE_USD),
          })
          .onConflictDoUpdate({
            target: [tenantQuotaLedgers.tenantId, tenantQuotaLedgers.billingCycleMonth],
            set: {
              dalleUsed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.dalleUsed} + 1`,
              estimatedCostUsd: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.estimatedCostUsd} + ${AiCostCalculator.DALLE3_COST_PER_IMAGE_USD}`,
              updatedAt: new Date(),
            },
          });
      });

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        'Poster berhasil dibuat ulang.',
        'COMPLETED',
        `/studio/artikel/${publicationId}`
      );
      await StudioContextHelper.emitNotification(
        tenantId,
        'Poster Berhasil Dibuat Ulang',
        `Poster untuk naskah "${article.title}" telah diperbarui.`
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        'Poster belum berhasil dibuat; naskah tetap aman.',
        'FAILED',
        undefined,
        message
      );
      await StudioContextHelper.emitNotification(
        tenantId,
        'Poster Belum Berhasil Dibuat',
        'Naskah tetap tersimpan. Pembuatan poster dapat dicoba kembali.'
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: apps/worker-crawler/src/processors/studio/handlers/studio-infographic-generation.handler.ts (377 baris, 13.8 KB) ---

import { Job } from 'bullmq';
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  db,
  contentPublications,
  mediaAssets,
  tenantQuotaLedgers,
  tenantMembers,
  electoralDistricts,
  mediaDiscourses,
  findSimilarKnowledgeChunks,
  withTenantContext,
} from '@polaris/database';
import { OpenAIAIEngineAdapter, AiTelemetryService } from '@polaris/ai-engine';
import { CloudflareR2StorageAdapter, CreativePosterCompositor } from '@polaris/storage';
import { ContentStatus, AssetType } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiCostCalculator } from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { StudioGenerationJobPayload } from '../../../queues/studio.queue.js';
import { StudioContextHelper } from '../studio-context.helper.js';

export class StudioInfographicGenerationHandler {
  public static async execute(
    job: Job<StudioGenerationJobPayload>,
    aiEngine: OpenAIAIEngineAdapter,
    storageAdapter: CloudflareR2StorageAdapter
  ): Promise<void> {
    const data = job.data;
    const { tenantId, publicationId, jobId } = data;

    console.log(
      `[StudioProcessor] Memulai pemrosesan INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY #${job.id} (PubID: ${publicationId})...`
    );

    try {
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        15,
        'Menganalisis konsep visual dan profil daerah...'
      );

      // 1. Ambil profil dewan & dapil
      const [member] = await db
        .select({
          fullName: tenantMembers.fullName,
          partyAffiliation: tenantMembers.partyAffiliation,
          dapilId: tenantMembers.electoralDistrictId,
          customDapil: tenantMembers.customDapilName,
          personalCoverage: tenantMembers.personalCoverage,
          photoUrl: tenantMembers.photoUrl,
          commissionName: tenantMembers.commissionName,
        })
        .from(tenantMembers)
        .where(eq(tenantMembers.id, tenantId))
        .limit(1);

      let dapilName = member?.customDapil || 'Dapil';
      let targetRegion = member?.personalCoverage?.[0] || 'NASIONAL';

      if (member?.dapilId) {
        const [dapil] = await db
          .select({
            dapilName: electoralDistricts.dapilName,
            regencyCoverage: electoralDistricts.regencyCoverage,
          })
          .from(electoralDistricts)
          .where(eq(electoralDistricts.id, member.dapilId))
          .limit(1);
        if (dapil) {
          if (!member.customDapil) dapilName = dapil.dapilName;
          if (!member.personalCoverage || member.personalCoverage.length === 0) {
            targetRegion = dapil.regencyCoverage?.[0] || 'NASIONAL';
          }
        }
      }

      // Bersihkan teks prompt dari constraint teknis dan nama tokoh agar AI TIDAK PERNAH menggambar manusia/wajah
      let cleanPrompt = (data.topic || '')
        .replace(/^Infografis Publik:\s*/i, '')
        .replace(/^Poster Publik:\s*/i, '')
        .replace(/\.\s*STRICT CONSTRAINT:.*$/i, '')
        .trim();

      if (member?.fullName) {
        const escapedName = member.fullName.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
        cleanPrompt = cleanPrompt.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${escapedName}\\b`, 'gi'), '');
      }
      cleanPrompt = cleanPrompt
        .replace(/menampilkan\s+[^,.]+(dengan|yang|untuk)/gi, '$1')
        .replace(/tokoh\s+[^,.]+/gi, '')
        .replace(/dengan latar belakang/gi, 'berlatar')
        .replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
        .trim();

      const headlineText =
        data.headline ||
        (cleanPrompt.length <= 80 ? cleanPrompt : cleanPrompt.slice(0, 77) + '...');
      const quoteText = `Transparansi Kebijakan dan Aspirasi Publik // ${dapilName.toUpperCase()}`;

      // Keputusan Desain Mutlak: AI tidak merender wajah tokoh dan sistem tidak menaruh foto tokoh pada poster
      const subjectPhotoBuffer: Buffer | undefined = undefined;
      const compositionMode: 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY' = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY';

      const tenantBrandHex = await StudioContextHelper.resolveTenantBrandColor(
        tenantId,
        member?.partyAffiliation
      );

      // Fast Grounding Lookup (RAG Perda & Diskursus Media Daerah)
      let groundedContextSummary = `Isu Utama: ${cleanPrompt}. Wilayah: ${dapilName} (${targetRegion}). Narasi: Edukasi publik, kebijakan solutif dan transparan.`;
      try {
        const ragSnippets: string[] = [];

        // 1. Vector Search Regulasi Daerah
        try {
          const embedRes = await aiEngine.generateEmbedding(cleanPrompt, tenantId);
          if (embedRes?.embedding && Array.isArray(embedRes.embedding) && embedRes.embedding.length === 1536) {
            const chunks = await findSimilarKnowledgeChunks(embedRes.embedding, 3, targetRegion, 0.55);
            if (chunks && chunks.length > 0) {
              ragSnippets.push(
                'KUTIPAN REGULASI & DATA DAERAH:\n' +
                  chunks
                    .map((c) => `- [${c.structuralReference || 'Regulasi'}]: ${c.chunkContent.slice(0, 300)}`)
                    .join('\n')
              );
            }
          }
        } catch (ragErr: any) {
          console.warn(`[StudioInfographic] Fast RAG lookup skipped: ${ragErr.message}`);
        }

        // 2. Berita Lokal Terbaru dari mediaDiscourses
        try {
          const recentNews = await db
            .select({
              title: mediaDiscourses.articleTitle,
              summary: mediaDiscourses.cleanSummary,
            })
            .from(mediaDiscourses)
            .where(
              targetRegion && targetRegion !== 'NASIONAL'
                ? eq(mediaDiscourses.regionScope, targetRegion)
                : sql`TRUE`
            )
            .orderBy(sql`${mediaDiscourses.publishedAt} DESC`)
            .limit(3);

          if (recentNews && recentNews.length > 0) {
            ragSnippets.push(
              'BERITA & DISKURSUS MEDIA LOKAL:\n' +
                recentNews
                  .map((n) => `- ${n.title}: ${n.summary.slice(0, 200)}`)
                  .join('\n')
            );
          }
        } catch (newsErr: any) {
          console.warn(`[StudioInfographic] Local news lookup skipped: ${newsErr.message}`);
        }

        if (ragSnippets.length > 0) {
          groundedContextSummary = [
            `TOPIK INFOGRAFIS: ${cleanPrompt}`,
            `WILAYAH DAPIL: ${dapilName} (${targetRegion})`,
            ...ragSnippets,
          ].join('\n\n');
        }
      } catch (lookupErr: any) {
        console.warn(`[StudioInfographic] Grounding context lookup error: ${lookupErr.message}`);
      }

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        35,
        'Merumuskan naskah editorial dan komposisi visual...'
      );

      // 3. Generate background visual tanpa teks
      const backgroundResult = await aiEngine.generateCreativePosterBackground({
        topic: cleanPrompt,
        groundedContextSummary,
        compositionMode,
        forcedArchetype: (data.forcedArchetype as any) || undefined,
        tenantBrandHex,
        tenantId,
      });

      // 4. Unduh background buffer
      let backgroundBuffer: Buffer;
      if (backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
        const base64Part = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.split(',')[1] || '';
        backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(base64Part, 'base64');
      } else {
        const resp = await fetch(backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl);
        backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(await resp.arrayBuffer());
      }

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        70,
        'Merender tipografi vektor Satori & grafis resolusi tinggi...'
      );

      // 5. Komposisi deterministik Satori
      const composedPoster = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
        backgroundBuffer,
        subjectPhotoBuffer,
        headline: headlineText,
        quote: quoteText,
        memberName: member?.fullName || 'ANGGOTA DEWAN',
        memberTitle: `${member?.commissionName || 'KOMISI DPR RI'} // ${dapilName}`,
        archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
        compositionMode,
        synthesizedCopy: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy,
        primaryAccentColor:
          backgroundResult.blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent || tenantBrandHex,
        canvasWidth: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.width,
        canvasHeight: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.height,
        editionTag: `POLARIS // ${dapilName.toUpperCase()}`,
        outputFormat: 'webp',
        compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
        enableVisualQA: true,
        autoHealContrast: true,
      });

      // Catat metrik evaluasi visual poster ke telemetry
      if (composedPoster.qaReport) {
        AiTelemetryService.recordPosterQAMetrics({
          tenantId,
          archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
          score: composedPoster.qaReport.score,
          passed: composedPoster.qaReport.passed,
          issues: composedPoster.qaReport.issues,
          metrics: composedPoster.qaReport.metrics,
          recommendation: composedPoster.qaReport.recommendation,
        }).catch((telErr) =>
          console.warn('[StudioProcessor] QA telemetry write skipped:', telErr?.message)
        );
      }

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(tenantId, jobId, 85, 'Menyimpan aset infografis HD...');

      // 6. Multi-tier upload
      let finalPublicUrl = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl;
      let finalSizeBytes = composedPoster.sizeBytes;
      const destPath = `posters/${tenantId}/${Date.now()}-${publicationId}.webp`;

      try {
        const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadBuffer(composedPoster.buffer, {
          destinationPath: destPath,
          contentType: 'image/webp',
          contentLength: composedPoster.sizeBytes,
        });
        finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
      } catch (storageErr) {
        console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Buffer upload gagal, beralih ke fallback:`, storageErr);
        try {
          const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadFromUrl(
            backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl,
            destPath
          );
          finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
          finalSizeBytes = uploadResult.sizeBytes;
        } catch {
          finalPublicUrl = `data:image/webp;base64,${composedPoster.buffer.toString('base64')}`;
        }
      }

      // 7. Simpan ke database
      await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
        await tx.insert(mediaAssets).values({
          publicationId,
          assetType: AssetType.DALLE_POSTER,
          r2StorageUrl: finalPublicUrl,
          cdnPublicUrl: finalPublicUrl,
          promptUsed: JSON.stringify({
            blueprintId: backgroundResult.blueprint.id,
            blueprint: backgroundResult.blueprint.name,
            archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
            compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
            compiledPrompt: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
            qaReport: composedPoster.qaReport,
          }),
          mimeType: 'image/webp',
          fileSizeBytes: finalSizeBytes,
        });

        await tx
          .update(contentPublications)
          .set({
            title: headlineText,
            excerpt: `Infografik Publik: ${headlineText}`,
            bodyContentMarkdown: `# ${headlineText}\n\n![${headlineText}](${finalPublicUrl})\n\n*Dipublikasikan melalui Studio Infografis Polaris Gov.*`,
            wordCount: 0,
            status: ContentStatus.DRAFT,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          })
          .where(eq(contentPublications.id, publicationId));

        const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
        await tx
          .insert(tenantQuotaLedgers)
          .values({
            tenantId,
            billingCycleMonth: currentMonth,
            articleLimit: 0,
            articleUsed: 0,
            dalleLimit: 0,
            dalleUsed: 1,
            totalTokensConsumed: 400,
            estimatedCostUsd: String(AiCostCalculator.DALLE3_COST_PER_IMAGE_USD),
          })
          .onConflictDoUpdate({
            target: [tenantQuotaLedgers.tenantId, tenantQuotaLedgers.billingCycleMonth],
            set: {
              dalleUsed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.dalleUsed} + 1`,
              totalTokensConsumed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.totalTokensConsumed} + 400`,
              estimatedCostUsd: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.estimatedCostUsd} + ${AiCostCalculator.DALLE3_COST_PER_IMAGE_USD}`,
              updatedAt: new Date(),
            },
          });
      });

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        'Infografis selesai dan siap digunakan!',
        'COMPLETED',
        finalPublicUrl,
        undefined,
        true,
        publicationId
      );

      await StudioContextHelper.emitNotification(
        tenantId,
        'Infografis Selesai!',
        `Infografis visual "${headlineText}" berhasil dibuat.`,
        '/studio/infografis'
      );

      console.log(`[StudioProcessor] Tugas INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY #${job.id} selesai 100% dengan sukses.`);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      console.error(`[StudioProcessor] Kegagalan INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY #${job.id}: ${message}`);

      await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) =>
        tx
          .update(contentPublications)
          .set({
            status: ContentStatus.DRAFT,
            excerpt: `[Pemrosesan Visual Gagal] ${message.slice(0, 450)}`,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          })
          .where(
            and(
              eq(contentPublications.id, publicationId),
              eq(contentPublications.status, ContentStatus.GENERATING)
            )
          )
      ).catch(() => null);

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        `Pemrosesan infografis gagal: ${message}`,
        'FAILED',
        undefined,
        message
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

--- BERKAS: apps/worker-crawler/src/processors/studio/handlers/studio-full-generation.handler.ts (810 baris, 32.0 KB) ---

import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import * as path from 'path';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import { Job } from 'bullmq';
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  db,
  contentPublications,
  mediaAssets,
  socialSyndicationPacks,
  tenantQuotaLedgers,
  tenantMembers,
  electoralDistricts,
  findSimilarKnowledgeChunks,
  withTenantContext,
  researchJobs,
  researchOutputs,
  datahubFiles,
  datahubDocumentRefs,
  datahubDocumentChunks,
} from '@polaris/database';
import {
  OpenAIAIEngineAdapter,
  ContextCapsuleBuilder,
  AutonomousResearchOrchestrator,
  DeterministicQualityGates,
  AiTelemetryService,
  DocumentExtractorService,
  ContextualChunkerService,
  DatahubEmbedderService,
} from '@polaris/ai-engine';
import { CloudflareR2StorageAdapter, CreativePosterCompositor } from '@polaris/storage';
import { ContentStatus, AssetType, ResearchJobStatus } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiCostCalculator } from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { StudioGenerationJobPayload } from '../../../queues/studio.queue.js';
import { StudioContextHelper } from '../studio-context.helper.js';

function cosineSimilarity(vecA: number[], vecB: number[]): number {
  if (!vecA || !vecB || vecA.length !== vecB.length) return 0;
  let dotProduct = 0;
  let normA = 0;
  let normB = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < vecA.length; i++) {
    dotProduct += vecA[i] * vecB[i];
    normA += vecA[i] * vecA[i];
    normB += vecB[i] * vecB[i];
  }
  if (normA === 0 || normB === 0) return 0;
  return dotProduct / (Math.sqrt(normA) * Math.sqrt(normB));
}

export class StudioFullGenerationHandler {
  public static async execute(
    job: Job<StudioGenerationJobPayload>,
    aiEngine: OpenAIAIEngineAdapter,
    storageAdapter: CloudflareR2StorageAdapter
  ): Promise<void> {
    const data = job.data;
    const { tenantId, publicationId, jobId } = data;

    console.log(
      `[StudioProcessor] Memulai pemrosesan tugas asinkron #${job.id} (PubID: ${publicationId})...`
    );

    try {
      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      // LANGKAH 1 (20%): Resolusi Wilayah & Ekstraksi Dokumen
      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        20,
        'Menyusuri Konteks Regulasi & Analisis Dokumen Lampiran...'
      );

      const [member] = await db
        .select({
          fullName: tenantMembers.fullName,
          partyAffiliation: tenantMembers.partyAffiliation,
          dapilId: tenantMembers.electoralDistrictId,
          customDapil: tenantMembers.customDapilName,
          personalCoverage: tenantMembers.personalCoverage,
          photoUrl: tenantMembers.photoUrl,
          commissionName: tenantMembers.commissionName,
          legislativeLevel: tenantMembers.legislativeLevel,
        })
        .from(tenantMembers)
        .where(eq(tenantMembers.id, tenantId))
        .limit(1);

      let targetRegion = member?.personalCoverage?.[0] || 'NASIONAL';
      let dapilName = member?.customDapil || 'Dapil';

      if (member?.dapilId) {
        const [dapil] = await db
          .select()
          .from(electoralDistricts)
          .where(eq(electoralDistricts.id, member.dapilId))
          .limit(1);

        if (dapil) {
          if (!member.customDapil) dapilName = dapil.dapilName;
          if (!member.personalCoverage || member.personalCoverage.length === 0) {
            targetRegion = dapil.regencyCoverage?.[0] || 'NASIONAL';
          }
        }
      }

      // Vektor Grounding Otentik (RAG) untuk Topik
      let queryVector: number[] | null = null;
      try {
        const embedResult = await aiEngine.generateEmbedding(data.topic, tenantId);
        queryVector = embedResult.embedding;
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Query embedding gagal: ${err.message}`);
      }

      // =======================================================================
      // LANGKAH 1A: MULTIMODAL INGESTION, DEDUPLIKASI SHA-256 & RAG CHUNKING
      // Hak Milik Dokumen: Terikat strictly ke Tokoh Binaan aktif (tenantId)
      // =======================================================================
      const parsedDocuments: Array<{ name: string; type: string; extractedContent: string }> = [];

      if (data.attachments && data.attachments.length > 0) {
        for (const att of data.attachments.slice(0, 5)) {
          try {
            if (!att.base64) continue;
            const cleanBase64 = att.base64.replace(/^data:[^;]+;base64,/, '');
            const fileBuffer = Buffer.from(cleanBase64, 'base64');
            if (fileBuffer.length === 0) continue;

            const contentSha256 = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fileBuffer).digest('hex');
            const cleanName = att.name || 'lampiran_lapangan';
            const cleanMime = (att.type || 'application/octet-stream').toLowerCase();

            // 1. Cek Deduplikasi Fisik di DataHub
            const [existingFile] = await db
              .select({
                id: datahubFiles.id,
                contentSha256: datahubFiles.contentSha256,
                extractionStatus: datahubFiles.extractionStatus,
                extractedRawText: datahubFiles.extractedRawText,
              })
              .from(datahubFiles)
              .where(eq(datahubFiles.contentSha256, contentSha256))
              .limit(1);

            let targetFileId: string;
            let fileChunks: Array<{
              chunkIndex: number;
              sectionTitle: string | null;
              chunkContent: string;
              vectorEmbedding: number[];
            }> = [];

            if (existingFile && existingFile.extractionStatus === 'COMPLETED') {
              // DEDUPLIKASI HIT! File fisik, ekstraksi & embedding sudah ada.
              console.log(
                `[StudioProcessor] Dokumen '${cleanName}' terdeduplikasi via SHA-256 (${contentSha256}). Skip ekstraksi & embedding ulang.`
              );
              targetFileId = existingFile.id;

              // Ambil chunk dan vektor yang sudah tersimpan di pgvector
              const loadedChunks = await db
                .select({
                  chunkIndex: datahubDocumentChunks.chunkIndex,
                  sectionTitle: datahubDocumentChunks.sectionTitle,
                  chunkContent: datahubDocumentChunks.chunkContent,
                  vectorEmbedding: datahubDocumentChunks.vectorEmbedding,
                })
                .from(datahubDocumentChunks)
                .where(eq(datahubDocumentChunks.fileId, targetFileId));

              fileChunks = loadedChunks;
            } else {
              // BERKAS BARU: Ekstraksi Teks Multimodal (PDF, DOCX, Gemini Vision OCR, CSV/Plaintext)
              const extraction = await DocumentExtractorService.extractText(
                fileBuffer,
                cleanMime,
                cleanName
              );
              const extractedText = (extraction.text || '').trim();

              // Simpan fisik file ke upload dir lokal
              const uploadDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'uploads/datahub');
              if (!fs.existsSync(uploadDir)) {
                fs.mkdirSync(uploadDir, { recursive: true });
              }
              const ext = path.extname(cleanName) || '.bin';
              const localFilePath = path.join(uploadDir, `${contentSha256}${ext}`);
              if (!fs.existsSync(localFilePath)) {
                fs.writeFileSync(localFilePath, fileBuffer);
              }

              // Simpan record master file
              const [newMasterFile] = await db
                .insert(datahubFiles)
                .values({
                  contentSha256,
                  originalFileName: cleanName,
                  mimeType: cleanMime,
                  fileSizeBytes: fileBuffer.length,
                  storagePath: `/uploads/datahub/${contentSha256}${ext}`,
                  extractionStatus: 'COMPLETED',
                  totalPages: extraction.totalPages || 1,
                  extractedRawText: extractedText.slice(0, 100000),
                })
                .returning({ id: datahubFiles.id });

              targetFileId = newMasterFile.id;

              // PEMBEDAAN CHUNKING SISTEM:
              // - Dokumen pendek / Foto OCR (<= 800 kata): Unified Single Context Chunk
              // - Dokumen panjang (Perda, APBD, LKPJ > 800 kata): Contextual Sliding Window Chunking
              const wordCount = extractedText.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
              let generatedChunks: any[] = [];

              if (wordCount <= 800 || extraction.extractorUsed === 'gemini-vision-ocr') {
                generatedChunks = [
                  {
                    chunkIndex: 0,
                    sectionTitle:
                      extraction.extractorUsed === 'gemini-vision-ocr'
                        ? 'Transkrip & Scene Lapangan'
                        : 'Ringkasan Rilis/Memo',
                    chunkContent: extractedText,
                    fullChunkText: `[Dokumen: ${cleanName}]\n${extractedText}`,
                    tokenCount: Math.ceil(extractedText.length / 4),
                  },
                ];
              } else {
                generatedChunks = ContextualChunkerService.chunkDocument(extractedText, {
                  documentTitle: cleanName,
                  categoryTag: 'DOKUMEN_INTERNAL',
                  targetChunkWords: 380,
                  overlapWords: 70,
                });
              }

              // Batch Embeddings & Simpan ke pgvector
              if (generatedChunks.length > 0) {
                const chunkTexts = generatedChunks.map((c) => c.fullChunkText || c.chunkContent);
                const embeddings = await DatahubEmbedderService.generateBatchEmbeddings(chunkTexts);

                const chunkRows = generatedChunks.map((c, idx) => ({
                  fileId: targetFileId,
                  chunkIndex: c.chunkIndex,
                  sectionTitle: c.sectionTitle || `Bagian ${idx + 1}`,
                  chunkContent: c.chunkContent,
                  contextualHeader: c.contextualHeader || null,
                  tokenCount: c.tokenCount || Math.ceil(c.chunkContent.length / 4),
                  vectorEmbedding: embeddings[idx],
                }));

                for (let i = 0; i < chunkRows.length; i += 50) {
                  const slice = chunkRows.slice(i, i + 50);
                  await db.insert(datahubDocumentChunks).values(slice);
                }

                fileChunks = chunkRows;
              }
            }

            // 2. KEPEMILIKAN DOKUMEN: Daftarkan Referensi Multi-Tenant ke Tokoh Binaan (tenantId)
            const [existingRef] = await db
              .select({ id: datahubDocumentRefs.id })
              .from(datahubDocumentRefs)
              .where(
                and(
                  eq(datahubDocumentRefs.fileId, targetFileId),
                  eq(datahubDocumentRefs.uploadedByTenantId, tenantId)
                )
              )
              .limit(1);

            if (!existingRef) {
              await db.insert(datahubDocumentRefs).values({
                fileId: targetFileId,
                uploadedByTenantId: tenantId, // Hak milik mutlak tokoh dewan binaan yang aktif!
                customTitle: cleanName,
                dapilCode: member?.customDapil || null,
                partyAffiliation: member?.partyAffiliation || null,
                branchType: 'LEGISLATIF',
                legislativeLevel: (member?.legislativeLevel as any) || 'DPRD_PROVINSI',
                privacyScope: 'PRIVATE_TENANT',
                categoryTag: 'DOKUMEN_INTERNAL',
              });
            }

            // 3. RAG TOP-K RELEVANCE FILTERING: Ambil chunk terbaik yang relevan dengan topik artikel
            if (fileChunks.length > 0) {
              let selectedChunks = fileChunks;

              if (fileChunks.length > 3 && queryVector && queryVector.length > 0) {
                const scored = fileChunks.map((ch) => ({
                  chunk: ch,
                  score: cosineSimilarity(queryVector!, ch.vectorEmbedding),
                }));
                scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
                selectedChunks = scored.slice(0, 4).map((s) => s.chunk);
              }

              const formattedContent = selectedChunks
                .map(
                  (ch, idx) =>
                    `[Lampiran Dokumen: ${cleanName} - ${ch.sectionTitle || 'Bagian ' + (idx + 1)}]:\n${ch.chunkContent}`
                )
                .join('\n\n');

              parsedDocuments.push({
                name: cleanName,
                type: cleanMime,
                extractedContent: formattedContent,
              });
            }
          } catch (attErr: any) {
            console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Gagal memproses lampiran ${att.name}: ${attErr.message}`);
          }
        }
      }

      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      // LANGKAH 1B (20%-35%): Autonomous Research Pipeline (Planner, Multi-Source, Evidence Spans)
      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        20,
        'Menjalankan Riset Otonom & Pencarian Bukti Terverifikasi...'
      );

      let researchJobId: string | undefined;
      let groundedEvidenceText = '';
      let evidenceMap = new Map<string, string>();

      try {
        const researchResult = await AutonomousResearchOrchestrator.executeResearchPipeline({
          tenantId,
          topic: data.topic,
          userPrompt: data.prompt || data.topic,
          targetRegion,
          queryEmbedding: queryVector || undefined,
          attachedDocuments: parsedDocuments,
          providedUrls: data.externalUrls || data.referencedUrls || [],
          onProgress: async (step, pct) => {
            const scaledPct = Math.round(20 + (pct / 100) * 15);
            await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(tenantId, jobId, scaledPct, step);
          },
        });
        researchJobId = researchResult.jobId;
        groundedEvidenceText = researchResult.groundedContextText;
        evidenceMap = researchResult.evidenceIdMap;
      } catch (researchErr: any) {
        console.warn(
          `[StudioProcessor] Autonomous research error, fallback ke RAG dasar: ${researchErr.message}`
        );
      }

      const similarChunks = queryVector
        ? await findSimilarKnowledgeChunks(queryVector, 3, targetRegion, 0.6)
        : [];

      const baseContext = ContextCapsuleBuilder.assembleContext({
        regionName: `${dapilName} (${targetRegion})`,
        regulations: similarChunks.map((c) => ({
          reference: c.structuralReference,
          content: c.chunkContent,
        })),
        recentNews: [],
        comparisonRegionData: data.comparisonRegion
          ? `Perbandingan: ${data.comparisonRegion}`
          : undefined,
        attachedDocuments: parsedDocuments,
        framingStance: data.framingStance,
      });

      const contextPayload = groundedEvidenceText
        ? `${baseContext}\n\n${groundedEvidenceText}`
        : baseContext;

      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      // LANGKAH 2 (50%): Sintesis Naskah Kebijakan Panjang (GPT-4o)
      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        35,
        'Menyusun Naskah Kebijakan Modular Berantai...'
      );

      const generatedArticle = await aiEngine.generateArticle({
        topic: data.topic,
        regionalContextData: contextPayload,
        targetAudience: data.targetAudience,
        targetLength: data.targetLength,
        writingStyle: data.writingStyle,
        framingStance: data.framingStance,
        toneOverride: data.toneOverride,
        tenantId,
        onProgress: async (step, pct) => {
          await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(tenantId, jobId, pct, step);
        },
      });

      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      // LANGKAH 2B: Deterministic Quality Control Gate & BR-05 Self-Healing Gate
      // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
      const validEvidenceKeys = new Set(evidenceMap.keys());
      let qcResult = DeterministicQualityGates.verifyCitationQualityControl(
        generatedArticle.contentMarkdown,
        validEvidenceKeys
      );

      // BR-05: Self-Healing Anti-Rogue Citation Gate
      if (qcResult.rogueCitations && qcResult.rogueCitations.length > 0) {
        console.warn(
          `[StudioProcessor] Terdeteksi ${qcResult.rogueCitations.length} rogue citation fiktif: [${qcResult.rogueCitations.join(', ')}]. Menjalankan Self-Healing Citation Gate...`
        );
        let healedMarkdown = generatedArticle.contentMarkdown;
        for (const rogueId of qcResult.rogueCitations) {
          const escaped = rogueId.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
          const rogueRegex = new RegExp(`\\[(?:\\^|Bukti:?\\s*)?${escaped}\\]`, 'gi');
          healedMarkdown = healedMarkdown.replace(rogueRegex, '');
        }
        healedMarkdown = healedMarkdown.replace(/[ \t]{2,}([.,;])/g, '$1');
        generatedArticle.contentMarkdown = healedMarkdown;

        // Re-evaluasi QC setelah pembersihan deterministik
        qcResult = DeterministicQualityGates.verifyCitationQualityControl(
          generatedArticle.contentMarkdown,
          validEvidenceKeys
        );
      }

      if (researchJobId) {
        try {
          await db
            .update(researchJobs)
            .set({
              qcPassed: qcResult.qcPassed,
              status: ResearchJobStatus.COMPLETED,
              updatedAt: new Date(),
            })
            .where(eq(researchJobs.id, researchJobId));

          await db.insert(researchOutputs).values({
            researchJobId,
            title: generatedArticle.title,
            executiveSummary: generatedArticle.excerpt,
            markdownBody: generatedArticle.contentMarkdown,
            bibliographyJson: Array.from(evidenceMap.entries()).map(([token, spanId]) => ({
              citationToken: token,
              evidenceSpanId: spanId,
            })),
            qcReport: qcResult,
          });
        } catch (dbErr: any) {
          console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Gagal menyimpan research outputs: ${dbErr.message}`);
        }
      }

      let socialPack = {
        instagramCaption: '',
        twitterThreads: [] as string[],
        whatsappBroadcast: `*${generatedArticle.title}*\n\n${generatedArticle.excerpt}`,
      };
      try {
        const generatedSocialPack = await aiEngine.generateSocialSnippets(
          generatedArticle.contentMarkdown
        );
        socialPack = {
          instagramCaption: generatedSocialPack.instagramCaption || '',
          twitterThreads: generatedSocialPack.twitterThreads || [],
          whatsappBroadcast:
            generatedSocialPack.whatsappBroadcast || socialPack.whatsappBroadcast,
        };
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
        console.warn(
          `[StudioProcessor] Social pack gagal dibuat; memakai ringkasan artikel: ${message}`
        );
      }

      // Commit naskah artikel sebelum operasi gambar opsional
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        69,
        'Menyimpan naskah dan paket teks...'
      );
      const textCostUsd = AiCostCalculator.calculateCostUsd(generatedArticle.totalTokensUsed, 0);

      // Self-healing sanitizer untuk rogue citations (Defect 6)
      let finalMarkdown = generatedArticle.contentMarkdown;
      if (qcResult.rogueCitations && qcResult.rogueCitations.length > 0) {
        console.warn(
          `[StudioProcessor] Menghapus ${qcResult.rogueCitations.length} rogue citation dari naskah: ${qcResult.rogueCitations.join(', ')}`
        );
        for (const rogueId of qcResult.rogueCitations) {
          const rogueRegex = new RegExp(`\\[(?:\\^|Bukti:?\\s*)?${rogueId}\\]`, 'gi');
          finalMarkdown = finalMarkdown.replace(rogueRegex, '');
        }
      }

      await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
        await tx
          .update(contentPublications)
          .set({
            title: generatedArticle.title,
            excerpt: generatedArticle.excerpt,
            bodyContentMarkdown: finalMarkdown,
            wordCount: generatedArticle.wordCount,
            status: ContentStatus.DRAFT,
            researchJobId: researchJobId || null,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          })
          .where(eq(contentPublications.id, publicationId));

        const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);

        await tx
          .insert(tenantQuotaLedgers)
          .values({
            tenantId,
            billingCycleMonth: currentMonth,
            articleLimit: 0,
            articleUsed: 1,
            dalleLimit: 0,
            dalleUsed: 0,
            totalTokensConsumed: generatedArticle.totalTokensUsed,
            estimatedCostUsd: String(textCostUsd),
          })
          .onConflictDoUpdate({
            target: [tenantQuotaLedgers.tenantId, tenantQuotaLedgers.billingCycleMonth],
            set: {
              articleUsed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.articleUsed} + 1`,
              totalTokensConsumed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.totalTokensConsumed} + ${generatedArticle.totalTokensUsed}`,
              estimatedCostUsd: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.estimatedCostUsd} + ${textCostUsd}`,
              updatedAt: new Date(),
            },
          });

        await tx
          .insert(socialSyndicationPacks)
          .values({
            publicationId,
            instagramCaption: socialPack.instagramCaption,
            twitterThreads: socialPack.twitterThreads,
            whatsappBroadcastText: socialPack.whatsappBroadcast,
          })
          .onConflictDoUpdate({
            target: socialSyndicationPacks.publicationId,
            set: {
              instagramCaption: socialPack.instagramCaption,
              twitterThreads: socialPack.twitterThreads,
              whatsappBroadcastText: socialPack.whatsappBroadcast,
            },
          });
      });

      // PROGRESSIVE INSTANT DELIVERY: Naskah teks 100% siap di database!
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        70,
        'Naskah kebijakan telah tersimpan. Merender materi visual...',
        'RUNNING',
        undefined,
        undefined,
        true,
        publicationId
      );

      let posterSucceeded = !data.generateDallePoster;
      let posterFailure: string | undefined;
      if (data.generateDallePoster) {
        await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
          tenantId,
          jobId,
          85,
          'Merender poster visual berestetika tinggi; naskah telah tersimpan...'
        );
        try {
          const headlineText = data.headline || generatedArticle.title;
          const quoteText = generatedArticle.excerpt?.slice(0, 160) || '';

          // Keputusan Desain Mutlak: AI tidak merender wajah tokoh dan sistem tidak menaruh foto tokoh pada poster
          const subjectPhotoBuffer: Buffer | undefined = undefined;
          const compositionMode: 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY' = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY';

          const tenantBrandHex = await StudioContextHelper.resolveTenantBrandColor(
            tenantId,
            member?.partyAffiliation
          );

          // 2. Ekstrak latar belakang berseni dan fakta kontekstual terstruktur
          const cleanTopic = (generatedArticle.title || data.topic || '').trim();
          const articleExcerpt = generatedArticle.excerpt || '';
          const articleSnippet = (generatedArticle.contentMarkdown || '').slice(0, 1200);
          const evidenceSnippet = (groundedEvidenceText || '').slice(0, 1000);

          const cleanContextSummary = [
            `JUDUL & RINGKASAN EKSEKUTIF ARTIKEL: ${generatedArticle.title} — ${articleExcerpt}`,
            articleSnippet ? `CUPLIKAN ISI & DATA ARTIKEL:\n${articleSnippet}` : '',
            evidenceSnippet ? `BUKTI RISET TERVERIFIKASI:\n${evidenceSnippet}` : '',
          ]
            .filter(Boolean)
            .join('\n\n')
            .trim();

          const backgroundResult = await aiEngine.generateCreativePosterBackground({
            topic: cleanTopic,
            groundedContextSummary: cleanContextSummary,
            compositionMode,
            forcedArchetype: (data.forcedArchetype as any) || undefined,
            tenantBrandHex,
            tenantId,
          });

          // 3. Unduh buffer background sementara dari AI
          let backgroundBuffer: Buffer;
          if (backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
            const base64Part = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl.split(',')[1] || '';
            backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(base64Part, 'base64');
          } else {
            const resp = await fetch(backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl);
            backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(await resp.arrayBuffer());
          }

          // 4. Komposisi deterministik Satori
          const composedPoster = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
            backgroundBuffer,
            subjectPhotoBuffer,
            headline: headlineText,
            quote: quoteText,
            memberName: member?.fullName || 'ANGGOTA DEWAN',
            memberTitle: `${member?.commissionName || 'KOMISI DPR RI'} // ${dapilName}`,
            archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
            compositionMode,
            synthesizedCopy: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy,
            primaryAccentColor:
              backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy?.primaryAccent ||
              backgroundResult.blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent ||
              tenantBrandHex,
            secondaryAccentColor:
              backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.synthesizedCopy?.secondaryAccent ||
              backgroundResult.blueprint.colorPalette.secondaryAccent,
            canvasWidth: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.width,
            canvasHeight: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.canvasDimensions.height,
            editionTag: `POLARIS // ${dapilName.toUpperCase()}`,
            outputFormat: 'webp',
            compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
            enableVisualQA: true,
            autoHealContrast: true,
          });

          // Catat metrik evaluasi visual poster ke telemetry
          if (composedPoster.qaReport) {
            AiTelemetryService.recordPosterQAMetrics({
              tenantId,
              archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
              score: composedPoster.qaReport.score,
              passed: composedPoster.qaReport.passed,
              issues: composedPoster.qaReport.issues,
              metrics: composedPoster.qaReport.metrics,
              recommendation: composedPoster.qaReport.recommendation,
            }).catch((telErr) =>
              console.warn('[StudioProcessor] QA telemetry write skipped:', telErr?.message)
            );
          }

          // 5. Unggah hasil komposit ke Cloudflare R2
          let finalPublicUrl = backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl;
          let finalSizeBytes = composedPoster.sizeBytes;
          const destPath = `posters/${tenantId}/${Date.now()}-${publicationId}.webp`;

          try {
            const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadBuffer(composedPoster.buffer, {
              destinationPath: destPath,
              contentType: 'image/webp',
              contentLength: composedPoster.sizeBytes,
            });
            finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
          } catch (storageErr) {
            console.warn(
              `[StudioProcessor] GCS/R2 buffer upload gagal, mencoba upload URL cadangan:`,
              storageErr
            );
            try {
              const uploadResult = await storageAdapter.uploadFromUrl(
                backgroundResult.temporaryImageUrl,
                destPath
              );
              finalPublicUrl = uploadResult.publicUrl;
              finalSizeBytes = uploadResult.sizeBytes;
            } catch (fallbackErr) {
              console.warn(
                `[StudioProcessor] Upload URL cadangan juga gagal, beralih ke data base64:`,
                fallbackErr
              );
              finalPublicUrl = `data:image/webp;base64,${composedPoster.buffer.toString('base64')}`;
            }
          }

          await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) => {
            await tx
              .delete(mediaAssets)
              .where(
                and(
                  eq(mediaAssets.publicationId, publicationId),
                  eq(mediaAssets.assetType, AssetType.DALLE_POSTER)
                )
              );

            await tx.insert(mediaAssets).values({
              publicationId,
              assetType: AssetType.DALLE_POSTER,
              r2StorageUrl: finalPublicUrl,
              cdnPublicUrl: finalPublicUrl,
              promptUsed: JSON.stringify({
                blueprintId: backgroundResult.blueprint.id,
                blueprint: backgroundResult.blueprint.name,
                archetype: backgroundResult.blueprint.archetype,
                compositionPlan: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.compositionPlan,
                compiledPrompt: backgroundResult.compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
                qaReport: composedPoster.qaReport,
              }),
              mimeType: 'image/webp',
              fileSizeBytes: finalSizeBytes,
            });

            await tx
              .update(tenantQuotaLedgers)
              .set({
                dalleUsed: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.dalleUsed} + 1`,
                estimatedCostUsd: sql`${tenantQuotaLedgers.estimatedCostUsd} + ${AiCostCalculator.DALLE3_COST_PER_IMAGE_USD}`,
                updatedAt: new Date(),
              })
              .where(
                and(
                  eq(tenantQuotaLedgers.tenantId, tenantId),
                  eq(tenantQuotaLedgers.billingCycleMonth, new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7))
                )
              );
          });
          posterSucceeded = true;
        } catch (error: unknown) {
          posterSucceeded = false;
          posterFailure = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
          console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Poster gagal; naskah tetap tersimpan: ${posterFailure}`);
        }
      }

      const completionStep = posterSucceeded
        ? 'Selesai! Naskah dan materi yang tersedia siap ditinjau.'
        : 'Naskah artikel berhasil dibuat (Poster visual tertunda).';
      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        completionStep,
        'COMPLETED',
        `/studio/artikel`,
        posterFailure
      );

      await StudioContextHelper.emitNotification(
        tenantId,
        posterSucceeded ? 'Naskah Kebijakan Selesai!' : 'Naskah Tersimpan, Poster Tertunda',
        posterSucceeded
          ? `Naskah "${generatedArticle.title}" siap ditinjau di Studio Artikel.`
          : `Naskah "${generatedArticle.title}" berhasil dibuat dan tersimpan. Poster visual dapat dibuat ulang nanti.`
      );

      console.log(
        `[StudioProcessor] Tugas asinkron #${job.id} selesai 100% dengan sukses. Canonical Trace ID: ${generatedArticle.canonicalTraceId}`
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      console.error(`[StudioProcessor] Kegagalan langkah kritis job #${job.id}: ${message}`);

      await withTenantContext(tenantId, async (tx) =>
        tx
          .update(contentPublications)
          .set({
            status: ContentStatus.DRAFT,
            excerpt: `[Pemrosesan Tertunda] ${message.slice(0, 450)}`,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          })
          .where(
            and(
              eq(contentPublications.id, publicationId),
              eq(contentPublications.status, ContentStatus.GENERATING)
            )
          )
      ).catch(() => null);

      await StudioContextHelper.emitProgress(
        tenantId,
        jobId,
        100,
        `Pemrosesan tertunda: ${message}`,
        'FAILED',
        undefined,
        message
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: apps/worker-crawler/src/processors/studio/studio-context.helper.ts (95 baris, 2.8 KB) ---
======================================================================
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  db,
  masterPoliticalParties,
  portalThemeSettings,
  portalConfigs,
} from '@polaris/database';
import { redisConnection } from '../../config/redis.connection.js';

export class StudioContextHelper {
  /**
   * Mengambil warna identitas resmi partai politik atau kustomisasi tema portal tenant
   */
  public static async resolveTenantBrandColor(
    tenantId: string,
    partyAffiliation?: string | null
  ): Promise<string> {
    let resolvedBrandHex = '#9E0B0F';
    try {
      if (partyAffiliation) {
        const [party] = await db
          .select({ primaryColor: masterPoliticalParties.primaryColor })
          .from(masterPoliticalParties)
          .where(eq(masterPoliticalParties.code, partyAffiliation))
          .limit(1);
        if (party?.primaryColor) {
          resolvedBrandHex = party.primaryColor;
        }
      }
      const [theme] = await db
        .select({ primaryHexColor: portalThemeSettings.primaryHexColor })
        .from(portalThemeSettings)
        .innerJoin(portalConfigs, eq(portalThemeSettings.portalId, portalConfigs.id))
        .where(eq(portalConfigs.tenantId, tenantId))
        .limit(1);
      if (theme?.primaryHexColor) {
        resolvedBrandHex = theme.primaryHexColor;
      }
    } catch (colorErr) {
      console.warn(`[StudioProcessor] Gagal memuat warna tema tenant, fallback:`, colorErr);
    }
    return resolvedBrandHex;
  }

  public static async emitProgress(
    tenantId: string,
    jobId: string,
    percent: number,
    step: string,
    status: 'RUNNING' | 'COMPLETED' | 'FAILED' = 'RUNNING',
    resultUrl?: string,
    errorDetails?: string,
    textReady?: boolean,
    publicationId?: string
  ) {
    const payload = {
      jobId,
      percent,
      step,
      status,
      resultUrl,
      errorDetails,
      textReady: textReady ?? false,
      publicationId,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(payload);
    await Promise.all([
      redisConnection.publish(`realtime:progress:${tenantId}`, serialized).catch(() => null),
      redisConnection.set(`job:progress:${tenantId}:${jobId}`, serialized, 'EX', 3600).catch(() => null),
    ]);
  }

  public static async emitNotification(
    tenantId: string,
    title: string,
    message: string,
    link: string = '/studio/artikel'
  ) {
    const payload = {
      id: `notif_${Date.now()}`,
      title,
      message,
      category: 'ARTICLE',
      link,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(payload);
    await Promise.all([
      redisConnection.publish(`realtime:notifications:${tenantId}`, serialized).catch(() => null),
      redisConnection.lpush(`history:notifications:${tenantId}`, serialized).catch(() => null),
      redisConnection.ltrim(`history:notifications:${tenantId}`, 0, 19).catch(() => null),
    ]);
  }
}

import { Queue } from 'bullmq';
import { redisConnection } from '../config/redis.connection.js';

export const STUDIO_GENERATION_QUEUE_NAME = 'studio-content-generation-queue';

export interface StudioGenerationJobPayload {
  taskType?: 'GENERATE_ARTICLE' | 'FULL_CAMPAIGN' | 'INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY' | 'ARTICLE_ONLY' | 'REGENERATE_POSTER';
  jobId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  publicationId: string;
  topic: string;
  prompt?: string;
  headline?: string;
  forcedArchetype?: string;
  targetAudience?: string;
  toneOverride?: string;
  comparisonRegion?: string;
  framingStance?: string;
  generateDallePoster: boolean;
  style?: string;
  aspectRatio?: string;
  includeUserPhoto?: boolean;
  compositionMode?: 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY' | 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE';
  customPhotoUrl?: string;
  targetLength?: string;
  writingStyle?: string;
  externalUrls?: string[];
  referencedUrls?: string[];
  attachments?: Array<{
    name: string;
    type: string;
    base64: string;
  }>;
}

export const studioGenerationQueue = new Queue<StudioGenerationJobPayload>(
  STUDIO_GENERATION_QUEUE_NAME,
  {
    connection: redisConnection,
    defaultJobOptions: {
      attempts: 2,
      backoff: {
        type: 'exponential',
        delay: 5000,
      },
      removeOnComplete: { count: 100 },
      removeOnFail: { count: 200 },
    },
  }
);

import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PromptRefinerService } from '@polaris/ai-engine';
import { GenerateArticleRequestDto, UpdateDraftArticleDto, UploadArticleImageDto } from './dto/studio.dto.js';
import {
  StudioRadarService,
  StudioGeneratorService,
  StudioArticleService,
  StudioResearchService,
} from './services/index.js';

/**
 * StudioService (Facade Coordinator)
 *
 * Menerapkan pola Facade untuk mendelegasikan domain Studio ke sub-services:
 * - StudioRadarService (Radar Intelijen Daerah & Morning Briefing)
 * - StudioGeneratorService (Antrean BullMQ & Generasi Asinkron)
 * - StudioArticleService (Siklus Hidup Naskah, Publikasi Portal, Upload Foto)
 * - StudioResearchService (Audit Trail Riset, Sitasi, Sumber Terverifikasi)
 */
@Injectable()
export class StudioService {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(StudioService.name);

  constructor(
    private readonly radarService: StudioRadarService,
    private readonly generatorService: StudioGeneratorService,
    private readonly articleService: StudioArticleService,
    private readonly researchService: StudioResearchService,
  ) {}

  // --- Domain: Radar & Morning Briefing ---
  async getMorningBriefing(tenantId: string) {
    return await this.radarService.getMorningBriefing(tenantId);
  }

  async getTrendingDapilTopics(tenantId: string) {
    return await this.radarService.getTrendingDapilTopics(tenantId);
  }

  // --- Domain: Generasi Konten Asinkron & Antrean ---
  async generateNewContentPackage(tenantId: string, dto: GenerateArticleRequestDto) {
    return await this.generatorService.generateNewContentPackage(tenantId, dto);
  }

  async getJobStatus(jobId: string, tenantId: string) {
    return await this.generatorService.getJobStatus(jobId, tenantId);
  }

  async regenerateArticlePoster(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.generatorService.regenerateArticlePoster(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  // --- Domain: Manajemen Artikel & Siklus Publikasi ---
  async listArticles(tenantId: string) {
    return await this.articleService.listArticles(tenantId);
  }

  async getArticleDetail(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.articleService.getArticleDetail(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  async updateDraftArticle(tenantId: string, articleId: string, dto: UpdateDraftArticleDto) {
    return await this.articleService.updateDraftArticle(tenantId, articleId, dto);
  }

  async publishArticle(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.articleService.publishArticle(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  async unpublishArticle(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.articleService.unpublishArticle(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  async deleteArticle(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.articleService.deleteArticle(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  async uploadArticleImage(tenantId: string, dto: UploadArticleImageDto) {
    return await this.articleService.uploadArticleImage(tenantId, dto);
  }

  // --- Domain: Jejak Audit Riset & Grounding Evidence ---
  async getArticleResearchAuditTrail(tenantId: string, articleId: string) {
    return await this.researchService.getArticleResearchAuditTrail(tenantId, articleId);
  }

  async addManualResearchSource(
    tenantId: string,
    articleId: string,
    dto: { url: string; title: string; publisherName: string; notes?: string }
  ) {
    return await this.researchService.addManualResearchSource(tenantId, articleId, dto);
  }

  async deleteResearchSource(tenantId: string, articleId: string, sourceId: string) {
    return await this.researchService.deleteResearchSource(tenantId, articleId, sourceId);
  }

  // --- Domain: Parafrase & Penyempurnaan Prompt Cerdas (Ultra-Hemat) ---
  async refinePrompt(
    tenantId: string,
    dto: {
      prompt: string;
      style?: 'poster' | 'infographic' | 'article_agenda';
      dapilName?: string;
      memberName?: string;
      partyAffiliation?: string;
      commissionName?: string;
    }
  ) {
    return await PromptRefinerService.refinePrompt({
      rawPrompt: dto.prompt,
      style: dto.style,
      dapilName: dto.dapilName,
      memberName: dto.memberName,
      partyAffiliation: dto.partyAffiliation,
      commissionName: dto.commissionName,
    });
  }
}

import { IsString, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsBoolean } from 'class-validator';

export class GenerateArticleRequestDto {
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty({ message: 'Topik artikel kebijakan wajib diisi.' })
  topic!: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  targetAudience?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  toneOverride?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  comparisonRegion?: string; // Misal: Membandingkan Cirebon dengan Bandung

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  framingStance?: string; // Misal: 'Solutif & Anggaran' | 'Pengawasan & Kritis' | 'Humanis & Lapangan'

  @IsOptional()
  externalUrls?: string[]; // Kumpulan tautan web rujukan

  @IsOptional()
  attachments?: Array<{
    name: string;
    type: string;
    base64: string;
  }>;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  targetLength?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  writingStyle?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  style?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  aspectRatio?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsBoolean()
  generateDallePoster: boolean = true;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsBoolean()
  includeUserPhoto?: boolean;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  compositionMode?: 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY' | 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE';

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  customPhotoUrl?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  taskType?: 'FULL_CAMPAIGN' | 'INFOGRAPHIC_ONLY' | 'ARTICLE_ONLY' | 'REGENERATE_POSTER';
}

export class UpdateDraftArticleDto {
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  title?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  excerpt?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  bodyContentMarkdown?: string;
}

export class UploadArticleImageDto {
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty({ message: 'Data gambar wajib disertakan.' })
  base64!: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  filename?: string;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  caption?: string;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = 'polaris_infographic_sessions_v1';

// Sesi Awal Bawaan (Default Seed)
const SEED_SESSIONS: InfographicSession[] = [

  {
