'use client';

import { ExternalLink, LoaderCircle, RotateCw } from 'lucide-react';

interface ArticlePosterSidebarProps {
  posterUrl: string | null;
  title: string;
  isRegenerating: boolean;
  onRegenerate: () => void;
}

export function ArticlePosterSidebar({ posterUrl, title, isRegenerating, onRegenerate }: ArticlePosterSidebarProps) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E131F] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm space-y-3">
      <span className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-300">
        Poster Visual
      </span>
      {posterUrl ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src={posterUrl} alt={title} className="w-full object-cover max-h-[320px]" />
          <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 text-[10px]">Cloudflare R2 WebP</span>
            <a
              href={posterUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 font-bold hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]"
            >
              Buka Resolusi Penuh <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="h-40 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-200">
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-white">Poster belum tersedia</span>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400">Naskah tetap tersimpan dan dapat ditinjau.</span>
        </div>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onRegenerate}
        disabled={isRegenerating}
        className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition hover:border-blue-500 dark:hover:border-blue-400 hover:text-blue-700 dark:hover:text-blue-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/80 shadow-xs cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {isRegenerating ? (
          <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
        ) : (
          <RotateCw aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        )}
        <span>{isRegenerating ? 'Poster masuk antrean...' : 'Buat ulang poster'}</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}


======================================================================
--- BERKAS: apps/web-dashboard/src/components/profile/PersonalPhotoCatalog.tsx (600 baris, 24.6 KB) ---
======================================================================
'use client';

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import {
  Images,
  UploadCloud,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Trash2,
  Star,
  Eye,
  X,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Info,
  CheckCircle2,
  ArrowLeftRight,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PersonalPhotoCatalogProps {
  photoGallery: string[];
  setPhotoGallery: (photos: string[]) => void;
  photoUrl: string;
  setPhotoUrl: (url: string) => void;
  isReadOnly?: boolean;
}

const MAX_PHOTOS = 10;

/**
 * Kompres dan standarisasi gambar menjadi Base64 yang efisien
 */
async function processImageFile(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = reject;
      img.onload = () => {
        const MAX_DIM = 1600;
        let { width, height } = img;
        if (width > MAX_DIM || height > MAX_DIM) {
          if (width > height) {
            height = Math.round((height * MAX_DIM) / width);
            width = MAX_DIM;
          } else {
            width = Math.round((width * MAX_DIM) / height);
            height = MAX_DIM;
          }
        }
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          resolve(e.target?.result as string);
          return;
        }
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
        // Pertahankan format PNG jika file adalah PNG transparan
        const format = file.type === 'image/png' ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg';
        const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL(format, 0.88);
        resolve(dataUrl);
      };
      img.src = e.target?.result as string;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

export function PersonalPhotoCatalog({
  photoGallery,
  setPhotoGallery,
  photoUrl,
  setPhotoUrl,
  isReadOnly = false,
}: PersonalPhotoCatalogProps) {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [showUrlInput, setShowUrlInput] = useState(false);
  const [urlInput, setUrlInput] = useState('');
  const [urlError, setUrlError] = useState('');
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);

  // Lightbox Preview State
  const [lightboxIndex, setLightboxIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const currentCount = photoGallery.length;
  const remainingSlots = MAX_PHOTOS - currentCount;
  const isMaxReached = currentCount >= MAX_PHOTOS;

  // Keyboard navigation for Lightbox
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lightboxIndex === null) return;

    function handleKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setLightboxIndex(null);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        setLightboxIndex((prev) => (prev !== null && prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : photoGallery.length - 1));
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        setLightboxIndex((prev) => (prev !== null && prev < photoGallery.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : 0));
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [lightboxIndex, photoGallery.length]);

  async function handleFilesSelected(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const files = e.target.files;
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    if (isMaxReached) {
      alert(`Batas maksimal ${MAX_PHOTOS} foto telah tercapai. Hapus salah satu foto untuk menambah foto baru.`);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
      return;
    }

    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const filesToProcess = Array.from(files).slice(0, remainingSlots);
      if (files.length > remainingSlots) {
        alert(`Hanya ${remainingSlots} foto yang dapat ditambahkan karena batas maksimal katalog adalah ${MAX_PHOTOS} foto.`);
      }

      const processedUrls: string[] = [];
      for (const file of filesToProcess) {
        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) continue;
        const dataUrl = await processImageFile(file);
        processedUrls.push(dataUrl);
      }

      if (processedUrls.length > 0) {
        const nextGallery = [...photoGallery, ...processedUrls];
        setPhotoGallery(nextGallery);

        // Jika foto utama belum diisi, jadikan foto pertama yang diunggah sebagai foto utama
        if (!photoUrl && processedUrls[0]) {
          setPhotoUrl(processedUrls[0]);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Gagal memproses file foto:', err);
      alert('Terjadi kesalahan saat memproses foto. Silakan coba lagi.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  }

  function handleAddUrl() {
    setUrlError('');
    const clean = urlInput.trim();
    if (!clean) return;

    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://') && !clean.startsWith('data:image/')) {
      setUrlError('URL foto wajib diawali dengan https:// atau http://');
      return;
    }

    if (isMaxReached) {
      setUrlError(`Batas maksimal ${MAX_PHOTOS} foto telah tercapai.`);
      return;
    }

    const nextGallery = [...photoGallery, clean];
    setPhotoGallery(nextGallery);

    if (!photoUrl) {
      setPhotoUrl(clean);
    }

    setUrlInput('');
    setShowUrlInput(false);
  }

  function handleRemovePhoto(indexToRemove: number) {
    const photoToRemove = photoGallery[indexToRemove];
    const nextGallery = photoGallery.filter((_, idx) => idx !== indexToRemove);
    setPhotoGallery(nextGallery);

    // Jika foto yang dihapus adalah foto utama, ganti ke foto berikutnya atau kosongkan
    if (photoUrl === photoToRemove) {
      setPhotoUrl(nextGallery.length > 0 ? nextGallery[0] : '');
    }

    if (lightboxIndex !== null) {
      if (nextGallery.length === 0) {
        setLightboxIndex(null);
      } else if (lightboxIndex >= nextGallery.length) {
        setLightboxIndex(nextGallery.length - 1);
      }
    }
  }

  function handleSetAsPrimary(photo: string) {
    setPhotoUrl(photo);
  }

  function handleMovePhoto(fromIndex: number, toIndex: number) {
    if (toIndex < 0 || toIndex >= photoGallery.length) return;
    const newArr = [...photoGallery];
    const [moved] = newArr.splice(fromIndex, 1);
    newArr.splice(toIndex, 0, moved);
    setPhotoGallery(newArr);
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xs border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-xl">
      {/* HEADER SECTION */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pb-2.5">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/60 border border-blue-200/60 dark:border-blue-800/60 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">
              <Images className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>Katalog Foto Pribadi & Dokumentasi Dewan</span>
            </h3>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">
            Simpan portofolio visual kenegaraan, interaksi dapil, dan potret resmi (maksimal {MAX_PHOTOS} foto per anggota).
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* COUNTER BADGE */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <div
            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-black tracking-tight border flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-2xs ${
              isMaxReached
                ? 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800'
                : 'bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/40 text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-400 border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-800'
            }`}
          >
            <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>
              {currentCount} / {MAX_PHOTOS} Foto
            </span>
            {isMaxReached && <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase">(Penuh)</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CAPACITY PROGRESS BAR */}
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div
            className={`h-full transition-all duration-300 rounded-full ${
              isMaxReached ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-blue-600'
            }`}
            style={{ width: `${(currentCount / MAX_PHOTOS) * 100}%` }}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
          <span>Kapasitas penyimpanan katalog personal</span>
          <span>{remainingSlots > 0 ? `Tersisa ${remainingSlots} slot foto` : 'Kapasitas maksimal tercapai'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ACTION UPLOAD CONTROLS */}
      {!isReadOnly && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
          <input
            type="file"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            onChange={handleFilesSelected}
            accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
            multiple
            className="hidden"
            disabled={isMaxReached || isProcessing}
          />

          <Button
            type="button"
            size="sm"
            disabled={isMaxReached || isProcessing}
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs px-3 py-1.5 h-8 rounded-lg gap-1.5 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <UploadCloud className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{isProcessing ? 'Memproses Foto...' : 'Unggah Foto Baru'}</span>
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={isMaxReached || isProcessing}
            onClick={() => setShowUrlInput(!showUrlInput)}
            className="border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 h-8 rounded-lg gap-1 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <LinkIcon className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>Tambah via URL</span>
          </Button>

          <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1 ml-auto">
            <Info className="h-3 w-3 text-slate-400" />
            <span>Mendukung format PNG, JPG, dan WEBP</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* URL INPUT COLLAPSIBLE */}
      {showUrlInput && !isReadOnly && (
        <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-1.5 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
          <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 uppercase">
            Tempel Tautan URL Gambar Langsung
          </label>
          <div className="flex gap-1.5">
            <input
              type="url"
              value={urlInput}
              onChange={(e) => setUrlInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://example.com/foto-kunjungan-dewan.jpg"
              className="block flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 focus:border-blue-600 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleAddUrl}
              className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs px-3 h-7 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
            >
              Tambahkan
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowUrlInput(false);
                setUrlError('');
              }}
              className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 text-xs px-2 h-7"
            >
              Batal
            </Button>
          </div>
          {urlError && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-600 font-bold">{urlError}</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* PHOTO GRID GALLERY */}
      {photoGallery.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3 pt-1">
          {photoGallery.map((photo, index) => {
            const isPrimary = photo === photoUrl;

            return (
              <div
                key={`gallery-${index}`}
                className={`group relative rounded-xl border overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 shadow-2xs transition-all duration-200 hover:shadow-md ${
                  isPrimary
                    ? 'border-emerald-500 ring-2 ring-emerald-500/40'
                    : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-blue-400'
                }`}
              >
                {/* PHOTO THUMBNAIL */}
                <div
                  onClick={() => setLightboxIndex(index)}
                  className="aspect-[4/5] w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-900/10 dark:bg-black/30 cursor-pointer relative"
                >
                  {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                  <img
                    src={photo}
                    alt={`Foto Katalog ${index + 1}`}
                    className="h-full w-full object-cover object-center group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300"
                  />

                  {/* OVERLAY HOVER SHADOW */}
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/70 via-black/20 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center">
                    <div className="p-2 rounded-full bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-xs text-white">
                      <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* NUMBER BADGE */}
                <div className="absolute top-1.5 left-1.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs text-white text-[9px] font-bold">
                  #{index + 1}
                </div>

                {/* PRIMARY BADGE */}
                {isPrimary && (
                  <div className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[9px] font-extrabold shadow-xs flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                    <span>Foto Utama</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* ACTION BAR AT BOTTOM */}
                {!isReadOnly && (
                  <div className="p-1.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                    {/* SET PRIMARY BUTTON */}
                    {isPrimary ? (
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1 pl-1">
                        <Star className="h-3 w-3 fill-emerald-500 text-emerald-500" />
                        <span>Utama</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          handleSetAsPrimary(photo);
                        }}
                        title="Jadikan Foto Profil Utama"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-blue-600 dark:hover:text-blue-400 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      >
                        <Star className="h-3 w-3 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-amber-500" />
                        <span>Jadikan Utama</span>
                      </button>
                    )}

                    {/* ACTION CONTROLS */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                      {/* MOVE LEFT */}
                      {index > 0 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleMovePhoto(index, index - 1);
                          }}
                          title="Pindahkan ke kiri"
                          className="p-1 rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <ChevronLeft className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </button>
                      )}

                      {/* MOVE RIGHT */}
                      {index < photoGallery.length - 1 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleMovePhoto(index, index + 1);
                          }}
                          title="Pindahkan ke kanan"
                          className="p-1 rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <ChevronRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </button>
                      )}

                      {/* DELETE */}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          handleRemovePhoto(index);
                        }}
                        title="Hapus dari katalog"
                        className="p-1 rounded text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* ADD PHOTO PLACEHOLDER TILE */}
          {!isReadOnly && !isMaxReached && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="aspect-[4/5] rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-blue-500 dark:hover:border-blue-500 bg-slate-50/60 dark:bg-slate-800/40 hover:bg-blue-50/40 dark:hover:bg-blue-950/20 transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3 text-center cursor-pointer group"
            >
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 group-hover:text-blue-600 group-hover:border-blue-300 shadow-2xs group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <UploadCloud className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mt-2 group-hover:text-blue-600 dark:group-hover:text-blue-400">
                + Tambah Foto
              </span>
              <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                Slot #{currentCount + 1}
              </span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* EMPTY STATE */
        <div className="py-8 px-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-800/30 text-center space-y-2.5">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/60 border border-blue-200/60 dark:border-blue-800/60 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 mx-auto shadow-2xs">
            <Images className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              Katalog Foto Pribadi Masih Kosong
            </h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
              Anda dapat mengunggah hingga {MAX_PHOTOS} foto dokumentasi kunjungan dapil, rapat parlemen, seminar kenegaraan, maupun potret resmi dewan.
            </p>
          </div>
          {!isReadOnly && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold text-xs px-3.5 py-1.5 h-8 rounded-lg gap-1.5 cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
            >
              <UploadCloud className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>Unggah Foto Pertama Sekarang</span>
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* LIGHTBOX MODAL */}
      {lightboxIndex !== null && photoGallery[lightboxIndex] && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200"
          onClick={() => setLightboxIndex(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="relative max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] w-full flex flex-col items-center"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            {/* CLOSE BUTTON */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setLightboxIndex(null)}
              className="absolute -top-10 right-0 text-white/80 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-xs transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>

            {/* MAIN IMAGE */}
            <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-black shadow-2xl border border-white/10 max-h-[75vh] flex items-center justify-center">
              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
              <img
                src={photoGallery[lightboxIndex]}
                alt={`Foto ${lightboxIndex + 1}`}
                className="max-h-[75vh] w-auto max-w-full object-contain"
              />

              {/* PREV BUTTON */}
              {photoGallery.length > 1 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setLightboxIndex((prev) => (prev !== null && prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : photoGallery.length - 1))
                  }
                  className="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-2 rounded-full bg-black/60 hover:bg-black/90 text-white transition backdrop-blur-xs cursor-pointer shadow-lg"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
                </button>
              )}

              {/* NEXT BUTTON */}
              {photoGallery.length > 1 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setLightboxIndex((prev) => (prev !== null && prev < photoGallery.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : 0))
                  }
                  className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-2 rounded-full bg-black/60 hover:bg-black/90 text-white transition backdrop-blur-xs cursor-pointer shadow-lg"
                >
                  <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* LIGHTBOX FOOTER CONTROLS */}
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between w-full max-w-2xl px-2 text-white">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/90">
                  Foto {lightboxIndex + 1} dari {photoGallery.length}
                </span>
                {photoGallery[lightboxIndex] === photoUrl && (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[10px] font-black">
                    ★ Foto Profil Utama
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              {!isReadOnly && photoGallery[lightboxIndex] !== photoUrl && (
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => handleSetAsPrimary(photoGallery[lightboxIndex])}
                  className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 h-7 rounded-lg gap-1.5 cursor-pointer shadow-xs"
                >
                  <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>Jadikan Foto Utama</span>
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  primary: string;
  secondary: string;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MINI DASHBOARD PREVIEW
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

function MiniDashboardPreview({ primary, secondary }: { primary: string; secondary: string }) {
  const isLight = useMemo(() => {
    const hex = secondary.replace('#', '');
    const r = parseInt(hex.substring(0, 2), 16);
    const g = parseInt(hex.substring(2, 4), 16);
    const b = parseInt(hex.substring(4, 6), 16);
    return (r * 299 + g * 587 + b * 114) / 1000 > 140;
  }, [secondary]);

  const fg = isLight ? '#334155' : '#CBD5E1';
  const muted = isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#64748B';

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 shadow-lg bg-white" style={{ height: 180 }}>
      <div className="flex h-full">
        <div className="flex flex-col shrink-0" style={{ width: 120, backgroundColor: secondary }}>
          <div className="p-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-3">
              <div className="h-5 w-5 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-[8px] font-black" style={{ backgroundColor: primary, color: '#FFF' }}>P</div>
              <span className="text-[8px] font-black" style={{ color: fg }}>POLARIS</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              {['Ringkasan', 'Studio AI', 'Aspirasi', 'Tema', 'Paket'].map((name, i) => (
                <div key={name} className="flex items-center px-2 py-1 rounded text-[7px] font-semibold"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: i === 0 ? `${primary}22` : 'transparent', color: i === 0 ? primary : muted }}>
                  <span>{name}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0">
          <div className="h-6 bg-white border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between px-2.5">
            <span className="text-[7px] font-bold text-slate-700">Dashboard</span>
            <span className="text-[6px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: `${primary}15`, color: primary }}>● Aktif</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 p-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <div className="h-4 rounded bg-white border border-slate-100" />
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1">
                <div className="h-7 rounded bg-white border border-slate-100" />
                <div className="h-7 rounded bg-white border border-slate-100" />
                <div className="h-7 rounded bg-white border border-slate-100" />
              </div>
              <div className="h-10 rounded bg-white border border-slate-100" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PREVIEW MODAL
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

function ThemePreviewModal({
  isOpen, onClose, onApply, primary, secondary, applying,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean; onClose: () => void; onApply: () => void;
  primary: string; secondary: string; applying: boolean;
}) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="w-full max-w-2xl bg-white dark:bg-[#0E131F] rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div>
            <h4 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Eye className="h-4 w-4" style={{ color: primary }} />
              Pratinjau Tema Baru
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 mt-0.5">Pastikan tampilannya sesuai keinginan Anda sebelum diterapkan.</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 space-y-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-xl shadow-inner border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: primary }} />
              <div><span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase block">Aksen</span><span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Tombol & Highlight</span></div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-xl shadow-inner border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: secondary }} />
              <div><span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase block">Sidebar</span><span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Panel Navigasi</span></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <MiniDashboardPreview primary={primary} secondary={secondary} />

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
            <button className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-sm" style={{ backgroundColor: primary }}>✨ Tombol Utama</button>
            <div className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold" style={{ backgroundColor: `${primary}15`, color: primary }}>● Status Aktif</div>
            <div className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold border" style={{ borderColor: `${primary}30`, color: primary }}>Badge</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 p-5 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/50">
          <button onClick={onClose} className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer">Batal</button>
          <button
            onClick={onApply} disabled={applying}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md hover:opacity-90 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
            style={{ backgroundColor: primary }}
          >
            {applying ? (<><span className="h-4 w-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" />Menerapkan...</>)
              : (<><Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />Terapkan Tema Ini</>)}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MAIN COMPONENT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

export function DashboardThemeCustomizer() {
  const { colors, setColors } = useTheme();
  const [stagePrimary, setStagePrimary] = useState(colors.primary);
  const [stageSecondary, setStageSecondary] = useState(colors.secondary);
  const [showPreview, setShowPreview] = useState(false);
  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
  const [showCustomPicker, setShowCustomPicker] = useState(false);

  const hasChanges = stagePrimary.toLowerCase() !== colors.primary.toLowerCase() || stageSecondary.toLowerCase() !== colors.secondary.toLowerCase();

  function applyPreset(p: ColorPalette) {
    setStagePrimary(p.primary);
    setStageSecondary(p.secondary);
    setShowCustomPicker(false);
  }

  async function handleApply() {
    setApplying(true);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
    setColors({ primary: stagePrimary, secondary: stageSecondary });
    try {
      const { ApiClient } = await import('@/lib/api-client');
      await ApiClient.request<any>('/cms/theme', {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({ primaryHexColor: stagePrimary, secondaryHexColor: stageSecondary, fontFamily: 'Inter, sans-serif' }),
      });
    } catch {}
    setApplying(false);
    setShowPreview(false);
  }

  function isActive(p: ColorPalette) {
    return p.primary.toLowerCase() === stagePrimary.toLowerCase() && p.secondary.toLowerCase() === stageSecondary.toLowerCase();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="space-y-8">

        {/* ═══════ SECTION 1: LIVE PREVIEW + CURRENT STATUS ═══════ */}
        <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2 items-start">
          {/* Left: current theme info */}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                <Palette className="h-4 w-4" style={{ color: stagePrimary }} />
                Warna Tema Dashboard Anda
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
                Pilih dari palet di bawah, atau buat kombinasi warna sendiri.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Current color badges */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                <div className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg shadow-inner border border-black/10 shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: stagePrimary }} />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase block leading-none">Aksen</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Tombol & highlight</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                <div className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg shadow-inner border border-black/10 shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: stageSecondary }} />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase block leading-none">Sidebar</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Panel navigasi</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Action row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
              {hasChanges && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPreview(true)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md hover:opacity-90 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: stagePrimary }}
                  >
                    <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    Pratinjau & Terapkan
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setStagePrimary(colors.primary); setStageSecondary(colors.secondary); }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" />
                    Reset
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Mini preview */}
          <MiniDashboardPreview primary={stagePrimary} secondary={stageSecondary} />
        </div>

        {/* ═══════ SECTION 2: CURATED PALETTES ═══════ */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Paintbrush className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Palet Warna Siap Pakai
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-300 mt-0.5">Pilih tema instan — klik langsung terapkan ke preview.</p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 md:grid-cols-6 gap-2">
            {CURATED_PALETTES.map((p) => {
              const active = isActive(p);
              return (
                <button key={p.name} type="button" onClick={() => applyPreset(p)}
                  className={`group relative flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-center transition-all hover:shadow-md hover:scale-[1.03] cursor-pointer ${
                    active
                      ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50/50 dark:bg-blue-950/40 ring-2 ring-blue-500/20'
                      : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                    <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-l-lg shadow-sm border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: p.primary }} />
                    <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-r-lg shadow-sm border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: p.secondary }} />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 truncate w-full">{p.name}</span>
                  {active && (
                    <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs">
                      <Check className="h-2.5 w-2.5 text-white stroke-[3]" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ═══════ SECTION 3: PARTY PRESETS (DISEMBUNYIKAN SEMENTARA) ═══════ */}
        {/* Disembunyikan sementara atas arahan user
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wide">Preset Partai Politik</h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
            {PARTY_PRESETS.map((p) => {
              const active = isActive(p);
              return (
                <button key={p.name} type="button" onClick={() => applyPreset(p)}
                  className={`group relative flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all hover:shadow-md hover:scale-[1.02] cursor-pointer ${
                    active
                      ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50/50 dark:bg-blue-950/40 ring-2 ring-blue-500/20'
                      : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 shrink-0">
                    <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-l-lg shadow-sm border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: p.primary }} />
                    <div className="h-6 w-4 rounded-r-md shadow-sm border border-black/10" style={{ backgroundColor: p.secondary }} />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">{p.name}</span>
                  {active && (
                    <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs">
                      <Check className="h-2.5 w-2.5 text-white stroke-[3]" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
        */}

        {/* ═══════ SECTION 4: CUSTOM COLOR PICKER (Expandable) ═══════ */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowCustomPicker(!showCustomPicker)}
            className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed transition-all cursor-pointer ${
              showCustomPicker
                ? 'border-blue-400 bg-blue-50/30 dark:bg-blue-950/20'
                : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-400 via-violet-500 to-cyan-400 flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
                <Palette className="h-4 w-4 text-white" />
              </div>
              <div className="text-left">
                <span className="block text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Pilih Warna Sendiri</span>
                <span className="block text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300">Buka color picker untuk memilih warna aksen & sidebar sesuka hati Anda</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className={`h-5 w-5 rounded-full border-2 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-transform ${showCustomPicker ? 'rotate-45' : ''}`}>
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold">{showCustomPicker ? '×' : '+'}</span>
            </div>
          </button>

          {/* Expandable color picker panel */}
          {showCustomPicker && (
            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200">
              <ColorPicker
                label="🎨 Warna Aksen Utama"
                value={stagePrimary}
                onChange={setStagePrimary}
              />
              <ColorPicker
                label="🌙 Warna Sidebar / Panel"
                value={stageSecondary}
                onChange={setStageSecondary}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ═══════ BOTTOM ACTION (sticky feel) ═══════ */}
        {hasChanges && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-900 dark:text-white shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 duration-200">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <div className="h-5 w-5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/20" style={{ backgroundColor: stagePrimary }} />
                <div className="h-5 w-5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/20" style={{ backgroundColor: stageSecondary }} />
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white/80">Tema baru siap diterapkan</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { setStagePrimary(colors.primary); setStageSecondary(colors.secondary); setShowCustomPicker(false); }}
              className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-white/60 dark:hover:text-white dark:hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Batal
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPreview(true)}
              className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md hover:opacity-90 transition-all flex items-center gap-2"
              style={{ backgroundColor: stagePrimary }}
            >
              <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Pratinjau & Terapkan
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* PREVIEW MODAL */}
      <ThemePreviewModal
        isOpen={showPreview}
        onClose={() => setShowPreview(false)}
        onApply={handleApply}
        primary={stagePrimary}
        secondary={stageSecondary}
        applying={applying}
      />
    </>
  );
}

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { PosterBlueprintSelector, PosterPromptCompiler } from '../packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/index.js';
import { CreativePosterCompositor } from '../packages/storage/src/creative-poster-compositor.js';

async function runTest() {
  console.log('--- 1. Testing Constrained RNG Selector ---');
  const topic = 'Kritik tajam kenaikan harga kebutuhan pokok dan revitalisasi pasar tradisional';
  const selectedBlueprint = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({ topic });
  console.log(`Topic: "${topic}"`);
  console.log(`Selected Blueprint: ${selectedBlueprint.name} (${selectedBlueprint.id})`);
  console.log(`Archetype: ${selectedBlueprint.archetype}`);
  console.log(`Tags:`, selectedBlueprint.tags);

  console.log('\n--- 2. Testing Prompt Compiler ---');
  const compiledPrompt = PosterPromptCompiler.compileScenePrompt({
    topic,
    blueprint: selectedBlueprint,
    tenantBrandHex: selectedBlueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent,
  });
  console.log('Compiled Prompt snippet:');
  console.log(compiledPrompt.englishPrompt.slice(0, 300) + '...\n');

  console.log('--- 3. Testing Real Image Compositing (Sharp Engine) ---');
  // Gunakan gambar referensi asli atau buat kanvas realistis
  const refPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'uploads/references/poster', selectedBlueprint.referenceFile);
  let bgBuffer: Buffer;
  if (fs.existsSync(refPath)) {
    console.log(`Using base reference texture from: ${selectedBlueprint.referenceFile}`);
    bgBuffer = fs.readFileSync(refPath);
  } else {
    bgBuffer = await sharp({
      create: { width: 1080, height: 1350, channels: 3, background: { r: 35, g: 39, b: 47 } }
    }).jpeg().toBuffer();
  }

  // Buat mockup cutout tokoh dewan
  const subjectCutout = await sharp({
    create: {
      width: 450,
      height: 600,
      channels: 4,
      background: { r: 220, g: 38, b: 38, alpha: 0.85 }
    }
  }).png().toBuffer();

  const composedResult = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
    backgroundBuffer: bgBuffer,
    subjectPhotoBuffer: subjectCutout,
    headline: 'PASAR TRADISIONAL HARUS DILINDUNGI',
    quote: 'Pedagang kecil butuh kepastian pasokan dan perlindungan dari gempuran ritel modern.',
    memberName: 'H. AHMAD FAUZI, S.T.',
    memberTitle: 'Ketua Komisi VI DPR RI // Fraksi Rakyat',
    archetype: selectedBlueprint.archetype,
    primaryAccentColor: selectedBlueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent,
    canvasWidth: 1080,
    canvasHeight: 1350,
    editionTag: 'POLARIS SUARA RAKYAT // EDISI 2026',
    outputFormat: 'png',
  });

  const outDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'uploads');
  const outPath = path.join(outDir, 'sample_rendered_poster.png');
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, composedResult.buffer);
  console.log(`\nSUCCESS! Composed poster saved to: ${outPath}`);
  console.log(`Dimensions: ${composedResult.width}x${composedResult.height}px, Size: ${(composedResult.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
}

runTest().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Error running test:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';
import sharp from 'sharp';

// Load .env from root
dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(process.cwd(), '.env') });

import { OpenAIAIEngineAdapter } from '../packages/ai-engine/src/ai-engine.adapter.js';
import { CreativePosterCompositor } from '../packages/storage/src/creative-poster-compositor.js';

async function testAirBersih() {
  console.log('=== MEMULAI TEST GENERASI POSTER: KELANGKAAN AIR BERSIH ===');
  const aiEngine = new OpenAIAIEngineAdapter();
  const topic = 'Krisis dan kelangkaan air bersih yang melanda permukiman padat warga pinggiran kota';

  console.log(`\n[1/4] Memilih Blueprint & Menghasilkan Background AI Berseni (Clean Art)...`);
  console.log(`Topik: "${topic}"`);

  const bgResult = await aiEngine.generateCreativePosterBackground({
    topic,
    tenantBrandHex: '#0284C7', // Sky Blue Accent
  });

  console.log(`Blueprint Terpilih: ${bgResult.blueprint.name} (${bgResult.blueprint.archetype})`);
  console.log(`Temporary URL AI: ${bgResult.temporaryImageUrl.slice(0, 80)}...`);

  console.log(`\n[2/4] Mengunduh background AI baru...`);
  let backgroundBuffer: Buffer;
  if (bgResult.temporaryImageUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
    const base64 = bgResult.temporaryImageUrl.split(',')[1] || '';
    backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(base64, 'base64');
  } else {
    const res = await fetch(bgResult.temporaryImageUrl);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Gagal download image dari OpenAI: ${res.statusText}`);
    backgroundBuffer = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  }

  console.log(`\n[3/4] Menyiapkan foto dewan asli dari avatar-dewan-budi.jpg...`);
  const avatarPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'apps/web-dashboard/public/images/avatar-dewan-budi.jpg');
  let subjectPhotoBuffer: Buffer | undefined;
  if (fs.existsSync(avatarPath)) {
    // Berikan border radius halus / crop untuk simulasi potret dewan
    subjectPhotoBuffer = await sharp(avatarPath)
      .resize(480, 600, { fit: 'cover' })
      .png()
      .toBuffer();
    console.log(`Foto dewan berhasil dimuat dari: ${avatarPath}`);
  }

  console.log(`\n[4/4] Merakit 3-Layer Compositing (Latar AI Baru + Foto Asli + Tipografi)...`);
  const composed = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
    backgroundBuffer,
    subjectPhotoBuffer,
    headline: 'DARURAT AIR BERSIH DI PEMUKIMAN',
    quote: 'Akses air bersih adalah hak paling mendasar setiap warga yang wajib dijamin negara tanpa alasan birokrasi.',
    memberName: 'H. BUDI PRASETYO, S.T.',
    memberTitle: 'Komisi V DPR RI // Pengawasan Infrastruktur Air',
    archetype: bgResult.blueprint.archetype,
    primaryAccentColor: bgResult.blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent,
    canvasWidth: 1080,
    canvasHeight: 1350,
    editionTag: 'POLARIS SUARA RAKYAT // KRISIS AIR 2026',
    outputFormat: 'png',
  });

  const outPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'uploads/poster_kelangkaan_air_bersih.png');
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, composed.buffer);

  // Salin ke artifacts folder antigravity ide
  const artifactPath = '/mnt/c/Users/PC/.gemini/antigravity-ide/brain/20183ae9-093f-4958-af8d-9d39f692ed3c/poster_kelangkaan_air_bersih.png';
  try {
    fs.copyFileSync(outPath, artifactPath);
  } catch {}

  console.log(`\n=== SUKSES BESAR! ===`);
  console.log(`File tersimpan di: ${outPath}`);
  console.log(`Resolusi: ${composed.width}x${composed.height}px, Ukuran: ${(composed.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
}

testAirBersih().catch((err) => {
  console.error('\n[TEST FAILED]:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/test/creative-poster.spec.ts (196 baris, 8.5 KB) ---

import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  PosterBlueprintSelector,
  PosterPromptCompiler,
  POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG,
} from '../src/creative-poster/index.js';
import { openaiClient } from '../src/client.js';

describe('POLARIS Creative Poster Engine', () => {
  it('harus memuat katalog blueprint pre-analyzed lengkap dengan slot manifest', () => {
    expect(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.length).toBe(22);

    const streetTemplate = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.find((b) => b.archetype === 'STREET_JOURNALISM');
    expect(streetTemplate).toBeDefined();
    expect(streetTemplate?.slotManifest.slots.headlineBlock.fontFamily).toBe('Anton');
    expect(streetTemplate?.colorPalette.primaryAccent).toBe('#DC2626');

    const heroTemplate = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.find((b) => b.archetype === 'POP_OUT_HERO');
    expect(heroTemplate).toBeDefined();
    expect(heroTemplate?.slotManifest.slots.subjectPhoto.popOutEffect).toBe(true);
  });

  it('harus memilih blueprint secara acak (RNG) dan mematuhi excludeBlueprintId', () => {
    const picked1 = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({
      topic: 'Kritik kenaikan harga sembako di pasar tradisional pedagang kaki lima',
    });
    expect(picked1).toBeDefined();
    expect(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.map((b) => b.id)).toContain(picked1.id);

    // Roll kedua dengan exclude template sebelumnya harus menghasilkan template yang berbeda
    const picked2 = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({
      topic: 'Kritik kenaikan harga sembako di pasar tradisional pedagang kaki lima',
      excludeBlueprintId: picked1.id,
    });
    expect(picked2.id).not.toBe(picked1.id);
  });

  it('harus memilih blueprint POP_OUT_HERO jika dipaksa forcedArchetype', () => {
    const picked = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({
      topic: 'Topik umum apa saja',
      forcedArchetype: 'POP_OUT_HERO',
    });

    expect(picked.archetype).toBe('POP_OUT_HERO');
  });

  it('harus mengompilasi prompt dengan larangan keras teks dan instruksi zero-logo', async () => {
    const blueprint = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0];
    const compiled = await PosterPromptCompiler.compileScenePrompt({
      topic: 'Pembangunan infrastruktur jalan desa terpencil',
      blueprint,
      tenantBrandHex: '#EA580C',
    });

    expect(compiled.englishPrompt).toContain('DO NOT generate any text');
    expect(compiled.englishPrompt).toContain('DO NOT generate any logos');
    expect(compiled.englishPrompt).not.toMatch(/#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,6}\b/);
    expect(compiled.canvasDimensions.width).toBe(1080);
    expect(compiled.canvasDimensions.height).toBe(1350);
  }, 15000);

  it('harus mengonversi kode warna HEX ke nama warna alami tanpa mencetak #HEX', () => {
    expect(PosterPromptCompiler.hexToNaturalColorName('#DC2626')).toBe('deep crimson red');
    expect(PosterPromptCompiler.hexToNaturalColorName('#0F172A')).toBe('deep midnight navy');
    expect(PosterPromptCompiler.hexToNaturalColorName('#F8FAFC')).toBe('clean alabaster white');
    expect(PosterPromptCompiler.hexToNaturalColorName('#0A0A0A')).toBe('deep charcoal black');
    expect(PosterPromptCompiler.hexToNaturalColorName(undefined)).toBe('neutral tone');
  });

  it('harus membersihkan prompt dari istilah grafis dan kode HEX liar', () => {
    const dirtyPrompt =
      'The upper 45% of the canvas MUST be clean negative space for giant manifesto typography with accent (#DC2626) and editorial summary cards for newspaper headline.';
    const cleaned = PosterPromptCompiler.sanitizeImageGenerationPrompt(dirtyPrompt);

    expect(cleaned).not.toMatch(/#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,6}\b/);
    expect(cleaned).not.toMatch(/\btypography\b/i);
    expect(cleaned).not.toMatch(/\bcards\b/i);
    expect(cleaned).not.toMatch(/\bheadline\b/i);
    expect(cleaned).not.toMatch(/\bnewspaper\b/i);
  });

  it('harus memvalidasi Zero HEX Leak & Zero UI Trigger Leak untuk SELURUH 22 blueprint katalog', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(PosterPromptCompiler, 'synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals').mockImplementation(
      async (topic, blueprint) => ({
        layoutVariant: 'TOP_BANNER',
        headerPlacement: 'top',
        cardArrangement: 'stacked',
        colorMood: 'EMERALD',
        primaryAccent: blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent,
        secondaryAccent: blueprint.colorPalette.secondaryAccent,
        mainBannerTitle: 'JUDUL UJI COBA',
        blocks: {},
      })
    );

    for (const blueprint of POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG) {
      const compiled = await PosterPromptCompiler.compileScenePrompt({
        topic: 'Modernisasi sistem irigasi persawahan berkelanjutan di pedesaan',
        blueprint,
        tenantBrandHex: '#DC2626',
      });

      // Asersi 1 (Zero HEX Leak): Pastikan TIDAK mengandung regex kode HEX (#...)
      expect(compiled.englishPrompt).not.toMatch(/#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,6}\b/);

      // Asersi 2 (Zero UI/Text Trigger Leak):
      // Ambil deskripsi adegan di luar blok mandat [CRITICAL ZERO-HUMAN / ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-COORDINATE MANDATE]
      const mandateHeader = '[CRITICAL ZERO-HUMAN / ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-COORDINATE MANDATE]';
      const scenePart = compiled.englishPrompt.split(mandateHeader)[0] || '';

      const forbiddenKeywords = [
        'headline',
        'headlines',
        'subheading',
        'typography',
        'typographic',
        'masthead',
        'card',
        'cards',
        'newspaper',
        'poster',
        'callout',
      ];

      for (const word of forbiddenKeywords) {
        const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`, 'i');
        const match = scenePart.match(regex);
        if (match) {
          throw new Error(
            `Prompt Leakage terdeteksi pada blueprint "${blueprint.name}" (${blueprint.archetype})! Kata terlarang ditemukan: "${match[0]}" di dalam prompt:\n${scenePart}`
          );
        }
        expect(scenePart).not.toMatch(regex);
      }
    }
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  describe('Fase 2: Grounding Data Nyata & Word-Safe Micro-Copywriting', () => {
    it('harus mempertahankan kata "INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN" (27 karakter) secara utuh tanpa terpotong "BERKELANJUTA"', () => {
      const title = 'INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN';
      const truncated = PosterPromptCompiler.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(title, 34);
      expect(truncated).toBe('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN');
      expect(truncated.endsWith('BERKELANJUTA')).toBe(false);
      expect(truncated.endsWith('BERKELANJUTAN')).toBe(true);
      expect(truncated).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus memotong pada batas kata tanpa memotong tengah kata dan tanpa tanda ellipsis', () => {
      const longTitle = 'INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN MENUJU DAERAH MAJU DAN SEJAHTERA';
      const truncated = PosterPromptCompiler.wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(longTitle, 34);
      expect(truncated.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(34);
      expect(truncated).toBe('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN MENUJU');
      expect(truncated).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus mengeliminasi angka kembar template "120+", "28 Unit", "1.450" dari fallback statCircles', async () => {
      vi.spyOn(openaiClient.chat.completions, 'create').mockRejectedValueOnce(
        new Error('Network timeout to test fallback')
      );

      const blueprint = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0];
      const copy = await PosterPromptCompiler.synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals(
        'Pengawasan Distribusi Pupuk Subsidi Petani Desa',
        blueprint
      );

      expect(copy.statCircles).toBeDefined();
      expect(copy.statCircles?.length).toBe(3);

      const allValues = copy.statCircles?.map((s) => s.value) || [];
      const allTopLabels = copy.statCircles?.map((s) => s.topLabel) || [];

      // Dilarang memuat nilai template 120+, 28, 1.450
      expect(allValues).not.toContain('120+');
      expect(allValues).not.toContain('28 Unit');
      expect(allValues).not.toContain('28');
      expect(allValues).not.toContain('1.450');

      // TopLabel harus 1 kata pendek tanpa titik-titik (...)
      for (const label of allTopLabels) {
        expect(label).not.toContain('...');
        expect(label.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(11);
      }
    });

    it('harus memotong kalimat tuntas dengan smartSentenceClamp tanpa tanda titik-titik (...) menggantung', () => {
      const longText =
        'Kebijakan pengawasan distribusi pupuk subsidi bertujuan meningkatkan produktivitas hasil tani daerah konstituen secara berkesinambungan dan terpadu';
      const clamped = PosterPromptCompiler.smartSentenceClamp(longText, 85);
      expect(clamped.endsWith('.')).toBe(true);
      expect(clamped).not.toContain('...');
      expect(clamped.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(86);
    });
  });
});

--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/test/blueprint-repository.spec.ts (120 baris, 4.3 KB) ---

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { PosterBlueprintRepository } from '../src/creative-poster/blueprint-repository.js';
import { POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG } from '../src/creative-poster/catalog.js';

describe('PosterBlueprintRepository', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    PosterBlueprintRepository.clearCache();
  });

  it('harus jatuh ke fallback katalog statis jika koneksi database error atau belum termigrasi', async () => {
    // Mock DB yang melempar error koneksi
    const mockFaultyDb = {
      select: vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => {
        throw new Error('relation "poster_reference_blueprints" does not exist');
      }),
    } as any;

    const catalog = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog(mockFaultyDb);

    // Harus tetap berhasil mengembalikan katalog tanpa melempar exception!
    expect(catalog).toBeDefined();
    expect(catalog.length).toBe(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.length);
    expect(catalog.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(22);
    expect(catalog[0].id).toBe(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0].id);
  });

  it('harus mengembalikan katalog statis jika database mengembalikan 0 baris APPROVED', async () => {
    const mockEmptyDb = {
      select: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
        from: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
          where: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
        }),
      }),
    } as any;

    const catalog = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog(mockEmptyDb);

    expect(catalog).toBeDefined();
    expect(catalog.length).toBe(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.length);
  });

  it('harus menggunakan in-memory cache jika dipanggil berulang dalam 60 detik', async () => {
    const mockDb = {
      select: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
        from: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
          where: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
        }),
      }),
    } as any;

    const catalogFirst = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog(mockDb);
    const catalogSecond = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog(mockDb);

    expect(catalogFirst).toBe(catalogSecond); // Referensi objek identik dari cache
    expect(mockDb.select).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // DB hanya diquery 1 kali!
  });

  it('harus menggabungkan blueprint APPROVED dari database dengan katalog statis', async () => {
    const mockApprovedRow = {
      id: 'TPL-99-CUSTOM-COMMUNITY',
      name: 'Custom Community Advocate',
      referenceFile: 'custom-advocate.png',
      archetype: 'POP_OUT_HERO',
      description: 'Template kustom hasil vision ingestion',
      tags: ['custom', 'advocacy'],
      aspectRatio: '4:5',
      colorPalette: {
        primaryAccent: '#059669',
        secondaryAccent: '#A7F3D0',
        textColor: '#FFFFFF',
        backgroundBase: '#064E3B',
        surfaceMode: 'SOLID_BAND',
      },
      scenePromptHint: 'clean community meeting background',
      negativeSpaceZone: 'top negative space',
      slotManifest: POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0].slotManifest,
      fixedConstraints: POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0].fixedConstraints,
      variationRules: POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG[0].variationRules,
      status: 'APPROVED',
      version: 1,
    };

    const mockDbWithApproved = {
      select: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
        from: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
          where: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([mockApprovedRow]),
        }),
      }),
    } as any;

    const catalog = await PosterBlueprintRepository.getActiveCatalog(mockDbWithApproved);

    expect(catalog.length).toBe(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.length + 1);
    const customBlueprint = catalog.find((b) => b.id === 'TPL-99-CUSTOM-COMMUNITY');
    expect(customBlueprint).toBeDefined();
    expect(customBlueprint?.name).toBe('Custom Community Advocate');
  });

  it('harus menangani seeding katalog statis ke database secara idempotent', async () => {
    const mockInsert = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
      values: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
        onConflictDoNothing: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ rowCount: 22 }),
      }),
    });

    const mockDb = {
      select: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
        from: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({
          where: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
        }),
      }),
      insert: mockInsert,
    } as any;

    const result = await PosterBlueprintRepository.seedStaticCatalogToDb(mockDb);

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.seededCount).toBe(POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.length);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer } from '../src/creative-poster/reference-vision-analyzer.js';

describe('PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer', () => {
  it('harus memvalidasi dan men-sanitasi payload mentah menjadi PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint yang valid', () => {
    const rawAiOutput = {
      name: 'Modern Civic Action',
      archetype: 'STREET_JOURNALISM',
      description: 'Gaya foto aksi jurnalistik lapangan dengan tipografi tebal',
      tags: ['civic', 'street', 'fieldwork'],
      aspectRatio: '4:5',
      colorPalette: {
        primaryAccent: '#DC2626',
        secondaryAccent: '#FEF08A',
        backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
        textColor: '#FFFFFF',
        surfaceMode: 'SCRIM_GRADIENT',
      },
      scenePromptHint: 'documentary photo of city council member inspecting flood wall',
      negativeSpaceZone: 'clean space on upper 35% for text placement',
      slotManifest: {
        canvas: { width: 1080, height: 1350, aspectRatio: '4:5' },
        slots: {
          headlineBlock: { x: 0.05, y: 0.08, w: 0.90, fontSizePx: 92 },
        },
      },
      fixedConstraints: {
        primaryAccentLocked: true,
        baseBackgroundTone: 'DARK',
        requiredFontFamilies: {
          headline: 'Bebas Neue',
          body: 'Plus Jakarta Sans',
        },
        imageNegativeAllow: ['border'],
        toneSafety: 'SERIOUS_ONLY',
      },
      variationRules: {
        allowedLayoutVariants: ['TOP_BANNER'],
        allowedHeaderPlacements: ['top'],
        allowedCardArrangements: ['stacked'],
      },
    };

    const blueprint = (PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer as any).validateAndSanitizeBlueprint(
      rawAiOutput,
      'test-ref-01.png'
    );

    expect(blueprint).toBeDefined();
    expect(blueprint.id).toContain('TPL-23-');
    expect(blueprint.referenceFile).toBe('test-ref-01.png');
    expect(blueprint.archetype).toBe('STREET_JOURNALISM');
    expect(blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent).toBe('#DC2626');
    expect(blueprint.fixedConstraints.baseBackgroundTone).toBe('DARK');
    expect(blueprint.variationRules.allowedLayoutVariants).toContain('TOP_BANNER');
  });

  it('harus jatuh ke fallback default jika output AI tidak menyertakan arketipe valid atau palet warna rusak', () => {
    const invalidOutput = {
      name: 'Unrecognized Style',
      archetype: 'ALIEN_FUTURISTIC_CYBERPUNK_INVALID',
      // colorPalette hilang
    };

    const blueprint = (PosterReferenceVisionAnalyzer as any).validateAndSanitizeBlueprint(
      invalidOutput,
      'corrupt-image.jpg'
    );

    expect(blueprint).toBeDefined();
    // Arketipe wajib fallback ke POP_OUT_HERO
    expect(blueprint.archetype).toBe('POP_OUT_HERO');
    // Palet warna harus terisi dengan nilai default
    expect(blueprint.colorPalette).toBeDefined();
    expect(blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent).toBe('#EA580C');
    expect(blueprint.colorPalette.backgroundBase).toBe('#0F172A');
    // Scene prompt hint wajib memiliki default yang mencegah teks dalam gambar
    expect(blueprint.scenePromptHint).toContain('zero text');
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { CreativePosterCompositor } from '../src/creative-poster-compositor.js';

describe('CreativePosterCompositor', () => {
  it('harus merakit 3 layer poster dengan Sharp dan menghasilkan WebP 1080x1350', async () => {
    // 1. Buat dummy background AI buffer 1000x1000
    const dummyBackground = await sharp({
      create: {
        width: 1000,
        height: 1000,
        channels: 3,
        background: { r: 30, g: 41, b: 59 },
      },
    })
      .jpeg()
      .toBuffer();

    // 2. Buat dummy cutout foto dewan transparan 400x500
    const dummySubjectCutout = await sharp({
      create: {
        width: 400,
        height: 500,
        channels: 4,
        background: { r: 234, g: 88, b: 12, alpha: 0.9 },
      },
    })
      .png()
      .toBuffer();

    // 3. Eksekusi komposisi untuk arketipe STREET_JOURNALISM
    const resultStreet = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
      backgroundBuffer: dummyBackground,
      subjectPhotoBuffer: dummySubjectCutout,
      headline: 'HARGA PUPUK MENCEKIK PETANI DAERAH',
      quote: 'Pemerintah harus menjamin ketersediaan pasokan pupuk subsidi di tingkat pengecer.',
      memberName: 'H. AHMAD FAUZI, S.T.',
      memberTitle: 'Anggota Komisi IV DPR RI // Fraksi Golkar',
      archetype: 'STREET_JOURNALISM',
      primaryAccentColor: '#DC2626',
      canvasWidth: 1080,
      canvasHeight: 1350,
      outputFormat: 'webp',
    });

    expect(resultStreet.mimeType).toBe('image/webp');
    expect(resultStreet.width).toBe(1080);
    expect(resultStreet.height).toBe(1350);
    expect(resultStreet.sizeBytes).toBeGreaterThan(1000);

    // 4. Eksekusi komposisi untuk arketipe POP_OUT_HERO
    const resultHero = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
      backgroundBuffer: dummyBackground,

      headline: 'THE REFORM',

      archetype: 'POP_OUT_HERO',
      primaryAccentColor: '#EA580C',
      outputFormat: 'png',
    });

    expect(resultHero.mimeType).toBe('image/png');
    expect(resultHero.width).toBe(1080);
    expect(resultHero.height).toBe(1350);
    expect(resultHero.sizeBytes).toBeGreaterThan(1000);

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/test/parametric-renderer.spec.ts (401 baris, 14.4 KB) ---
