import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import {
  buildMastheadPrimitive,
  buildHeadlinePrimitive,
  buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive,
  buildStatBadgesPrimitive,
  buildPhotoFramePrimitive,
  buildMemberSignaturePrimitive,
} from '../src/creative-poster/primitives/index.js';
import { ParametricSatoriRenderer } from '../src/creative-poster/core/parametric-renderer.js';
import { MultiRegionCropper } from '../src/multi-region-cropper.js';
import { CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens } from '@polaris/shared-types';

describe('Parametric Satori Primitives & Renderer', () => {
  const dummyColors: ResolvedColorTokens = {
    primaryAccent: '#DC2626',
    secondaryAccent: '#FEF08A',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.75)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.3)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT',
    isDark: true,
  };

  describe('1. Masthead Primitive', () => {
    it('harus merender gaya BROADSHEET_CLASSIC dengan garis border ganda', () => {
      const vnode = buildMastheadPrimitive({
        style: 'BROADSHEET_CLASSIC',
        title: 'THE S NEWS',
        editionTag: 'SPECIAL EDITION',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(vnode).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.type).toBe('div');
      expect(vnode.props.style.flexDirection).toBe('column');
      expect(vnode.props.style.borderTop).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.props.style.borderBottom).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('harus merender gaya MODERN_MINIMAL secara ringkas', () => {
      const vnode = buildMastheadPrimitive({
        style: 'MODERN_MINIMAL',
        title: 'POLARIS DISPATCH',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(vnode).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.props.style.justifyContent).toBe('space-between');
    });

    it('harus merender gaya BOLD_STAMP dan VINTAGE_RIBBON', () => {
      const stamp = buildMastheadPrimitive({
        style: 'BOLD_STAMP',
        title: 'URGENT BULLETIN',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(stamp.props.style.border).toContain(dummyColors.primaryAccent);
      expect(stamp.props.style.boxShadow).toContain(dummyColors.primaryAccent);

      const ribbon = buildMastheadPrimitive({
        style: 'VINTAGE_RIBBON',
        title: 'GAZETTE REPORT',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(ribbon.props.style.borderBottom).toContain(dummyColors.primaryAccent);
    });
  });

  describe('2. Headline Primitive', () => {
    it('harus mematuhi EditorialSurface NONE tanpa kartu pembungkus buatan', () => {
      const vnode = buildHeadlinePrimitive({
        headline: 'REFORMASI ANGGARAN DAERAH',
        subHeader: 'Kawal transparansi hingga ke level desa',
        surface: 'NONE',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        maxFontSize: 80,
      });

      expect(vnode.type).toBe('div');
      expect(vnode.props.style.backgroundColor).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('harus menerapkan SCRIM_GRADIENT atau SOLID_BAND dengan benar', () => {
      const scrim = buildHeadlinePrimitive({
        headline: 'PERINGATAN DINI BENCANA',
        surface: 'SCRIM_GRADIENT',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(scrim.props.style.background).toContain('linear-gradient');

      const band = buildHeadlinePrimitive({
        headline: 'SIARAN PERS RESMI',
        surface: 'SOLID_BAND',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(band.props.style.background).toBeDefined();
      expect(band.props.style.borderTop).toContain(dummyColors.primaryAccent);
    });
  });

  describe('3. Editorial Columns Primitive', () => {
    const sampleBlocks = [
      { kicker: 'ISU 01', title: 'Distribusi Logistik', body: 'Memastikan stok pangan cukup.' },
      { kicker: 'ISU 02', title: 'Stabilitas Harga', body: 'Menjaga daya beli masyarakat.' },
      { kicker: 'ISU 03', title: 'Dukungan UMKM', body: 'Akses modal murah terjamin.' },
    ];

    it('harus merender 2 kolom saat arrangement horizontal_split', () => {
      const vnode = buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive({
        blocks: sampleBlocks,
        arrangement: 'horizontal_split',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(vnode).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.props.style.flexDirection).toBe('row');
      expect(vnode.props.children).toHaveLength(sampleBlocks.length);
    });

    it('harus merender susunan stacked callouts secara vertikal', () => {
      const vnode = buildEditorialColumnsPrimitive({
        blocks: sampleBlocks,
        arrangement: 'stacked',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(vnode).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.props.style.flexDirection).toBe('column');
      expect(vnode.props.children).toHaveLength(sampleBlocks.length);
    });
  });

  describe('4. Stat Badges, Photo Frame, & Member Signature Primitives', () => {
    it('harus merender lingkaran statistik dengan nilai dan label', () => {
      const vnode = buildStatBadgesPrimitive({
        stats: [{ value: '85%', label: 'Penyaluran Beras' }, { value: '1.2T', label: 'Alokasi Bansos' }],
        displayMode: 'stat_circles',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(vnode.props.children).toHaveLength(2);
    });

    it('harus merender polaroid dan clean editorial frame untuk photo sub-regions', () => {
      const polaroid = buildPhotoFramePrimitive({
        photoBase64: 'data:image/png;base64,dummy',
        style: 'polaroid',
        caption: 'Kunjungan Kerja Pasar Induk',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(polaroid.props.style.boxShadow).toBeDefined();

      const circular = buildPhotoFramePrimitive({
        photoBase64: 'data:image/png;base64,dummy',
        style: 'circular_inset',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });
      expect(circular.props.style.borderRadius).toContain('px');
    });

    it('harus merender atribusi member signature lengkap dengan barcode aksen', () => {
      const vnode = buildMemberSignaturePrimitive({
        name: 'H. AHMAD FAUZI, S.T.',
        title: 'Anggota Komisi IV DPR RI',
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        showBarcodeAccent: true,
      });

      expect(vnode).toBeDefined();
      expect(vnode.props.style.display).toBe('flex');
    });
  });

  describe('5. ParametricSatoriRenderer Engine', () => {
    const basePlan: CompositionPlan = {
      archetype: 'SWISS_DUOTONE_EDITORIAL',
      layoutVariant: 'TOP_BANNER',
      headerPlacement: 'top',
      cardArrangement: 'horizontal_split',
      aspectRatio: '4:5',
      subjectBoundingBox: { x: 0.1, y: 0.3, width: 0.8, height: 0.4 },
      zones: [
        { role: 'PRIMARY_HEADLINE', rect: { x: 54, y: 80, width: 972, height: 260 } },
        { role: 'EDITORIAL_CARDS', rect: { x: 54, y: 820, width: 972, height: 380 } },
      ],
    };

    it('harus menghasilkan struktur VNode berbeda untuk TOP_BANNER vs BOTTOM_ANCHOR', () => {
      const topBannerVNode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
        plan: basePlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        headline: 'JUDUL ATAS BERSIH',
        quote: 'Kutipan singkat berita.',
        memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
        memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
        skinConfig: {
          mastheadStyle: 'MODERN_MINIMAL',
        },
      });

      const bottomAnchorVNode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
        plan: {
          ...basePlan,
          layoutVariant: 'BOTTOM_ANCHOR',
          headerPlacement: 'bottom',
        },
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        headline: 'JUDUL BAWAH BERSIH',
        quote: 'Kutipan singkat berita.',
        memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
        memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
      });

      expect(topBannerVNode.props.style.flexDirection).toBe('column');
      expect(bottomAnchorVNode.props.style.flexDirection).toBe('column');
      // Verifikasi struktur anak berbeda sesuai posisi header
      expect(topBannerVNode).not.toEqual(bottomAnchorVNode);
    });

    it('harus mendukung EDITORIAL_SPLIT dan CENTERED_HERO variants', () => {
      const splitVNode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
        plan: { ...basePlan, layoutVariant: 'EDITORIAL_SPLIT' },
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        headline: 'SPLIT EDITORIAL',
      });
      expect(splitVNode).toBeDefined();

      const heroVNode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
        plan: { ...basePlan, layoutVariant: 'CENTERED_HERO' },
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        headline: 'CENTERED HERO STATEMENT',
      });
      expect(heroVNode).toBeDefined();
    });

    describe('4 Structural Families Visual Differentiation', () => {
      const dummyCopy = {
        statCircles: [
          { topLabel: 'CAPAIAN', value: '100%', description: 'Realisasi program tepat sasaran.' },
          { topLabel: 'PENERIMA', value: '2.500+', description: 'Warga terdata mendapat manfaat.' },
          { topLabel: 'EVALUASI', value: 'TERUJI', description: 'Transparansi anggaran berkala.' },
        ],
        blocks: {
          b1: { header: 'FOKUS KEBIJAKAN', body: 'Deskripsi fokus kerja.' },
          b2: { header: 'AKSI NYATA', body: 'Deskripsi aksi nyata.' },
        },
      };

      function hasStatCircleNode(node: any): boolean {
        if (!node) return false;
        if (
          node.props?.style?.borderRadius === '50%' ||
          node.props?.style?.borderRadius === '58px' ||
          node.props?.style?.borderRadius === '60px'
        ) {
          return true;
        }
        if (Array.isArray(node.props?.children)) {
          return node.props.children.some((c: any) => hasStatCircleNode(c));
        }
        if (node.props?.children && typeof node.props.children === 'object') {
          return hasStatCircleNode(node.props.children);
        }
        return false;
      }

      it('CINEMATIC_MINIMAL: TIDAK boleh memiliki lingkaran statistik sama sekali (0 stat circles) walau statCircles ada di copy', () => {
        const vnode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
          plan: basePlan,
          copy: dummyCopy,
          resolvedColors: dummyColors,
          headline: 'PETUALANGAN DI PUNCAK',
          quote: 'Keindahan alam yang harus dijaga bersama.',
          memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
          memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
          skinConfig: {
            structuralFamily: 'CINEMATIC_MINIMAL',
          },
        });

        expect(hasStatCircleNode(vnode)).toBe(false);
      });

      it('EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE: TIDAK boleh memiliki lingkaran statistik (0 stat circles) dan memiliki 2 kolom berdampingan', () => {
        const vnode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
          plan: basePlan,
          copy: dummyCopy,
          resolvedColors: dummyColors,
          headline: 'TINJAUAN STRATEGIS KEBIJAKAN',
          memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
          memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
          skinConfig: {
            structuralFamily: 'EDITORIAL_MAGAZINE',
          },
        });

        expect(hasStatCircleNode(vnode)).toBe(false);
      });

      it('BOLD_MANIFESTO: TIDAK boleh memiliki lingkaran statistik (0 stat circles) dan memiliki 2 kartu aksi', () => {
        const vnode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
          plan: basePlan,
          copy: dummyCopy,
          resolvedColors: dummyColors,
          headline: 'GERAKAN REFORMASI TOTAL',
          memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
          memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
          skinConfig: {
            structuralFamily: 'BOLD_MANIFESTO',
          },
        });

        expect(hasStatCircleNode(vnode)).toBe(false);
      });

      it('DATA_INFOGRAPHIC: HANYA family ini yang merender lingkaran statistik (buildStatBadgesPrimitive)', () => {
        const vnode = ParametricSatoriRenderer.renderParametricPoster({
          plan: basePlan,
          copy: dummyCopy,
          resolvedColors: dummyColors,
          headline: 'INDIKATOR KINERJA PEMBANGUNAN',
          memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
          memberTitle: 'DPR RI',
          skinConfig: {
            structuralFamily: 'DATA_INFOGRAPHIC',
          },
        });

        expect(hasStatCircleNode(vnode)).toBe(true);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('6. MultiRegionCropper Variance Fallback (Anti-Langit Kosong)', () => {
    it('harus mendeteksi area polos/hampa (avgStdev < 18) di bagian atas dan otomatis menggeser crop ke zona tengah berobjek', async () => {
      // Buat gambar uji 1000x1000:
      // - 0% - 30% atas: PUTIH POLOS (#FFFFFF)
      // - 35% - 75% tengah: BERWARNA KONTRAS TINGGI DENGAN POLA
      const width = 1000;
      const height = 1000;

      // Buat background putih
      const baseWhite = await sharp({
        create: {
          width,
          height,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      // Buat overlay warna-warni kontras di tengah (y: 350 s/d 750)
      const contrastSvg = Buffer.from(`
        <svg width="${width}" height="${height}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <rect x="50" y="360" width="900" height="380" fill="#DC2626" />
          <circle cx="500" cy="550" r="150" fill="#0F172A" />
          <rect x="200" y="420" width="600" height="60" fill="#FBBF24" />
        </svg>
      `);

      const testImageBuffer = await sharp(baseWhite)
        .composite([{ input: contrastSvg, top: 0, left: 0 }])
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      // Minta crop di topRatio: 0.05 (yang jika tanpa fallback akan menghasilkan 100% putih polos)
      const croppedResults = await MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions(
        testImageBuffer,
        [
          {
            id: 'skyCard',
            leftRatio: 0.1,
            topRatio: 0.05, // Zona putih polos
            widthRatio: 0.5,
            heightRatio: 0.25,
          },
        ]
      );

      expect(croppedResults.skyCard).toBeDefined();
      expect(croppedResults.skyCard.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,')).toBe(true);

      // Decode hasil potongan untuk mengecek deviasi standar
      const base64Data = croppedResults.skyCard.replace('data:image/png;base64,', '');
      const cropBuf = Buffer.from(base64Data, 'base64');
      const cropStats = await sharp(cropBuf).stats();
      const numChannels = Math.min(3, cropStats.channels.length);
      const avgStdev = cropStats.channels.slice(0, numChannels).reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.stdev, 0) / numChannels;

      // Karena MultiRegionCropper otomatis menggeser ke zona tengah (fallbackTopRatio ~ 0.38),
      // maka avgStdev potongan gambar pasti tinggi (> 18), TIDAK putih polos lagi!
      expect(avgStdev).toBeGreaterThan(18);
    });
  });
});

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/test/adaptive-typography.spec.ts (105 baris, 4.6 KB) ---

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  getAdaptiveFontSize,
  wordSafeHeadlineTruncate,
  cleanCircleLabel,
  smartSentenceClamp,
  smartTruncate,
} from '../src/creative-poster/core/adaptive-typography.js';

describe('Adaptive Typography & Word-Safe Micro-Copywriting', () => {
  describe('wordSafeHeadlineTruncate', () => {
    it('harus mempertahankan kata "INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN" (27 karakter) secara utuh tanpa terpotong', () => {
      const title = 'INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN';
      const result = wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(title, 34);
      expect(result).toBe('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN');
      expect(result.endsWith('BERKELANJUTA')).toBe(false);
      expect(result.endsWith('BERKELANJUTAN')).toBe(true);
      expect(result).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus memotong pada batas kata tanpa memecah huruf tengah kata dan tanpa tanda ellipsis', () => {
      const longTitle = 'INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN MENUJU DAERAH MAJU DAN SEJAHTERA';
      const result = wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(longTitle, 34);
      expect(result.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(34);
      expect(result).toBe('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN MENUJU');
      expect(result).not.toContain('...');

      // Jika batasnya 30 karakter, kata MENUJU tidak boleh masuk dan tidak boleh terpotong tengah kata
      const result30 = wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(longTitle, 30);
      expect(result30).toBe('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN');
      expect(result30.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30);
      expect(result30).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus mempertahankan kata pertama jika kata pertama sudah sangat panjang', () => {
      const singleLongWord = 'PERTANGGUNGJAWABAN';
      const result = wordSafeHeadlineTruncate(singleLongWord, 10);
      expect(result).toBe('PERTANGGUNGJAWABAN');
    });
  });

  describe('cleanCircleLabel', () => {
    it('harus menghasilkan 1 kata pendek tanpa ellipsis untuk "Cakupan Layanan"', () => {
      const result = cleanCircleLabel('Cakupan Layanan', 11);
      expect(result).toBe('CAKUPAN');
      expect(result).not.toContain('...');
      expect(result).not.toContain(' ');
    });

    it('harus menghasilkan 1 kata pendek untuk "Fasilitas Siaga"', () => {
      const result = cleanCircleLabel('Fasilitas Siaga', 11);
      expect(result).toBe('FASILITAS');
      expect(result).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus membersihkan titik di ujung label', () => {
      const result = cleanCircleLabel('Setidaknya...', 11);
      expect(result).toBe('SETIDAKNYA');
      expect(result).not.toContain('.');
    });
  });

  describe('smartSentenceClamp', () => {
    it('harus memotong pada akhir kalimat utuh terdekat (. atau ;) jika posisi berada di >= 55% batas', () => {
      const text =
        'Pembangunan jalan desa telah rampung tepat waktu. Seluruh warga kini menikmati akses transportasi yang jauh lebih lancar dan aman untuk perekonomian lokal.';
      const clamped = smartSentenceClamp(text, 70);
      expect(clamped).toBe('Pembangunan jalan desa telah rampung tepat waktu.');
      expect(clamped.endsWith('.')).toBe(true);
      expect(clamped).not.toContain('...');
    });

    it('harus mengakhiri kalimat dengan titik bersih jika terpaksa memotong pada batas kata', () => {
      const longSentence =
        'Kebijakan pengawasan distribusi pupuk subsidi bertujuan meningkatkan produktivitas hasil tani daerah konstituen secara berkesinambungan dan terpadu';
      const clamped = smartSentenceClamp(longSentence, 80);
      expect(clamped.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(81);
      expect(clamped.endsWith('.')).toBe(true);
      expect(clamped).not.toContain('...');
    });
  });

  describe('getAdaptiveFontSize', () => {
    it('harus menurunkan ukuran font secara proporsional jika teks memuat kata panjang >= 13 karakter', () => {
      const baseSize = 88;
      const minSize = 48;
      const sizeNormal = getAdaptiveFontSize('PEMBANGUNAN DESA', baseSize, minSize, 20);
      const sizeLongWord = getAdaptiveFontSize('INFRASTRUKTUR BERKELANJUTAN', baseSize, minSize, 20);

      expect(sizeLongWord).toBeLessThan(sizeNormal);
      expect(sizeLongWord).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(52);
      expect(sizeLongWord).toBeLessThanOrEqual(68);
    });
  });

  describe('smartTruncate fallback', () => {
    it('harus mengalihkan teks paragraf panjang (>= 60 char) ke smartSentenceClamp tanpa ellipsis menggantung', () => {
      const paragraph =
        'Pemerintah daerah bersama dinas terkait melakukan inspeksi berkala. Pengawalan terus berlanjut hingga seluruh program terlaksana secara optimal.';
      const truncated = smartTruncate(paragraph, 85);
      expect(truncated.endsWith('.')).toBe(true);
      expect(truncated).not.toContain('...');
    });
  });
});

--- BERKAS: packages/storage/test/poster-visual-qa.spec.ts (345 baris, 11.1 KB) ---

import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import {
  PosterVisualQAGate,
  RevisionPolicy,
  CreativePosterCompositor,
} from '../src/index.js';
import { CompositionPlan, ResolvedColorTokens } from '@polaris/shared-types';

describe('Fase 3: Visual Quality Evaluation & Self-Healing Revision Loop', () => {
  const dummyColors: ResolvedColorTokens = {
    primaryAccent: '#DC2626',
    secondaryAccent: '#FEF08A',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    subTextColor: '#94A3B8',
    cardBackground: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.75)',
    cardBorder: 'rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.3)',
    source: 'BLUEPRINT',
    isDark: true,
  };

  const samplePlan: CompositionPlan = {
    blueprintId: 'TPL-01-HERO',
    archetype: 'POP_OUT_HERO',
    layoutVariant: 'TOP_BANNER',
    headerPlacement: 'top',
    cardArrangement: 'stacked',
    zones: [
      {
        role: 'PRIMARY_HEADLINE' as any,
        rect: { x: 54, y: 80, w: 972, h: 260 },
      },
    ],
    primaryAccent: '#DC2626',
    secondaryAccent: '#FEF08A',
    textColor: '#FFFFFF',
    backgroundBase: '#0F172A',
    surfaceMode: 'NONE',
    subjectBoundingBox: { x: 0.5, y: 0.4, w: 0.45, h: 0.6 },
  };

  describe('1. PosterVisualQAGate (Deterministik & Multimodal)', () => {
    it('harus meluluskan poster jika kontras teks tinggi dan margin aman', async () => {
      // Background gelap pekat (Luminance ~0) dengan teks putih (Luminance ~1.0) -> Kontras sangat tinggi
      const darkBuffer = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1080,
          height: 1350,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 15, g: 23, b: 42 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      const report = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: darkBuffer,
        plan: samplePlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors, // Teks putih
        surfaceMode: 'NONE',
      });

      expect(report.passed).toBe(true);
      expect(report.score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.70);
      expect(report.metrics.contrastRatio).toBeGreaterThan(4.5);
      expect(report.metrics.overflowDetected).toBe(false);
      expect(report.recommendation.action).toBe('NONE');
    });

    it('harus mendeteksi LOW_CONTRAST_HEADLINE dan merekomendasikan ADJUST_SURFACE', async () => {
      // Background putih terang dengan teks putih tanpa scrim -> Kontras sangat rendah (< 1.5:1)
      const brightBuffer = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1080,
          height: 1350,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 250, g: 250, b: 250 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      const report = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: brightBuffer,
        plan: samplePlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors, // Teks putih pada latar putih
        surfaceMode: 'NONE',
      });

      expect(report.passed).toBe(false);
      expect(report.issues.some((i) => i.includes('LOW_CONTRAST_HEADLINE'))).toBe(true);
      expect(report.recommendation.action).toBe('ADJUST_SURFACE');
      expect(report.recommendation.suggestedSurfaceMode).toBe('SCRIM_GRADIENT');
    });

    it('harus mendeteksi ZONE_CANVAS_OVERFLOW jika koordinat zona keluar kanvas', async () => {
      const normalBuffer = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1080,
          height: 1350,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 20, g: 20, b: 30 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      const overflowPlan: CompositionPlan = {
        ...samplePlan,
        zones: [
          {
            role: 'PRIMARY_HEADLINE' as any,
            rect: { x: 50, y: 1200, w: 1000, h: 300 }, // 1200 + 300 = 1500 > 1350 (OVERFLOW!)
          },
        ],
      };

      const report = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: normalBuffer,
        plan: overflowPlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(report.passed).toBe(false);
      expect(report.metrics.overflowDetected).toBe(true);
      expect(report.issues.some((i) => i.includes('ZONE_CANVAS_OVERFLOW'))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('harus mendeteksi MAJOR_SUBJECT_OCCLUSION dan merekomendasikan REGENERATE_IMAGE', async () => {
      const normalBuffer = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1080,
          height: 1350,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 20, g: 20, b: 30 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      const severeCollisionPlan: CompositionPlan = {
        ...samplePlan,
        // Subjek foto menempati posisi yang sama persis dengan headline
        subjectBoundingBox: { x: 54, y: 80, w: 972, h: 260 },
      };

      const report = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: normalBuffer,
        plan: severeCollisionPlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
      });

      expect(report.passed).toBe(false);
      expect(report.metrics.subjectCollisionScore).toBeGreaterThan(0.65);
      expect(report.recommendation.action).toBe('REGENERATE_IMAGE');
    });

    it('harus memadukan evaluasi Multimodal Vision Auditor dan bertahan anggun jika vision gagal', async () => {
      const normalBuffer = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1080,
          height: 1350,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 20, g: 20, b: 30 },
        },
      })
        .png()
        .toBuffer();

      // Mock Vision Auditor berhasil
      const mockVisionAuditor = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        score: 0.95,
        issues: [],
      });

      const report = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: normalBuffer,
        plan: samplePlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        visionAuditor: mockVisionAuditor,
      });

      expect(report.stage).toBe('VISION_AUDIT');
      expect(mockVisionAuditor).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(report.passed).toBe(true);

      // Mock Vision Auditor melempar error koneksi -> harus graceful fallback ke deterministik
      const failingVision = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('Rate limit exceeded'));
      const fallbackReport = await PosterVisualQAGate.evaluateQuality({
        compositeBuffer: normalBuffer,
        plan: samplePlan,
        resolvedColors: dummyColors,
        visionAuditor: failingVision,
      });

      expect(fallbackReport).toBeDefined();
      expect(fallbackReport.score).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });
  });

  describe('2. RevisionPolicy (Zero-Waste Self-Healing Logic)', () => {
    it('harus mengembalikan action NONE jika poster telah lulus QA', () => {
      const decision = RevisionPolicy.evaluateDecision({
        report: {
          passed: true,
          score: 0.92,
          stage: 'DETERMINISTIC',
          issues: [],
          metrics: {
            marginIntegrityScore: 1.0,
            contrastRatio: 6.5,
            overflowDetected: false,
            subjectCollisionScore: 0.1,
          },
          recommendation: { action: 'NONE' },
        },
      });

      expect(decision.action).toBe('NONE');
      expect(decision.healed).toBe(false);
    });

    it('harus memutuskan RE_RENDER_SATORI dengan SCRIM_GRADIENT untuk isu kontras rendah', () => {
      const decision = RevisionPolicy.evaluateDecision({
        report: {
          passed: false,
          score: 0.65,
          stage: 'DETERMINISTIC',
          issues: ['LOW_CONTRAST_HEADLINE'],
          metrics: {
            marginIntegrityScore: 1.0,
            contrastRatio: 2.2,
            overflowDetected: false,
            subjectCollisionScore: 0.0,
          },
          recommendation: {
            action: 'ADJUST_SURFACE',
            suggestedSurfaceMode: 'SCRIM_GRADIENT',
          },
        },
        currentSurfaceMode: 'NONE',
      });

      expect(decision.action).toBe('RE_RENDER_SATORI');
      expect(decision.healed).toBe(true);
      expect(decision.newSurfaceMode).toBe('SCRIM_GRADIENT');
    });

    it('harus mengizinkan REGENERATE_IMAGE hanya 1 kali jika terjadi benturan berat', () => {
      // Percobaan pertama (imageRegenerationCount === 0) -> Boleh regenerate AI
      const firstDecision = RevisionPolicy.evaluateDecision({
        report: {
          passed: false,
          score: 0.40,
          stage: 'DETERMINISTIC',
          issues: ['MAJOR_SUBJECT_OCCLUSION'],
          metrics: {
            marginIntegrityScore: 1.0,
            contrastRatio: 4.5,
            overflowDetected: false,
            subjectCollisionScore: 0.85,
          },
          recommendation: {
            action: 'REGENERATE_IMAGE',
            reason: 'Subjek menutupi headline',
          },
        },
        imageRegenerationCount: 0,
        maxImageRegenerations: 1,
      });

      expect(firstDecision.action).toBe('REGENERATE_IMAGE');
      expect(firstDecision.healed).toBe(false);

      // Percobaan kedua (imageRegenerationCount >= 1) -> DILARANG looping! Wajib fallback ke SOLID_BAND satori
      const secondDecision = RevisionPolicy.evaluateDecision({
        report: {
          passed: false,
          score: 0.40,
          stage: 'DETERMINISTIC',
          issues: ['MAJOR_SUBJECT_OCCLUSION'],
          metrics: {
            marginIntegrityScore: 1.0,
            contrastRatio: 4.5,
            overflowDetected: false,
            subjectCollisionScore: 0.85,
          },
          recommendation: {
            action: 'REGENERATE_IMAGE',
            reason: 'Subjek masih menutupi headline',
          },
        },
        imageRegenerationCount: 1,
        maxImageRegenerations: 1,
      });

      expect(secondDecision.action).toBe('RE_RENDER_SATORI');
      expect(secondDecision.healed).toBe(true);
      expect(secondDecision.newSurfaceMode).toBe('SOLID_BAND');
    });
  });

  describe('3. End-to-End Self-Healing di CreativePosterCompositor', () => {
    it('harus otomatis menyembuhkan kontras rendah melalui re-render Satori saat autoHealContrast aktif', async () => {
      // Buat background putih terang
      const brightBackground = await sharp({
        create: {
          width: 1000,
          height: 1000,
          channels: 3,
          background: { r: 252, g: 252, b: 252 },
        },
      })
        .jpeg()
        .toBuffer();

      // Jalankan composePoster dengan teks putih pada background putih (kontras rendah)
      // tetapi dengan autoHealContrast: true
      const result = await CreativePosterCompositor.composePoster({
        backgroundBuffer: brightBackground,
        headline: 'ADVOKASI ANGGARAN RAKYAT',
        quote: 'Keterbukaan informasi demi keadilan sosial.',
        memberName: 'AHMAD FAUZI',
        memberTitle: 'KOMISI IV DPR RI',
        archetype: 'SWISS_DUOTONE_EDITORIAL',
        primaryAccentColor: '#DC2626',
        resolvedColors: {
          ...dummyColors,
          textColor: '#FFFFFF', // Teks putih pada latar terang -> memicu self-healing
          backgroundBase: '#FFFFFF',
        },
        canvasWidth: 1080,
        canvasHeight: 1350,
        outputFormat: 'webp',
        enableVisualQA: true,
        autoHealContrast: true,
      });

      expect(result).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.qaReport).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.qaReport?.recommendation.reason).toContain('Self-healed via Satori');
    });
  });
});
